`CLAUDE.md`:

```md
# BREACH: working notes for Claude

A browser hacking terminal game. Next.js (App Router) + TypeScript + Tailwind, static export (`output: 'export'`).
No backend, no network calls, no external assets. Progress lives in localStorage.

## Commands

- `npm run dev`: dev server
- `npm test`: Vitest. Runs every mission's intended solution plus engine tests.
- `npm run build`: type-checks and writes the static site to `out/`

Run `npm test` and `npm run build` before calling a change done.

## Architecture

Strict layers. Dependencies only point downward.

```
src/app/            Next shell (layout, page, globals.css with palettes and CRT effects, robots.ts/sitemap.ts,
                    error.tsx + global-error.tsx as last-resort crash screens)
src/components/     React UI. Reads session state, never mutates game rules.
src/lib/            save (localStorage), audio (Web Audio), themes, progress (summary, unlocks, share card),
                    site (the one SITE_URL), analytics (Vercel event wrapper), origins (CORS allow-list matching,
                    shared with the Edge Functions), feedback (mailto builder), version
src/engine/         Pure game logic. No React, no DOM, no storage. Runs under Node in tests.
  types.ts          MachineDef, MissionDef, Line, SoundEvent, ... (the data contract)
  session.ts        GameSession: one attempt at one mission. Trace, prompts, pivoting, flag, endings, completion.
  commands/         One file per domain (files, network, crypto, system). index.ts is the registry.
  fs.ts             Flat-map virtual filesystem, path normalisation, permissions
  sql.ts            Tiny SQL evaluator for the staff login puzzle
  encoding.ts       base64, rot13, hex (used by decode and by mission data)
  scoring.ts        Mission score and ranks
src/missions/       Mission DATA ONLY. One file per mission, plus index.ts and shared.ts.
tests/              missions.test.ts (plays every mission), engine.test.ts
```

Key ideas:

- **Machines form a graph.** Each machine lists `neighbors` (IPs). `scan` and `connect` only reach neighbours.
  Pivoting means connecting from a compromised host to one of its neighbours. `exit` pops back up the stack.
- **Input goes through `GameSession.input(raw)`.** It returns an `Out` (lines + sounds + clear/quit flags).
  The UI prints those lines with a typing effect and plays the sounds. Tests call the same method.
- **Pending prompts** (`pending`) capture the next line: password, recovery questions (triggered by `?` at a
  password prompt), web form username/password, and the ending choice. `inputMode()` tells the UI how to render the prompt.
- **Trace** is a 0-100 meter. Noisy actions call `session.noise(n, out)`. A running security process
  (`security: true` in `processes`) multiplies gain by 1.5. Killing it lowers trace by 20. At 100 the attempt fails.
- **The flag check** is exact string match in `submitFlag`. Missions with `endings` open a choice after the flag.
- **Scoring** is computed from elapsed seconds, trace and hints used (`engine/scoring.ts`).

## Adding a mission

1. Create `src/missions/mNN-name.ts` that default-exports a `MissionDef`. Copy an existing one as a template.
   - `machines`: include the rig (`id: 'rig'`, use `makeRig` from `shared.ts`) plus every target.
   - `start`: the rig machine id and the starting directory.
   - `flag`, `hints` (exactly three, vague to specific), `briefing`, `debrief`.
   - `solution`: the exact inputs a player types, in order, to win. Include password answers and `submit`. Tests replay this.
   - Optional: `hashes` (for `crack`), `inbox` (for `mail`), `endings` (for a choice after the flag).
2. Append it to `MISSIONS` in `src/missions/index.ts`. Keep the array in campaign order.
3. Run `npm test`. The new mission is covered automatically: it must be beatable, must reject a wrong flag,
   and must have three hints, a rig, and valid neighbour IPs.

Build data with helpers from `engine/encoding.ts` (`toBase64`, `rot13`, `toHex`) so the strings in
the files and the solution cannot drift apart.

## Adding a command

Write a `CommandSpec` (name, usage, summary, example, run) in a file under `src/engine/commands/`, add it to the list in
`commands/index.ts`. `example` is a runnable line shown by `help <name>`, and `qol.test.ts` requires it to be non-empty.
Use `s.noise(n, out)` for anything that should raise trace. `help`, `help <name>`, "Did you mean" and tab completion pick it up.
Use `s.discover(category, text)` where a command reveals something the clue log should keep (see `engine/clues.ts`).

## Conventions

- Shell-like prompts show the user, host and directory. Root uses `#`.
- Password input is never echoed. Only `*` characters are shown.
- Output kinds (`Line.kind`) map to colours in `Terminal.tsx`. Keep new kinds in sync there.
- Save access is wrapped in try/catch everywhere. The game must run with storage blocked.
- Keep the engine free of React and DOM references so the tests stay fast and Node-only.
- Mobile: inputs are 16px to stop iOS zoom. Quick keys sit above the keyboard. Nothing may force horizontal page scroll
  except tables and code (the logo and quick-key bar scroll inside their own containers).

## Gotchas

- `tokenize` in `engine/sql.ts` is the SQL lexer. The shell uses `splitCommand` in `session.ts`. Do not mix them.
- The web form evaluates WHERE with AND binding tighter than OR. That is intentional: it is what makes the
  injection lesson real.
- `tests/missions.test.ts` checks that a mission is beatable, not that it is fun. Playtest changes to briefings and hints in the browser.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# BREACH

A browser hacking terminal game. Take freelance jobs from anonymous clients, breach fake networks built for the
mission, and learn real security ideas — weak passwords, bad hashes, SQL injection, social engineering — by doing
them. Nothing here touches a real system, and the leaderboard can't be faked because the browser never gets to
grade its own run.

**Play it: [breachgame.xyz](https://breachgame.xyz)**

![BREACH landing page](docs/landing.jpg)
![A mission in progress](docs/mission.jpg)

## Features

- **Kali-style terminal** — a real shell: tab completion, command history, piping, aliases, password prompts that
  don't echo, the works.
- **Three difficulty tiers** — Easy, Medium and Hard change trace speed, hint limits, decoys and (on Hard) a clock.
  The solution never changes, only how forgiving the run is.
- **8-job story campaign** — a continuing plot across QUILL's postings, playable on any tier, any number of times.
- **Daily challenge** — one procedurally generated job, the same for everyone, every day, under five minutes. Every
  Friday it's the harder **Hard Friday** variant: no hints, faster trace, every decoy live, its own badge and its
  own leaderboard (dailies are already scoped per calendar day).
- **Real-world security debriefs** — every job and every daily ends with what the mistake actually is, in the field.
- **Achievements**, a **profile**, and an **Ops Log** that replays any past attempt command-by-command, at the
  engine's own speed, so you can watch exactly what happened.
- **Cloud save with a recovery key** — progress follows you across devices without an email or a password; the key
  is shown once and the server only ever stores a hash of it.

## How the anti-cheat works

The browser **never sends a score**. When you finish a run, the client sends the one thing it actually knows for
certain: the commands you typed, and when. A Supabase Edge Function takes that log and replays it, command by
command, through the exact same deterministic engine that ran in your browser — same mission, same tier, same
rules — and computes the score itself, from the replay, from scratch. If the replay doesn't reach a `submit` with
the right flag, or the timing between commands is faster than a person can plausibly type (checked against a
minimum gap and a minimum average pace), the run is rejected outright. If a client sends a claimed score anyway,
the server only accepts it when it matches the replay exactly.

That validator (`src/engine/replay.ts`) is pure: no DOM, no storage, no network. It's the same module the game
itself uses to drive a mission, bundled into the Edge Function by `scripts/build-edge.mjs` and tested in
`tests/replay.test.ts` and `tests/missions.test.ts`, which play every mission's real solution through it. The
server trusts its own replay, never the client's word.

## Tech stack

- **Next.js** (App Router, static export) + **TypeScript**, styled with **Tailwind**
- **Vitest** for the engine, mission and UI-logic tests
- **Supabase**: Postgres (read-only to the browser via RLS) + Edge Functions (Deno) for every write
- **Web Audio API**: real recordings for keystrokes and every discrete sound effect; a small generative music
  engine for the soundtrack's alternate tracks and its trace-driven tension layer (see `assets/audio/LICENSES.md`)

## Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    subgraph Browser
        UI[React UI<br/>src/components]
        Engine[Game engine<br/>src/engine<br/>pure, no DOM]
        Save[(localStorage<br/>src/lib/save)]
        UI --> Engine
        Engine --> Save
    end

    subgraph Supabase
        Submit[submit-run<br/>Edge Function]
        Account[account<br/>Edge Function]
        SharedEngine[_shared/engine.js<br/>bundled copy of src/engine]
        DB[(Postgres<br/>RLS: read-only to anon)]
        Submit --> SharedEngine
        Submit --> DB
        Account --> DB
    end

    UI -- "command log + timing, never a score" --> Submit
    UI -- "handle, recovery key, sync token" --> Account
    UI -- "read-only board queries" --> DB
```

The engine is the one piece of logic that has to behave identically in two places — the browser and the Edge
Function — so it's the same source, not a reimplementation: `scripts/build-edge.mjs` bundles `src/engine` into
`supabase/functions/_shared/engine.js` as part of `npm run build`, and `tests/origins.test.ts` /
`tests/replay.test.ts` cover the pieces both sides share (CORS origin matching, the replay validator).

## Project structure

```
src/app/            Next shell: layout, page, globals.css, robots.ts/sitemap.ts, crash screens
src/components/     React UI. Reads session state, never mutates game rules.
src/lib/            save, audio, music, themes, progress, site, analytics, origins, feedback, leaderboard, cloud...
src/engine/         Pure game logic (no React, no DOM, no storage): session, commands, fs, sql, encoding, scoring
src/missions/       Mission data: one file per campaign job, plus the daily generator's shared pieces
supabase/
  functions/        account and submit-run Edge Functions, plus the bundled shared engine
  migrations/       Postgres schema: players, runs, rate_limits, events (anonymous funnel analytics), RLS policies
tests/              missions.test.ts (plays every mission), engine.test.ts, replay.test.ts, and the rest
scripts/            build-edge.mjs (bundles the engine), build-audio.mjs (builds public/audio/ from raw recordings)
assets/audio/       LICENSES.md — every audio file's source, author and license
```

## Running it locally

```bash
npm install
npm run dev       # http://localhost:3000
```

Cloud save and the leaderboard are optional: without `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` and
`NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` set (copy `.env.example` to `.env.local`), the game runs entirely offline —
progress just stays on the device.

## Tests

```bash
npm test     # vitest run — 544 tests across 23 files
npm run build  # type-checks and writes the static site to out/
```

`tests/missions.test.ts` plays every campaign job's and every generated daily's real solution end to end, on every
tier, and checks it's beatable, rejects a wrong flag, and has a rig, three hints and valid neighbour IPs.

## Security

- **Read-only database.** Row-level security grants `select` on `players` and `runs` to anonymous and authenticated
  roles and nothing else — no insert, update or delete policy exists for them. `rate_limits` has RLS on with no
  policies at all: nothing but the service role can see it.
- **Every write goes through an Edge Function**, using the service-role key, which never reaches the browser.
- **Recovery keys are never stored in readable form.** They're hashed with HMAC under a server-side pepper
  (`RECOVERY_PEPPER`, an environment variable, never in source); sync tokens are stored as a SHA-256 hash.
- **Rate limiting** on submissions, account restores (per caller and globally) and sync, enforced server-side in
  Postgres, independent of anything the client claims.
- **An origin allow-list** (`src/lib/origins.ts`, shared with the Edge Functions) gates CORS: only the game's own
  hosts get `access-control-allow-origin` back.
- Found a way around any of this? **breachgame.contact@gmail.com** — leaderboard exploits are genuinely welcome to
  report.

## Credits

Built with [Next.js](https://nextjs.org/) and [Supabase](https://supabase.com/). Interface sounds by
[Kenney](https://kenney.nl/) (CC0). Keyboard, alarm, modem and ambience recordings from Freesound contributors
stu556, za-games, wtermini, alphatone, rhinenux, soundandmelodies and music_is_wiggly_air (CC0). Soundtrack
"Cyberpunk Drone" by Leberche (Pixabay Content License). Full list with links in
[`assets/audio/LICENSES.md`](assets/audio/LICENSES.md).

## License

Code is [MIT](LICENSE). Audio assets keep their own licenses — see
[`assets/audio/LICENSES.md`](assets/audio/LICENSES.md).

---

`assets/audio/LICENSES.md`:

```md
# Audio licenses

Every recording BREACH ships is CC0 (public domain) or covered by the Pixabay Content License. Raw sources live in
`sound-source/` on the machine that built them (never committed — see `.gitignore`); this file documents what was
used and where it came from. `scripts/build-audio.mjs` trims, normalises and fades each one into the files actually
shipped under `public/audio/`.

## Kenney Interface Sounds (CC0)

- **Pack**: Interface Sounds (1.0)
- **Author**: Kenney (www.kenney.nl)
- **License**: CC0 1.0 Universal — <http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/>
- **Source**: <https://kenney.nl/assets/interface-sounds>
- **Files used**: `select_003`, `error_002`, `error_006`, `error_008`, `open_002`, `confirmation_002`,
  `confirmation_004`, `tick_001`, `tick_004`, `glitch_002`, `bong_001`, `click_002`, `click_004`, `question_002`,
  `scroll_001`, `maximize_003`, `switch_002`, `switch_006`, `pluck_002` (`.ogg`, converted to `.mp3`)

Credit is not mandatory under CC0, but Kenney is credited on the About page anyway.

## Freesound recordings (CC0)

All of the following are Freesound's "CC0" license (Creative Commons Zero / public domain). The URL pattern
`https://freesound.org/people/<user>/sounds/<id>/` is Freesound's standard sound-page link, built from each file's
ID and uploader.

| Original file | Author | Freesound ID | URL |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `mechanical-keyboard-typing-treble-version.wav` | stu556 | 450282 | <https://freesound.org/people/stu556/sounds/450282/> |
| `deep-boom.wav` | za-games | 539968 | <https://freesound.org/people/za-games/sounds/539968/> |
| `the-sound-of-dial-up-internet.mp3` | wtermini | 546450 | <https://freesound.org/people/wtermini/sounds/546450/> |
| `papatone-pictures-klaxon-rapid.wav` | alphatone | 555465 | <https://freesound.org/people/alphatone/sounds/555465/> |
| `blue-switch-mechanical-keyboard-typing.wav` | rhinenux | 631609 | <https://freesound.org/people/rhinenux/sounds/631609/> |
| `sfx-roomtone-server-room-hum.wav` | soundandmelodies | 776267 | <https://freesound.org/people/soundandmelodies/sounds/776267/> |
| `heartbeat-sub-kick-softer.wav` | music_is_wiggly_air | 784658 | <https://freesound.org/people/music_is_wiggly_air/sounds/784658/> |

What each one became in the build:

- The two keyboard recordings → every `key-*`, `space-1`, `enter-1` and `backspace-1` clip under `public/audio/keys/`.
- The klaxon → `sfx/alert.mp3` (one blast) and `sfx/alarm.mp3` (a build-up of blasts).
- The deep boom → `sfx/boom.mp3`.
- The dial-up recording → `sfx/chatter.mp3` (the cracker's chatter, an earlier slice) and `sfx/win.mp3` /
  `sfx/transfer.mp3` (the same later, settled slice, used for both — "short slice of the modem into a satisfying
  confirm sound").
- The server room hum → `ambient/server-hum.mp3`, looped quietly under everything.
- The heartbeat → `music/heartbeat.mp3`, triggered by the music engine's procedural tension layer above 75% trace.

## Pixabay Content License

| Original file | Title | Artist | Pixabay track ID |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `leberch-cyberpunk-drone-375259.mp3` | Cyberpunk Drone | Leberche | 375259 |

License: [Pixabay Content License](https://pixabay.com/service/license-summary/) — free for commercial and
non-commercial use, no attribution required. Credited on the About page anyway. (The exact pixabay.com page URL for
this track was not re-verified when writing this file; search Pixabay for the track ID above if you need the direct
link again.)

Became `public/audio/music/cyberpunk-drone.mp3`, the game's default soundtrack.

## What stayed procedural

The generative soundtrack in `src/lib/music.ts` (the Night Shift / Signal / Cold Room tracks, and the tension layer
that builds with trace) is synthesised with the Web Audio API, not a recording — kept on purpose, since those tracks
exist specifically to never loop audibly over a long session, which a fixed recording can't do. The tension layer's
timing and density are still procedural even on the Cyberpunk Drone track; only the heartbeat *sound* itself was
swapped from a synthesised sine sweep to the real recording above.

```

`next.config.mjs`:

```mjs
import path from 'node:path';

/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */
const nextConfig = {
  output: 'export',
  reactStrictMode: true,
  trailingSlash: true,
  // Relative asset URLs so the export also works from a nested folder (itch.io serves from a CDN subpath).
  assetPrefix: './',
  // Several lockfiles exist above this folder; pin tracing to the project root.
  outputFileTracingRoot: path.resolve('.'),
};

export default nextConfig;

```

`scripts/build-audio.mjs`:

```mjs
// Builds public/audio/ from the raw recordings in sound-source/ (never committed; see .gitignore).
// Run manually with `npm run audio:build` after dropping new files into sound-source/. Requires ffmpeg on PATH.
//
// Each entry below is one clip: a source file, where to cut it, and how loud to make it. Effects are trimmed,
// peak-normalised and given short fades so nothing clicks. Music and the ambient bed are loudness-normalised
// (loudnorm) instead, since they are long enough for that to be meaningful.
//
// Sources, all CC0 or Pixabay Content License (see assets/audio/LICENSES.md for credits):
//   Kenney Interface Sounds (CC0)              -- most UI and feedback sounds
//   Freesound recordings (CC0, see LICENSES.md) -- keyboards, klaxon, boom, modem, hum, heartbeat
//   Pixabay "Cyberpunk Drone" by Leberche        -- the main soundtrack

import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';

const run = promisify(execFile);
const SRC = path.resolve('sound-source');
const OUT = path.resolve('public', 'audio');
const KENNEY = path.join(SRC, 'kenney_interface-sounds', 'Audio');

function src(file) {
  return path.join(SRC, file);
}

async function ffmpeg(args) {
  await run('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', ...args]);
}

/** A short effect: trim to [start, start+duration], fade in/out, peak-normalise, mono, mp3. */
async function clip(inputFile, outFile, { start = 0, duration, fadeIn = 0.003, fadeOut = 0.02, gainDb = 0, mono = true } = {}) {
  const out = path.join(OUT, outFile);
  await mkdir(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  const fadeOutStart = duration !== undefined ? Math.max(0, duration - fadeOut) : undefined;
  const filters = [gainDb ? `volume=${gainDb}dB` : null, `afade=t=in:d=${fadeIn}`, fadeOutStart !== undefined ? `afade=t=out:st=${fadeOutStart}:d=${fadeOut}` : null].filter(Boolean).join(',');
  const args = ['-ss', String(start)];
  if (duration !== undefined) args.push('-t', String(duration));
  args.push('-i', inputFile, '-af', filters, '-ar', '44100');
  if (mono) args.push('-ac', '1');
  args.push('-b:a', '96k', out);
  await ffmpeg(args);
  console.log(`wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), out)}`);
}

/** Music or an ambient loop: loudness-normalised, short fades at both ends, stereo kept for music. */
async function longClip(inputFile, outFile, { start = 0, duration, targetLufs = -16, mono = false, fade = 1.0 } = {}) {
  const out = path.join(OUT, outFile);
  await mkdir(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  const fadeOutStart = duration !== undefined ? Math.max(0, duration - fade) : undefined;
  const filters = [`loudnorm=I=${targetLufs}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11`, `afade=t=in:d=${fade}`, fadeOutStart !== undefined ? `afade=t=out:st=${fadeOutStart}:d=${fade}` : null].filter(Boolean).join(',');
  const args = ['-ss', String(start)];
  if (duration !== undefined) args.push('-t', String(duration));
  args.push('-i', inputFile, '-af', filters, '-ar', '44100');
  if (mono) args.push('-ac', '1');
  args.push('-b:a', '128k', out);
  await ffmpeg(args);
  console.log(`wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), out)}`);
}

const KEYBOARD_TREBLE = src('450282__stu556__mechanical-keyboard-typing-treble-version.wav');
const KEYBOARD_BLUE = src('631609__rhinenux__blue-switch-mechanical-keyboard-typing.wav');
const KLAXON = src('555465__alphatone__papatone-pictures-klaxon-rapid.wav');
const BOOM = src('539968__za-games__deep-boom.wav');
const MODEM = src('546450__wtermini__the-sound-of-dial-up-internet.mp3');
const HUM = src('776267__soundandmelodies__sfx-roomtone-server-room-hum.wav');
const HEARTBEAT = src('784658__music_is_wiggly_air__heartbeat-sub-kick-softer.wav');
const CYBERPUNK_DRONE = src('leberch-cyberpunk-drone-375259.mp3');

function kenney(name) {
  return path.join(KENNEY, name);
}

async function main() {
  // Keystrokes: isolated single hits picked by hand from silence-gap timing in each recording (see the
  // comment in src/lib/audio.ts for how they are played back). Six or more, plus space, enter and backspace.
  const keys = [
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-1.mp3', 1.577, 0.18],
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-2.mp3', 2.92, 0.2],
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-3.mp3', 5.684, 0.18],
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-4.mp3', 14.015, 0.18],
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-5.mp3', 51.923, 0.18],
    [KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'key-6.mp3', 57.8, 0.18],
    [KEYBOARD_BLUE, 'key-7.mp3', 2.411, 0.16],
    [KEYBOARD_BLUE, 'key-8.mp3', 3.253, 0.16],
  ];
  for (const [file, out, start, duration] of keys) await clip(file, `keys/${out}`, { start, duration, gainDb: 2 });

  await clip(KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'keys/space-1.mp3', { start: 2.025, duration: 0.3, gainDb: 1 });
  await clip(KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'keys/enter-1.mp3', { start: 82.739, duration: 0.35, gainDb: 3 });
  await clip(KEYBOARD_TREBLE, 'keys/backspace-1.mp3', { start: 5.595, duration: 0.09, gainDb: 0 });

  // UI and feedback effects, mostly Kenney with a few Freesound moments where the real recording does it better.
  await clip(kenney('select_003.ogg'), 'sfx/command.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('error_002.ogg'), 'sfx/error.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('open_002.ogg'), 'sfx/enter.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('confirmation_002.ogg'), 'sfx/granted.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('error_006.ogg'), 'sfx/denied.mp3', { gainDb: 3 });
  await clip(kenney('tick_001.ogg'), 'sfx/trace.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('glitch_002.ogg'), 'sfx/glitch.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('tick_004.ogg'), 'sfx/tick.mp3', { gainDb: 3 });
  await clip(kenney('bong_001.ogg'), 'sfx/rank.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('confirmation_004.ogg'), 'sfx/sting.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('error_008.ogg'), 'sfx/fail.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('click_002.ogg'), 'sfx/ping.mp3', { gainDb: 0 });
  await clip(kenney('question_002.ogg'), 'sfx/hint.mp3', { gainDb: 2 });
  await clip(kenney('scroll_001.ogg'), 'sfx/hover.mp3', { gainDb: -6 });
  await clip(kenney('click_004.ogg'), 'sfx/click.mp3', { gainDb: 1 });
  await clip(kenney('maximize_003.ogg'), 'sfx/transition.mp3', { gainDb: 1 });
  await clip(kenney('switch_002.ogg'), 'sfx/crt-on.mp3', { gainDb: 1 });
  await clip(kenney('switch_006.ogg'), 'sfx/crt-off.mp3', { gainDb: 1 });
  await clip(kenney('pluck_002.ogg'), 'sfx/achievement.mp3', { gainDb: 3 });

  // Real recordings where they beat a UI pack: the klaxon for trace warnings, the boom for a traced-out ending,
  // and two different slices of the same dial-up recording for the cracker's chatter and the transfer confirm.
  await clip(KLAXON, 'sfx/alert.mp3', { start: 0.09, duration: 0.45, gainDb: -2 });
  await clip(KLAXON, 'sfx/alarm.mp3', { start: 1.38, duration: 2.2, gainDb: -2 });
  await clip(BOOM, 'sfx/boom.mp3', { duration: 1.6, gainDb: 0, fadeOut: 0.3 });
  await clip(MODEM, 'sfx/chatter.mp3', { start: 2.2, duration: 1.1, gainDb: -1 });
  await clip(MODEM, 'sfx/win.mp3', { start: 27.3, duration: 1.2, gainDb: 2, fadeOut: 0.25 });
  await clip(MODEM, 'sfx/transfer.mp3', { start: 27.3, duration: 1.2, gainDb: 2, fadeOut: 0.25 });

  // Music and the ambient bed. The heartbeat keeps the drum-hit length (loudnorm would mangle something this short).
  await longClip(CYBERPUNK_DRONE, 'music/cyberpunk-drone.mp3', { fade: 1.5 });
  await longClip(HUM, 'ambient/server-hum.mp3', { mono: true, fade: 1.2 });
  await clip(HEARTBEAT, 'music/heartbeat.mp3', { duration: 0.25, fadeIn: 0.001, fadeOut: 0.04, gainDb: 1, mono: true });

  console.log('audio build done.');
}

main().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err.stderr ?? err.message ?? err);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

```

`scripts/build-edge.mjs`:

```mjs
// Bundles the game engine for the Supabase Edge Functions. The output lives in supabase/functions/_shared/, the folder
// Supabase treats as shared code, so every function that imports it deploys with it.
// Runs as part of `npm run build`, so the bundle can never be older than the source it was built from.

import { build } from 'esbuild';
import path from 'node:path';

const entry = path.resolve('supabase/functions/_shared/engine-entry.ts');
const outfile = path.resolve('supabase/functions/_shared/engine.js');

await build({
  entryPoints: [entry],
  bundle: true,
  format: 'esm',
  platform: 'neutral',
  target: 'es2022',
  outfile,
  logLevel: 'warning',
});
console.log(`bundled ${path.relative(process.cwd(), outfile)}`);

```

`scripts/make-og.mjs`:

```mjs
// Builds public/og.png: the 1200x630 card used by Open Graph (WhatsApp, Discord, Slack) and Twitter.
// Runs before `next build`, so the static export ships the image. Drawn in the game's style: phosphor green on black,
// scanlines, a glowing title, a live-looking prompt and attack arcs.

import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import sharp from 'sharp';

const W = 1200;
const H = 630;
const GREEN = '#33ff66';
const DIM = '#1b7a3a';
const EDGE = '#14412a';

// Arcs live in the empty right-hand column, so they never cross the title, the tagline or the prompt.
const arcs = [
  { x1: 860, y1: 160, x2: 1120, y2: 120 },
  { x1: 880, y1: 120, x2: 1080, y2: 230 },
  { x1: 860, y1: 470, x2: 1120, y2: 540 },
];

const arcPath = ({ x1, y1, x2, y2 }) => {
  const cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;
  const cy = Math.min(y1, y2) - Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1) * 0.35;
  return `<path d="M${x1} ${y1} Q${cx} ${cy} ${x2} ${y2}" fill="none" stroke="${GREEN}" stroke-width="2" opacity="0.55"/>
  <circle cx="${x2}" cy="${y2}" r="5" fill="${GREEN}" opacity="0.9"/>`;
};

const scan = Array.from({ length: Math.ceil(H / 4) }, (_, i) => `<rect x="0" y="${i * 4 + 2}" width="${W}" height="1" fill="#000" opacity="0.28"/>`).join('');

const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}">
  <defs>
    <radialGradient id="bg" cx="50%" cy="45%" r="75%">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#06180c"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#010402"/>
    </radialGradient>
    <filter id="glow" x="-20%" y="-40%" width="140%" height="180%">
      <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="9" result="b"/>
      <feMerge><feMergeNode in="b"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge>
    </filter>
  </defs>
  <rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="url(#bg)"/>
  ${arcs.map(arcPath).join('\n  ')}
  <rect x="48" y="48" width="${W - 96}" height="${H - 96}" fill="none" stroke="${EDGE}" stroke-width="3" rx="6"/>
  <text x="96" y="250" font-family="Consolas, 'Courier New', monospace" font-size="150" font-weight="700" letter-spacing="28" fill="${GREEN}" filter="url(#glow)">BREACH</text>
  <text x="100" y="318" font-family="Consolas, 'Courier New', monospace" font-size="34" fill="${GREEN}" opacity="0.9">Take jobs. Breach fake networks. Mind your trace.</text>
  <text x="100" y="400" font-family="Consolas, 'Courier New', monospace" font-size="30" fill="${GREEN}" filter="url(#glow)">guest@breach:~$ start</text>
  <rect x="100" y="420" width="18" height="30" fill="${GREEN}" opacity="0.9"/>
  <g font-family="Consolas, 'Courier New', monospace" font-size="24" letter-spacing="4">
    <text x="100" y="530" fill="${DIM}">EASY</text>
    <text x="260" y="530" fill="${DIM}">MEDIUM</text>
    <text x="440" y="530" fill="${DIM}">HARD</text>
    <text x="100" y="570" fill="${GREEN}" opacity="0.7">FREELANCE INTRUSION TERMINAL</text>
  </g>
  ${scan}
</svg>`;

const out = path.resolve('public', 'og.png');
await mkdir(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
const png = await sharp(Buffer.from(svg)).png({ compressionLevel: 9 }).toBuffer();
await writeFile(out, png);
console.log(`wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), out)} (${png.length} bytes)`);

```

`src/app/error.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Catches a render error anywhere under the root layout, so a crash shows "SYSTEM FAULT" instead of a blank page.
// Styled like the rest of the game; globals.css is still in scope here, since the root layout (which loads it) stays
// mounted around this boundary. See global-error.tsx for the rarer case where the layout itself fails.

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import Icon from '../components/Icon';
import { Header, Screen } from '../components/ui';

export default function Error({ error, reset }: { error: Error & { digest?: string }; reset: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('[BREACH] system fault:', error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="SYSTEM FAULT" subtitle="Something in the rig crashed. Your save lives on this device and should still be there." icon="alert" />
      <p className="mb-8 text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">{error.digest ? `Fault code: ${error.digest}` : 'No fault code was given.'}</p>
      <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
          <Icon name="reset" size={16} />
          Reload BREACH
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={reset}>
          Try again without reloading
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

```

`src/app/global-error.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Last resort: catches an error in the root layout itself, which error.tsx cannot cover (it lives inside that layout).
// This replaces <html> and <body> entirely, so it is kept deliberately plain — no imported components, no dependency
// on globals.css having loaded — because its one job is to never fail itself.

export default function GlobalError({ reset }: { error: Error & { digest?: string }; reset: () => void }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body style={{ margin: 0, background: '#030a05', color: '#33ff66', fontFamily: 'Consolas, "Courier New", monospace' }}>
        <main
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            minHeight: '100dvh',
            flexDirection: 'column',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '1.5rem',
            padding: '1.5rem',
            textAlign: 'center',
          }}
        >
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.25em', margin: 0 }}>SYSTEM FAULT</h1>
          <p style={{ maxWidth: '28rem', fontSize: '0.9rem', color: '#1b7a3a', margin: 0 }}>
            BREACH could not start. Reloading usually fixes it; your save lives on this device and is untouched.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
            style={{
              border: '1px solid #1fbf4a',
              background: 'transparent',
              color: '#33ff66',
              padding: '0.6rem 1.4rem',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
              fontSize: '0.9rem',
              letterSpacing: '0.06em',
              textTransform: 'uppercase',
              cursor: 'pointer',
            }}
          >
            Reload BREACH
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={reset}
            style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: '#1b7a3a', fontSize: '0.8rem', textDecoration: 'underline', cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            Try again without reloading
          </button>
        </main>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

`src/app/robots.ts`:

```ts
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { SITE_URL } from '../lib/site';

// Required for output: 'export' — without it, Next treats this route as dynamic and the export fails.
export const dynamic = 'force-static';

// Generated as a static robots.txt at build time.
export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: { userAgent: '*', allow: '/' },
    sitemap: `${SITE_URL}/sitemap.xml`,
  };
}

```

`src/app/sitemap.ts`:

```ts
import type { MetadataRoute } from 'next';
import { SITE_URL } from '../lib/site';

// Required for output: 'export' — without it, Next treats this route as dynamic and the export fails.
export const dynamic = 'force-static';

// Generated as a static sitemap.xml at build time. The whole game lives on one route (CLAUDE.md), so one entry is right.
export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  return [{ url: SITE_URL, lastModified: new Date(), changeFrequency: 'weekly', priority: 1 }];
}

`src/components/AccountPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Account settings: your handle, your recovery key (shown again on this device), creating a key if you skipped, and
// restoring an account from a key on a new device. Everything works offline except the parts that need the server.

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { cloudConfigured, registerAccount, renameAccount, restoreAccount, type RestoreResult } from '../lib/cloud';
import { getPlayerId } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { nicknameProblem } from '../lib/nickname';
import { isWellFormedKey, normalizeRecoveryKey } from '../lib/recoverykey';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface AccountPanelProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => void;
  onRestored: (restored: RestoreResult) => void;
}

export default function AccountPanel({ save, onChange, onRestored }: AccountPanelProps) {
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState(save.nickname ?? '');
  const [note, setNote] = useState<{ kind: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [typedKey, setTypedKey] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const registered = Boolean(save.syncToken && save.playerId);

  const rename = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const next = handle.trim();
    const problem = nicknameProblem(next);
    if (problem) return setNote({ kind: 'err', text: problem });
    setBusy(true);
    if (registered && cloudConfigured()) {
      const r = await renameAccount(save.playerId!, save.syncToken!, next);
      if (!r.ok) {
        setBusy(false);
        return setNote({ kind: 'err', text: r.reason });
      }
    }
    onChange({ nickname: next });
    setBusy(false);
    setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: registered ? 'Handle changed everywhere.' : 'Handle changed on this device.' });
  };

  const createKey = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const playerId = save.playerId ?? getPlayerId();
    const r = await registerAccount(playerId, save.nickname ?? '', { ...save, playerId });
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setNote({ kind: 'err', text: r.reason });
    onChange({ playerId, recoveryKey: r.value.recoveryKey, syncToken: r.value.syncToken });
    setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Recovery key created. Save it now.' });
  };

  const restore = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isWellFormedKey(typedKey)) return setNote({ kind: 'err', text: 'That does not look like a recovery key. It has four words and four digits.' });
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await restoreAccount(normalizeRecoveryKey(typedKey));
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) return setNote({ kind: 'err', text: r.reason });
    onRestored(r.value);
    setTypedKey('');
    setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: `Restored ${r.value.nickname}. Your progress now includes the cloud copy.` });
  };

  const copy = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="mb-8 space-y-6" aria-label="account">
      <div>
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
          HANDLE
        </h2>
        <form onSubmit={rename} className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <input className="term-input panel flex-1 px-3 py-2" value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} maxLength={16} aria-label="handle" spellCheck={false} />
          <button type="submit" className="btn" disabled={busy}>
            Change handle
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="lock" size={16} />
          RECOVERY KEY
        </h2>
        {save.recoveryKey ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="glow break-all text-xl font-bold tracking-widest">{save.recoveryKey}</p>
            <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={() => copy(save.recoveryKey!)}>
              <Icon name="card" size={16} />
              {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy key'}
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : registered ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">Your key was shown on the device where you registered. This device is signed in with its own sync token.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">You have no recovery key yet. Create one to sync your progress and restore it on another device.</p>
            <button type="button" className="btn text-sm" onClick={createKey} disabled={busy || !cloudConfigured()}>
              Create recovery key
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        <p className="mt-3 border-l-2 border-[var(--warn)] pl-3 text-xs leading-relaxed">
          Browsers can clear their data, and iPhones delete a site&apos;s data after seven days unused. Without this key, your progress cannot come back.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="reset" size={16} />
          RESTORE FROM KEY
        </h2>
        <form onSubmit={restore} className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <input
            className="term-input panel flex-1 px-3 py-2 uppercase"
            value={typedKey}
            onChange={(e) => setTypedKey(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419"
            aria-label="recovery key"
            autoComplete="off"
            spellCheck={false}
          />
          <button type="submit" className="btn" disabled={busy || !typedKey.trim()}>
            Restore
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      {note ? (
        <p className={`text-sm ${note.kind === 'err' ? 'text-[var(--alert)]' : 'text-[var(--dim)]'}`} role="status">
          {note.text}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Achievements.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Achievement UI: the toast that pops when something unlocks (with a chime), and the achievements screen.
// Hidden achievements stay as question marks until they are earned.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { play } from '../lib/audio';
import { ACHIEVEMENTS, type AchievementDef } from '../lib/achievements';
import Icon from './Icon';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';

const TOAST_MS = 3200;

/**
 * Queues unlocked achievements one at a time, in a corner so they never sit over the score panel.
 * Stays empty until `active` (the debrief holds it off until its own stat count-up is done), then
 * shows the queue one card at a time and calls `onDone` once it has worked through all of them.
 */
export function AchievementToast({ items, active, onDone }: { items: AchievementDef[]; active: boolean; onDone: () => void }) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIndex(0);
  }, [items]);

  const current = active && index < items.length ? items[index] : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!current) return;
    play('achievement');
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setIndex((i) => i + 1), TOAST_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [current]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (active && items.length > 0 && index >= items.length) onDone();
  }, [active, items.length, index, onDone]);

  if (!current) return null;
  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-3 right-3 z-[70] max-w-[min(90vw,20rem)]" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => setIndex((i) => i + 1)} className="panel flex w-full items-center gap-3 p-3 text-left text-sm">
        <Icon name={current.icon} size={22} />
        <span>
          <span className="block text-xs tracking-[0.25em] text-[var(--dim)]">ACHIEVEMENT UNLOCKED</span>
          <span className="glow font-semibold">{current.title}</span>
        </span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

interface AchievementsScreenProps {
  unlocked: string[];
  onBack: () => void;
}

export default function AchievementsScreen({ unlocked, onBack }: AchievementsScreenProps) {
  const have = new Set(unlocked);
  const earned = ACHIEVEMENTS.filter((a) => have.has(a.id)).length;
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="ACHIEVEMENTS" subtitle={`${earned} of ${ACHIEVEMENTS.length} unlocked. Stored on this device only.`} icon="check" />
      <ul className="mb-8 space-y-2">
        {ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => {
          const got = have.has(a.id);
          const hidden = a.hidden && !got;
          return (
            <li key={a.id} className={`panel flex items-start gap-3 p-3 text-sm ${got ? '' : 'opacity-60'}`}>
              <Icon name={got ? a.icon : 'lock'} size={22} />
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className={got ? 'glow font-semibold' : 'font-semibold'}>{hidden ? '??? hidden achievement' : a.title}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{hidden ? 'Keep looking. It will show up when you find it.' : a.description}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">{got ? 'UNLOCKED' : 'LOCKED'}</span>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      <div className="mt-auto">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onBack}>
          <Icon name="back" size={16} />
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

`src/components/BoardPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Posts a finished run to the global board. One step: enter a handle, post the run, and — the first time, when
// there is no account yet — create the cloud account and show the recovery key in the same place. The celebratory
// "you're on the board" line only ever shows once the post is actually verified; see lib/board.ts for that rule.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { nicknameProblem } from '../lib/nickname';
import { leaderboardConfigured, submitRun, type Submission, type SubmitResult } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { cloudConfigured, registerAccount } from '../lib/cloud';
import { boardHeadline, percentileLine } from '../lib/board';
import { trackFunnelScorePosted } from '../lib/funnel';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface BoardPanelProps {
  nickname?: string;
  /** Builds the request from the saved command log. Called when the run is sent. */
  build: (nickname: string) => Submission;
  onNickname: (nickname: string) => void;
  tier: Tier;
  tierLabel: string;
  missionId: string;
  /** Fires once the server's answer is known, so the debrief can fold a percentile line into the share text. */
  onResult?: (result: SubmitResult) => void;
  /**
   * Set when there is no account yet. Posting the score also registers the handle and shows the
   * recovery key, once, as part of the same step, instead of a separate screen later.
   */
  registration?: {
    playerId: string;
    save: SaveData;
    onRegistered: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => void;
  };
}

type State = { phase: 'idle' } | { phase: 'sending' } | { phase: 'done'; result: SubmitResult; recoveryKey?: string; keyOffline?: boolean };

export default function BoardPanel({ nickname, build, onNickname, tier, tierLabel, missionId, onResult, registration }: BoardPanelProps) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<State>({ phase: 'idle' });
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(nickname ?? '');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const sent = useRef(false);

  const report = (result: SubmitResult) => {
    if (result.status === 'saved') trackFunnelScorePosted(tier, missionId);
    onResult?.(result);
  };

  const send = async (name: string) => {
    if (sent.current) return;
    sent.current = true;
    setState({ phase: 'sending' });
    const result = await submitRun(build(name));
    setState({ phase: 'done', result });
    report(result);
  };

  /** First-ever post: create the cloud account (if one can be reached) and post the run in the same step. */
  const registerAndSend = async (name: string, reg: NonNullable<BoardPanelProps['registration']>) => {
    if (sent.current) return;
    sent.current = true;
    setState({ phase: 'sending' });
    if (cloudConfigured()) {
      const result = await registerAccount(reg.playerId, name, { ...reg.save, nickname: name, playerId: reg.playerId });
      if (result.ok) {
        reg.onRegistered({ nickname: name, playerId: reg.playerId, recoveryKey: result.value.recoveryKey, syncToken: result.value.syncToken });
        const submitted = await submitRun(build(name));
        setState({ phase: 'done', result: submitted, recoveryKey: result.value.recoveryKey });
        report(submitted);
        return;
      }
      // Offline or refused: the handle still saves locally, and a key can be made later from Settings.
      reg.onRegistered({ nickname: name, playerId: reg.playerId, registerSkipped: false });
    } else {
      reg.onRegistered({ nickname: name, registerSkipped: false });
    }
    const submitted = await submitRun(build(name));
    setState({ phase: 'done', result: submitted, keyOffline: true });
    report(submitted);
  };

  // With a nickname already on file, the run goes up as soon as the panel opens. No form needed.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!leaderboardConfigured()) {
      setState({ phase: 'done', result: { status: 'offline', reason: 'The global board is not set up on this build. Your score is saved on this device.' } });
      return;
    }
    if (nickname) void send(nickname);
    // send is recreated each render, but this should run once when the panel opens.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const pick = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const name = draft.trim();
    const problem = nicknameProblem(name);
    if (problem) {
      setError(problem);
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    if (registration) void registerAndSend(name, registration);
    else {
      onNickname(name);
      void send(name);
    }
  };

  const copy = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  if (state.phase === 'done' && state.result.status === 'offline' && !state.recoveryKey && !state.keyOffline) {
    return (
      <section className="panel mb-6 p-4 text-sm" aria-label="global board">
        <p className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
          GLOBAL BOARD
        </p>
        <p>{state.result.reason}</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (!nickname && state.phase === 'idle') {
    return (
      <section className="panel mb-6 p-4 text-sm" aria-label="global board">
        <p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
          POST TO THE GLOBAL BOARD
        </p>
        <form onSubmit={pick} className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <label className="flex-1">
            <span className="sr-only">Nickname</span>
            <input
              className="term-input panel w-full px-3 py-2"
              value={draft}
              onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
              maxLength={16}
              placeholder="nickname (3-16 characters)"
              autoComplete="off"
              spellCheck={false}
            />
          </label>
          <button type="submit" className="btn">
            Post score
          </button>
        </form>
        {error ? <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--alert)]">{error}</p> : null}
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {registration
            ? "Only your nickname and the run leave this device. No account, no email. You'll get a recovery key for this handle right after."
            : 'Only your nickname and the run leave this device. No account, no email.'}
        </p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="panel mb-6 p-4 text-sm" aria-label="global board">
      <p className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
        <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
        {state.phase === 'done' ? boardHeadline(state.result) : 'GLOBAL BOARD'}
      </p>
      {state.phase === 'sending' ? <p>Checking your run...</p> : null}

      {state.phase === 'done' && state.recoveryKey ? (
        <div className="my-3 space-y-2 border-l-2 border-[var(--warn)] pl-3">
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">YOUR RECOVERY KEY</p>
          <p className="glow break-all text-lg font-bold tracking-widest">{state.recoveryKey}</p>
          <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-2 text-xs" onClick={() => copy(state.recoveryKey!)}>
            <Icon name="card" size={14} />
            {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy key'}
          </button>
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--warn)]">Save this somewhere safe. It is the only way to get your progress back on another device, and it is shown once.</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">You can see it again on this device in Settings.</p>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {state.phase === 'done' && state.keyOffline ? (
        <p className="my-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Cloud save is offline, so your handle is saved on this device only. Make a recovery key from Settings once you are online.</p>
      ) : null}

      {state.phase === 'done' && state.result.status === 'saved' ? (
        <>
          <p>
            Verified: <span className="glow">{state.result.score}</span> points on the board
            {state.result.improved ? '' : ' (your best is already higher)'}.
          </p>
          {percentileLine(state.result, tierLabel) ? <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--dim)]">{percentileLine(state.result, tierLabel)}</p> : null}
        </>
      ) : null}
      {state.phase === 'done' && state.result.status === 'rejected' ? <p className="text-[var(--alert)]">Not posted: {state.result.reason}</p> : null}
      {state.phase === 'done' && state.result.status === 'offline' ? <p>{state.result.reason}</p> : null}
    </section>
  );
}

`src/components/BoardScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The global leaderboard: campaign totals, one job, or today's daily, per tier. Top 50 and your own place.
// Shows OFFLINE when the board cannot be reached. Nothing else in the game depends on it.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import { utcDate } from '../lib/daily';
import { fetchCampaignBoard, fetchMissionBoard, fetchMyRank, getPlayerId, leaderboardConfigured, type CampaignRow, type MissionRow } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';
import Icon from './Icon';

type Scope = { kind: 'campaign' } | { kind: 'mission'; index: number } | { kind: 'daily' };
type Load =
  | { state: 'idle' }
  | { state: 'loading' }
  | { state: 'offline' }
  | { state: 'ready'; campaign?: CampaignRow[]; missions?: MissionRow[]; rank: number | null };

interface BoardScreenProps {
  tier: Tier;
  onTier: (tier: Tier) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export default function BoardScreen({ tier, onTier, onBack }: BoardScreenProps) {
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<Scope>({ kind: 'campaign' });
  const [load, setLoad] = useState<Load>({ state: 'idle' });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!leaderboardConfigured()) {
      setLoad({ state: 'offline' });
      return;
    }
    let cancelled = false;
    setLoad({ state: 'loading' });
    const run = async () => {
      try {
        const playerId = getPlayerId();
        if (scope.kind === 'campaign') {
          const [rows, rank] = await Promise.all([fetchCampaignBoard(tier), fetchMyRank(playerId, tier).catch(() => null)]);
          if (!cancelled) setLoad({ state: 'ready', campaign: rows, rank });
        } else {
          const id = scope.kind === 'daily' ? `daily-${utcDate()}` : MISSIONS[scope.index].id;
          const slot = scope.kind === 'daily' ? `daily:${utcDate()}` : 'campaign';
          const rows = await fetchMissionBoard(id, tier, slot);
          if (!cancelled) setLoad({ state: 'ready', missions: rows, rank: null });
        }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) setLoad({ state: 'offline' });
      }
    };
    void run();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [scope, tier]);

  const tabs: { label: string; scope: Scope }[] = [
    { label: 'Campaign', scope: { kind: 'campaign' } },
    { label: "Today's daily", scope: { kind: 'daily' } },
  ];

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="GLOBAL BOARD" subtitle="Scores are checked by replaying your run. Top 50 shown." icon="radar" />

      <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="difficulty">
        {TIERS.map((t) => (
          <button key={t} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={tier === t} className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 !min-h-[40px] text-xs sm:text-sm" onClick={() => onTier(t)}>
            <Icon name="tier" level={(TIERS.indexOf(t) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3} size={14} />
            {DIFFICULTY[t].label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="tablist" aria-label="board">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <button key={t.label} type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={scope.kind === t.scope.kind} className="btn text-xs" onClick={() => setScope(t.scope)}>
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">Job</span>
          <select
            className="panel px-2 py-2 text-xs"
            value={scope.kind === 'mission' ? scope.index : ''}
            onChange={(e) => setScope({ kind: 'mission', index: Number(e.target.value) })}
          >
            <option value="" disabled>
              pick a job
            </option>
            {MISSIONS.map((m, i) => (
              <option key={m.id} value={i}>
                {String(m.number).padStart(2, '0')} {m.codename}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>

      {load.state === 'offline' ? (
        <section className="panel p-4 text-sm" aria-label="offline">
          <p className="mb-1 font-semibold tracking-widest">OFFLINE</p>
          <p>The global board cannot be reached right now. Your own scores are still saved on this device, and the game works the same.</p>
        </section>
      ) : null}
      {load.state === 'loading' ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">Loading the board...</p> : null}

      {load.state === 'ready' && load.campaign ? (
        <section className="mb-6">
          {load.rank !== null ? <p className="mb-3 text-sm">Your place on the {DIFFICULTY[tier].label} board: <span className="glow">#{load.rank}</span></p> : null}
          {load.campaign.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">Nobody has posted yet. Be the first.</p> : null}
          <ol className="space-y-1 text-sm">
            {load.campaign.map((r, i) => (
              <li key={`${r.nickname}-${i}`} className="panel flex justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2">
                <span>
                  {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}  {r.nickname}
                </span>
                <span className="tabular-nums">
                  {r.points} pts · {r.missions}/{MISSIONS.length}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {load.state === 'ready' && load.missions ? (
        <section className="mb-6">
          {load.missions.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">No scores yet on this board.</p> : null}
          <ol className="space-y-1 text-sm">
            {load.missions.map((r, i) => (
              <li key={`${r.nickname}-${i}`} className="panel flex justify-between gap-3 px-3 py-2">
                <span>
                  {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}  {r.nickname}
                </span>
                <span className="tabular-nums">
                  {r.score} · {r.seconds}s · trace {r.trace}%
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-auto">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onBack}>
          <Icon name="back" size={16} />
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

`src/components/Boot.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Fast boot: about two seconds of lines, then the landing page. Any key or tap finishes it at once.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { play } from '../lib/audio';

interface BootLine {
  text: string;
  kind?: 'warn' | 'glitch';
}

const LINES: BootLine[] = [
  { text: 'BREACH BIOS v4.2.7  (c) 2031 ghost-rig' },
  { text: 'MEMORY ........................ 640K OK' },
  { text: 'NIC eth0 ...................... LINK UP' },
  { text: 'TRACE DAEMON .................. ACTIVE' },
  { text: 'WARNING: this terminal is being observed.', kind: 'warn' },
  { text: 'B R E A C H', kind: 'glitch' },
];

const STEP_MS = 230;
const HOLD_MS = 380;

interface BootProps {
  onDone: () => void;
}

export default function Boot({ onDone }: BootProps) {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);
  const ready = count >= LINES.length;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) {
      const done = window.setTimeout(onDone, HOLD_MS);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(done);
    }
    const next = window.setTimeout(() => setCount((c) => c + 1), STEP_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(next);
  }, [count, ready, onDone]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const skip = () => setCount(LINES.length);
    window.addEventListener('keydown', skip);
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', skip);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', skip);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', skip);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) play('enter');
  }, [ready]);

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col justify-center px-6 py-10 screen-curve">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl space-y-1 text-sm leading-relaxed sm:text-base">
        {LINES.slice(0, count).map((line, i) => {
          if (line.kind === 'glitch') {
            return (
              <p key={i} className="pt-6 text-center text-2xl font-bold tracking-[0.4em]">
                <span className="glitch glow" data-text={line.text}>
                  {line.text}
                </span>
              </p>
            );
          }
          const cls = line.kind === 'warn' ? 'text-[var(--warn)]' : 'text-[var(--fg)]';
          return (
            <p key={i} className={cls}>
              {line.text}
            </p>
          );
        })}
        {!ready ? (
          <p className="text-[var(--dim)]">
            <span className="caret">▮</span>
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

'use client';

import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { MissionDef, Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { Header, Screen, Story } from './ui';

interface BriefingProps {
  mission: MissionDef;
  tier: Tier;
  attempts: number;
  completed: boolean;
  /** Whether this mission is unlocked on each difficulty. A tier can be unlocked for mission 1 but not mission 2. */
  unlockedTiers: Record<Tier, boolean>;
  onTier: (tier: Tier) => void;
  onAccept: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export default function Briefing({ mission, tier, attempts, completed, unlockedTiers, onTier, onAccept, onBack }: BriefingProps) {
  const config = DIFFICULTY[tier];
  const tierLocked = !unlockedTiers[tier];
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title={`JOB ${String(mission.number).padStart(2, '0')}`} subtitle={`${mission.codename} :: ${mission.title}`} icon="terminal" />

      <div className="panel mb-6 p-4 text-sm">
        <p>
          <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">CLIENT </span>
          <span className="glow">{mission.client}</span>
        </p>
        <p>
          <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">STATUS </span>
          {completed ? 'Cleared on this difficulty. Run it again for more points.' : 'Open'}
        </p>
        {attempts > 0 ? (
          <p>
            <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">ATTEMPTS </span>
            {attempts}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6" role="radiogroup" aria-label="difficulty for this job">
        <p className="mb-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">DIFFICULTY</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          {TIERS.map((t) => {
            const locked = !unlockedTiers[t];
            return (
              <button
                key={t}
                type="button"
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={tier === t}
                aria-disabled={locked}
                disabled={locked}
                className="btn !min-h-[40px] text-xs sm:text-sm"
                onClick={() => onTier(t)}
              >
                {locked ? '🔒 ' : tier === t ? '● ' : '○ '}
                {DIFFICULTY[t].label} x{DIFFICULTY[t].pointMultiplier}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {tierLocked ? 'Clear the previous job on this difficulty to unlock it here.' : config.description}
        </p>
      </div>

      {config.briefingTip ? (
        <div className="mb-6 border-l-2 border-[var(--warn)] pl-3 text-sm">
          <p className="text-[var(--warn)]">TIP</p>
          <p>{mission.hints[0]}</p>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mb-8">
        <Story lines={mission.briefing} />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onAccept} disabled={tierLocked} aria-disabled={tierLocked}>
          {tierLocked ? `Locked on ${config.label}` : 'Accept job'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onBack}>
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

`src/components/DailyScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The daily challenge: today's job, the countdown to the next UTC reset, your streak, and one attempt per tier per day.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { dailyMission, dailyNumber, isHardFriday, msUntilReset, streakFor, utcDate } from '../lib/daily';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface DailyScreenProps {
  save: SaveData;
  tier: Tier;
  onTier: (tier: Tier) => void;
  onStart: () => void;
  onBoard: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

function countdown(ms: number): string {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  const h = Math.floor(total / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60);
  const s = total % 60;
  return [h, m, s].map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')).join(':');
}

export default function DailyScreen({ save, tier, onTier, onStart, onBoard, onBack }: DailyScreenProps) {
  const today = utcDate();
  const job = dailyMission(today);
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(msUntilReset());

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setLeft(msUntilReset()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const doneDates = Object.keys(save.dailies).filter((d) => Object.values(save.dailies[d] ?? {}).some(Boolean));
  const streak = streakFor(doneDates, today);
  const result = save.dailies[today]?.[tier];
  const attempted = (save.attempts[tier][job.id] ?? 0) > 0;
  const blocked = Boolean(result) || attempted;

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header
        title={isHardFriday(today) ? `DAILY ${dailyNumber(today)} ⚠ HARD FRIDAY` : `DAILY ${dailyNumber(today)}`}
        subtitle={isHardFriday(today) ? `${job.title}. No hints today, and trace runs fast on every tier.` : `${job.title}. Same job, everywhere, until UTC midnight.`}
        icon="calendar"
      />

      <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="panel p-3">
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">NEXT JOB IN</p>
          <p className="glow tabular-nums">{countdown(left)}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="panel p-3">
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">STREAK</p>
          <p className="glow tabular-nums">
            {streak} day{streak === 1 ? '' : 's'}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="panel col-span-2 p-3 sm:col-span-1">
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">TODAY ON {DIFFICULTY[tier].label.toUpperCase()}</p>
          <p>{result ? `Cleared: ${result.points} pts` : attempted ? 'Attempted. One try per day.' : 'Open'}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6" role="radiogroup" aria-label="difficulty for the daily">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          {TIERS.map((t) => (
            <button key={t} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={tier === t} className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 !min-h-[40px] text-xs sm:text-sm" onClick={() => onTier(t)}>
              <Icon name="tier" level={(TIERS.indexOf(t) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3} size={14} />
              {DIFFICULTY[t].label} x{DIFFICULTY[t].pointMultiplier}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">One attempt per day on each difficulty. The job resets at midnight UTC.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onStart} disabled={blocked}>
          <Icon name="play" size={16} />
          {blocked ? 'Already attempted today' : 'Start today\'s job'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onBoard}>
          <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
          Today's board
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onBack}>
          <Icon name="back" size={16} />
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
