`src/components/Debrief.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Job complete. Stats count up one by one with a tick, the rank stamps in with a glitch, then the story types out.
// Reduced motion skips straight to the finished state.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { MissionResult } from '../engine/scoring';
import type { MissionDef, StoryLine } from '../engine/types';
import { DIFFICULTY } from '../engine/difficulty';
import { play } from '../lib/audio';
import { formatTime, Header, Screen } from './ui';
import RealWorldCard from './RealWorldCard';
import BoardPanel from './BoardPanel';
import ShareBar from './ShareBar';
import type { RealWorldCard as Card } from '../lib/realworld';
import type { DailyStats, ShareFacts } from '../lib/share';
import type { Submission, SubmitResult } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { percentileLine } from '../lib/board';
import { buildFeedbackMailto } from '../lib/feedback';
import { GAME_VERSION } from '../lib/version';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';

interface DebriefProps {
  mission: MissionDef;
  result: MissionResult;
  hintLimit: number;
  rankName: string;
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  isLast: boolean;
  onNext: () => void;
  onCampaignEnd: () => void;
  onMenu: () => void;
  nextLabel?: string;
  /** Fires once the stat count-up lands, so an achievement toast can wait for it instead of covering the panel mid-count. */
  onStatsSettled?: () => void;
  card: Card | null;
  share: ShareFacts;
  /** Set for a daily: switches ShareBar to the copyable emoji-row result, image card second. */
  daily?: DailyStats;
  fileName: string;
  board: {
    nickname?: string;
    build: (nickname: string) => Submission;
    onNickname: (nickname: string) => void;
    tier: Tier;
    tierLabel: string;
    missionId: string;
    registration?: { playerId: string; save: SaveData; onRegistered: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => void };
  } | null;
}

const COUNT_FRAMES = 18;

function paragraphs(lines: StoryLine[]): string[] {
  return lines.map((l) => (typeof l === 'string' ? l : l.text));
}

/** Cuts a list of paragraphs at `n` total characters, the way a typewriter reveals them. */
function revealed(paras: string[], n: number): string[] {
  let left = n;
  return paras.map((p) => {
    const take = Math.max(0, Math.min(p.length, left));
    left -= take;
    return p.slice(0, take);
  });
}

export default function Debrief({ mission, result, hintLimit, rankName, reduceMotion, isLast, nextLabel, onStatsSettled, onNext, onCampaignEnd, onMenu, card, share, daily, fileName, board }: DebriefProps) {
  const ending = mission.endings?.find((e) => e.id === result.endingId);
  const story = paragraphs(mission.debrief);
  const storyLength = story.reduce((n, p) => n + p.length, 0);

  const stats = [
    { label: 'SCORE', to: result.points, show: (v: number) => String(v) },
    { label: 'TIME', to: result.seconds, show: (v: number) => formatTime(v) },
    { label: 'TRACE', to: result.trace, show: (v: number) => `${v}%` },
    { label: 'HINTS', to: result.hints, show: (v: number) => `${v}/${hintLimit}` },
  ];

  const [statIndex, setStatIndex] = useState(reduceMotion ? stats.length : 0);
  const [statValue, setStatValue] = useState(0);
  const [stamped, setStamped] = useState(reduceMotion);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState(reduceMotion ? storyLength : 0);
  /** Known only once the board post resolves, so the share text can carry it too. */
  const [percentileNote, setPercentileNote] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    play('sting');
  }, []);

  // Count the stats up, one after another.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (reduceMotion || statIndex >= stats.length) return;
    const target = stats[statIndex].to;
    if (statValue >= target) {
      const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
        setStatIndex((i) => i + 1);
        setStatValue(0);
      }, 220);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
    }
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setStatValue((v) => Math.min(target, v + Math.max(1, Math.ceil(target / COUNT_FRAMES))));
      play('tick');
    }, 28);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [statIndex, statValue, reduceMotion]);

  // Tell the parent the count-up landed, once, so a queued achievement toast can start.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (statIndex >= stats.length) onStatsSettled?.();
    // Fires once stats finish counting; onStatsSettled and stats.length are not triggers of their own.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [statIndex]);

  // Stamp the rank once every stat has landed.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (stamped || statIndex < stats.length) return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setStamped(true);
      play('rank');
    }, 320);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [statIndex, stamped, stats.length]);

  // Type the story out once the stamp lands.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!stamped || typed >= storyLength) return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setTyped((n) => n + 2), 16);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [stamped, typed, storyLength]);

  const storyDone = typed >= storyLength;
  const paras = revealed(story, typed);

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="JOB COMPLETE" subtitle={`${mission.codename} :: ${mission.title} :: ${DIFFICULTY[result.tier].label}`} icon="check" />

      <div className="panel mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-3 p-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-4">
        {stats.map((s, i) => {
          const value = i < statIndex || reduceMotion ? s.to : i === statIndex ? statValue : 0;
          return (
            <div key={s.label}>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{s.label}</div>
              <div className="glow text-base font-semibold tabular-nums">{s.show(value)}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 flex min-h-[2.5rem] items-center [overflow-x:clip]">
        {stamped ? (
          <span className="stamp" aria-label={`rank ${rankName}`}>
            JOB RANK {rankName.toUpperCase()}
          </span>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 space-y-3 leading-relaxed" aria-live="polite">
        {paras.map((p, i) => (p ? <p key={i}>{p}</p> : null))}
        {!storyDone && stamped ? <span className="caret">▮</span> : null}
      </div>

      {storyDone && ending ? (
        <div className="mb-8 border-l-2 border-[var(--fg)] pl-4">
          <p className="mb-2 text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">ENDING</p>
          <p className="mb-3 glow font-semibold">{ending.label}</p>
          <div className="space-y-3 leading-relaxed">
            {ending.text.map((t, i) => (
              <p key={i}>{t}</p>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {storyDone && card ? <RealWorldCard card={card} /> : null}

      {storyDone && board ? (
        <BoardPanel
          nickname={board.nickname}
          build={board.build}
          onNickname={board.onNickname}
          tier={board.tier}
          tierLabel={board.tierLabel}
          missionId={board.missionId}
          registration={board.registration}
          onResult={(result: SubmitResult) => setPercentileNote(percentileLine(result, board.tierLabel))}
        />
      ) : null}

      {storyDone ? (
        <div className="mb-6">
          <ShareBar
            facts={daily || !percentileNote ? share : { ...share, detail: percentileNote }}
            fileName={fileName}
            daily={daily}
            percentileNote={percentileNote}
          />
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mt-auto flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        {isLast ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onCampaignEnd}>
            See your result card
          </button>
        ) : (
          <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onNext}>
            {nextLabel ?? 'Next job'}
          </button>
        )}
        <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onMenu}>
          Main menu
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-3 text-center text-xs">
        <a className="underline underline-offset-4 text-[var(--dim)] hover:text-[var(--fg)]" href={buildFeedbackMailto({ version: GAME_VERSION, tier: result.tier, mission: mission.codename })}>
          Report a bug on this job
        </a>
      </p>
    </Screen>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Decrypt.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Text that scrambles and then settles on its real letters, like a decrypt. The first render shows the final text,
// so the static export and the first paint agree. Reduced motion skips the effect.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const GLYPHS = '#%&@$<>/\\|=+*01';
const STEP_MS = 40;
const STEPS = 14;

export default function Decrypt({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(text);

  useEffect(() => {
    const reduce = document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion');
    if (reduce) {
      setShown(text);
      return;
    }
    let step = 0;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      step++;
      const settled = Math.floor((step / STEPS) * text.length);
      setShown(
        text
          .split('')
          .map((ch, i) => {
            if (ch === ' ' || i < settled) return ch;
            return GLYPHS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLYPHS.length)];
          })
          .join(''),
      );
      if (step >= STEPS) {
        window.clearInterval(timer);
        setShown(text);
      }
    }, STEP_MS);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [text]);

  return <>{shown}</>;
}

```

`src/components/EndScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { DIFFICULTY } from '../engine/difficulty';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import { buildShareCard, summarize } from '../lib/progress';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';
import ShareBar from './ShareBar';

interface EndScreenProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onMenu: () => void;
}

async function copyText(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    return true;
  } catch {
    // Clipboard API is blocked in some embeds. Fall back to a hidden textarea.
    try {
      const area = document.createElement('textarea');
      area.value = text;
      area.setAttribute('readonly', '');
      area.style.position = 'fixed';
      area.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.appendChild(area);
      area.select();
      const ok = document.execCommand('copy');
      area.remove();
      return ok;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }
}

export default function EndScreen({ save, onMenu }: EndScreenProps) {
  const summary = summarize(save);
  const tier = save.tier;
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<'idle' | 'ok' | 'failed'>('idle');
  const endingMission = MISSIONS[MISSIONS.length - 1];
  const endingId = save.completed[tier][endingMission.id]?.endingId ?? null;
  const endingLabel = endingMission.endings?.find((e) => e.id === endingId)?.label ?? null;
  const card = buildShareCard(summary, endingLabel);
  const campaignDone = summary.completed === summary.total;

  const onCopy = async () => {
    const ok = await copyText(card);
    setCopied(ok ? 'ok' : 'failed');
  };

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title={campaignDone ? 'CAMPAIGN COMPLETE' : 'CAMPAIGN STATUS'} subtitle={`${DIFFICULTY[tier].label} difficulty. Share it, then vanish.`} icon="card" />

      <div className="panel mb-6 p-5">
        <p className="glow text-3xl font-bold">{summary.rank.name}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm">
          {summary.completed}/{summary.total} missions · {summary.points} points · {summary.hints} hint{summary.hints === 1 ? '' : 's'} · avg trace {summary.avgTrace}%
        </p>
        <p className="mt-3 text-xl tracking-widest">{summary.grid.join(' ')}</p>
        {endingLabel ? <p className="mt-3 text-sm">Ending: {endingLabel}</p> : null}
      </div>

      <pre className="panel mb-4 overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap p-4 text-sm leading-relaxed" aria-label="share text">
        {card}
      </pre>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <ShareBar
          facts={{
            label: 'CAMPAIGN',
            tier: DIFFICULTY[tier].label,
            rank: summary.rank.name,
            score: summary.points,
            detail: endingLabel ? `${summary.completed}/${summary.total} jobs · ${endingLabel}` : `${summary.completed}/${summary.total} jobs`,
          }}
          fileName="breach-campaign"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onCopy}>
          {copied === 'ok' ? 'Copied to clipboard' : copied === 'failed' ? 'Copy failed. Select the text above.' : 'Copy result'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn flex-1" onClick={onMenu}>
          Main menu
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`src/components/GameApp.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Top-level screen router. Owns the save data, the current attempt, the run log that feeds the leaderboard and the ops
// log, achievements, cloud sync, and transitions between screens.

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type MouseEvent as ReactMouseEvent, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';
import { GameSession } from '../engine/session';
import { rankForScore, scoreMission, type MissionResult } from '../engine/scoring';
import { DIFFICULTY, pointsFor } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { MissionDef, Tier } from '../engine/types';
import type { RunCommand } from '../engine/replay';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import { play, setAmbienceActive, setAudioSettings, setMusicTrack, setTension, startMusic } from '../lib/audio';
import { setHapticsEnabled } from '../lib/haptics';
import { DEFAULT_SAVE, effectiveReduceMotion, loadSave, persistSave, type SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import { campaignDone, factsFromWin, newlyUnlocked, type AchievementDef } from '../lib/achievements';
import { dailyMission, dailyNumber, isHardFriday, streakFor, utcDate } from '../lib/daily';
import { realWorldFor } from '../lib/realworld';
import { trackDailyPlayed, trackMissionCleared, trackMissionStarted, trackMissionTraced, trackRestoreUsed } from '../lib/analytics';
import {
  trackFunnelFirstCommand,
  trackFunnelMissionAborted,
  trackFunnelMissionCleared,
  trackFunnelMissionStarted,
  trackFunnelMissionTraced,
  trackFunnelRestoreUsed,
} from '../lib/funnel';
import { buildSubmission, getPlayerId, leaderboardConfigured, setPlayerId, startRun, type StartedRun, type Submission } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { applyFlagVariation } from '../lib/variation';
import { debounce, syncSave, type RestoreResult } from '../lib/cloud';
import { addAttempt, type AttemptRecord, type Outcome } from '../lib/history';
import { mergeSaves } from '../lib/merge';
import { missionUnlocked, summarize, tierWithProgress, unlockedTiers } from '../lib/progress';
import type { DailyStats, ShareFacts } from '../lib/share';
import Achievements, { AchievementToast } from './Achievements';
import Boot from './Boot';
import BoardScreen from './BoardScreen';
import Briefing from './Briefing';
import Debrief from './Debrief';
import DailyScreen from './DailyScreen';
import EndScreen from './EndScreen';
import Landing from './Landing';
import MainMenu from './MainMenu';
import MissionScreen from './MissionScreen';
import MissionSelect from './MissionSelect';
import About from './About';
import OpsLog from './OpsLog';
import Privacy from './Privacy';
import Profile from './Profile';
import Register from './Register';
import Settings from './Settings';
import Transfer from './Transfer';

type Screen =
  | { name: 'boot' }
  | { name: 'landing' }
  | { name: 'register' }
  | { name: 'menu' }
  | { name: 'select' }
  | { name: 'settings' }
  | { name: 'card' }
  | { name: 'daily' }
  | { name: 'board' }
  | { name: 'achievements' }
  | { name: 'history' }
  | { name: 'profile' }
  /** Reachable from the landing page (before a handle exists) and from Settings, so Back needs to know which. */
  | { name: 'privacy'; from: 'landing' | 'settings' }
  | { name: 'about'; from: 'landing' | 'settings' }
  | { name: 'briefing'; index: number }
  /** `date` is set for the daily job, which is generated from the date rather than taken from the list. */
  | { name: 'play'; index: number; date?: string }
  | { name: 'transfer'; index: number; result: MissionResult; date?: string }
  | { name: 'debrief'; index: number; result: MissionResult; date?: string };

/** How long the CRT switch-off takes before the job board appears. */
const CRT_OFF_MS = 420;
/** Sync waits this long after the last change, so several changes send one request. */
const SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS = 4000;

/** What a finished attempt sends to the board, kept until the debrief posts it. */
interface LastRun {
  missionId: string;
  tier: Tier;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  date?: string;
  commands: RunCommand[];
  /** The run token this attempt was started with, if any. Unset means it can only be saved locally. */
  token?: string;
}

function missionFor(index: number, date?: string): MissionDef {
  return date ? dailyMission(date) : MISSIONS[index];
}

/** Keys the run-token prefetch cache. Must match exactly what openSession asks startRun for, or the cached
 * (possibly still in-flight) token for the warmed-up mission/tier/scope just goes unused. */
function tokenCacheKey(missionId: string, tier: Tier, scope: 'campaign' | 'daily', date?: string): string {
  return `${scope}|${missionId}|${tier}|${date ?? ''}`;
}

function newAttemptId(): string {
  return `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
}

/** True for a visitor who has never started or finished anything — the one who gets the quick-start path and skips the boot sequence. */
function isFreshVisitor(s: SaveData): boolean {
  return (
    !s.nickname &&
    !s.registerSkipped &&
    s.history.length === 0 &&
    Object.values(s.completed).every((t) => Object.keys(t).length === 0) &&
    Object.values(s.attempts).every((t) => Object.keys(t).length === 0)
  );
}

export default function GameApp() {
  const [save, setSave] = useState<SaveData>(DEFAULT_SAVE);
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>({ name: 'boot' });
  const [session, setSession] = useState<GameSession | null>(null);
  const [attemptKey, setAttemptKey] = useState(0);
  /** The board is configured, but this attempt's run token couldn't be fetched at start. See openSession. */
  const [boardUnreachable, setBoardUnreachable] = useState(false);
  const [wipeKey, setWipeKey] = useState(0);
  const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false);
  const [systemReduce, setSystemReduce] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<AchievementDef[]>([]);
  /** Held off until the debrief's own stat count-up finishes, so a toast never covers it mid-animation. */
  const [toastsReady, setToastsReady] = useState(false);
  const [lastRun, setLastRun] = useState<LastRun | null>(null);
  const lastScreen = useRef<Screen['name']>('boot');
  /** The attempt's command log, timed from the moment the session opened. Replayed by the server and the viewer. */
  const run = useRef<{ startedAt: number; commands: RunCommand[]; recorded: boolean; token: string | null }>({
    startedAt: 0,
    commands: [],
    recorded: false,
    token: null,
  });
  /** Set when a save change came from a sync merge, so the change does not send another sync. */
  const fromSync = useRef(false);
  /**
   * Run tokens requested early (while the briefing or daily screen is open), keyed by tokenCacheKey, so a
   * cold-started submit-run has time to finish in the background before the player actually starts the
   * mission. openSession consumes (and removes) the entry for the mission it opens; a miss just falls back
   * to a fresh request there.
   */
  const tokenCache = useRef<Map<string, Promise<StartedRun | null>>>(new Map());

  // Storage and matchMedia are read on the client only, so the static export pre-renders safely.
  useEffect(() => {
    const loaded = loadSave();
    setSave(loaded);
    // A returning player has already seen the boot sequence. Go straight to the landing page.
    if (!isFreshVisitor(loaded)) setScreen({ name: 'landing' });
    const mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
    setSystemReduce(mq.matches);
    const onChange = () => setSystemReduce(mq.matches);
    mq.addEventListener('change', onChange);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = save.theme;
  }, [save.theme]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setAudioSettings({
      volume: save.volume,
      sfx: save.sfx,
      music: save.music,
      musicVolume: save.musicVolume,
      track: save.track,
      keySounds: save.keySounds,
      keySoundsVolume: save.keySoundsVolume,
    });
  }, [save.volume, save.sfx, save.music, save.musicVolume, save.track, save.keySounds, save.keySoundsVolume]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHapticsEnabled(save.keyHaptics);
  }, [save.keyHaptics]);

  // On phones, the visual viewport shrinks when the keyboard opens. Size the app to it so the prompt stays visible.
  useEffect(() => {
    const vv = window.visualViewport;
    const apply = () => {
      const h = vv ? vv.height : window.innerHeight;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--app-h', `${Math.round(h)}px`);
    };
    apply();
    vv?.addEventListener('resize', apply);
    vv?.addEventListener('scroll', apply);
    return () => {
      vv?.removeEventListener('resize', apply);
      vv?.removeEventListener('scroll', apply);
    };
  }, []);

  // Glitch wipe on every screen change (not on first paint).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastScreen.current !== screen.name) {
      lastScreen.current = screen.name;
      setWipeKey((k) => k + 1);
      play('transition');
    }
  }, [screen.name]);

  // Menus and the landing page are calm. The soundtrack only follows the trace inside a mission.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (screen.name !== 'play') setTension(0);
    setAmbienceActive(screen.name === 'play');
  }, [screen.name]);

  /** Warms the run-token cache. A no-op if that mission/tier/scope is already cached (in flight or settled). */
  const prefetchToken = useCallback((missionId: string, tier: Tier, scope: 'campaign' | 'daily', date?: string) => {
    if (!leaderboardConfigured()) return;
    const key = tokenCacheKey(missionId, tier, scope, date);
    if (tokenCache.current.has(key)) return;
    tokenCache.current.set(key, startRun({ playerId: getPlayerId(), missionId, tier, scope, date }));
  }, []);

  // Warm up a token as soon as the briefing opens, well before Accept, so a cold-started submit-run has time
  // to finish in the background. Re-warms if the tier picker changes while the briefing is still open.
  const briefingIndex = screen.name === 'briefing' ? screen.index : null;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (briefingIndex === null) return;
    prefetchToken(MISSIONS[briefingIndex].id, save.tier, 'campaign');
  }, [briefingIndex, save.tier, prefetchToken]);

  // Same idea for the daily menu, keyed by today's date.
  const onDailyScreen = screen.name === 'daily';
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!onDailyScreen) return;
    const date = utcDate();
    prefetchToken(dailyMission(date).id, save.tier, 'daily', date);
  }, [onDailyScreen, save.tier, prefetchToken]);

  const reduceMotion = effectiveReduceMotion(save.motion, systemReduce);

  const update = useCallback((patch: Partial<SaveData>) => {
    setSave((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev, ...patch };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  // Cloud sync. Local saves are the source of truth; a sync sends the local copy and takes back the merged one.
  const syncNow = async (snapshot: SaveData) => {
    if (!snapshot.playerId || !snapshot.syncToken) return;
    const r = await syncSave(snapshot.playerId, snapshot.syncToken, snapshot);
    if (!r.ok) return;
    fromSync.current = true;
    setSave((prev) => {
      const merged = mergeSaves(prev, r.value.save);
      persistSave(merged);
      return merged;
    });
  };
  const scheduleSync = useMemo(() => debounce((s: SaveData) => void syncNow(s), SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (fromSync.current) {
      fromSync.current = false;
      return;
    }
    if (save.syncToken) scheduleSync(save);
    // Sync follows every save change, except the ones a sync itself made.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [save]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMusicTrack(save.track);
  }, [save.track]);

  const onBootDone = useCallback(() => setScreen({ name: 'landing' }), []);
  // Music loads and starts the moment the player leaves the landing page for a menu or a mission, never on landing
  // itself. Each of these is reached from a click, which is the user gesture browsers require before audio can start.
  const toMenu = useCallback(() => {
    startMusic();
    setScreen({ name: 'menu' });
  }, []);
  const toDaily = useCallback(() => {
    startMusic();
    setScreen({ name: 'daily' });
  }, []);

  const setTier = (next: Tier) => update({ tier: next });

  const muted = !save.sfx && !save.music;
  const toggleMute = () => update({ sfx: muted, music: muted });

  /** A restore brings the account's identity and the cloud copy onto this device. Device settings stay. */
  const adoptRestored = (r: RestoreResult) => {
    trackRestoreUsed();
    trackFunnelRestoreUsed();
    fromSync.current = false;
    // getPlayerId() otherwise keeps returning the fresh id this device generated for itself before the
    // restore, which belongs to no account: submitting under it would try to create a new player row with
    // the restored nickname, which is already taken by the real account. Adopt the server's id instead, and
    // drop any token already warming up under the old one.
    setPlayerId(r.playerId);
    tokenCache.current.clear();
    setSave((prev) => {
      const base: SaveData = { ...prev, playerId: r.playerId, nickname: r.nickname, syncToken: r.syncToken, recoveryKey: undefined, registerSkipped: false };
      const merged = r.save ? mergeSaves(base, r.save) : base;
      // mergeSaves keeps this device's own tier selection; a fresh device's default shouldn't shadow progress
      // the restored save actually has elsewhere, or the menu says "start first job" on a cleared campaign.
      const next = { ...merged, tier: tierWithProgress(merged) ?? merged.tier };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
  };

  /** Sudo tries are a lifetime counter. Add this attempt's tries when the attempt ends. */
  const flushSudo = (s: GameSession | null) => {
    if (!s || s.sudoTries === 0) return;
    setSave((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev, counters: { ...prev.counters, sudoTries: prev.counters.sudoTries + s.sudoTries } };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
  };

  /**
   * Opens a new attempt. When the leaderboard is reachable, this first asks the server for a run token (which
   * carries the seed that varies the flag for this attempt) before building the session, so the player and the
   * server are always looking at the same mission. It reuses whatever prefetchToken already warmed up for this
   * mission/tier/scope (see the briefing/daily effects above) and only falls back to a fresh request here.
   *
   * The attempt's clock (startedAt, so every command's `t` is measured from it) is only set once the token
   * settles — not when this is called. A token request started earlier (the common, prefetched case) already
   * finished by then, so this is instant and startedAt lands at essentially the same moment as normal. But if
   * it wasn't prefetched and this has to wait on a slow or cold-started request, starting the clock here
   * instead of at the top would make the server's token.started_at (set when it actually processed the
   * request, before the slow round trip even finished) look like it came *after* the clock already started —
   * eating into the server-elapsed tolerance in checkToken for no reason the player caused. Waiting keeps
   * token.started_at always at or before the clock's zero point, by construction.
   *
   * A run started without a token (offline, or both tries timed out) still plays normally on the unvaried
   * mission; it just can't be posted later (see submitRun).
   */
  const openSession = (mission: MissionDef, sessionTier: Tier, opts?: { quickBriefing?: boolean; scope?: 'campaign' | 'daily'; date?: string }) => {
    const scope = opts?.scope ?? 'campaign';
    const thisRun: { startedAt: number; commands: RunCommand[]; recorded: boolean; token: string | null } = {
      startedAt: 0,
      commands: [],
      recorded: false,
      token: null,
    };
    run.current = thisRun;
    setSession(null);
    setBoardUnreachable(false);
    setAttemptKey((k) => k + 1);
    void (async () => {
      let finalMission = mission;
      if (leaderboardConfigured()) {
        const key = tokenCacheKey(mission.id, sessionTier, scope, opts?.date);
        const cached = tokenCache.current.get(key);
        tokenCache.current.delete(key);
        const started = await (cached ?? startRun({ playerId: getPlayerId(), missionId: mission.id, tier: sessionTier, scope, date: opts?.date }));
        // A newer attempt may have started while this was in flight; don't resurrect a stale one.
        if (run.current !== thisRun) return;
        if (started) {
          thisRun.token = started.token;
          finalMission = applyFlagVariation(mission, started.seed);
        } else {
          setBoardUnreachable(true);
        }
      }
      if (run.current !== thisRun) return;
      thisRun.startedAt = Date.now();
      setSession(new GameSession(finalMission, { tier: sessionTier, quickBriefing: opts?.quickBriefing }));
    })();
  };

  /** "Start hacking" for a visitor who has never played: straight into job 1 on Easy, briefing printed in the terminal. */
  const quickStartFirstMission = () => {
    startMusic();
    flushSudo(session);
    const mission = MISSIONS[0];
    setSave((prev) => {
      const next: SaveData = {
        ...prev,
        tier: 'easy',
        attempts: { ...prev.attempts, easy: { ...prev.attempts.easy, [mission.id]: (prev.attempts.easy[mission.id] ?? 0) + 1 } },
      };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
    openSession(mission, 'easy', { quickBriefing: true });
    trackMissionStarted('easy', 'campaign');
    trackFunnelMissionStarted('easy', mission.id);
    setScreen({ name: 'play', index: 0 });
  };

  /** An attempt that ended without a win (trace, clock or abort) goes into the ops log once. */
  const logAttempt = (s: GameSession, mission: MissionDef, date: string | undefined, outcome: Outcome) => {
    if (run.current.recorded) return;
    run.current.recorded = true;
    const attemptNo = save.attempts[s.tier][mission.id] ?? 1;
    const record: AttemptRecord = {
      id: newAttemptId(),
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      missionId: mission.id,
      scope: date ? 'daily' : 'campaign',
      tier: s.tier,
      attemptNo,
      outcome,
      score: 0,
      points: 0,
      seconds: s.elapsedSeconds(),
      trace: s.trace,
      hints: s.hintsUsed,
      commands: [...run.current.commands],
    };
    update({ history: addAttempt(save.history, record) });
  };

  const beginAttempt = (index: number) => {
    // Defense in depth: the briefing's tier picker already disables a locked tier, but a stale screen should not start one.
    if (!missionUnlocked(save, save.tier, index)) return;
    flushSudo(session);
    const mission = MISSIONS[index];
    setSave((prev) => {
      const next = {
        ...prev,
        attempts: {
          ...prev.attempts,
          [prev.tier]: { ...prev.attempts[prev.tier], [mission.id]: (prev.attempts[prev.tier][mission.id] ?? 0) + 1 },
        },
      };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
    openSession(mission, save.tier);
    trackMissionStarted(save.tier, 'campaign');
    trackFunnelMissionStarted(save.tier, mission.id);
    setScreen({ name: 'play', index });
  };

  /** One attempt per day per tier. The daily screen blocks a second start. */
  const beginDaily = () => {
    flushSudo(session);
    const date = utcDate();
    const mission = dailyMission(date);
    setSave((prev) => {
      const next = {
        ...prev,
        attempts: {
          ...prev.attempts,
          [prev.tier]: { ...prev.attempts[prev.tier], [mission.id]: (prev.attempts[prev.tier][mission.id] ?? 0) + 1 },
        },
      };
      persistSave(next);
      return next;
    });
    openSession(mission, save.tier, { scope: 'daily', date });
    trackMissionStarted(save.tier, 'daily');
    trackDailyPlayed(save.tier);
    trackFunnelMissionStarted(save.tier, mission.id);
    setScreen({ name: 'play', index: -1, date });
  };

  const onCommand = (raw: string, bs: number) => {
    const isFirst = run.current.commands.length === 0;
    run.current.commands.push({ t: Math.max(0, Date.now() - run.current.startedAt), cmd: raw, bs });
    if (isFirst && session && screen.name === 'play') {
      trackFunnelFirstCommand(session.tier, missionFor(screen.index, screen.date).id);
    }
  };

  const onWon = (index: number, s: GameSession, date?: string) => {
    const mission = missionFor(index, date);
    const base = scoreMission(s.elapsedSeconds(), s.trace, s.hintsUsed);
    const result: MissionResult = {
      score: base,
      points: pointsFor(base, s.tier),
      tier: s.tier,
      seconds: s.elapsedSeconds(),
      trace: s.trace,
      hints: s.hintsUsed,
      endingId: s.endingId ?? undefined,
    };
    const now = new Date();
    const today = utcDate(now);
    const isDaily = Boolean(date);
    trackMissionCleared(s.tier, isDaily ? 'daily' : 'campaign');
    trackFunnelMissionCleared(s.tier, mission.id, result.seconds);

    // Next save state, computed once so achievements and the ops log read the same numbers that get saved.
    const tierResults = save.completed[s.tier];
    const best = tierResults[mission.id];
    const keep = !best || result.points >= best.points ? result : best;
    const completed = isDaily ? save.completed : { ...save.completed, [s.tier]: { ...tierResults, [mission.id]: keep } };
    const dailies = isDaily ? { ...save.dailies, [today]: { ...save.dailies[today], [s.tier]: result } } : save.dailies;
    const sudoTotal = save.counters.sudoTries + s.sudoTries;
    const doneDates = Object.keys(dailies).filter((d) => Object.values(dailies[d] ?? {}).some(Boolean));
    const streak = streakFor(doneDates, today);
    const campaignDoneOnTier = !isDaily && campaignDone(completed[s.tier]);

    const facts = factsFromWin(s, { score: base, isDaily, campaignDoneOnTier, streak, sudoTotal, now });
    const unlocked = newlyUnlocked(facts, save.achievements);

    const record: AttemptRecord = {
      id: newAttemptId(),
      at: now.toISOString(),
      missionId: mission.id,
      scope: isDaily ? 'daily' : 'campaign',
      tier: s.tier,
      attemptNo: save.attempts[s.tier][mission.id] ?? 1,
      outcome: 'cleared',
      score: base,
      points: result.points,
      seconds: s.elapsedSeconds(),
      trace: s.trace,
      hints: s.hintsUsed,
      commands: [...run.current.commands],
    };
    run.current.recorded = true;

    const next: SaveData = {
      ...save,
      completed,
      dailies,
      counters: { ...save.counters, sudoTries: sudoTotal },
      achievements: [...save.achievements, ...unlocked.map((a) => a.id)],
      history: addAttempt(save.history, record),
    };
    persistSave(next);
    setSave(next);
    setToastsReady(false);
    if (unlocked.length) setToast(unlocked);

    setLastRun({
      missionId: mission.id,
      tier: s.tier,
      scope: isDaily ? 'daily' : 'campaign',
      date,
      commands: [...run.current.commands],
      token: run.current.token ?? undefined,
    });
    setSession(null);
    setScreen({ name: 'transfer', index, result, date });
  };

  const onFailed = (index: number, s: GameSession, date?: string) => {
    const outcome = s.failReason === 'time' ? 'timed-out' : 'traced';
    const mission = missionFor(index, date);
    if (outcome === 'traced') {
      trackMissionTraced(s.tier, date ? 'daily' : 'campaign');
      trackFunnelMissionTraced(s.tier, mission.id, s.elapsedSeconds());
    }
    logAttempt(s, mission, date, outcome);
  };

  const onTransferDone = useCallback(() => {
    setScreen((current) =>
      current.name === 'transfer' ? { name: 'debrief', index: current.index, result: current.result, date: current.date } : current,
    );
  }, []);

  const abortMission = () => {
    flushSudo(session);
    const back: Screen = screen.name === 'play' && screen.date ? { name: 'daily' } : { name: 'select' };
    if (session && !session.finished) {
      const mission = missionFor(screen.name === 'play' ? screen.index : -1, screen.name === 'play' ? screen.date : undefined);
      logAttempt(session, mission, screen.name === 'play' ? screen.date : undefined, 'aborted');
      trackFunnelMissionAborted(session.tier, mission.id, session.elapsedSeconds());
    }
    if (reduceMotion) {
      setSession(null);
      setScreen(back);
      return;
    }
    // CRT switch-off, then the job board.
    setClosing(true);
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      setClosing(false);
      setSession(null);
      setScreen(back);
    }, CRT_OFF_MS);
  };

  const resetProgress = () => {
    // A reset is local. The device is signed out of its cloud copy, so the old progress does not sync back.
    // The account can still be restored with its recovery key.
    const next: SaveData = {
      ...DEFAULT_SAVE,
      completed: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
      attempts: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
      tier: save.tier,
      theme: save.theme,
      crt: save.crt,
      typing: save.typing,
      sfx: save.sfx,
      music: save.music,
      volume: save.volume,
      musicVolume: save.musicVolume,
      track: save.track,
      motion: save.motion,
      nickname: save.nickname,
      registerSkipped: save.registerSkipped,
    };
    persistSave(next);
    setSave(next);
  };

  const doneDates = Object.keys(save.dailies).filter((d) => Object.values(save.dailies[d] ?? {}).some(Boolean));
  const dailyStreak = streakFor(doneDates, utcDate());
  const hasSave = summarize(save).completed > 0 || Object.keys(save.attempts[save.tier]).length > 0;

  let body: React.ReactNode = null;
  switch (screen.name) {
    case 'boot':
      body = <Boot onDone={onBootDone} />;
      break;
    case 'landing':
      body = (
        <Landing
          tier={save.tier}
          hasSave={hasSave}
          dailyStreak={dailyStreak}
          muted={muted}
          onToggleMute={toggleMute}
          onRestored={adoptRestored}
          onStart={() => {
            if (isFreshVisitor(save)) {
              quickStartFirstMission();
              return;
            }
            startMusic();
            setScreen(!save.nickname && !save.registerSkipped ? { name: 'register' } : { name: 'menu' });
          }}
          onContinue={() => {
            startMusic();
            setScreen({ name: 'select' });
          }}
          onDaily={toDaily}
          onPrivacy={() => setScreen({ name: 'privacy', from: 'landing' })}
          onAbout={() => setScreen({ name: 'about', from: 'landing' })}
        />
      );
      break;
    case 'register':
      body = (
        <Register
          save={save}
          onRegistered={(patch) => update(patch)}
          onDone={toMenu}
          onSkip={() => {
            update({ registerSkipped: true });
            toMenu();
          }}
        />
      );
      break;
    case 'menu':
      body = (
        <MainMenu
          save={save}
          onNavigate={(target) => {
            if (target === 'select') setScreen({ name: 'select' });
            else if (target === 'settings') setScreen({ name: 'settings' });
            else if (target === 'daily') setScreen({ name: 'daily' });
            else if (target === 'board') setScreen({ name: 'board' });
            else if (target === 'achievements') setScreen({ name: 'achievements' });
            else if (target === 'history') setScreen({ name: 'history' });
            else if (target === 'profile') setScreen({ name: 'profile' });
            else setScreen({ name: 'card' });
          }}
        />
      );
      break;
    case 'select':
      body = <MissionSelect save={save} onTier={setTier} onOpen={(index) => setScreen({ name: 'briefing', index })} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'daily':
      body = <DailyScreen save={save} tier={save.tier} onTier={setTier} onStart={beginDaily} onBoard={() => setScreen({ name: 'board' })} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'board':
      body = <BoardScreen tier={save.tier} onTier={setTier} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'achievements':
      body = <Achievements unlocked={save.achievements} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'history':
      body = <OpsLog save={save} tier={save.tier} onTier={setTier} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'profile':
      body = <Profile save={save} onBack={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'settings':
      body = (
        <Settings
          save={save}
          onChange={update}
          onReset={resetProgress}
          onBack={toMenu}
          onRestored={adoptRestored}
          onPrivacy={() => setScreen({ name: 'privacy', from: 'settings' })}
          onAbout={() => setScreen({ name: 'about', from: 'settings' })}
        />
      );
      break;
    case 'privacy':
      body = <Privacy onBack={() => setScreen(screen.from === 'landing' ? { name: 'landing' } : { name: 'settings' })} />;
      break;
    case 'about':
      body = <About onBack={() => setScreen(screen.from === 'landing' ? { name: 'landing' } : { name: 'settings' })} />;
      break;
    case 'card':
      body = <EndScreen save={save} onMenu={toMenu} />;
      break;
    case 'briefing': {
      const mission = MISSIONS[screen.index];
      body = (
        <Briefing
          mission={mission}
          tier={save.tier}
          attempts={save.attempts[save.tier][mission.id] ?? 0}
          completed={Boolean(save.completed[save.tier][mission.id])}
          unlockedTiers={unlockedTiers(save, screen.index)}
          onTier={setTier}
          onAccept={() => beginAttempt(screen.index)}
          onBack={() => setScreen({ name: 'select' })}
        />
      );
      break;
    }
    case 'play': {
      if (!session) {
        body = <p className="p-6 text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">Booting session...</p>;
        break;
      }
      const index = screen.index;
      const date = screen.date;
      const mission = missionFor(index, date);
      body = (
        <div key={attemptKey} className={closing ? 'crt-off' : 'crt-on'}>
          <MissionScreen
            mission={mission}
            session={session}
            attemptKey={attemptKey}
            speed={save.typing}
            reduceMotion={reduceMotion}
            onWon={(s) => onWon(index, s, date)}
            onRetry={() => (date ? toDaily() : beginAttempt(index))}
            onAbort={abortMission}
            onCommand={onCommand}
            onFailed={(s) => onFailed(index, s, date)}
            boardUnreachable={boardUnreachable}
          />
        </div>
      );
      break;
    }
    case 'transfer':
      body = <Transfer reduceMotion={reduceMotion} onDone={onTransferDone} />;
      break;
    case 'debrief': {
      const index = screen.index;
      const date = screen.date;
      const mission = missionFor(index, date);
      const result = screen.result;
      const rank = rankForScore(result.score).name;
      const share: ShareFacts = date
        ? { label: `DAILY ${dailyNumber(date)}`, tier: DIFFICULTY[result.tier].label, rank, score: result.points, streak: streakFor(doneDates, date) }
        : { label: `JOB ${String(mission.number).padStart(2, '0')} · ${mission.codename}`, tier: DIFFICULTY[result.tier].label, rank, score: result.points };
      const daily: DailyStats | undefined = date
        ? {
            dailyNum: dailyNumber(date),
            tier: isHardFriday(date) ? `${DIFFICULTY[result.tier].label} · Hard Friday` : DIFFICULTY[result.tier].label,
            seconds: result.seconds,
            trace: result.trace,
            hintsUsed: result.hints,
          }
        : undefined;
      const posting = lastRun && lastRun.missionId === mission.id && lastRun.tier === result.tier ? lastRun : null;
      const board = posting
        ? {
            nickname: save.nickname,
            build: (nickname: string): Submission =>
              buildSubmission({
                playerId: getPlayerId(),
                nickname,
                scope: posting.scope,
                date: posting.date,
                missionId: posting.missionId,
                tier: posting.tier,
                commands: posting.commands,
                token: posting.token,
              }),
            onNickname: (nickname: string) => update({ nickname }),
            tier: posting.tier,
            tierLabel: DIFFICULTY[posting.tier].label,
            missionId: posting.missionId,
            // No handle yet: posting the score also registers it and shows the recovery key, in this one step.
            registration: save.nickname ? undefined : { playerId: getPlayerId(), save, onRegistered: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => update(patch) },
          }
        : null;
      body = (
        <Debrief
          mission={mission}
          result={result}
          hintLimit={DIFFICULTY[result.tier].hintLimit}
          rankName={rank}
          reduceMotion={reduceMotion}
          isLast={!date && index === MISSIONS.length - 1}
          nextLabel={date ? 'Daily menu' : undefined}
          onStatsSettled={() => setToastsReady(true)}
          onNext={() => setScreen(date ? { name: 'daily' } : { name: 'briefing', index: index + 1 })}
          onCampaignEnd={() => setScreen({ name: 'card' })}
          onMenu={toMenu}
          card={realWorldFor(mission)}
          share={share}
          daily={daily}
          fileName={date ? `breach-daily-${date}` : `breach-job-${String(mission.number).padStart(2, '0')}`}
          board={board}
        />
      );
      break;
    }
  }

  const lastHoverEl = useRef<Element | null>(null);
  const onPointerOverCapture = (e: ReactPointerEvent) => {
    const btn = (e.target as Element).closest('.btn');
    if (btn && btn !== lastHoverEl.current) {
      lastHoverEl.current = btn;
      play('hover');
    } else if (!btn) {
      lastHoverEl.current = null;
    }
  };
  const onClickCapture = (e: ReactMouseEvent) => {
    if ((e.target as Element).closest('.btn')) play('click');
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`relative min-h-dvh overflow-x-hidden ${reduceMotion ? 'reduce-motion' : ''}`}
      onPointerOver={onPointerOverCapture}
      onClick={onClickCapture}
    >
      {body}
      {wipeKey > 0 && !reduceMotion ? <div key={wipeKey} className="wipe" aria-hidden="true" /> : null}
      <AchievementToast items={toast} active={toastsReady} onDone={() => setToast([])} />
      {save.crt ? (
        <>
          <div className="crt-scanlines" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="crt-vignette" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="crt-flicker" aria-hidden="true" />
          <div className="crt-frame" aria-hidden="true" />
        </>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Icon.tsx`:

```tsx
// The game's icon set. Drawn on a 24px grid with one stroke weight and the theme glow, so every icon reads as part of the same screen.
// No emoji and no image files: each icon is an inline SVG that takes the current text colour.

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

export type IconName =
  | 'terminal'
  | 'play'
  | 'settings'
  | 'sound-on'
  | 'sound-off'
  | 'card'
  | 'tier'
  | 'lock'
  | 'unlock'
  | 'calendar'
  | 'back'
  | 'radar'
  | 'alert'
  | 'map'
  | 'check'
  | 'help'
  | 'motion'
  | 'text'
  | 'colour'
  | 'crt'
  | 'reset'
  | 'transfer'
  | 'mail';

const PATHS: Record<Exclude<IconName, 'tier'>, ReactNode> = {
  terminal: (
    <>
      <rect x="2.5" y="4" width="19" height="16" rx="1.5" />
      <polyline points="6,9 9,12 6,15" />
      <line x1="11" y1="15" x2="17" y2="15" />
    </>
  ),
  play: <polygon points="7,5 19,12 7,19" />,
  settings: (
    <>
      <line x1="4" y1="6" x2="20" y2="6" />
      <circle cx="9" cy="6" r="2" />
      <line x1="4" y1="12" x2="20" y2="12" />
      <circle cx="15" cy="12" r="2" />
      <line x1="4" y1="18" x2="20" y2="18" />
      <circle cx="7" cy="18" r="2" />
    </>
  ),
  'sound-on': (
    <>
      <polygon points="4,9 8,9 13,5 13,19 8,15 4,15" />
      <path d="M16 9a4 4 0 0 1 0 6" />
      <path d="M19 6a8 8 0 0 1 0 12" />
    </>
  ),
  'sound-off': (
    <>
      <polygon points="4,9 8,9 13,5 13,19 8,15 4,15" />
      <line x1="16" y1="9" x2="22" y2="15" />
      <line x1="22" y1="9" x2="16" y2="15" />
    </>
  ),
  card: (
    <>
      <rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="1.5" />
      <line x1="7" y1="9" x2="17" y2="9" />
      <line x1="7" y1="13" x2="13" y2="13" />
    </>
  ),
  lock: (
    <>
      <rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="10" rx="1.5" />
      <path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3" />
    </>
  ),
  unlock: (
    <>
      <rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="10" rx="1.5" />
      <path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 7.7-1.5" />
    </>
  ),
  calendar: (
    <>
      <rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="16" rx="1.5" />
      <line x1="3" y1="10" x2="21" y2="10" />
      <line x1="8" y1="3" x2="8" y2="7" />
      <line x1="16" y1="3" x2="16" y2="7" />
    </>
  ),
  back: <polyline points="15,6 9,12 15,18" />,
  radar: (
    <>
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4.5" />
      <line x1="12" y1="12" x2="18.5" y2="6.5" />
    </>
  ),
  alert: (
    <>
      <path d="M12 3 22 20H2Z" />
      <line x1="12" y1="9.5" x2="12" y2="14" />
      <circle cx="12" cy="17" r="0.6" />
    </>
  ),
  map: <polygon points="3,6 9,4 15,6 21,4 21,18 15,20 9,18 3,20" />,
  check: <polyline points="5,12.5 10,17.5 19,7" />,
  help: (
    <>
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
      <path d="M9.5 9.2a2.5 2.5 0 1 1 3.5 2.3c-.6.3-1 .9-1 1.6" />
      <circle cx="12" cy="17" r="0.6" />
    </>
  ),
  motion: (
    <>
      <path d="M3 12h4l2-5 3 10 2-5h7" />
    </>
  ),
  text: (
    <>
      <polyline points="5,7 5,5 19,5 19,7" />
      <line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19" />
      <line x1="8" y1="19" x2="16" y2="19" />
    </>
  ),
  colour: (
    <>
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" />
      <path d="M12 3a9 9 0 0 1 0 18z" />
    </>
  ),
  crt: (
    <>
      <rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="13" rx="2" />
      <line x1="8" y1="21" x2="16" y2="21" />
      <line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12" y2="21" />
    </>
  ),
  reset: (
    <>
      <path d="M4 12a8 8 0 1 0 2.4-5.7" />
      <polyline points="4,4 4,9 9,9" />
    </>
  ),
  transfer: (
    <>
      <line x1="4" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12" />
      <polyline points="14,7 19,12 14,17" />
      <line x1="4" y1="5" x2="4" y2="19" />
    </>
  ),
  mail: (
    <>
      <rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="1.5" />
      <polyline points="3.5,6 12,13 20.5,6" />
    </>
  ),
};

interface IconProps {
  name: IconName;
  size?: number;
  /** For the tier icon: how many of the three signal bars are lit, 1 to 3. */
  level?: 1 | 2 | 3;
  className?: string;
}

export default function Icon({ name, size = 20, level = 1, className = '' }: IconProps) {
  const common = {
    width: size,
    height: size,
    viewBox: '0 0 24 24',
    fill: 'none',
    stroke: 'currentColor',
    strokeWidth: 1.6,
    strokeLinecap: 'round' as const,
    strokeLinejoin: 'round' as const,
    'aria-hidden': true,
    focusable: false,
    className: `icon-glow shrink-0 ${className}`,
  };

  if (name === 'tier') {
    // Three signal bars. The lit ones carry the glow, the rest stay dim.
    const bars = [
      { x: 4, y: 15, h: 5 },
      { x: 10, y: 11, h: 9 },
      { x: 16, y: 7, h: 13 },
    ];
    return (
      <svg {...common}>
        {bars.map((b, i) => (
          <rect key={i} x={b.x} y={b.y} width="3" height={b.h} rx="0.5" fill={i < level ? 'currentColor' : 'none'} opacity={i < level ? 1 : 0.35} />
        ))}
      </svg>
    );
  }

  return <svg {...common}>{PATHS[name]}</svg>;
}
```

`src/components/Landing.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The first screen people see. A live terminal with a typed tagline, a big START button for people who do not type,
// the difficulty tiers, a daily challenge slot, and Continue when a save exists. Behind it runs the world backdrop.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { Line, Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { landingReply, TAGLINE } from '../lib/landing';
import { trackLandingView, trackStart } from '../lib/analytics';
import { trackFunnelDailyClicked, trackFunnelLandingView, trackFunnelStartClicked } from '../lib/funnel';
import { keyVariantFor, play } from '../lib/audio';
import { vibrateKey } from '../lib/haptics';
import { dailyNumber, isHardFriday, msUntilReset, utcDate } from '../lib/daily';
import Decrypt from './Decrypt';
import Icon from './Icon';
import RestoreInline from './RestoreInline';
import type { RestoreResult } from '../lib/cloud';
import WorldBackdrop from './WorldBackdrop';

interface LandingProps {
  tier: Tier;
  hasSave: boolean;
  onStart: () => void;
  onContinue: () => void;
  onDaily: () => void;
  dailyStreak: number;
  muted: boolean;
  onToggleMute: () => void;
  onRestored: (restored: RestoreResult) => void;
  onPrivacy: () => void;
  onAbout: () => void;
}

const INTRO: Line[] = [
  { text: 'guest@breach:~$ cat tagline.txt', kind: 'echo' },
];

const LOG_LIMIT = 10;

function countdownText(ms: number): string {
  const total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  return [Math.floor(total / 3600), Math.floor((total % 3600) / 60), total % 60].map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')).join(':');
}

export default function Landing({ tier, hasSave, onStart, onContinue, onDaily, dailyStreak, muted, onToggleMute, onRestored, onPrivacy, onAbout }: LandingProps) {
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState(0);
  const [untilReset, setUntilReset] = useState(msUntilReset());
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setUntilReset(msUntilReset()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const [log, setLog] = useState<Line[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    trackLandingView();
    trackFunnelLandingView();
  }, []);

  const start = () => {
    trackStart();
    trackFunnelStartClicked();
    onStart();
  };

  const daily = () => {
    trackFunnelDailyClicked();
    onDaily();
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
    const variant = keyVariantFor(e.key);
    if (variant) {
      play(variant);
      vibrateKey();
    }
  };

  // Typed tagline. Reduced motion shows it all at once.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (document.documentElement.classList.contains('reduce-motion')) {
      setTyped(TAGLINE.length);
      return;
    }
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      setTyped((n) => {
        if (n >= TAGLINE.length) {
          window.clearInterval(timer);
          return n;
        }
        return n + 1;
      });
    }, 28);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const raw = input;
    setInput('');
    if (!raw.trim()) return;
    const reply = landingReply(raw);
    if (reply.action === 'clear') {
      setLog([]);
      return;
    }
    const echo: Line = { text: `guest@breach:~$ ${raw.trim()}`, kind: 'echo' };
    setLog((prev) => [...prev, echo, ...reply.lines].slice(-LOG_LIMIT));
    if (reply.action === 'start') start();
  };

  const visibleTagline = TAGLINE.slice(0, typed);
  const tagDone = typed >= TAGLINE.length;

  return (
    <main className="relative flex min-h-dvh flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden px-4 py-10">
      <WorldBackdrop />
      <div className="landing-vignette pointer-events-none fixed inset-0" aria-hidden="true" />

      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onToggleMute}
        className="fixed right-3 top-3 z-20 flex items-center gap-2 border border-[var(--edge)] bg-[var(--panel)] px-2 py-1 text-[10px] tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)] hover:text-[var(--fg)]"
        aria-pressed={muted}
      >
        <Icon name={muted ? 'sound-off' : 'sound-on'} size={14} />
        {muted ? 'SOUND OFF' : 'SOUND ON'}
      </button>

      <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-2xl" data-keep-clear>
        <h1 className="glow mb-1 text-center text-3xl font-bold tracking-[0.35em] sm:text-4xl">
          <Decrypt text="BREACH" />
        </h1>
        <p className="mb-6 text-center text-xs tracking-[0.3em] text-[var(--dim)]">FREELANCE INTRUSION TERMINAL</p>

        <section className="panel mb-6 p-4 text-sm" aria-label="live terminal">
          {INTRO.map((l, i) => (
            <p key={i} className="text-[var(--fg)] opacity-70">
              {l.text}
            </p>
          ))}
          <p className="my-2 min-h-[3.2rem] leading-relaxed text-[var(--fg)]">
            {visibleTagline}
            {!tagDone ? <span className="caret">▮</span> : null}
          </p>

          <div className="term-scroll max-h-40 space-y-0.5 overflow-y-auto text-[13px] leading-snug">
            {log.map((l, i) => (
              <p key={i} className={l.kind === 'err' ? 'text-[var(--alert)]' : l.kind === 'warn' ? 'text-[var(--warn)]' : l.kind === 'ok' ? 'glow' : l.kind === 'dir' ? 'kali-dir' : l.kind === 'echo' ? 'opacity-70' : ''}>
                {l.text}
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-2 flex items-baseline gap-2">
            <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-pre glow">
              <span className="kali-user">guest@breach</span>
              <span>:</span>
              <span className="kali-path">~</span>
              <span>$ </span>
            </span>
            <input
              ref={inputRef}
              className="term-input"
              value={input}
              onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
              type="text"
              autoComplete="off"
              autoCorrect="off"
              autoCapitalize="off"
              spellCheck={false}
              aria-label="landing terminal input"
              placeholder="type start to begin"
            />
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="panel mb-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-3 border-[var(--fg)] p-5 text-center" aria-label="daily challenge">
          <Icon name="calendar" size={28} />
          <p className="tracking-[0.3em] text-[var(--dim)]">
            TODAY&apos;S JOB · DAILY {dailyNumber(utcDate())}
            {isHardFriday(utcDate()) ? <span className="ml-2 text-[var(--alert)]">⚠ HARD FRIDAY</span> : null}
          </p>
          <p className="text-sm">
            One shared job, everyone, every day. Next job in <span className="glow tabular-nums">{countdownText(untilReset)}</span>
            {dailyStreak > 0 ? <> · streak {dailyStreak} day{dailyStreak === 1 ? '' : 's'}</> : null}
          </p>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-start" onClick={daily}>
            <Icon name="play" size={18} />
            Play today's job
          </button>
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Under 5 minutes. Share your result when you&apos;re done.</p>
        </section>

        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <p className="text-xs tracking-[0.3em] text-[var(--dim)]">OR PLAY THE 8-JOB CAMPAIGN</p>
          <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-2" onClick={start}>
            <Icon name="play" size={16} />
            Start hacking
          </button>
          {hasSave ? (
            <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={onContinue}>
              <Icon name="back" size={16} className="rotate-180" />
              Continue jobs
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <div className="mb-6 flex justify-center">
          <RestoreInline onRestored={onRestored} />
        </div>

        <section className="mb-6" aria-label="difficulty tiers">
          <p className="mb-2 text-center text-xs tracking-[0.3em] text-[var(--dim)]">CHOOSE YOUR HEAT</p>
          <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {TIERS.map((t, i) => (
              <div key={t} className={`panel flex items-start gap-3 p-3 text-xs ${t === tier ? 'border-[var(--fg)]' : ''}`}>
                <Icon name="tier" level={(i + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3} size={24} />
                <div>
                  <p className="glow font-semibold tracking-widest">{DIFFICULTY[t].label.toUpperCase()} x{DIFFICULTY[t].pointMultiplier}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 leading-snug text-[var(--dim)]">{DIFFICULTY[t].description}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <footer className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-4 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          <button type="button" className="underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-[var(--fg)]" onClick={onPrivacy}>
            Privacy
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-[var(--fg)]" onClick={onAbout}>
            About
          </button>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`src/components/MainMenu.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import { summarize } from '../lib/progress';
import Icon, { type IconName } from './Icon';
import { Screen } from './ui';

const LOGO = `██████╗ ██████╗ ███████╗ █████╗  ██████╗██╗  ██╗
██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔════╝██╔══██╗██╔════╝██║  ██║
██████╔╝██████╔╝█████╗  ███████║██║     ███████║
██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔══╝  ██╔══██║██║     ██╔══██║
██████╔╝██║  ██║███████╗██║  ██║╚██████╗██║  ██║
╚═════╝ ╚═╝  ╚═╝╚══════╝╚═╝  ╚═╝ ╚═════╝╚═╝  ╚═╝`;

export type MenuTarget = 'select' | 'settings' | 'card' | 'daily' | 'board' | 'achievements' | 'history' | 'profile';

interface MainMenuProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onNavigate: (target: MenuTarget) => void;
}

interface Item {
  label: string;
  icon: IconName;
  target: MenuTarget;
  show?: boolean;
}

export default function MainMenu({ save, onNavigate }: MainMenuProps) {
  const summary = summarize(save);
  const started = summary.completed > 0 || Object.keys(save.attempts[save.tier]).length > 0;
  const items: Item[] = [
    { label: started ? 'Continue jobs' : 'Start first job', icon: 'play', target: 'select' },
    { label: 'Daily challenge', icon: 'calendar', target: 'daily' },
    { label: 'Global board', icon: 'radar', target: 'board' },
    { label: 'Ops log', icon: 'terminal', target: 'history', show: save.history.length > 0 },
    { label: 'Profile', icon: 'card', target: 'profile' },
    { label: 'Achievements', icon: 'check', target: 'achievements' },
    { label: 'Result card', icon: 'card', target: 'card', show: summary.completed > 0 },
    { label: 'Settings', icon: 'settings', target: 'settings' },
  ];

  return (
    <Screen className="justify-center !py-3">
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
        <p>
          <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">HANDLE </span>
          <span className="glow font-semibold">{save.nickname ?? 'unregistered'}</span>
        </p>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {DIFFICULTY[save.tier].label.toUpperCase()}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* The logo is clipped, never scrolled: the menu must fit on a phone without a page scrollbar. */}
      <pre
        className="glow mb-3 max-w-full overflow-hidden font-bold leading-none"
        style={{ fontSize: 'min(calc(100vw / 62), 10px)' }}
        aria-label="BREACH"
      >
        {LOGO}
      </pre>

      <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
        A freelance hacking terminal. Take jobs from anonymous clients. Breach fake networks. Mind your trace.
      </p>

      <nav className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2" aria-label="menu">
        {items
          .filter((i) => i.show !== false)
          .map((i) => (
            <button key={i.target} type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-3 text-left !min-h-[40px] text-sm" onClick={() => onNavigate(i.target)}>
              <Icon name={i.icon} size={18} />
              {i.label}
            </button>
          ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="panel mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 p-2 text-xs">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[var(--dim)]">JOBS</div>
          <div className="glow text-sm tabular-nums">
            {TIERS.map((t) => `${DIFFICULTY[t].label[0]} ${summarize(save, t).completed}/${summarize(save, t).total}`).join(' · ')}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="text-[var(--dim)]">CAMPAIGN RANK</div>
          <div className="glow text-sm">{summary.rank.name}</div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[var(--dim)]">
            <Icon name="tier" level={DIFFICULTY[save.tier].pointMultiplier >= 2.5 ? 3 : DIFFICULTY[save.tier].pointMultiplier >= 1.5 ? 2 : 1} size={14} />
            DIFFICULTY
          </div>
          <div className="glow text-sm">{DIFFICULTY[save.tier].label}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`src/components/MissionScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { GameSession } from '../engine/session';
import type { MissionDef } from '../engine/types';
import type { TypingSpeed } from '../lib/save';
import { play, setTension } from '../lib/audio';
import { trackFunnelHintUsed } from '../lib/funnel';
import Icon from './Icon';
import { formatTime, pad2 } from './ui';
import Terminal from './Terminal';
import TraceMeter from './TraceMeter';

interface MissionScreenProps {
  mission: MissionDef;
  session: GameSession;
  attemptKey: number;
  speed: TypingSpeed;
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  onWon: (session: GameSession) => void;
  onRetry: () => void;
  onAbort: () => void;
  onCommand?: (raw: string, bs: number) => void;
  /** Called once when the attempt fails, by trace or by the clock. */
  onFailed?: (session: GameSession) => void;
  /** Set when the board is configured but this attempt's run token couldn't be fetched at start. */
  boardUnreachable?: boolean;
}

type Phase = 'playing' | 'tearing' | 'traced';
type Flash = { kind: 'granted' | 'denied'; id: number } | null;

const ALARM_TRACE = 75;
const COUNTDOWN_WARN = 120;

export default function MissionScreen({ mission, session, attemptKey, speed, reduceMotion, onWon, onRetry, onAbort, onCommand, onFailed, boardUnreachable }: MissionScreenProps) {
  const [, setTick] = useState(0);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>('playing');
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState<Flash>(null);
  const prevHints = useRef(0);

  // A fresh attempt starts its hint count over.
  useEffect(() => {
    prevHints.current = 0;
  }, [attemptKey]);

  // Once per second: refresh the clock and check the countdown on timed tiers.
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (session.expireIfLate()) setPhase('tearing');
      setTick((t) => t + 1);
    }, 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [session]);

  // Drone tension follows the trace.
  useEffect(() => {
    setTension(session.trace);
  }, [session.trace]);

  // Report a failed attempt once, so the ops log can record it.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === 'tearing') onFailed?.(session);
    // onFailed is read at the moment the attempt fails; it is not a trigger of its own.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [phase]);

  // A failed attempt tears the screen, then cuts to the TRACED card. Reduced motion skips the tear.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== 'tearing') return;
    // A trace kill ends on an explosion: the alarm has been climbing, then the boom lands as the screen tears.
    const traceKill = session.failReason === 'trace';
    if (reduceMotion) {
      if (traceKill) play('boom');
      setPhase('traced');
      return;
    }
    const boom = traceKill ? window.setTimeout(() => play('boom'), 180) : null;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setPhase('traced'), 900);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      if (boom !== null) window.clearTimeout(boom);
    };
  }, [phase, reduceMotion, session.failReason]);

  // Banners disappear by themselves.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!flash) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 1600);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [flash]);

  const handleUpdate = () => {
    setTick((t) => t + 1);
    if (session.hintsUsed > prevHints.current) {
      prevHints.current = session.hintsUsed;
      trackFunnelHintUsed(session.tier, mission.id, session.hintsUsed);
    }
    if (session.status === 'won') onWon(session);
    if (session.status === 'failed' && phase === 'playing') setPhase('tearing');
  };

  const handleFlash = (kind: 'granted' | 'denied') => setFlash({ kind, id: Date.now() });

  const trace = session.trace;
  const alarmed = trace >= ALARM_TRACE && phase === 'playing';
  const remaining = session.timeRemaining();
  const showCountdown = session.config.showTimer && remaining !== null;

  return (
    <div className="relative mx-auto flex h-[var(--app-h,100dvh)] w-full max-w-5xl flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <div className={`flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col ${phase === 'tearing' ? 'tear' : ''} ${alarmed && !reduceMotion ? 'shake' : ''}`}>
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-1 border-b border-[var(--edge)] px-3 py-2 text-sm">
          <div className="glow font-semibold">
            MISSION {pad2(mission.number)} // {mission.codename}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1">
            {showCountdown ? (
              <span className={remaining <= COUNTDOWN_WARN ? 'trace-hot' : ''} aria-label="time remaining">
                LEFT {formatTime(remaining)}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span>T+ {formatTime(session.elapsedSeconds())}</span>
            )}
            <span>
              HINTS {session.hintsUsed}/{session.hintLimit}
            </span>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger !min-h-[32px] !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" onClick={onAbort}>
              ABORT
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="px-3 py-2">
          <TraceMeter value={trace} />
          {mission.objective ? <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{mission.objective}</p> : null}
          {boardUnreachable ? (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--warn)]" role="status">
              Couldn&apos;t reach the board for this run. Your score will still save locally.
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        {phase !== 'traced' ? (
          <Terminal
            key={attemptKey}
            session={session}
            speed={speed}
            onUpdate={handleUpdate}
            onQuit={onAbort}
            onFlash={handleFlash}
            onCommand={onCommand}
          />
        ) : null}
      </div>

      {alarmed && !reduceMotion ? <div className="alarm-vignette" aria-hidden="true" /> : null}
      {phase === 'tearing' && session.failReason === 'trace' && !reduceMotion ? <div className="boom-flash" aria-hidden="true" /> : null}

      {flash ? (
        <div key={flash.id} className={`banner banner-${flash.kind}`} role="status">
          {flash.kind === 'granted' ? 'ACCESS GRANTED' : 'ACCESS DENIED'}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {phase === 'traced' ? <Traced reason={session.failReason} onRetry={onRetry} onAbort={onAbort} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Traced({ reason, onRetry, onAbort }: { reason: 'trace' | 'time' | null; onRetry: () => void; onAbort: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    play('fail');
  }, []);
  const headline = reason === 'time' ? 'TIME EXPIRED' : 'TRACED';
  const body =
    reason === 'time'
      ? 'The clock ran out. The client has already moved on, and so has the job.'
      : 'Your rig was located. The connection is gone and the contract is void. Your notes are still yours.';
  return (
    <div className="traced-in absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md text-center">
        <Icon name="alert" size={40} className="trace-hot mx-auto mb-3" />
        <p className="trace-hot break-words text-3xl font-bold tracking-[0.2em] sm:text-4xl sm:tracking-[0.3em]">{headline}</p>
        <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-relaxed">{body}</p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={onRetry}>
            Retry mission
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="btn btn-danger" onClick={onAbort}>
            Back to job board
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/MissionSelect.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import { missionUnlocked } from '../lib/progress';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';
import TierBadge from './TierBadge';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface MissionSelectProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onTier: (tier: Tier) => void;
  onOpen: (index: number) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export default function MissionSelect({ save, onTier, onOpen, onBack }: MissionSelectProps) {
  const tier = save.tier;
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="JOB BOARD" subtitle="Anonymous postings. Pay on delivery." icon="map" />

      <div className="mb-6" role="radiogroup" aria-label="difficulty">
        <p className="mb-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">DIFFICULTY</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          {TIERS.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              type="button"
              role="radio"
              aria-checked={tier === t}
              className="btn !min-h-[40px] text-xs sm:text-sm"
              onClick={() => onTier(t)}
            >
              <Icon name="tier" level={(TIERS.indexOf(t) + 1) as 1 | 2 | 3} size={16} />
              {tier === t ? '● ' : '○ '}
              {DIFFICULTY[t].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{DIFFICULTY[tier].description}</p>
      </div>

      <ol className="space-y-3">
        {MISSIONS.map((m, index) => {
          const unlocked = missionUnlocked(save, tier, index);
          const result = save.completed[tier][m.id];
          const attempts = save.attempts[tier][m.id] ?? 0;
          const status = !unlocked ? 'LOCKED' : result ? `CLEARED  ${result.points} pts` : 'OPEN';
          return (
            <li key={m.id} className="boot-in" style={{ animationDelay: `${index * 120}ms` }}>
              <button
                type="button"
                className={`btn flex w-full flex-col items-start gap-1 text-left sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between ${!unlocked ? 'locked-flicker' : ''}`}
                disabled={!unlocked}
                onClick={() => onOpen(index)}
                aria-label={`Mission ${m.number} ${m.codename}, ${status.toLowerCase()}`}
              >
                <span>
                  <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">{String(m.number).padStart(2, '0')}  </span>
                  <span className="glow font-semibold">{m.codename}</span>
                  <span className="block text-xs normal-case tracking-normal text-[var(--dim)]">
                    {unlocked ? m.title : 'Complete the previous job on this difficulty to unlock.'}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                  <span className="flex gap-1" aria-label="cleared on difficulties">
                    {TIERS.map((t) => (
                      <TierBadge key={t} tier={t} cleared={Boolean(save.completed[t][m.id])} />
                    ))}
                  </span>
                  <span>
                    {status}
                    {attempts ? `  (${attempts} attempt${attempts === 1 ? '' : 's'})` : ''}
                  </span>
                </span>
              </button>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>

      <div className="mt-auto pt-8">
        <button type="button" className="btn w-full" onClick={onBack}>
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```
