`src/components/OpsLog.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The ops log: every attempt on this device, styled like reading the history file on your rig. Per-job stats, a replay
// of any of your own attempts, and the best run on the global board once you have cleared that job on that tier.

import { useState } from 'react';
import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { RunCommand } from '../engine/replay';
import type { MissionDef, Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import { dailyMission } from '../lib/daily';
import { favouriteCommand, missionStats, totalSeconds, type AttemptRecord, type Outcome } from '../lib/history';
import { fetchTopRun, leaderboardConfigured } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import ReplayViewer from './ReplayViewer';
import { formatTime, Header, Screen } from './ui';

interface OpsLogProps {
  save: SaveData;
  tier: Tier;
  onTier: (tier: Tier) => void;
  onBack: () => void;
}

const OUTCOME_LABEL: Record<Outcome, string> = {
  cleared: 'CLEARED',
  traced: 'TRACED',
  aborted: 'ABORTED',
  'timed-out': 'TIMED OUT',
};

const OUTCOME_CLASS: Record<Outcome, string> = {
  cleared: 'glow',
  traced: 'text-[var(--alert)]',
  aborted: 'text-[var(--dim)]',
  'timed-out': 'text-[var(--warn)]',
};

/** The mission an attempt was for. Daily attempts are rebuilt from their id, which carries the date. */
function missionOf(attempt: AttemptRecord): MissionDef | null {
  if (attempt.scope === 'daily') return dailyMission(attempt.missionId.slice('daily-'.length));
  return MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === attempt.missionId) ?? null;
}

function labelOf(attempt: AttemptRecord): string {
  if (attempt.scope === 'daily') return `DAILY ${attempt.missionId.slice('daily-'.length)}`;
  const m = MISSIONS.find((x) => x.id === attempt.missionId);
  return m ? `${String(m.number).padStart(2, '0')} ${m.codename}` : attempt.missionId;
}

function stamp(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return `${d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} ${d.toISOString().slice(11, 16)}Z`;
}

export default function OpsLog({ save, tier, onTier, onBack }: OpsLogProps) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [replay, setReplay] = useState<{ title: string; mission: MissionDef; tier: Tier; commands: RunCommand[] } | null>(null);
  const [topState, setTopState] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'none' | 'offline'>('idle');

  const shown = save.history.filter((a) => a.tier === tier && (filter === 'all' || a.missionId === filter));
  const fav = favouriteCommand(save.history);
  const hours = (totalSeconds(save.history) / 3600).toFixed(1);

  const watchTop = async (m: MissionDef) => {
    if (!leaderboardConfigured()) {
      setTopState('offline');
      return;
    }
    setTopState('loading');
    try {
      const top = await fetchTopRun(m.id, tier);
      if (!top) {
        setTopState('none');
        return;
      }
      setTopState('idle');
      setReplay({ title: `#1 ${top.nickname} · ${m.codename} · ${DIFFICULTY[tier].label}`, mission: m, tier, commands: top.commands });
    } catch {
      setTopState('offline');
    }
  };

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="OPS LOG" subtitle={`~/.bash_history for this rig. ${save.history.length} attempts recorded. Newest first.`} icon="terminal" />

      <div className="mb-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-4">
        <div className="panel p-2">
          <p className="text-[var(--dim)]">TIME HACKING</p>
          <p className="glow tabular-nums">{hours} h</p>
        </div>
        <div className="panel p-2">
          <p className="text-[var(--dim)]">FAVOURITE COMMAND</p>
          <p className="glow">{fav ? `${fav.name} (${fav.count})` : 'none yet'}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="panel p-2 sm:col-span-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="difficulty">
          <p className="mb-1 text-[var(--dim)]">DIFFICULTY</p>
          <div className="flex gap-1">
            {TIERS.map((t) => (
              <button key={t} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={tier === t} className="btn !min-h-[32px] !px-2 text-xs" onClick={() => onTier(t)}>
                {DIFFICULTY[t].label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">Job</span>
          <select className="panel px-2 py-2 text-xs" value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="all">all jobs</option>
            {MISSIONS.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                {String(m.number).padStart(2, '0')} {m.codename}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        {filter !== 'all' ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn !min-h-[36px] text-xs" onClick={() => watchTop(MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === filter)!)} disabled={!save.completed[tier][filter]}>
            {save.completed[tier][filter] ? 'Watch the #1 run' : 'Clear this job to unlock the #1 run'}
          </button>
        ) : null}
        {topState === 'none' ? <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">Nobody has a replayable run for this job yet.</span> : null}
        {topState === 'offline' ? <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">The global board is offline right now.</span> : null}
        {topState === 'loading' ? <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">Fetching the board...</span> : null}
      </div>

      {filter !== 'all' ? <JobStats save={save} missionId={filter} tier={tier} /> : null}

      {replay ? (
        <ReplayViewer key={replay.title} title={replay.title} mission={replay.mission} tier={replay.tier} commands={replay.commands} onClose={() => setReplay(null)} />
      ) : null}

      {shown.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">No attempts on {DIFFICULTY[tier].label} yet. Nothing here is made up: every line is something you typed.</p> : null}

      <ol className="space-y-2 text-xs sm:text-sm">
        {shown.map((a) => {
          const m = missionOf(a);
          return (
            <li key={a.id} className="panel p-3 font-mono">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">{stamp(a.at)}</span>
                <span className={`${OUTCOME_CLASS[a.outcome]} font-semibold tracking-widest`}>{OUTCOME_LABEL[a.outcome]}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1">
                {labelOf(a)} <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">· attempt {a.attemptNo} · {DIFFICULTY[a.tier].label}</span>
              </p>
              <p className="tabular-nums text-[var(--dim)]">
                {a.outcome === 'cleared' ? `score ${a.score} · ${a.points} pts · ` : ''}time {formatTime(a.seconds)} · trace {a.trace}% · hints {a.hints}
              </p>
              {m && a.commands.length > 0 ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn mt-2 !min-h-[32px] !px-2 !py-1 text-xs"
                  onClick={() => setReplay({ title: `${labelOf(a)} · ${stamp(a.at)}`, mission: m, tier: a.tier, commands: a.commands })}
                >
                  Replay this run
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>

      <div className="mt-auto pt-6">
        <button type="button" className="btn w-full" onClick={onBack}>
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

function JobStats({ save, missionId, tier }: { save: SaveData; missionId: string; tier: Tier }) {
  const s = missionStats(save.history, missionId, tier);
  return (
    <dl className="panel mb-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-2 p-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-5">
      <div>
        <dt className="text-[var(--dim)]">ATTEMPTS</dt>
        <dd className="glow tabular-nums">{s.attempts}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-[var(--dim)]">FIRST CLEAR</dt>
        <dd className="glow">{s.firstClearAttempt ? `attempt ${s.firstClearAttempt}` : 'not yet'}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-[var(--dim)]">BEST SCORE</dt>
        <dd className="glow tabular-nums">{s.bestScore ?? '-'}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-[var(--dim)]">BEST TIME</dt>
        <dd className="glow tabular-nums">{s.bestSeconds !== null ? formatTime(s.bestSeconds) : '-'}</dd>
      </div>
      <div>
        <dt className="text-[var(--dim)]">CLEAR RATE</dt>
        <dd className="glow tabular-nums">{Math.round(s.clearRate * 100)}%</dd>
      </div>
    </dl>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

// Privacy, read like a briefing file: what's kept on this device, what the cloud keeps when you register, that there
// are no tracking cookies, and how to ask for your data to be deleted.

import { BUG_REPORT_CONTACT } from '../missions/shared';
import Icon from './Icon';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';

export default function Privacy({ onBack }: { onBack: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="PRIVACY" subtitle="What this game keeps, and where. Short, because that's how it should be." icon="lock" />

      <div className="mb-8 space-y-6 text-sm leading-relaxed">
        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
            <Icon name="terminal" size={16} />
            ON THIS DEVICE
          </h2>
          <p>
            Your progress, achievements, settings, nickname, and the ops log — including the commands you typed — live in this browser&apos;s local
            storage. None of it leaves the device unless you register a handle.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
            <Icon name="radar" size={16} />
            IN THE CLOUD, IF YOU REGISTER
          </h2>
          <p className="mb-2">Registering a handle sends: your nickname, a one-way hash of your recovery key, a one-way hash of this device&apos;s sync token, and a copy of your save for syncing. Posting to the global board sends a job&apos;s command log, so the score can be checked by replaying it.</p>
          <p>
            Nothing you send is tied to your name, your email, or any account. The recovery key and sync token are never stored readable on the
            server — only their hashes.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
            <Icon name="sound-off" size={16} />
            NO TRACKING COOKIES
          </h2>
          <p>This is a static site. It does not set tracking cookies. Analytics, where enabled, runs without cookies and without identifying you.</p>
          <p className="mt-2">
            It also counts anonymous gameplay funnel steps — things like a job starting, a hint being used, or a run being traced — tagged only with a
            random id for this browser tab, never your nickname or device id. That id disappears when the tab closes. These counts are never shown
            with any player&apos;s name, and nothing is collected at all if this browser has Do Not Track or Global Privacy Control turned on.
          </p>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
            <Icon name="mail" size={16} />
            DELETING YOUR DATA
          </h2>
          <p>
            Clearing this browser&apos;s site data removes everything stored locally. To remove a registered handle and its cloud save, email{' '}
            <a className="underline underline-offset-4" href={`mailto:${BUG_REPORT_CONTACT}`}>
              {BUG_REPORT_CONTACT}
            </a>{' '}
            with your handle or recovery key. There is no self-serve delete yet — a person reads every request.
          </p>
        </section>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-auto">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2" onClick={onBack}>
          <Icon name="back" size={16} />
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`src/components/Profile.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The player profile: handle, campaign rank per tier, jobs cleared, streaks, achievements, favourite command, total time.
// Everything here is read from the local save, so it works offline. The card at the bottom shares it as an image.

import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import { ACHIEVEMENTS } from '../lib/achievements';
import { bestStreak, streakFor, utcDate } from '../lib/daily';
import { favouriteCommand, totalSeconds } from '../lib/history';
import { summarize } from '../lib/progress';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import ShareBar from './ShareBar';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';

interface ProfileProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onBack: () => void;
}

export default function Profile({ save, onBack }: ProfileProps) {
  const handle = save.nickname ?? 'unregistered';
  const perTier = TIERS.map((t) => ({ tier: t, summary: summarize(save, t) }));
  const jobsCleared = perTier.reduce((n, p) => n + p.summary.completed, 0);
  const points = perTier.reduce((n, p) => n + p.summary.points, 0);
  const doneDates = Object.keys(save.dailies).filter((d) => Object.values(save.dailies[d] ?? {}).some(Boolean));
  const current = streakFor(doneDates, utcDate());
  const best = bestStreak(doneDates);
  const unlocked = save.achievements.length;
  const fav = favouriteCommand(save.history);
  const hours = (totalSeconds(save.history) / 3600).toFixed(1);

  const bestRank = perTier.map((p) => p.summary.rank.name).sort((a, b) => rankOrder(b) - rankOrder(a))[0];

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="PROFILE" subtitle="Your record on this rig. Shared as a card, never as a file." icon="card" />

      <section className="panel mb-6 p-4">
        <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">HANDLE</p>
        <p className="glow text-2xl font-bold">{handle}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm">
          Best rank so far: <span className="glow">{bestRank}</span> · {points} points
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-3">
        {perTier.map(({ tier, summary }) => (
          <div key={tier} className="panel p-3">
            <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">{DIFFICULTY[tier].label.toUpperCase()} RANK</p>
            <p className="glow">{summary.rank.name}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
              {summary.completed}/{summary.total} jobs
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>

      <dl className="panel mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 p-4 text-sm sm:grid-cols-4">
        <Stat label="JOBS CLEARED" value={String(jobsCleared)} />
        <Stat label="DAILY STREAK" value={`${current} day${current === 1 ? '' : 's'}`} />
        <Stat label="BEST STREAK" value={`${best} day${best === 1 ? '' : 's'}`} />
        <Stat label="ACHIEVEMENTS" value={`${unlocked}/${ACHIEVEMENTS.length}`} />
        <Stat label="FAVOURITE COMMAND" value={fav ? `${fav.name} (${fav.count})` : 'none yet'} />
        <Stat label="TIME HACKING" value={`${hours} h`} />
        <Stat label="ATTEMPTS LOGGED" value={String(save.history.length)} />
      </dl>

      <ShareBar
        fileName="breach-profile"
        facts={{
          label: `PROFILE · ${handle.toUpperCase()}`,
          tier: 'all tiers',
          rank: bestRank,
          score: points,
          streak: best > 0 ? best : undefined,
          detail: `${jobsCleared} jobs · ${unlocked} achievements`,
        }}
      />

      <div className="mt-auto pt-6">
        <button type="button" className="btn w-full" onClick={onBack}>
          Back
        </button>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}

const RANK_ORDER = ['Script Kiddie', 'Packet Sniffer', 'Keyboard Cowboy', 'Black Hat', 'Shadow Broker', 'Ghost'];
function rankOrder(name: string): number {
  return RANK_ORDER.indexOf(name);
}

function Stat({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <dt className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="glow tabular-nums">{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/QuickKeys.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Tap targets above the on-screen keyboard. Run-now keys execute immediately; insert keys fill the prompt.

import type { InputMode } from '../engine/session';

interface QuickKeysProps {
  mode: InputMode;
  onRun: (cmd: string) => void;
  onInsert: (text: string) => void;
  onTab: () => void;
  onHistory: (dir: 'up' | 'down') => void;
}

const RUN_NOW = ['help', 'ls', 'pwd', 'nmap', 'ps', 'mail', 'hint', 'whoami', 'exit'];
const INSERT = ['ls -la', 'cd ', 'cat ', 'ssh ', 'su ', 'john ', 'base64 -d', 'grep -r ', 'find ', 'kill ', 'submit '];

export default function QuickKeys({ mode, onRun, onInsert, onTab, onHistory }: QuickKeysProps) {
  const shell = mode === 'shell';
  return (
    <div className="border-t border-[var(--edge)] bg-[var(--panel)] px-2 py-2" style={{ paddingBottom: 'max(0.5rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))' }}>
      <div className="term-scroll flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1" role="toolbar" aria-label="quick keys">
        <button type="button" className="quick-key" disabled={!shell} onClick={onTab}>
          TAB
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="quick-key" disabled={!shell} onClick={() => onHistory('up')}>
          ↑
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="quick-key" disabled={!shell} onClick={() => onHistory('down')}>
          ↓
        </button>
        {RUN_NOW.map((cmd) => (
          <button key={cmd} type="button" className="quick-key" disabled={!shell} onClick={() => onRun(cmd)}>
            {cmd}
          </button>
        ))}
        {INSERT.map((text) => (
          <button key={text} type="button" className="quick-key" disabled={!shell} onClick={() => onInsert(text)}>
            {text.trim()}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/RealWorldCard.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The debrief's classified file: the real attack the job copied, one real example, and the defence. Under thirty
// seconds to read, and skippable at any time.

import { useState } from 'react';
import type { RealWorldCard as Card } from '../lib/realworld';
import Icon from './Icon';

export default function RealWorldCard({ card }: { card: Card }) {
  const [skipped, setSkipped] = useState(false);
  if (skipped) return null;

  return (
    <section className="panel relative mb-8 p-4 text-sm" aria-label="real world file">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs tracking-[0.25em] text-[var(--warn)]">
          <Icon name="lock" size={16} />
          CLASSIFIED // REAL WORLD FILE
        </p>
        <button type="button" className="btn !min-h-[32px] !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => setSkipped(true)}>
          Skip
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="mb-3 text-base font-semibold tracking-wide glow">{card.attack}</p>
      <div className="space-y-3 leading-relaxed">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">REAL EXAMPLE</p>
          <p>{card.example}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">HOW DEFENDERS STOP IT</p>
          <p>{card.defence}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`src/components/Register.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// "Register your handle": the first screen after the landing page. Pick a handle, get a recovery key, shown once.
// Skippable. Without a key, progress stays on this device, so the screen explains why the key matters.

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { cloudConfigured, registerAccount } from '../lib/cloud';
import { trackHandleRegistered } from '../lib/analytics';
import { getPlayerId } from '../lib/leaderboard';
import { nicknameProblem } from '../lib/nickname';
import type { SaveData } from '../lib/save';
import Icon from './Icon';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';

interface RegisterProps {
  save: SaveData;
  /** Called with the new handle, and the key and token when the account was created. */
  onRegistered: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => void;
  /** Leaves the register screen once the key is saved, or the player is done with it. */
  onDone: () => void;
  onSkip: () => void;
}

type Step = { name: 'handle' } | { name: 'key'; recoveryKey: string; online: boolean } | { name: 'busy' };

export default function Register({ save, onRegistered, onDone, onSkip }: RegisterProps) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(save.nickname ?? '');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>({ name: 'handle' });
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const handle = draft.trim();
    const problem = nicknameProblem(handle);
    if (problem) {
      setError(problem);
      return;
    }
    setError(null);

    if (!cloudConfigured()) {
      onRegistered({ nickname: handle, registerSkipped: false });
      trackHandleRegistered(false);
      setStep({ name: 'key', recoveryKey: '', online: false });
      return;
    }
    setStep({ name: 'busy' });
    const playerId = getPlayerId();
    const result = await registerAccount(playerId, handle, { ...save, nickname: handle, playerId });
    if (!result.ok) {
      // Offline or refused: keep the handle on this device and say why. The key can be made later from Settings.
      setError(result.reason);
      onRegistered({ nickname: handle, playerId, registerSkipped: false });
      trackHandleRegistered(false);
      setStep({ name: 'handle' });
      return;
    }
    onRegistered({ nickname: handle, playerId, recoveryKey: result.value.recoveryKey, syncToken: result.value.syncToken });
    trackHandleRegistered(true);
    setStep({ name: 'key', recoveryKey: result.value.recoveryKey, online: true });
  };

  const copy = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  };

  if (step.name === 'key') {
    return (
      <Screen>
        <Header title="RECOVERY KEY" subtitle="Save it now. It is shown once on this screen." icon="lock" />
        {step.online ? (
          <>
            <p className="mb-2 text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">YOUR KEY</p>
            <p className="glow mb-3 break-all text-2xl font-bold tracking-widest sm:text-3xl">{step.recoveryKey}</p>
            <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={() => copy(step.recoveryKey)}>
                <Icon name="card" size={16} />
                {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy key'}
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="mb-6 space-y-2 border-l-2 border-[var(--warn)] pl-3 text-sm">
              <p className="text-[var(--warn)]">Save this somewhere safe.</p>
              <p>
                Browsers can clear their data, and iPhones delete a site&apos;s data after seven days unused. This key is the only way to get your progress back on
                another device or browser.
              </p>
              <p className="text-[var(--dim)]">You can see it again on this device in Settings. It is never stored on our servers in readable form.</p>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p className="mb-6 text-sm">
            Cloud save is offline, so your handle is saved on this device only. Create your recovery key from Settings once you are online.
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="mt-auto">
          <button type="button" className="btn w-full" onClick={onDone}>
            I have saved it. Continue
          </button>
        </div>
      </Screen>
    );
  }

  const busy = step.name === 'busy';
  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="REGISTER YOUR HANDLE" subtitle="Every operator needs a name on the board." icon="terminal" />

      <form onSubmit={submit} className="mb-6 space-y-4">
        <label className="block">
          <span className="mb-2 block text-xs tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">HANDLE</span>
          <input
            className="term-input panel w-full px-3 py-3"
            value={draft}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
            maxLength={16}
            autoComplete="off"
            spellCheck={false}
            placeholder="3 to 16 characters"
            aria-describedby="handle-help"
            disabled={busy}
          />
        </label>
        <p id="handle-help" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          Letters, numbers, _ and -. Your handle shows on the global board. It is not tied to an email or an account.
        </p>
        {error ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--alert)]">{error}</p> : null}
        <button type="submit" className="btn flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2" disabled={busy}>
          <Icon name="check" size={16} />
          {busy ? 'Registering...' : 'Register handle'}
        </button>
      </form>

      <div className="mt-auto">
        <button type="button" className="btn w-full text-sm" onClick={onSkip} disabled={busy}>
          Skip for now
        </button>
        <p className="mt-3 text-center text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Skipping keeps everything on this device. You can register later from Settings.</p>
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`src/components/ReplayViewer.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Watch a run type itself out. The commands are re-run through the engine, so what you see is what the original attempt
// printed. Play, pause, restart, and 1x, 2x or 4x. Long pauses in the original are shortened so nothing drags.

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { replayTimeline, type RunCommand } from '../engine/replay';
import type { Line, MissionDef, Tier } from '../engine/types';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface ReplayViewerProps {
  mission: MissionDef;
  tier: Tier;
  commands: RunCommand[];
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

const SPEEDS = [1, 2, 4] as const;
/** Gaps longer than this are cut short, so a pause while reading does not stall the replay. */
const MAX_GAP_MS = 1500;

function lineClass(kind: Line['kind']): string {
  if (kind === 'err') return 'text-[var(--alert)]';
  if (kind === 'warn') return 'text-[var(--warn)]';
  if (kind === 'ok' || kind === 'sys' || kind === 'title') return 'glow';
  if (kind === 'dim' || kind === 'echo') return 'text-[var(--dim)]';
  if (kind === 'dir') return 'kali-dir';
  if (kind === 'exec') return 'kali-exec';
  return '';
}

export default function ReplayViewer({ mission, tier, commands, title, onClose }: ReplayViewerProps) {
  const timeline = useMemo(() => replayTimeline(mission, tier, commands), [mission, tier, commands]);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(0);
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(true);
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState<(typeof SPEEDS)[number]>(1);

  // Schedule the next command after the gap the original had, scaled by speed.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing || done >= timeline.length) return;
    const prevT = done === 0 ? 0 : timeline[done - 1].t;
    const gap = Math.min(MAX_GAP_MS, Math.max(40, timeline[done].t - prevT));
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setDone((n) => n + 1), gap / speed);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [playing, done, speed, timeline]);

  const finished = done >= timeline.length;
  const shown = timeline.slice(0, done);

  return (
    <section className="panel mb-6 flex flex-col gap-3 p-3" aria-label="replay">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
        <p className="glow font-semibold tracking-widest">REPLAY // {title}</p>
        <button type="button" className="btn !min-h-[32px] !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" onClick={onClose}>
          Close
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="term-scroll max-h-[46vh] min-h-[10rem] overflow-y-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words rounded border border-[var(--edge)] bg-[var(--bg)] p-2 text-[13px] leading-snug" role="log" aria-live="off">
        {shown.map((step, i) =>
          step.lines.map((l, j) => (
            <div key={`${i}-${j}`} className={lineClass(l.kind)}>
              {l.text || ' '}
            </div>
          )),
        )}
        {!finished && playing ? <span className="caret">▮</span> : null}
        {finished ? <p className="mt-2 text-[var(--dim)]">end of recording</p> : null}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center gap-2 !min-h-[36px] text-xs" onClick={() => (finished ? (setDone(0), setPlaying(true)) : setPlaying((p) => !p))}>
          <Icon name={playing && !finished ? 'reset' : 'play'} size={14} />
          {finished ? 'Replay again' : playing ? 'Pause' : 'Play'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn !min-h-[36px] text-xs" onClick={() => { setDone(0); setPlaying(true); }}>
          Restart
        </button>
        <div className="flex gap-1" role="radiogroup" aria-label="playback speed">
          {SPEEDS.map((s) => (
            <button key={s} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={speed === s} className="btn !min-h-[36px] !px-3 text-xs" onClick={() => setSpeed(s)}>
              {s}x
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <span className="tabular-nums text-[var(--dim)]">
          {done}/{timeline.length} commands
        </span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`src/components/RestoreInline.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// "Restore from key" for the landing page: a single line that opens a field. Works on a new device or browser.

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { restoreAccount, type RestoreResult } from '../lib/cloud';
import { isWellFormedKey, normalizeRecoveryKey } from '../lib/recoverykey';

export default function RestoreInline({ onRestored }: { onRestored: (r: RestoreResult) => void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [key, setKey] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isWellFormedKey(key)) {
      setError('That does not look like a recovery key.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const r = await restoreAccount(normalizeRecoveryKey(key));
    setBusy(false);
    if (!r.ok) {
      setError(r.reason);
      return;
    }
    onRestored(r.value);
    setKey('');
    setOpen(false);
  };

  if (!open) {
    return (
      <button type="button" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)] underline underline-offset-4" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        Restore from recovery key
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="panel flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col gap-2 p-3 text-xs">
      <label className="block">
        <span className="mb-1 block tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">RECOVERY KEY</span>
        <input
          className="term-input w-full uppercase"
          value={key}
          onChange={(e) => setKey(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419"
          autoComplete="off"
          spellCheck={false}
          autoFocus
        />
      </label>
      {error ? <p className="text-[var(--alert)]">{error}</p> : null}
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button type="submit" className="btn !min-h-[36px] flex-1 text-xs" disabled={busy || !key.trim()}>
          {busy ? 'Restoring...' : 'Restore'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn !min-h-[36px] text-xs" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
          Cancel
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { play } from '../lib/audio';
import { THEMES, isThemeUnlocked, type ThemeId } from '../lib/themes';
import type { MotionSetting, SaveData, TypingSpeed } from '../lib/save';
import { clearedOnAnyTier } from '../lib/progress';
import Icon from './Icon';
import { Header, Screen } from './ui';
import AccountPanel from './AccountPanel';
import { TRACKS, TRACK_LABELS } from '../lib/music';
import type { RestoreResult } from '../lib/cloud';
import { buildFeedbackMailto } from '../lib/feedback';
import { GAME_VERSION } from '../lib/version';
import { hapticsSupported } from '../lib/haptics';

interface SettingsProps {
  save: SaveData;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<SaveData>) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
  onBack: () => void;
  onRestored: (restored: RestoreResult) => void;
  onPrivacy: () => void;
  onAbout: () => void;
}

const SPEEDS: { id: TypingSpeed; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'fast', label: 'Fast' },
  { id: 'normal', label: 'Normal' },
  { id: 'instant', label: 'Instant' },
];

const MOTION: { id: MotionSetting; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'system', label: 'Follow system' },
  { id: 'full', label: 'Full' },
  { id: 'reduce', label: 'Reduced' },
];

export default function Settings({ save, onChange, onReset, onBack, onRestored, onPrivacy, onAbout }: SettingsProps) {
  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false);
  const [canVibrate, setCanVibrate] = useState(false);
  const cleared = clearedOnAnyTier(save);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCanVibrate(hapticsSupported());
  }, []);

  return (
    <Screen>
      <Header title="SETTINGS" subtitle="Preferences are saved on this device." icon="settings" />

      <section className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]"><Icon name="colour" size={16} />COLOUR</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          {THEMES.map((t) => {
            const unlocked = isThemeUnlocked(t, cleared);
            return (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                type="button"
                className="btn text-left"
                disabled={!unlocked}
                aria-pressed={save.theme === t.id}
                onClick={() => onChange({ theme: t.id as ThemeId })}
              >
                <span className="block">
                  {save.theme === t.id ? '● ' : '○ '}
                  {t.label}
                </span>
                <span className="block text-xs normal-case tracking-normal">
                  {unlocked ? 'unlocked' : `locked: clear ${t.unlockAfter} job${t.unlockAfter === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]"><Icon name="sound-on" size={16} />SOUND</h2>
        <label className="mb-4 block text-sm">
          <span className="mb-2 flex justify-between">
            <span>Volume</span>
            <span className="tabular-nums">{Math.round(save.volume * 100)}%</span>
          </span>
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={100}
            step={5}
            value={Math.round(save.volume * 100)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ volume: Number(e.target.value) / 100 })}
            className="w-full accent-[var(--fg)]"
            aria-label="volume"
          />
        </label>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <button type="button" className="btn" aria-pressed={save.sfx} onClick={() => onChange({ sfx: !save.sfx })}>
            Effects: {save.sfx ? 'on' : 'off'}
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="btn" aria-pressed={save.music} onClick={() => onChange({ music: !save.music })}>
            Music: {save.music ? 'on' : 'off'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8" aria-label="keyboard sound">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="terminal" size={16} />
          KEYBOARD
        </h2>
        <label className="mb-4 block text-sm">
          <span className="mb-2 flex justify-between">
            <span>Typing sound volume</span>
            <span className="tabular-nums">{Math.round(save.keySoundsVolume * 100)}%</span>
          </span>
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={100}
            step={5}
            value={Math.round(save.keySoundsVolume * 100)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ keySoundsVolume: Number(e.target.value) / 100 })}
            className="w-full accent-[var(--fg)]"
            aria-label="keyboard sound volume"
          />
        </label>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <button type="button" className="btn" aria-pressed={save.keySounds} onClick={() => onChange({ keySounds: !save.keySounds })}>
            Keyboard sounds: {save.keySounds ? 'on' : 'off'}
          </button>
          {canVibrate ? (
            <button type="button" className="btn" aria-pressed={save.keyHaptics} onClick={() => onChange({ keyHaptics: !save.keyHaptics })}>
              Keyboard haptics: {save.keyHaptics ? 'on' : 'off'}
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">A mechanical click per keystroke, separate from Effects. Haptics is a tiny buzz on devices that support it.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]"><Icon name="motion" size={16} />MOTION</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {MOTION.map((m) => (
            <button key={m.id} type="button" className="btn !min-h-[40px] text-xs sm:text-sm" aria-pressed={save.motion === m.id} onClick={() => onChange({ motion: m.id })}>
              {save.motion === m.id ? '● ' : ''}
              {m.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Reduced turns off shake, flashes, wipes and the screen tear. Layout and text stay the same.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn"
          aria-pressed={save.crt}
          onClick={() => {
            play(save.crt ? 'crt-off' : 'crt-on');
            onChange({ crt: !save.crt });
          }}
        >
          <Icon name="crt" size={16} />CRT effect: {save.crt ? 'on' : 'off'}
        </button>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]"><Icon name="text" size={16} />TEXT SPEED</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {SPEEDS.map((s) => (
            <button key={s.id} type="button" className="btn" aria-pressed={save.typing === s.id} onClick={() => onChange({ typing: s.id })}>
              {save.typing === s.id ? '● ' : ''}
              {s.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Tap or press Esc on the terminal to finish a line instantly.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="mb-8" aria-label="soundtrack">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="sound-on" size={16} />
          SOUNDTRACK
        </h2>
        <div className="mb-4 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3" role="radiogroup" aria-label="soundtrack">
          {TRACKS.map((t) => (
            <button key={t} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={save.track === t} className="btn text-xs sm:text-sm" onClick={() => onChange({ track: t })}>
              {save.track === t ? '● ' : '○ '}
              {TRACK_LABELS[t]}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <label className="block text-sm">
          <span className="mb-2 flex justify-between">
            <span>Music volume</span>
            <span className="tabular-nums">{Math.round(save.musicVolume * 100)}%</span>
          </span>
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={100}
            step={5}
            value={Math.round(save.musicVolume * 100)}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ musicVolume: Number(e.target.value) / 100 })}
            className="w-full accent-[var(--fg)]"
            aria-label="music volume"
          />
        </label>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">Separate from effects. The soundtrack gets quieter than the effects at any setting.</p>
      </section>

      <AccountPanel save={save} onChange={onChange} onRestored={onRestored} />

      <section className="mb-8" aria-label="support">
        <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name="help" size={16} />
          SUPPORT
        </h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <a className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" href={buildFeedbackMailto({ version: GAME_VERSION, tier: save.tier })}>
            <Icon name="mail" size={16} />
            Report bug / feedback
          </a>
          <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={onPrivacy}>
            <Icon name="lock" size={16} />
            Privacy
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={onAbout}>
            <Icon name="help" size={16} />
            About
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="mt-auto space-y-3 pt-8">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="btn btn-danger w-full"
          onClick={() => {
            if (!confirmReset) {
              setConfirmReset(true);
              return;
            }
            setConfirmReset(false);
            onReset();
          }}
        >
          {confirmReset ? 'Tap again to erase all progress' : 'Reset progress'}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="btn w-full" onClick={onBack}>
          Back
        </button>
      </section>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`src/components/ShareBar.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Share buttons for a finished job, a daily or the campaign. Download the PNG, share it through the phone's sheet
// where the browser supports sharing files, or copy the text. Each button says what happened.

import { useState } from 'react';
import { dailyShareText, renderShareCard, shareText, type DailyStats, type ShareFacts } from '../lib/share';
import { trackShareClicked } from '../lib/analytics';
import { trackFunnelShareClicked } from '../lib/funnel';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface ShareBarProps {
  facts: ShareFacts;
  /** File name stem, such as breach-lighthouse. */
  fileName: string;
  /** For a daily: switches the default share to the copyable emoji row, image card second. */
  daily?: DailyStats;
  /** Extra line appended to a daily's share text once the board post resolves. The campaign path folds this
   * into facts.detail instead, since shareText() already has a slot for it. */
  percentileNote?: string | null;
}

type Note = { kind: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null;

export default function ShareBar({ facts, fileName, daily, percentileNote }: ShareBarProps) {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<Note>(null);
  const [copiedResult, setCopiedResult] = useState(false);
  const text = daily ? [dailyShareText(daily), percentileNote].filter(Boolean).join('\n') : shareText(facts);

  const download = async () => {
    trackShareClicked('download');
    trackFunnelShareClicked();
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const blob = await renderShareCard(facts);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `${fileName}.png`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);
      setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Card saved.' });
    } catch {
      setNote({ kind: 'err', text: 'Could not draw the card on this device.' });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const share = async () => {
    trackShareClicked('share');
    trackFunnelShareClicked();
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const blob = await renderShareCard(facts);
      const file = new File([blob], `${fileName}.png`, { type: 'image/png' });
      const data = { title: 'BREACH', text, files: [file] };
      if (navigator.canShare?.({ files: [file] })) {
        await navigator.share(data);
        setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Shared.' });
      } else {
        // No file sharing here. Share the text and link instead.
        await navigator.share({ title: 'BREACH', text });
        setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Shared.' });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // The user closing the share sheet is not an error.
      if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === 'AbortError') setNote(null);
      else setNote({ kind: 'err', text: 'Sharing is not available here. Use Copy text.' });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const copy = async () => {
    trackShareClicked('copy');
    trackFunnelShareClicked();
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setNote({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Copied to clipboard.' });
    } catch {
      setNote({ kind: 'err', text: 'Copy failed. Select the text instead.' });
    }
  };

  const copyResult = async () => {
    trackShareClicked('copy');
    trackFunnelShareClicked();
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopiedResult(true);
      window.setTimeout(() => setCopiedResult(false), 2000);
    } catch {
      setNote({ kind: 'err', text: 'Copy failed. Select the text instead.' });
    }
  };

  const canNativeShare = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.share === 'function';

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2" aria-label="share">
      {daily ? (
        <div className="panel space-y-2 p-3">
          <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">{text}</pre>
          <button type="button" className="btn flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={copyResult}>
            <Icon name="card" size={16} />
            {copiedResult ? 'Copied' : 'Copy result'}
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={download} disabled={busy}>
          <Icon name="transfer" size={16} />
          Download card
        </button>
        {canNativeShare ? (
          <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={share} disabled={busy}>
            <Icon name="map" size={16} />
            Share
          </button>
        ) : null}
        <button type="button" className="btn flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm" onClick={copy} disabled={busy}>
          <Icon name="card" size={16} />
          Copy text
        </button>
      </div>
      {note ? (
        <p className={`text-xs ${note.kind === 'err' ? 'text-[var(--alert)]' : 'text-[var(--dim)]'}`} role="status">
          {note.text}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

```

`src/components/Terminal.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The terminal view: typed output with a skippable typing effect, a Kali-style prompt that follows the session's mode
// (shell, password, form, choice), history, tab completion (a second tab lists the options), Ctrl+L and Ctrl+C,
// a fast-cycling password guesser while a cracker runs, and the mobile quick-key bar.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { WORDLIST } from '../engine/commands/crypto';
import { CANCEL_MARK } from '../engine/replay';
import { Out, type GameSession } from '../engine/session';
import type { Line } from '../engine/types';
import { keyVariantFor, play } from '../lib/audio';
import { skippable, type Skippable } from '../lib/deferred';
import { vibrateKey } from '../lib/haptics';
import type { TypingSpeed } from '../lib/save';
import { advance, completeAll, toEntries, type Entry } from '../lib/typeout';
import QuickKeys from './QuickKeys';

export interface TerminalProps {
  session: GameSession;
  speed: TypingSpeed;
  onUpdate: () => void;
  onQuit: () => void;
  onFlash: (kind: 'granted' | 'denied') => void;
  /**
   * Every line the player sends, in order, with Ctrl+C at a question logged as CANCEL_MARK. Feeds the replay.
   * `bs` is how many Backspace/Delete keystrokes were typed while composing that line (a suspicion signal; see
   * engine/replay.ts's suspicionFor). Reset to 0 after each call.
   */
  onCommand?: (raw: string, bs: number) => void;
}

/** Commands that run a password attack. They show the guesser for a moment before the result. */
const CRACK_COMMAND = /^\s*(john|hashcat|crack)\b/;
const GUESS_MS = 1300;

const KIND_CLASS: Record<Line['kind'], string> = {
  out: 'text-[var(--fg)]',
  echo: 'text-[var(--fg)] opacity-70',
  err: 'text-[var(--alert)]',
  ok: 'text-[var(--fg)] glow font-semibold',
  warn: 'text-[var(--warn)]',
  dim: 'text-[var(--dim)]',
  sys: 'text-[var(--fg)] glow',
  title: 'text-[var(--fg)] glow font-bold tracking-widest',
  glitch: 'text-[var(--fg)]',
  dir: 'kali-dir',
  exec: 'kali-exec',
};

function Row({ entry }: { entry: Entry }) {
  const { line, shown } = entry;
  const text = line.text.slice(0, shown);
  const typing = shown < line.text.length;
  if (line.kind === 'glitch') {
    return (
      <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap py-0.5">
        <span className="glitch glow" data-text={text}>
          {text}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (line.segments && !typing) {
    return (
      <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">
        {line.segments.map((piece, i) => (
          <span key={i} className={KIND_CLASS[piece.kind]}>
            {piece.text}
          </span>
        ))}
        {!line.text ? ' ' : null}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className={`whitespace-pre-wrap break-words ${KIND_CLASS[line.kind]}`}>
      {text || ' '}
      {typing ? <span className="caret">▮</span> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/** The prompt, coloured the way Kali colours it: green user@host, blue path, red for root. */
function Prompt({ session }: { session: GameSession }) {
  if (session.pending) return <>{session.prompt()}</>;
  const p = session.promptParts();
  return (
    <>
      <span className={p.root ? 'kali-root' : 'kali-user'}>
        {p.user}@{p.host}
      </span>
      <span>:</span>
      <span className="kali-path">{p.path}</span>
      <span>
        {p.symbol}{' '}
      </span>
    </>
  );
}

export default function Terminal({ session, speed, onUpdate, onQuit, onFlash, onCommand }: TerminalProps) {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<Entry[]>(() => toEntries(session.intro(), speed));
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [histIndex, setHistIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [guess, setGuess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [, setTick] = useState(0);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  /** The input that last produced an ambiguous tab. A second tab on the same input lists the options. */
  const lastTab = useRef<string | null>(null);
  /** The guesser's pending result, while a crack command runs. Skipped early so a keystroke is never lost to it. */
  const crackTimer = useRef<Skippable | null>(null);
  /** Backspace/delete keystrokes typed since the last command was sent. A soft suspicion signal; see TerminalProps. */
  const bsCount = useRef(0);

  // One timer drives the typing effect for the whole terminal. Output is revealed in order.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (speed === 'instant') return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setEntries((prev) => advance(prev, speed === 'fast' ? 6 : 3)), 16);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [speed]);

  // The guesser cycles through the wordlist while a cracker runs.
  const cracking = guess !== null;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!cracking) return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setGuess(WORDLIST[Math.floor(Math.random() * WORDLIST.length)]), 55);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [cracking]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = scrollRef.current;
    if (el) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
  }, [entries, guess]);

  const mode = session.inputMode();
  const finished = session.finished;

  const append = (lines: Line[], clear: boolean) => {
    setEntries((prev) => {
      if (clear) return [];
      return [...completeAll(prev), ...toEntries(lines, speed)];
    });
  };

  const emit = (out: Out) => {
    append(out.lines, out.clear);
    for (const sound of out.sounds) play(sound);
    if (out.sounds.includes('granted')) onFlash('granted');
    else if (out.sounds.includes('denied')) onFlash('denied');
    setTick((t) => t + 1);
    onUpdate();
    if (out.quit) onQuit();
  };

  /** Finishes any reply still typing out, and any running cracker, right away. Called on every keystroke, so none are lost to an animation. */
  const skipAnimations = () => {
    setEntries(completeAll);
    crackTimer.current?.skip();
  };

  const run = (raw: string) => {
    if (session.finished) return;
    skipAnimations();
    onCommand?.(raw, bsCount.current);
    bsCount.current = 0;
    setInput('');
    setHistIndex(null);
    const out = session.input(raw);
    if (mode === 'shell' && CRACK_COMMAND.test(raw)) {
      // The command echo shows at once. The guesser runs, then the result lands.
      append(out.lines.slice(0, 1), false);
      setGuess(WORDLIST[0]);
      crackTimer.current = skippable(() => {
        setGuess(null);
        const rest = new Out();
        rest.lines.push(...out.lines.slice(1));
        rest.sounds.push(...out.sounds);
        rest.quit = out.quit;
        emit(rest);
        crackTimer.current = null;
      }, GUESS_MS);
      return;
    }
    if (mode === 'shell' && raw.trim()) play(out.lines.some((l) => l.kind === 'err') ? 'error' : 'command');
    emit(out);
  };

  const tab = () => {
    const before = input;
    const result = session.complete(before);
    setInput(result.value);
    if (result.options.length > 1 && result.value === before) {
      // Nothing more to complete. The first tab does nothing, the second lists the options, like bash.
      if (lastTab.current === before) {
        append([{ text: result.options.join('  '), kind: 'dim' }], false);
        lastTab.current = null;
      } else {
        lastTab.current = before;
      }
    } else {
      lastTab.current = null;
    }
  };

  const recall = (direction: 'up' | 'down') => {
    if (mode !== 'shell') return;
    const hist = session.history;
    if (hist.length === 0) return;
    let next: number | null;
    if (direction === 'up') {
      next = histIndex === null ? hist.length - 1 : Math.max(0, histIndex - 1);
    } else {
      next = histIndex === null ? null : histIndex + 1 >= hist.length ? null : histIndex + 1;
    }
    setHistIndex(next);
    setInput(next === null ? '' : hist[next]);
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    skipAnimations();
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' || e.key === 'Delete') bsCount.current++;
    const key = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (e.ctrlKey && key === 'l') {
      e.preventDefault();
      setEntries([]);
      return;
    }
    if (e.ctrlKey && key === 'c' && !window.getSelection()?.toString()) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (mode === 'shell') {
        append([{ text: `${session.prompt()}${input}^C`, kind: 'echo' }], false);
        setInput('');
        setHistIndex(null);
      } else {
        const o = new Out();
        if (session.cancelPending(o)) {
          onCommand?.(CANCEL_MARK, bsCount.current);
          bsCount.current = 0;
          append(o.lines, false);
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      setEntries(completeAll);
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      e.preventDefault();
      recall(e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? 'up' : 'down');
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (mode === 'shell') tab();
      return;
    }
    if (!e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
      const variant = keyVariantFor(e.key);
      if (variant) {
        play(variant);
        vibrateKey();
      }
    }
  };

  const onSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    run(input);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col" onClick={() => inputRef.current?.focus()}>
      <div ref={scrollRef} className="term-scroll min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2 leading-relaxed">
        {entries.map((entry, i) => (
          <Row key={i} entry={entry} />
        ))}
        {cracking ? (
          <p className="whitespace-pre text-[var(--warn)]" aria-live="off">
            {`  guessing  ${guess}`}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {!finished ? (
          <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
            <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-pre-wrap glow">
              <Prompt session={session} />
            </span>
            <input
              ref={inputRef}
              className="term-input"
              value={input}
              onChange={(e) => {
                // Not just onKeyDown: paste and IME input change the value without a plain keydown.
                skipAnimations();
                setInput(e.target.value);
              }}
              onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
              onFocus={() => {
                const el = scrollRef.current;
                if (el) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
              }}
              type={mode === 'password' ? 'password' : 'text'}
              autoComplete="off"
              autoCorrect="off"
              autoCapitalize="off"
              spellCheck={false}
              aria-label="terminal input"
              autoFocus
            />
          </form>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {!finished ? (
        <QuickKeys
          mode={mode}
          onRun={(cmd) => run(cmd)}
          onInsert={(text) => {
            skipAnimations();
            setInput(text);
            inputRef.current?.focus();
          }}
          onTab={() => {
            skipAnimations();
            if (mode === 'shell') tab();
            inputRef.current?.focus();
          }}
          onHistory={(dir) => {
            skipAnimations();
            recall(dir);
            inputRef.current?.focus();
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
```

`src/components/TraceMeter.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// ASCII trace meter. Turns amber past 50% and pulses red past 75%.

export default function TraceMeter({ value }: { value: number }) {
  const width = 24;
  const filled = Math.round((value / 100) * width);
  const bar = '█'.repeat(filled) + '░'.repeat(width - filled);
  const tone = value >= 75 ? 'trace-hot' : value >= 50 ? 'trace-warm' : '';
  return (
    <div className={`whitespace-pre text-sm ${tone}`} role="meter" aria-label="trace level" aria-valuenow={value} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100}>
      {`TRACE [${bar}] ${String(value).padStart(3)}%`}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/Transfer.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// The moment between the last command and the stats: the payload uploads, a bar fills, and ACCESS GRANTED lands.
// Reduced motion skips straight through.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { play } from '../lib/audio';
import Icon from './Icon';

interface TransferProps {
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  onDone: () => void;
}

const TOTAL_STEPS = 48;
const STEP_MS = 42;
const LOG = [
  'opening tunnel to drop 10.66.0.99',
  'encrypting payload with the client key',
  'packing evidence: 4 files, 1 flag',
  'streaming to QUILL',
];

export default function Transfer({ reduceMotion, onDone }: TransferProps) {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(reduceMotion ? TOTAL_STEPS : 0);
  const [granted, setGranted] = useState(reduceMotion);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (step >= TOTAL_STEPS) {
      if (!granted) {
        setGranted(true);
        play('transfer');
      }
      return;
    }
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setStep((s) => s + 1), STEP_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [step, granted]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!granted) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(onDone, reduceMotion ? 0 : 900);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [granted, onDone, reduceMotion]);

  const pct = Math.round((step / TOTAL_STEPS) * 100);
  const logged = Math.min(LOG.length, Math.floor((step / TOTAL_STEPS) * (LOG.length + 1)));

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-10 screen-curve">
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl">
        <p className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm tracking-widest text-[var(--dim)]">
          <Icon name={granted ? 'unlock' : 'lock'} size={22} />
          {granted ? 'ACCESS GRANTED' : 'DATA TRANSFER'}
        </p>

        <div className="mb-4 whitespace-pre text-sm" role="progressbar" aria-valuenow={pct} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-label="transfer progress">
          {`[${'█'.repeat(Math.round(pct / 4)).padEnd(25, '░')}] ${String(pct).padStart(3)}%`}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1 text-sm">
          {LOG.slice(0, logged).map((l) => (
            <p key={l} className="text-[var(--dim)]">
              &gt; {l}
            </p>
          ))}
          {granted ? <p className="glow mt-4 text-lg font-bold tracking-[0.3em]">ACCESS GRANTED</p> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`src/components/WorldBackdrop.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

// Landing backdrop: a dot-matrix world, attack arcs pinging between cities, and falling code.
// One canvas, one requestAnimationFrame loop, capped near 60fps. It pauses when the tab is hidden and draws a single
// still frame when reduced motion is on. Continent outlines are coarse hand-drawn polygons, not survey data.

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

type Pt = [number, number]; // [longitude, latitude]

// Coarse continent outlines, longitude then latitude.
const LAND: Pt[][] = [
  // North America
  [[-168, 66], [-156, 71], [-140, 70], [-125, 70], [-110, 73], [-95, 72], [-82, 68], [-78, 62], [-64, 60], [-56, 52], [-66, 45], [-76, 38], [-81, 31], [-80, 25], [-83, 29], [-90, 30], [-97, 27], [-97, 21], [-90, 19], [-86, 21], [-88, 16], [-83, 10], [-77, 8], [-80, 7], [-86, 12], [-92, 15], [-105, 20], [-112, 30], [-118, 34], [-124, 40], [-124, 48], [-130, 55], [-140, 60], [-152, 58], [-165, 62]],
  // Greenland
  [[-55, 60], [-44, 60], [-20, 70], [-20, 82], [-60, 82], [-72, 77]],
  // South America
  [[-78, 10], [-72, 12], [-62, 11], [-50, 0], [-35, -5], [-39, -15], [-48, -26], [-58, -35], [-65, -42], [-68, -52], [-74, -50], [-74, -40], [-71, -25], [-70, -18], [-81, -5], [-80, 0]],
  // Eurasia
  [[-10, 36], [-9, 43], [-2, 44], [-5, 48], [2, 51], [8, 54], [10, 57], [5, 60], [8, 63], [15, 68], [25, 71], [40, 68], [60, 70], [75, 73], [90, 77], [110, 76], [140, 72], [160, 70], [180, 66], [178, 62], [160, 58], [163, 53], [155, 58], [140, 50], [135, 43], [128, 38], [126, 34], [121, 30], [118, 24], [108, 21], [106, 10], [100, 13], [100, 4], [104, 1], [98, 8], [97, 16], [92, 22], [85, 21], [80, 15], [77, 8], [72, 20], [66, 25], [58, 26], [52, 28], [48, 30], [40, 28], [36, 35], [28, 37], [26, 40], [20, 40], [16, 38], [12, 44], [8, 44], [3, 42], [-2, 37], [-5, 36]],
  // Africa
  [[-17, 21], [-10, 30], [-6, 36], [10, 37], [20, 32], [32, 31], [35, 28], [43, 12], [51, 12], [40, -3], [40, -15], [35, -25], [27, -34], [18, -34], [12, -18], [13, -5], [9, 4], [-8, 4], [-17, 14]],
  // Australia
  [[114, -22], [122, -18], [130, -12], [137, -12], [142, -11], [146, -18], [153, -27], [150, -37], [141, -38], [131, -31], [116, -35]],
];

const CITIES: { name: string; lon: number; lat: number }[] = [
  { name: 'new york', lon: -74, lat: 40.7 },
  { name: 'mexico city', lon: -99.1, lat: 19.4 },
  { name: 'sao paulo', lon: -46.6, lat: -23.5 },
  { name: 'london', lon: -0.1, lat: 51.5 },
  { name: 'lagos', lon: 3.4, lat: 6.5 },
  { name: 'cairo', lon: 31.2, lat: 30 },
  { name: 'moscow', lon: 37.6, lat: 55.7 },
  { name: 'mumbai', lon: 72.9, lat: 19.1 },
  { name: 'beijing', lon: 116.4, lat: 39.9 },
  { name: 'seoul', lon: 127, lat: 37.6 },
  { name: 'tokyo', lon: 139.7, lat: 35.7 },
  { name: 'sydney', lon: 151.2, lat: -33.9 },
];

const LAT_TOP = 78;
const LAT_BOTTOM = -58;
const CODE_CHARS = '01ABCDEF<>/{}[]=';

function inside(lon: number, lat: number, poly: Pt[]): boolean {
  let hit = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = poly[i];
    const [xj, yj] = poly[j];
    if (yi > lat !== yj > lat && lon < ((xj - xi) * (lat - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) hit = !hit;
  }
  return hit;
}

/** Dots that fall on land, as 0..1 coordinates so they scale with the canvas. */
function landDots(stepX: number, stepY: number): [number, number][] {
  const dots: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let lat = LAT_TOP; lat >= LAT_BOTTOM; lat -= stepY) {
    for (let lon = -180; lon < 180; lon += stepX) {
      if (LAND.some((poly) => inside(lon, lat, poly))) {
        dots.push([(lon + 180) / 360, (LAT_TOP - lat) / (LAT_TOP - LAT_BOTTOM)]);
      }
    }
  }
  return dots;
}

interface Arc {
  from: number;
  to: number;
  t: number;
  speed: number;
}

export default function WorldBackdrop() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = ref.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    let width = 0;
    let height = 0;
    let dots: [number, number][] = [];
    let columns: { x: number; y: number; speed: number }[] = [];
    let arcs: Arc[] = [];
    let colour = '#33ff66';
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let frame = 0;

    const cityPoint = (i: number): [number, number] => {
      const c = CITIES[i];
      return [(c.lon + 180) / 360, (LAT_TOP - c.lat) / (LAT_TOP - LAT_BOTTOM)];
    };

    const newArc = (): Arc => {
      const from = Math.floor(Math.random() * CITIES.length);
      let to = Math.floor(Math.random() * CITIES.length);
      if (to === from) to = (to + 1) % CITIES.length;
      return { from, to, t: 0, speed: 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.25 };
    };

    const resize = () => {
      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5);
      width = window.innerWidth;
      height = window.innerHeight;
      canvas.width = Math.round(width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.round(height * dpr);
      canvas.style.width = `${width}px`;
      canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      // Fewer dots on narrow screens keeps the frame cheap on mid-range phones.
      const narrow = width < 640;
      dots = landDots(narrow ? 7 : 5, narrow ? 6 : 5);
      const colsCount = Math.ceil(width / 16);
      columns = Array.from({ length: colsCount }, (_, i) => ({ x: i * 16, y: Math.random() * height, speed: 20 + Math.random() * 60 }));
      arcs = Array.from({ length: 3 }, newArc);
      colour = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--fg').trim() || colour;
    };

    const project = ([x, y]: [number, number]): [number, number] => [x * width, y * height];

    const keepEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-keep-clear]');
    let keep: DOMRect | null = null;
    const clear = (x: number, y: number) => keep !== null && x > keep.left - 8 && x < keep.right + 8 && y > keep.top - 8 && y < keep.bottom + 8;

    const draw = (dt: number) => {
      frame++;
      keep = keepEl ? keepEl.getBoundingClientRect() : null;
      if (frame % 60 === 0) colour = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--fg').trim() || colour;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      // Land dots.
      ctx.fillStyle = colour;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.24;
      for (const d of dots) {
        const [x, y] = project(d);
        ctx.fillRect(x, y, 2, 2);
      }

      // Falling code.
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.14;
      ctx.font = '12px ui-monospace, Consolas, monospace';
      for (const col of columns) {
        if (!reduce) col.y += (col.speed * dt) / 1000;
        if (col.y > height + 20) col.y = -20;
        if (!clear(col.x, col.y)) ctx.fillText(CODE_CHARS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CODE_CHARS.length)], col.x, col.y);
      }

      // Attack arcs with a bright head and a ping where each one lands.
      for (let i = 0; i < arcs.length; i++) {
        const arc = arcs[i];
        if (!reduce) arc.t += (arc.speed * dt) / 1000;
        if (arc.t >= 1) {
          arcs[i] = newArc();
          continue;
        }
        const [x0, y0] = project(cityPoint(arc.from));
        const [x1, y1] = project(cityPoint(arc.to));
        const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2;
        const cy = Math.min(y0, y1) - Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) * 0.35;
        const steps = 28;
        const headAt = Math.min(1, arc.t);
        ctx.strokeStyle = colour;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        let pen = false;
        for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
          const u = (s / steps) * headAt;
          const px = (1 - u) * (1 - u) * x0 + 2 * (1 - u) * u * cx + u * u * x1;
          const py = (1 - u) * (1 - u) * y0 + 2 * (1 - u) * u * cy + u * u * y1;
          if (clear(px, py)) {
            pen = false;
            continue;
          }
          if (pen) ctx.lineTo(px, py);
          else ctx.moveTo(px, py);
          pen = true;
        }
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
        const hu = headAt;
        const hx = (1 - hu) * (1 - hu) * x0 + 2 * (1 - hu) * hu * cx + hu * hu * x1;
        const hy = (1 - hu) * (1 - hu) * y0 + 2 * (1 - hu) * hu * cy + hu * hu * y1;
        if (!clear(hx, hy)) ctx.fillRect(hx - 1.5, hy - 1.5, 3, 3);
        if (arc.t > 0.9) {
          ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - arc.t) * 3;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(x1, y1, 4 + (arc.t - 0.9) * 120, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };

    const loop = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(64, now - last || 16);
      last = now;
      draw(dt);
      raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };

    const start = () => {
      if (reduce) {
        draw(0);
        return;
      }
      last = performance.now();
      raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    const stop = () => window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    const onVisibility = () => (document.hidden ? stop() : start());

    resize();
    start();
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      stop();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, []);

  return <canvas ref={ref} aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-0 -z-0 opacity-90" />;
}

```

```tsx
// Small presentational helpers shared by the screens.

import type { StoryLine } from '../engine/types';
import Decrypt from './Decrypt';
import Icon, { type IconName } from './Icon';

export function formatTime(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const m = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
  const s = totalSeconds % 60;
  return `${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function pad2(n: number): string {
  return String(n).padStart(2, '0');
}

/** Story text. Lines marked glitch get the glitch treatment. */
export function Story({ lines }: { lines: StoryLine[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 leading-relaxed">
      {lines.map((line, i) => {
        if (typeof line === 'string') return <p key={i}>{line}</p>;
        return (
          <p key={i}>
            <span className="glitch glow" data-text={line.text}>
              {line.text}
            </span>
          </p>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Screen({ children, className = '' }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <main className={`mx-auto flex min-h-dvh w-full max-w-3xl flex-col px-4 py-6 screen-curve ${className}`}>{children}</main>
  );
}

export function Header({ title, subtitle, icon }: { title: string; subtitle?: string; icon?: IconName }) {
  return (
    <header className="mb-6 border-b border-[var(--edge)] pb-3">
      <h1 className="glow flex items-center gap-3 text-xl font-bold tracking-[0.25em]">
        {icon ? <Icon name={icon} size={22} /> : null}
        <Decrypt text={title} />
      </h1>
      {subtitle ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">{subtitle}</p> : null}
    </header>
  );
}
```
