`src/engine/clues.ts`:

```ts
// The clue log: important discoveries from this attempt, plus the player's own notes.
// On tiers with autoClues, the session scans command output for the secrets built here and records what turns up.
// Secrets come from the mission data, so the log only ever holds things the player could actually see.

import type { MissionDef } from './types';

export type ClueCategory = 'ip' | 'username' | 'password' | 'hash' | 'decoded';

export interface Clue {
  category: ClueCategory;
  text: string;
}

export type Secret = Clue;

export const CLUE_LABELS: Record<ClueCategory, string> = {
  ip: 'IP',
  username: 'USER',
  password: 'PASS',
  hash: 'HASH',
  decoded: 'DECODED',
};

/** Account the engine never logs as a username: it appears in almost every `ps` listing. */
const SKIPPED_USERNAMES = new Set(['root']);

/**
 * Every secret the player can discover on this mission's remote hosts. The rig is excluded: it is the player's own machine.
 */
export function buildSecrets(mission: MissionDef): Secret[] {
  const secrets: Secret[] = [];
  for (const machine of mission.machines) {
    if (machine.id === 'rig') continue;
    secrets.push({ category: 'ip', text: machine.ip });
    for (const user of machine.users) {
      if (!SKIPPED_USERNAMES.has(user.name)) secrets.push({ category: 'username', text: user.name });
      if (user.password) secrets.push({ category: 'password', text: user.password });
    }
  }
  for (const entry of mission.hashes) {
    secrets.push({ category: 'hash', text: entry.hash });
    secrets.push({ category: 'password', text: entry.plaintext });
  }
  return secrets;
}

const WORD_CHAR = /[A-Za-z0-9_]/;

/** True when `token` appears in `line` with no letter, digit or underscore touching either end. */
export function containsWord(line: string, token: string): boolean {
  let at = line.indexOf(token);
  while (at >= 0) {
    const before = line[at - 1] ?? '';
    const after = line[at + token.length] ?? '';
    if (!WORD_CHAR.test(before) && !WORD_CHAR.test(after)) return true;
    at = line.indexOf(token, at + 1);
  }
  return false;
}

export class ClueLog {
  private readonly discovered: Clue[] = [];
  private readonly seen = new Set<string>();
  private readonly playerNotes: string[] = [];

  /** Records a discovery once per category and text. Returns true when it is new. */
  record(category: ClueCategory, text: string): boolean {
    const key = `${category}\u0000${text}`;
    if (this.seen.has(key)) return false;
    this.seen.add(key);
    this.discovered.push({ category, text });
    return true;
  }

  addNote(text: string): void {
    this.playerNotes.push(text);
  }

  get clues(): readonly Clue[] {
    return this.discovered;
  }

  get notes(): readonly string[] {
    return this.playerNotes;
  }
}

```

`src/engine/commands/crypto.ts`:

```ts
// Cryptography commands: john (crack and hashcat are aliases), base64 and decode.

import { fromBase64, fromHex, rot13, toBase64 } from '../encoding';
import { canRead, isFile } from '../fs';
import type { CommandSpec, Stdin } from './types';
import type { GameSession, Out } from '../session';

/** The fake wordlist. A hash only cracks if its plaintext is in here. */
export const WORDLIST: readonly string[] = [
  'password',
  '123456',
  'letmein',
  'admin',
  'welcome',
  'monkey',
  'dragon',
  'qwerty',
  'sunshine',
  'princess',
  'football',
  'iloveyou',
  'trustno1',
  'shadow',
  'master',
  'summer',
  'winter',
  'ghost',
  'canary',
  'hunter2',
];

/** The text a base64 command reads: a named file if given, otherwise the pipe. */
function sourceText(s: GameSession, file: string | undefined, stdin: Stdin, out: Out): string | null {
  if (file) {
    const abs = s.resolve(file);
    if (!isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
      out.print(`base64: ${file}: No such file or directory`, 'err');
      return null;
    }
    if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) {
      out.print(`base64: ${file}: Permission denied`, 'err');
      return null;
    }
    return s.machine.files[abs].content.trim();
  }
  if (stdin) return stdin.join('\n').trim();
  out.print('usage: base64 [-d] [file]   (or pipe input in)', 'err');
  return null;
}

export const cryptoCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'john',
    aliases: ['hashcat', 'crack'],
    usage: 'john <hash>',
    summary: 'wordlist attack on a hash (only common passwords fall). hashcat and crack also work',
    example: 'john 3f2a9c7e1b4d8f60a5c2e9d17b8a4f3e',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const hash = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-')).pop();
      if (!hash) {
        out.print('usage: john <hash>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`Loaded 1 password hash. Wordlist: ${WORDLIST.length} entries.`, 'dim');
      out.sound('chatter');
      const entry = s.mission.hashes.find((h) => h.hash === hash);
      if (entry && WORDLIST.includes(entry.plaintext)) {
        out.print(`${hash.slice(0, 16)}...  (${entry.algo})`, 'dim');
        out.print(`MATCH (${entry.algo}): ${entry.plaintext}`, 'ok');
        out.sound('granted');
      } else {
        out.print('No password hashes cracked (wordlist exhausted).', 'err');
        out.sound('denied');
      }
      s.noise(12, out);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'base64',
    usage: 'base64 [-d] [file]',
    summary: 'encode text, or with -d decode it. reads a file or a pipe',
    example: 'echo aGVsbG8= | base64 -d',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      const decode = args.includes('-d') || args.includes('--decode');
      const file = args.find((a) => !a.startsWith('-'));
      const text = sourceText(s, file, stdin, out);
      if (text === null) return;
      if (!decode) {
        out.print(toBase64(text));
        return;
      }
      try {
        const decoded = fromBase64(text);
        out.print(decoded, 'ok');
        s.discover('decoded', decoded);
      } catch {
        out.print('base64: invalid input', 'err');
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'decode',
    usage: 'decode <base64|rot13|hex> <text>',
    summary: 'decode an encoded string (the short form of base64 -d)',
    example: 'decode base64 aGVsbG8=',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const [encoding, ...rest] = args;
      const text = rest.join(' ');
      if (!encoding || !text) {
        out.print('usage: decode <base64|rot13|hex> <text>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      let decoded: string;
      try {
        switch (encoding.toLowerCase()) {
          case 'base64':
            decoded = fromBase64(text);
            break;
          case 'rot13':
            decoded = rot13(text);
            break;
          case 'hex':
            decoded = fromHex(text);
            break;
          default:
            out.print(`decode: unknown encoding '${encoding}' (try base64, rot13, hex)`, 'err');
            return;
        }
      } catch {
        out.print(`decode: input is not valid ${encoding.toLowerCase()}`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(decoded, 'ok');
      s.discover('decoded', decoded);
    },
  },
];

```

`src/engine/commands/files.ts`:

```ts
// Filesystem commands: ls, cd, pwd, cat, grep, find.

import { canRead, filesUnder, isDir, isFile, listDir, type DirEntry } from '../fs';
import type { GameSession, Out } from '../session';
import type { LineKind } from '../types';
import type { CommandSpec } from './types';

function escapeRegExp(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

/** After a "no such file" error, offers the closest real path the user can read. */
function didYouMean(s: GameSession, target: string, out: Out): void {
  const fixed = s.suggestPath(target);
  if (fixed) out.print(`Did you mean: ${fixed}?`, 'dim');
}

/** Colour for a directory entry: directories blue, executables green, everything else plain. */
function entryKind(e: DirEntry): LineKind {
  if (e.type === 'dir') return 'dir';
  return e.executable ? 'exec' : 'out';
}

/** ls -l permission string, the way the real one shows it. */
function permissions(e: DirEntry): string {
  if (e.type === 'dir') return `d${e.private ? 'rwx------' : 'rwxr-xr-x'}`;
  if (e.private) return e.executable ? '-rwx------' : '-rw-------';
  return e.executable ? '-rwxr-xr-x' : '-rw-r--r--';
}

/** Shell wildcard to regular expression, for find -name. */
function globToRegExp(glob: string): RegExp {
  const source = glob.split('').map((c) => (c === '*' ? '.*' : c === '?' ? '.' : escapeRegExp(c))).join('');
  return new RegExp(`^${source}$`);
}

export const fileCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'ls',
    usage: 'ls [-la] [path...]',
    summary: 'list directory contents (-a shows hidden, -l long format with permissions and owners)',
    example: 'ls -la /home',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const flags = args.filter((a) => a.startsWith('-')).join('');
      const paths = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'));
      const showAll = flags.includes('a');
      const long = flags.includes('l');
      const targets = paths.length ? paths : ['.'];

      for (const target of targets) {
        const abs = s.resolve(target);
        if (isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
          if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) out.print(`ls: cannot access '${target}': Permission denied`, 'err');
          else out.print(target);
          continue;
        }
        if (!isDir(s.machine, abs)) {
          out.print(`ls: cannot access '${target}': No such file or directory`, 'err');
          didYouMean(s, target, out);
          continue;
        }
        if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) {
          out.print(`ls: cannot open directory '${target}': Permission denied`, 'err');
          continue;
        }

        if (targets.length > 1) out.print(`${target}:`, 'dim');
        const entries = listDir(s.machine, abs).filter((e) => showAll || !e.name.startsWith('.'));
        if (long) {
          for (const e of entries) {
            const owner = (e.owner ?? 'root').padEnd(8);
            const size = String(e.size).padStart(5);
            const label = e.type === 'dir' ? `${e.name}/` : e.name;
            out.printSegments([
              { text: `${permissions(e)} 1 ${owner} ${size} `, kind: 'out' },
              { text: label, kind: entryKind(e) },
            ]);
          }
        } else if (entries.length) {
          const parts: { text: string; kind: LineKind }[] = [];
          entries.forEach((e, i) => {
            if (i > 0) parts.push({ text: '  ', kind: 'out' });
            parts.push({ text: e.type === 'dir' ? `${e.name}/` : e.name, kind: entryKind(e) });
          });
          out.printSegments(parts);
        }
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'cd',
    usage: 'cd [path | - | ..]',
    summary: 'change directory. no argument goes home, cd - goes back',
    example: 'cd /srv/logs',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const back = args[0] === '-';
      const target = back ? s.frame.prev ?? s.frame.cwd : args[0] ?? '~';
      const abs = s.resolve(target);
      if (isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
        out.print(`cd: not a directory: ${target}`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      if (!isDir(s.machine, abs)) {
        out.print(`cd: no such file or directory: ${target}`, 'err');
        didYouMean(s, target, out);
        return;
      }
      if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) {
        out.print(`cd: permission denied: ${target}`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      s.frame.prev = s.frame.cwd;
      s.frame.cwd = abs;
      if (back) out.print(abs);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'pwd',
    usage: 'pwd',
    summary: 'print the current directory',
    example: 'pwd',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      out.print(s.frame.cwd);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'cat',
    usage: 'cat <file...>',
    summary: 'print file contents. with no file, prints piped input',
    example: 'cat notes.txt',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      if (args.length === 0) {
        if (stdin) {
          for (const line of stdin) out.print(line);
          return;
        }
        out.print('cat: missing operand. Give a file, or pipe something in.', 'err');
        return;
      }
      for (const target of args) {
        const abs = s.resolve(target);
        if (isDir(s.machine, abs) && !isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
          out.print(`cat: ${target}: Is a directory`, 'err');
        } else if (!isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
          out.print(`cat: ${target}: No such file or directory`, 'err');
          didYouMean(s, target, out);
        } else if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) {
          out.print(`cat: ${target}: Permission denied`, 'err');
        } else {
          s.reads.add(`${s.frame.machineId}:${abs}`);
          for (const line of s.machine.files[abs].content.split('\n')) out.print(line);
        }
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'grep',
    usage: 'grep [-rin] <pattern> [file|dir...]',
    summary: 'search files for a pattern (-r recursive, -i ignore case, -n line numbers). reads a pipe when no file is given',
    example: 'grep -rin "password" /srv',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      const opts = new Set<string>();
      const rest: string[] = [];
      for (const a of args) {
        if (/^-[a-zA-Z]+$/.test(a)) for (const c of a.slice(1)) opts.add(c);
        else rest.push(a);
      }
      if (rest.length === 0) {
        out.print('Usage: grep [-rin] <pattern> [file|dir...]', 'err');
        return;
      }
      const [pattern, ...targets] = rest;
      const flags = opts.has('i') ? 'i' : '';
      let re: RegExp;
      try {
        re = new RegExp(pattern, flags);
      } catch {
        re = new RegExp(escapeRegExp(pattern), flags);
      }

      if (targets.length === 0 && stdin) {
        stdin.forEach((line, idx) => {
          if (!re.test(line)) return;
          out.print(`${opts.has('n') ? `${idx + 1}:` : ''}${line}`);
        });
        return;
      }

      const recursive = opts.has('r');
      if (targets.length === 0 && !recursive) {
        out.print('grep: no input file given', 'err');
        return;
      }

      const files: string[] = [];
      for (const target of targets.length ? targets : ['.']) {
        const abs = s.resolve(target);
        if (isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
          if (canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) files.push(abs);
          else out.print(`grep: ${target}: Permission denied`, 'err');
        } else if (isDir(s.machine, abs)) {
          if (!recursive) {
            out.print(`grep: ${target}: Is a directory`, 'err');
            continue;
          }
          files.push(...filesUnder(s.machine, abs).filter((p) => canRead(s.machine, p, s.frame.user)));
        } else {
          out.print(`grep: ${target}: No such file or directory`, 'err');
          didYouMean(s, target, out);
        }
      }

      const showName = files.length > 1 || recursive || targets.length > 1;
      for (const file of files) {
        s.machine.files[file].content.split('\n').forEach((line, idx) => {
          if (!re.test(line)) return;
          const prefix = `${showName ? `${file}:` : ''}${opts.has('n') ? `${idx + 1}:` : ''}`;
          out.print(`${prefix}${line}`);
        });
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'find',
    usage: 'find [path] [-name pattern] [-type f|d]',
    summary: 'list files and directories under a path, filtered by name or type',
    example: 'find /srv -name "*.txt" -type f',
    run(s, args, out) {
      let base = '.';
      let namePattern: RegExp | null = null;
      let type: 'f' | 'd' | null = null;
      for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '-name') namePattern = globToRegExp(args[++i] ?? '*');
        else if (args[i] === '-type') type = args[++i] === 'd' ? 'd' : 'f';
        else if (!args[i].startsWith('-')) base = args[i];
      }

      const root = s.resolve(base);
      if (!isDir(s.machine, root) && !isFile(s.machine, root)) {
        out.print(`find: '${base}': No such file or directory`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      if (!canRead(s.machine, root, s.frame.user)) {
        out.print(`find: '${base}': Permission denied`, 'err');
        return;
      }

      const dirname = (p: string) => p.slice(0, p.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/';
      const found = new Set<string>([root]);
      for (const file of filesUnder(s.machine, root)) {
        if (!canRead(s.machine, file, s.frame.user)) continue;
        found.add(file);
        // Every directory between the file and the root is found too, as long as the user can read it.
        for (let parent = dirname(file); parent !== root && parent.length > root.length - (root === '/' ? 1 : 0); parent = dirname(parent)) {
          if (canRead(s.machine, parent, s.frame.user)) found.add(parent);
        }
      }

      for (const path of [...found].sort()) {
        const isDirectory = isDir(s.machine, path) && !isFile(s.machine, path);
        if (type === 'f' && isDirectory) continue;
        if (type === 'd' && !isDirectory) continue;
        const name = path.slice(path.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
        if (namePattern && !namePattern.test(name)) continue;
        // Show paths the way the player typed the start point.
        out.print(path === root ? base : base + path.slice(root.length));
      }
    },
  },
];

```

`src/engine/commands/index.ts`:

```ts
// Command registry. Add a command by writing a CommandSpec and listing it here. Aliases are registered from the spec.

import { closest } from '../suggest';
import type { MissionDef } from '../types';
import { cryptoCommands } from './crypto';
import { fileCommands } from './files';
import { networkCommands } from './network';
import { notesCommands } from './notes';
import { systemCommands } from './system';
import { textCommands } from './text';
import type { CommandSpec } from './types';
import type { Out } from "../session";

/** The commands this mission's own solution actually uses, aliases resolved, plus help itself. Shown first on Easy. */
function missionCommands(mission: MissionDef): CommandSpec[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const specs: CommandSpec[] = [];
  const add = (spec: CommandSpec | undefined) => {
    if (!spec || seen.has(spec.name)) return;
    seen.add(spec.name);
    specs.push(spec);
  };
  for (const step of mission.solution) add(COMMANDS.get(step.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? ''));
  add(COMMANDS.get('help'));
  return specs;
}

const helpCommand: CommandSpec = {
  name: 'help',
  usage: 'help [command]',
  summary: 'list commands, or show one command with an example',
  example: 'help grep',
  run(s, args, out) {
    if (args.length > 0 && args[0] !== 'all') {
      showCommand(args[0], out, 'help');
      return;
    }
    const scoped = args[0] !== 'all' && s.tier === 'easy';
    out.print('BREACH shell. Tab completes (twice lists), up/down recalls history, Ctrl+L clears.', 'sys');
    out.print('');
    const list = scoped ? missionCommands(s.baseMission) : ALL;
    for (const c of list) out.print(`  ${c.usage.padEnd(30)} ${c.summary}`);
    out.print('');
    if (scoped) out.print("That's what this job needs. Type 'help all' to see every command on this rig.", 'dim');
    out.print('Password prompts do not echo.', 'dim');
    if (s.config.recoveryHints !== 'none') {
      out.print('At a Password prompt, type ? to start account recovery (security questions), if the host offers it.', 'dim');
      out.print('Recovery answers are checked exactly as you type them, ignoring case.', 'dim');
    }
  },
};

const manCommand: CommandSpec = {
  name: 'man',
  usage: 'man <command>',
  summary: 'show the manual page for a command',
  example: 'man nmap',
  run(_s, args, out) {
    if (args.length === 0) {
      out.print('What manual page do you want? usage: man <command>', 'err');
      return;
    }
    showCommand(args[0], out, 'man');
  },
};

/**
 * help <command> prints usage, summary and a runnable example. man <command> prints the same as a manual page.
 * Unknown names get a "Did you mean" line.
 */
function showCommand(name: string, out: Out, style: 'help' | 'man'): void {
  const spec = COMMANDS.get(name);
  if (!spec) {
    out.print(style === 'man' ? `No manual entry for ${name}` : `help: no command '${name}'`, 'err');
    const near = closest(name, COMMAND_LIST);
    if (near.length) out.print(`Did you mean: ${near.join(', ')}?`, 'dim');
    return;
  }
  const alias = spec.name !== name ? ` (alias of ${spec.name})` : '';
  if (style === 'man') {
    out.print('NAME', 'title');
    out.print(`  ${spec.name} - ${spec.summary}${alias}`);
    out.print('SYNOPSIS', 'title');
    out.print(`  ${spec.usage}`);
    out.print('EXAMPLE', 'title');
    out.print(`  ${spec.example}`);
    return;
  }
  out.print(`${spec.usage}${alias}`, 'title');
  out.print(`  ${spec.summary}`);
  out.print(`  e.g. ${spec.example}`, 'dim');
}

const ALL: CommandSpec[] = [
  helpCommand,
  manCommand,
  ...fileCommands,
  ...textCommands,
  ...networkCommands,
  ...cryptoCommands,
  ...systemCommands,
  ...notesCommands,
];

/** Every name a command answers to: its own name and its aliases. */
export const COMMANDS = new Map<string, CommandSpec>(ALL.flatMap((c) => [c.name, ...(c.aliases ?? [])].map((n) => [n, c] as const)));
export const COMMAND_LIST: string[] = [...COMMANDS.keys()];

```

`src/engine/commands/network.ts`:

```ts
// Network commands: nmap, ssh, su, exit, ping, ifconfig and ip. The old names scan, connect and login are aliases.

import type { CommandSpec } from './types';

function broadcast(ip: string): string {
  const parts = ip.split('.');
  return [...parts.slice(0, 3), '255'].join('.');
}

export const networkCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'nmap',
    aliases: ['scan'],
    usage: 'nmap [-sV] [-sn] [ip]',
    summary: 'no argument: discover neighbours. with an ip: port scan (-sV adds service versions)',
    example: 'nmap -sV 10.0.0.20',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const m = s.machine;
      const flags = args.filter((a) => a.startsWith('-'));
      const targets = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'));
      const versions = flags.includes('-sV');

      if (targets.length === 0 || flags.includes('-sn')) {
        out.print(`Starting Nmap (breach-sim) host discovery from ${m.hostname} (${m.ip})`, 'dim');
        if (m.neighbors.length === 0) out.print('No hosts responded.', 'warn');
        for (const ip of m.neighbors) {
          const host = s.findByIp(ip);
          if (host) out.print(`Nmap scan report for ${host.hostname} (${ip})`);
        }
        out.print(`Nmap done: ${m.neighbors.length} IP address(es) (${m.neighbors.length} host(s) up) scanned.`);
        s.noise(5, out);
        return;
      }

      const ip = targets[0];
      const host = m.neighbors.includes(ip) ? s.findByIp(ip) : undefined;
      if (!host) {
        out.print(`Note: host ${ip} seems down. No route from ${m.hostname}.`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`Starting Nmap (breach-sim) against ${ip}`, 'dim');
      out.print(`Nmap scan report for ${host.hostname} (${ip})`);
      out.print(versions ? 'PORT      STATE  SERVICE    VERSION' : 'PORT      STATE  SERVICE');
      for (const p of host.ports) {
        const row = `${`${p.port}/tcp`.padEnd(9)} open   ${p.service.padEnd(10)}`;
        out.print(versions ? `${row} ${p.banner}` : row.trimEnd());
        out.sound('ping');
      }
      if (host.ports.length === 0) out.print('All scanned ports are closed.', 'dim');
      out.print(`Nmap done: 1 IP address (1 host up) scanned.`);
      s.noise(10, out);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'ssh',
    aliases: ['connect'],
    usage: 'ssh [user@]<ip>',
    summary: 'open a session on a neighbouring host. ssh user@ip asks for that user\'s password',
    example: 'ssh rfoster@10.0.0.20',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const target = args[0];
      if (!target) {
        out.print('usage: ssh [user@]<ip>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      const at = target.lastIndexOf('@');
      const user = at >= 0 ? target.slice(0, at) : null;
      const ip = at >= 0 ? target.slice(at + 1) : target;
      const host = s.machine.neighbors.includes(ip) ? s.findByIp(ip) : undefined;
      if (!host) {
        out.print(`ssh: connect to host ${ip} port 22: No route to host`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      s.pushFrame(host.id);
      out.print(`Connected to ${host.hostname} (${ip}) as ${s.frame.user}.`, 'ok');
      out.sound('enter');
      s.noise(3, out);
      if (user && user !== s.frame.user) s.requestPassword(user);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'su',
    aliases: ['login'],
    usage: 'su <user> [password]',
    summary: 'switch to a user. no argument on a web host opens its login form',
    example: 'su admin',
    run(s, args, out) {
      if (args.length === 0) {
        if (s.machine.webForm) {
          s.openForm(out);
          return;
        }
        out.print('usage: su <user>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      const user = args[0];
      if (args.length >= 2) {
        s.attemptLogin(user, args.slice(1).join(' '), out);
        return;
      }
      s.requestPassword(user);
      if (s.machine.recovery?.[user] && s.config.recoveryHints === 'direct') out.print('Forgot it? Type ? at the prompt to start account recovery.', 'dim');
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'exit',
    usage: 'exit',
    summary: 'close the current session (from your rig, abort the mission)',
    example: 'exit',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      if (s.stack.length > 1) {
        const closed = s.machine.hostname;
        s.popFrame();
        out.print(`Connection to ${closed} closed. Back on ${s.machine.hostname}.`, 'dim');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`Disconnecting from ${s.machine.hostname}. Aborting mission.`, 'sys');
      out.quit = true;
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'ping',
    usage: 'ping [-c count] <ip>',
    summary: 'send echo requests to a neighbouring host',
    example: 'ping -c 2 10.0.0.20',
    run(s, args, out) {
      let count = 4;
      let ip: string | undefined;
      for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
        if (args[i] === '-c') count = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Number(args[++i]) || 4));
        else if (!args[i].startsWith('-')) ip = args[i];
      }
      if (!ip) {
        out.print('usage: ping [-c count] <ip>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`PING ${ip} (${ip}) 56(84) bytes of data.`);
      if (!s.machine.neighbors.includes(ip) || !s.findByIp(ip)) {
        out.print(`From ${s.machine.ip} icmp_seq=1 Destination Host Unreachable`, 'err');
        s.noise(2, out);
        return;
      }
      for (let seq = 1; seq <= count; seq++) {
        out.print(`64 bytes from ${ip}: icmp_seq=${seq} ttl=64 time=${(0.8 + seq * 0.3).toFixed(1)} ms`);
      }
      out.print(`--- ${ip} ping statistics ---`, 'dim');
      out.print(`${count} packets transmitted, ${count} received, 0% packet loss`, 'dim');
      s.noise(2, out);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'ifconfig',
    usage: 'ifconfig',
    summary: 'show the network interface address',
    example: 'ifconfig',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      out.print('eth0: flags=4163<UP,BROADCAST,RUNNING,MULTICAST>  mtu 1500');
      out.print(`        inet ${s.machine.ip}  netmask 255.255.255.0  broadcast ${broadcast(s.machine.ip)}`);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'ip',
    usage: 'ip a',
    summary: 'show addresses (ip a, ip addr)',
    example: 'ip a',
    run(s, args, out) {
      if (!['a', 'addr', 'address'].includes(args[0] ?? '')) {
        out.print('usage: ip a', 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`2: eth0: <BROADCAST,MULTICAST,UP,LOWER_UP> mtu 1500 state UP`);
      out.print(`    inet ${s.machine.ip}/24 brd ${broadcast(s.machine.ip)} scope global eth0`);
    },
  },
];

```

`src/engine/commands/notes.ts`:

```ts
// Notes: the clue log (filled automatically on Easy and Medium) and the player's own notes.

import { CLUE_LABELS } from '../clues';
import type { CommandSpec } from './types';

const NOTE_LIMIT = 200;

export const notesCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'notes',
    usage: 'notes [add <text>]',
    summary: 'show the clue log and your notes. notes add <text> writes one',
    example: 'notes add mirror has her pet and her hometown',
    run(s, args, out) {
      if (args[0] === 'add') {
        const text = args.slice(1).join(' ').trim();
        if (!text) {
          out.print('usage: notes add <text>', 'err');
          return;
        }
        if (text.length > NOTE_LIMIT) {
          out.print(`notes: keep a note under ${NOTE_LIMIT} characters.`, 'err');
          return;
        }
        s.clues.addNote(text);
        out.print('Note saved.', 'ok');
        return;
      }
      if (args.length > 0) {
        out.print('usage: notes [add <text>]', 'err');
        return;
      }

      if (s.config.autoClues) {
        out.print('CLUE LOG (automatic)', 'title');
        if (s.clues.clues.length === 0) out.print('  Nothing yet. Scan, read files and decode what you find.', 'dim');
        for (const clue of s.clues.clues) out.print(`  ${CLUE_LABELS[clue.category].padEnd(8)} ${clue.text}`);
      } else {
        out.print('No automatic clue log on this tier. Keep your own with notes add <text>.', 'dim');
      }

      out.print('');
      out.print('NOTES', 'title');
      if (s.clues.notes.length === 0) out.print('  None yet.', 'dim');
      s.clues.notes.forEach((note, i) => out.print(`  ${i + 1}. ${note}`));
    },
  },
];
```

```ts
// System commands: whoami, hostname, ps, kill, mail, submit, hint, history, echo, clear.

import type { CommandSpec } from './types';

export const systemCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'whoami',
    usage: 'whoami',
    summary: 'print the account you are logged in as',
    example: 'whoami',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      out.print(s.frame.user);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'hostname',
    usage: 'hostname',
    summary: 'print the name of the current host',
    example: 'hostname',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      out.print(s.machine.hostname);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'ps',
    usage: 'ps',
    summary: 'list running processes on this host',
    example: 'ps',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      out.print('  PID USER     COMMAND', 'dim');
      for (const p of s.processes) {
        out.print(`${String(p.pid).padStart(5)} ${p.user.padEnd(8)} ${p.name}`);
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'kill',
    usage: 'kill <pid>',
    summary: 'terminate a process you own (root can kill anything)',
    example: 'kill 412',
    run(s, args, out) {
      if (!args[0]) {
        out.print('usage: kill <pid>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      const pid = Number(args[0]);
      const index = s.processes.findIndex((p) => p.pid === pid);
      if (!Number.isInteger(pid) || index < 0) {
        out.print(`kill: (${args[0]}) - No such process`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      const proc = s.processes[index];
      if (proc.user !== s.frame.user && s.frame.user !== 'root') {
        out.print(`kill: (${pid}) - Operation not permitted`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      s.processes.splice(index, 1);
      out.print(`Terminated ${proc.name} (pid ${pid})`, 'ok');
      if (proc.security) {
        s.lowerTrace(20);
        out.print('[sentry] monitoring gap detected. Trace multiplier lifted on this host.', 'warn');
      }
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'mail',
    usage: 'mail [n]',
    summary: 'read the inbox (mail <n> opens one message)',
    example: 'mail 2',
    run(s, args, out) {
      const inbox = s.mission.inbox;
      if (args.length === 0) {
        if (inbox.length === 0) out.print('No mail.', 'dim');
        inbox.forEach((m, i) => out.print(`[${i + 1}] ${m.date}  ${m.from.padEnd(8)} ${m.subject}`));
        return;
      }
      const n = Number(args[0]);
      const message = Number.isInteger(n) ? inbox[n - 1] : undefined;
      if (!message) {
        out.print(`mail: no message ${args[0]}`, 'err');
        return;
      }
      out.print(`From: ${message.from}`, 'dim');
      out.print(`Date: ${message.date}`, 'dim');
      out.print(`Subj: ${message.subject}`, 'dim');
      out.print('');
      for (const line of message.body.split('\n')) out.print(line);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'submit',
    usage: 'submit <flag>',
    summary: 'submit the flag you found to complete the job',
    example: 'submit BREACH{example_flag}',
    run(s, args, out) {
      if (args.length === 0) {
        out.print('usage: submit <flag>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      s.submitFlag(args.join(' '), out);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'hint',
    usage: 'hint',
    summary: 'reveal the next hint (each one costs score)',
    example: 'hint',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      if (s.hintsUsed >= s.hintLimit) {
        out.print('No hints left. Trust your notes.', 'warn');
        return;
      }
      s.hintsUsed++;
      out.print(`[HINT ${s.hintsUsed}/${s.hintLimit}] ${s.mission.hints[s.hintsUsed - 1]}`, 'warn');
      out.sound('hint');
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'history',
    usage: 'history',
    summary: 'list the commands you have typed',
    example: 'history',
    run(s, _args, out) {
      s.history.forEach((cmd, i) => out.print(`${String(i + 1).padStart(4)}  ${cmd}`));
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'echo',
    usage: 'echo <text>',
    summary: 'print text',
    example: 'echo hello world',
    run(_s, args, out) {
      out.print(args.join(' '));
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'clear',
    usage: 'clear',
    summary: 'clear the screen',
    example: 'clear',
    run(_s, _args, out) {
      out.clear = true;
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'sudo',
    usage: 'sudo <command>',
    summary: 'run a command as root, if your account is in the sudoers file',
    example: 'sudo cat /etc/shadow',
    run(s, args, out) {
      s.sudoTries++;
      if (args.length === 0) {
        out.print('usage: sudo <command>', 'err');
        return;
      }
      if (!s.canSudo()) {
        out.print(`[sudo] password for ${s.frame.user}: `, 'dim');
        out.print(`${s.frame.user} is not in the sudoers file. This incident will be reported.`, 'err');
        out.sound('denied');
        s.noise(3, out);
        return;
      }
      s.runAsRoot(args.map((a) => (/\s/.test(a) ? `"${a}"` : a)).join(' '), out);
    },
  },
];
```

`src/engine/commands/text.ts`:

```ts
// Text filters that work on a file or a pipe: head, tail, wc and tr.

import { canRead, isFile } from '../fs';
import type { GameSession, Out } from '../session';
import type { CommandSpec, Stdin } from './types';

/** The lines a filter works on: the named file if there is one, otherwise the pipe. Null when neither exists. */
function inputLines(s: GameSession, file: string | undefined, stdin: Stdin, out: Out): string[] | null {
  if (file) {
    const abs = s.resolve(file);
    if (!isFile(s.machine, abs)) {
      out.print(`${file}: No such file or directory`, 'err');
      return null;
    }
    if (!canRead(s.machine, abs, s.frame.user)) {
      out.print(`${file}: Permission denied`, 'err');
      return null;
    }
    return s.machine.files[abs].content.split('\n');
  }
  if (stdin) return stdin;
  out.print('usage: needs a file or piped input', 'err');
  return null;
}

/** Reads -n N or -N from the arguments. Returns the count and the rest of the arguments. */
function countArg(args: string[], fallback: number): { n: number; rest: string[] } {
  const rest: string[] = [];
  let n = fallback;
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    if (args[i] === '-n') n = Math.max(0, Number(args[++i]) || 0);
    else if (/^-\d+$/.test(args[i])) n = Math.max(0, Number(args[i].slice(1)));
    else rest.push(args[i]);
  }
  return { n, rest };
}

/** Expands a tr set such as A-Za-z into its characters. */
function expandSet(set: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < set.length; i++) {
    if (set[i + 1] === '-' && i + 2 < set.length) {
      const from = set.charCodeAt(i);
      const to = set.charCodeAt(i + 2);
      for (let c = from; c <= to; c++) out.push(String.fromCharCode(c));
      i += 2;
    } else {
      out.push(set[i]);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export const textCommands: CommandSpec[] = [
  {
    name: 'head',
    usage: 'head [-n N] [file]',
    summary: 'print the first lines of a file or a pipe (default 10)',
    example: 'cat /var/log/auth.log | head -n 5',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      const { n, rest } = countArg(args, 10);
      const lines = inputLines(s, rest[0], stdin, out);
      if (lines) for (const line of lines.slice(0, n)) out.print(line);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'tail',
    usage: 'tail [-n N] [file]',
    summary: 'print the last lines of a file or a pipe (default 10)',
    example: 'cat /var/log/auth.log | tail -n 3',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      const { n, rest } = countArg(args, 10);
      const lines = inputLines(s, rest[0], stdin, out);
      if (!lines) return;
      // A trailing newline in a file is not a line of its own.
      const trimmed = lines.length && lines[lines.length - 1] === '' ? lines.slice(0, -1) : lines;
      for (const line of trimmed.slice(Math.max(0, trimmed.length - n))) out.print(line);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'wc',
    usage: 'wc [-l] [-w] [-c] [file]',
    summary: 'count lines, words and characters in a file or a pipe',
    example: 'cat /etc/passwd | wc -l',
    run(s, args, out, stdin) {
      const flags = args.filter((a) => a.startsWith('-')).join('');
      const file = args.find((a) => !a.startsWith('-'));
      const lines = inputLines(s, file, stdin, out);
      if (!lines) return;
      const text = lines.join('\n');
      const counts = {
        l: lines.length - (text.endsWith('\n') ? 1 : 0),
        w: text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length,
        c: text.length,
      };
      const picked = (['l', 'w', 'c'] as const).filter((k) => flags.includes(k));
      const shown = picked.length ? picked.map((k) => counts[k]) : [counts.l, counts.w, counts.c];
      out.print(`${shown.join(' ')}${file ? ` ${file}` : ''}`);
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'tr',
    usage: 'tr <from> <to>',
    summary: 'translate characters in a pipe. ranges like A-Z are allowed',
    example: "echo Uryyb | tr 'A-Za-z' 'N-ZA-Mn-za-m'",
    run(_s, args, out, stdin) {
      if (args.length < 2) {
        out.print("usage: tr <from> <to>   (e.g. tr 'A-Za-z' 'N-ZA-Mn-za-m')", 'err');
        return;
      }
      if (!stdin) {
        out.print('tr: needs piped input', 'err');
        return;
      }
      const from = expandSet(args[0]);
      const to = expandSet(args[1]);
      for (const line of stdin) {
        out.print(
          [...line]
            .map((ch) => {
              const idx = from.indexOf(ch);
              if (idx < 0) return ch;
              return to[Math.min(idx, to.length - 1)] ?? ch;
            })
            .join(''),
        );
      }
    },
  },
];

```

```ts
/** Lines piped in from the previous command in a pipeline, or null when the command starts the pipeline. */
export type Stdin = string[] | null;

export interface CommandSpec {

  /** Other names the command answers to. The old game names live here after the Kali renames. */

  usage: string;
  summary: string;
  /** A runnable example shown by `help <name>` and `man <name>`. */
```

`src/engine/difficulty.ts`:

```ts
// Difficulty tiers. This is the one place that tunes how each tier plays.
// Tiers change how a mission plays, never what it asks for: the solution still works on every tier.

import type { MissionDef, Tier } from './types';

export interface TierConfig {
  label: string;
  description: string;
  /** How many hints the hint command will give. */
  hintLimit: number;
  /** Multiplies every trace gain. Below 1 is forgiving, above 1 punishes noise. */
  traceMultiplier: number;
  /** Multiplies the points a completed mission awards. Rank is unaffected. */
  pointMultiplier: number;
  /** Seconds before the attempt fails. null means no limit. */
  timeLimitSeconds: number | null;
  showTimer: boolean;
  /** Show the first hint inside the briefing. */
  briefingTip: boolean;
  /**
   * How much account-recovery help the host gives away.
   * direct: the login "Forgot it?" line and the help footer. footer: help footer only. none: neither.
   */
  recoveryHints: 'direct' | 'footer' | 'none';
  /** The clue log fills itself from command output. Off means the player adds notes by hand. */
  autoClues: boolean;
}

export const TIERS: Tier[] = ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'];

export const DIFFICULTY: Record<Tier, TierConfig> = {
  easy: {
    label: 'Easy',
    description: 'Forgiving trace, full tab completion, a tip in every briefing.',
    hintLimit: 3,
    traceMultiplier: 0.6,
    pointMultiplier: 1,
    timeLimitSeconds: null,
    showTimer: false,
    briefingTip: true,
    recoveryHints: 'direct',
    autoClues: true,
  },
  medium: {
    label: 'Medium',
    description: 'The standard balance. Three hints, full completion.',
    hintLimit: 3,
    traceMultiplier: 1,
    pointMultiplier: 1.5,
    timeLimitSeconds: null,
    showTimer: false,
    briefingTip: false,
    recoveryHints: 'footer',
    autoClues: true,
  },
  hard: {
    label: 'Hard',
    description: 'One hint, fast trace, decoys in every machine, a 20 minute clock.',
    hintLimit: 1,
    traceMultiplier: 1.6,
    pointMultiplier: 2.5,
    timeLimitSeconds: 20 * 60,
    showTimer: true,
    briefingTip: false,
    recoveryHints: 'none',
    autoClues: false,
  },
};

export function isTier(value: unknown): value is Tier {
  return value === 'easy' || value === 'medium' || value === 'hard';
}

/** Points for a completed mission on a tier. Rounded to a whole number. */
export function pointsFor(baseScore: number, tier: Tier): number {
  return Math.round(baseScore * DIFFICULTY[tier].pointMultiplier);
}

/**
 * Returns the mission as it plays on `tier`: the base data with that tier's overrides merged in.
 * The input is never mutated, so the campaign list stays the same for every tier.
 */
export function applyTier(mission: MissionDef, tier: Tier): MissionDef {
  const override = mission.tierOverrides?.[tier];
  if (!override) return mission;

  const machines = mission.machines.map((machine) => {
    const extra = override.machines?.[machine.id];
    if (!extra) return machine;
    return {
      ...machine,
      files: { ...machine.files, ...(extra.files ?? {}) },
      users: [...machine.users, ...(extra.users ?? [])],
      processes: [...(machine.processes ?? []), ...(extra.processes ?? [])],
    };
  });

  const hidden = new Set(override.hideInbox ?? []);
  return {
    ...mission,
    machines,
    inbox: [...mission.inbox.filter((m) => !hidden.has(m.subject)), ...(override.inbox ?? [])],
  };
}
```

`src/engine/encoding.ts`:

```ts
// Encodings used by the decode command and by mission data. Browser-safe (TextEncoder, atob/btoa).

export function toBase64(text: string): string {
  let binary = '';
  for (const byte of new TextEncoder().encode(text)) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte);
  return btoa(binary);
}

/** Throws if the input is not valid base64. */
export function fromBase64(input: string): string {
  const binary = atob(input.trim());
  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

export function rot13(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/[a-zA-Z]/g, (c) => {
    const base = c <= 'Z' ? 65 : 97;
    return String.fromCharCode(((c.charCodeAt(0) - base + 13) % 26) + base);
  });
}

export function toHex(text: string): string {
  return Array.from(new TextEncoder().encode(text), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/** Throws if the input is not an even-length hex string. */
export function fromHex(input: string): string {
  const clean = input.replace(/\s+/g, '');
  if (!/^([0-9a-f]{2})+$/i.test(clean)) throw new Error('invalid hex');
  const bytes = Uint8Array.from(clean.match(/../g) ?? [], (h) => parseInt(h, 16));
  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
}

```

`src/engine/fs.ts`:

```ts
// Virtual filesystem helpers. A machine's files are a flat map of absolute paths; directories are implied
// by file paths and can also be declared explicitly (to set owner or privacy).

import type { MachineDef } from './types';

export interface DirEntry {
  name: string;
  type: 'file' | 'dir';
  owner?: string;
  size: number;
  private?: boolean;
  executable?: boolean;
}

/** Resolves `input` against `cwd`, expanding ~ to `home`, "." and "..". Always returns an absolute path. */
export function normalizePath(input: string, cwd: string, home: string): string {
  let target = input;
  if (target === '~' || target.startsWith('~/')) target = home + target.slice(1);
  const full = target.startsWith('/') ? target : `${cwd}/${target}`;
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const seg of full.split('/')) {
    if (seg === '' || seg === '.') continue;
    if (seg === '..') parts.pop();
    else parts.push(seg);
  }
  return `/${parts.join('/')}`;
}

export function isFile(m: MachineDef, abs: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(m.files, abs);
}

export function isDir(m: MachineDef, abs: string): boolean {
  if (abs === '/' || m.dirs?.[abs]) return true;
  const prefix = `${abs}/`;
  return Object.keys(m.files).some((p) => p.startsWith(prefix));
}

/** A path is readable unless it, or an ancestor, is private to someone other than `user`. Root reads everything. */
export function canRead(m: MachineDef, abs: string, user: string): boolean {
  if (user === 'root') return true;
  let cur = '';
  for (const seg of abs.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
    cur += `/${seg}`;
    const node = isFile(m, cur) ? m.files[cur] : m.dirs?.[cur];
    if (node?.private && node.owner !== user) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

export function listDir(m: MachineDef, abs: string): DirEntry[] {
  const prefix = abs === '/' ? '/' : `${abs}/`;
  const entries = new Map<string, DirEntry>();

  for (const [path, file] of Object.entries(m.files)) {
    if (!path.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
    const rest = path.slice(prefix.length);
    const slash = rest.indexOf('/');
    if (slash === -1) {
      entries.set(rest, { name: rest, type: 'file', owner: file.owner, size: file.content.length, private: file.private, executable: file.executable });
    } else {
      const name = rest.slice(0, slash);
      if (!entries.has(name)) {
        const dir = m.dirs?.[`${prefix}${name}`];
        entries.set(name, { name, type: 'dir', owner: dir?.owner, size: 0, private: dir?.private });
      }
    }
  }

  for (const [path, dir] of Object.entries(m.dirs ?? {})) {
    if (path === abs || !path.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
    const name = path.slice(prefix.length).split('/')[0];
    if (!entries.has(name)) entries.set(name, { name, type: 'dir', owner: dir.owner, size: 0, private: dir.private });
  }

  return [...entries.values()].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

/** All file paths at or below `abs`, sorted. */
export function filesUnder(m: MachineDef, abs: string): string[] {
  const prefix = abs === '/' ? '/' : `${abs}/`;
  return Object.keys(m.files)
    .filter((p) => p.startsWith(prefix))
    .sort();
}

```

`src/engine/replay.ts`:

```ts
// Replay validator for leaderboard runs. The client never sends a score. It sends the commands it typed and when,
// and this module replays them through the same deterministic engine. The score comes from the replay alone.
// Pure: no network, no storage, no DOM. The Edge Function calls this with the mission list it bundles.

import { pointsFor } from './difficulty';
import { scoreMission } from './scoring';
import { GameSession, Out } from './session';
import { TIERS } from './difficulty';
import type { Line, MissionDef, Tier } from './types';

/** Logged in place of a command when the player presses Ctrl+C at a password or question prompt. */
export const CANCEL_MARK = '\u0003';

export interface RunCommand {
  /** Milliseconds since the attempt started. Must be whole, non-decreasing. */
  t: number;
  cmd: string;
  /** Backspace/delete keystrokes typed while composing this line. Used only as a soft suspicion signal. */
  bs?: number;
}

export interface RunLog {
  missionId: string;
  tier: Tier;
  commands: RunCommand[];
  /** Optional. If the client sends one, it must match the replayed score exactly, or the run is rejected. */
  claimedScore?: number;
  /** The server-issued run token this attempt was started with. Required to post a run; see checkToken. */
  token?: string;
}

export type ReplayResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      score: number;
      points: number;
      seconds: number;
      trace: number;
      hints: number;
      endingId: string | null;
      usedAliases: boolean;
      usedPipe: boolean;
      /** How many commands produced an error-kind line. A soft suspicion signal: see suspicionFor. */
      errorCommands: number;
    }
  | { ok: false; reason: string };

export const RUN_LIMITS = {
  maxCommands: 2000,
  maxCommandLength: 300,
  /** Two commands cannot land closer together than this. Faster than a person can type or paste one by one. */
  minGapMs: 120,
  /**
   * Averaged over the whole run. A fast player who already knows a mission can clear 300-600ms a command
   * with no reading time at all, so this sits well under that, not at a "typical" human pace: it exists to
   * catch a script whose gaps sit just above minGapMs on every single command, not to second-guess a quick player.
   */
  minAverageMs: 200,
} as const;

const fail = (reason: string): ReplayResult => ({ ok: false, reason });

/** Replays one run and returns the score the engine computes, or why the run is not valid. */
export function validateRun(run: RunLog, missions: readonly MissionDef[]): ReplayResult {
  const mission = missions.find((m) => m.id === run.missionId);
  if (!mission) return fail('unknown mission');
  if (!TIERS.includes(run.tier)) return fail('unknown tier');
  if (!Array.isArray(run.commands) || run.commands.length === 0) return fail('empty run');
  if (run.commands.length > RUN_LIMITS.maxCommands) return fail('too many commands');

  let prev = -1;
  for (const c of run.commands) {
    if (typeof c !== 'object' || c === null) return fail('malformed command');
    if (!Number.isInteger(c.t) || c.t < 0) return fail('bad timestamp');
    if (typeof c.cmd !== 'string' || c.cmd.length > RUN_LIMITS.maxCommandLength) return fail('bad command text');
    if (c.t < prev) return fail('timestamps go backwards');
    if (prev >= 0 && c.t - prev < RUN_LIMITS.minGapMs) return fail('impossible timing: commands too close together');
    prev = c.t;
  }
  if (prev < run.commands.length * RUN_LIMITS.minAverageMs) return fail('impossible timing: the run is too fast overall');

  // The engine's clock is driven by the log, so the replay sees the same times the player did.
  let clock = 0;
  let errorCommands = 0;
  const session = new GameSession(mission, { tier: run.tier, now: () => clock });
  for (const c of run.commands) {
    if (session.finished) return fail('commands after the attempt ended');
    clock = c.t;
    let out: Out;
    if (c.cmd === CANCEL_MARK) {
      out = new Out();
      session.cancelPending(out);
    } else {
      out = session.input(c.cmd);
    }
    if (out.lines.some((l) => l.kind === 'err')) errorCommands++;
  }

  if (session.status === 'failed') return fail(session.failReason === 'time' ? 'the clock ran out' : 'trace reached 100%');
  if (session.status !== 'won') return fail('incomplete run: the flag was never accepted');

  const seconds = session.elapsedSeconds();
  const score = scoreMission(seconds, session.trace, session.hintsUsed);
  if (run.claimedScore !== undefined && run.claimedScore !== score) return fail('claimed score does not match the replay');

  return {
    ok: true,
    score,
    points: pointsFor(score, run.tier),
    seconds,
    trace: session.trace,
    hints: session.hintsUsed,
    endingId: session.endingId,
    usedAliases: session.usedAliases,
    usedPipe: session.usedPipe,
    errorCommands,
  };
}

/** The server's side of a run-token check. Hard rejects only — see RUN_LIMITS for the pre-existing timing checks. */
export interface TokenRow {
  playerId: string;
  missionId: string;
  tier: Tier;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  /** The daily's UTC date, or null for a campaign token. */
  date: string | null;
  /** Epoch ms when the server issued the token. */
  startedAt: number;
  /** Epoch ms when the token was consumed, or null if unused. */
  usedAt: number | null;
  /** Epoch ms when the token stops being valid. */
  expiresAt: number;
}

export interface TokenClaim {
  playerId: string;
  missionId: string;
  tier: Tier;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  date?: string;
}

/** Clock skew and request latency allowance for the server-elapsed-vs-claimed-duration check. */
export const TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS = 5000;

/**
 * Checks a run token against the run it's being used to post. `lastCommandMs` is the run's own claimed
 * duration (the last command's `t`). `nowMs` is the server's clock at submit time. Every failure here is a
 * hard reject: a missing, reused, expired, mismatched or physically-impossible token.
 */
export function checkToken(token: TokenRow, claim: TokenClaim, lastCommandMs: number, nowMs: number): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  if (token.usedAt !== null) return { ok: false, reason: 'that run token has already been used' };
  if (nowMs > token.expiresAt) return { ok: false, reason: 'that run token has expired' };
  if (token.playerId !== claim.playerId) return { ok: false, reason: 'that run token belongs to a different player' };
  if (token.missionId !== claim.missionId || token.tier !== claim.tier) return { ok: false, reason: 'that run token was issued for a different mission' };
  if (token.scope !== claim.scope || (token.date ?? undefined) !== claim.date) return { ok: false, reason: 'that run token was issued for a different attempt' };
  const serverElapsed = nowMs - token.startedAt;
  if (serverElapsed < lastCommandMs - TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS) return { ok: false, reason: 'impossible timing: the server saw less time pass than the run claims' };
  return { ok: true };
}

/** A run is hidden from the public board (but kept, and still visible to its own player) at or above this suspicion. */
export const SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD = 70; // keep in sync with the RLS policy in the run_tokens migration

export interface SuspicionResult {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
}

/** Below this many commands, timing/correction signals are too noisy to mean anything. */
const SUSPICION_MIN_COMMANDS = 8;

/**
 * Soft signals only: never a reason to reject a run, only to keep it off the public top 10 pending review.
 * A legitimate, skilled, fast player can trip one of these; the score and reasons are there so a human can
 * look at the run (see the flagged_runs view) and clear it.
 */
export function suspicionFor(run: RunLog, result: Extract<ReplayResult, { ok: true }>): SuspicionResult {
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  let score = 0;
  const commands = run.commands;
  if (commands.length >= SUSPICION_MIN_COMMANDS) {
    const gaps: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < commands.length; i++) gaps.push(commands[i].t - commands[i - 1].t);
    const mean = gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / gaps.length;
    const variance = gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / gaps.length;
    if (mean > 0 && Math.sqrt(variance) / mean < 0.15) {
      score += 40;
      reasons.push('unusually regular timing between commands');
    }

    if (commands.every((c) => (c.bs ?? 0) === 0)) {
      score += 25;
      reasons.push('no corrections typed in the whole run');
    }

    if (result.errorCommands === 0) {
      score += 15;
      reasons.push('no mistyped or rejected commands in the whole run');
    }
  }
  return { score: Math.min(100, score), reasons };
}

/** Below this many stored runs for a mission/tier/slot, a percentile is too noisy to show. */
export const MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE = 5;

export interface PercentileResult {
  /** Percent of other players this score beats, rounded. Null until MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE runs exist. */
  percentile: number | null;
  /** Set instead of percentile while there are too few runs: "one of the first N players to clear this." */
  firstOfN: number | null;
}

/**
 * `scores` is every stored score for one mission, tier and slot, including this run's own (already-kept)
 * score. Ties are not "beaten": a tie with the leader never reads as beating 100% of everyone else.
 */
export function percentileFor(scores: readonly number[], mine: number): PercentileResult {
  const total = scores.length;
  if (total < MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE) return { percentile: null, firstOfN: total };
  const better = scores.filter((s) => s < mine).length;
  return { percentile: Math.round((better / (total - 1)) * 100), firstOfN: null };
}

export interface TimelineStep {
  /** When the command was typed, in ms from the start of the attempt. */
  t: number;
  /** The command as typed (or the cancel marker). */
  cmd: string;
  /** Every line the engine printed for it, the echo line first. */
  lines: Line[];
}

/**
 * Re-runs a logged attempt through the engine and keeps what each command printed. The viewer plays this back.
 * Because the engine is deterministic, the output matches the original attempt line for line.
 */
export function replayTimeline(mission: MissionDef, tier: Tier, commands: readonly RunCommand[]): TimelineStep[] {
  let clock = 0;
  const session = new GameSession(mission, { tier, now: () => clock });
  return commands.map((c) => {
    clock = c.t;
    if (c.cmd === CANCEL_MARK) {
      const out = new Out();
      session.cancelPending(out);
      return { t: c.t, cmd: c.cmd, lines: out.lines };
    }
    return { t: c.t, cmd: c.cmd, lines: session.input(c.cmd).lines };
  });
}
```
