`src/engine/scoring.ts`:

```ts
// Scoring and ranks. Pure functions so they are easy to test and tune.
import type { Tier } from './types';

export const MAX_MISSION_SCORE = 1000;
export const MIN_MISSION_SCORE = 100;

export interface MissionResult {
  /** Base score (1000 scale) used for rank. */
  score: number;
  /** Points awarded after the tier multiplier. */
  points: number;
  tier: Tier;
  seconds: number;
  trace: number;
  hints: number;
  endingId?: string;
}

/** 1000 base. Time, trace and hints each take points away. Completed missions never score below 100. */
export function scoreMission(seconds: number, trace: number, hints: number): number {
  const timePenalty = Math.min(400, Math.floor(seconds / 3));
  const tracePenalty = Math.min(300, trace * 2);
  const hintPenalty = hints * 120;
  return Math.max(MIN_MISSION_SCORE, MAX_MISSION_SCORE - timePenalty - tracePenalty - hintPenalty);
}

export interface Rank {
  name: string;
  minAverage: number;
}

/** Ordered from highest to lowest. */
export const RANKS: Rank[] = [
  { name: 'Ghost', minAverage: 850 },
  { name: 'Shadow Broker', minAverage: 750 },
  { name: 'Black Hat', minAverage: 600 },
  { name: 'Keyboard Cowboy', minAverage: 450 },
  { name: 'Packet Sniffer', minAverage: 300 },
  { name: 'Script Kiddie', minAverage: 0 },
];

/** Rank for one mission, from that mission's own base score. Each job is ranked on its own. */
export function rankForScore(score: number): Rank {
  return RANKS.find((r) => score >= r.minAverage) ?? RANKS[RANKS.length - 1];
}

/**
 * Campaign rank: the average over every mission in the campaign. Missions you have not finished count as zero,
 * so Ghost requires finishing all of them with strong scores.
 */
export function rankFor(results: Record<string, MissionResult | undefined>, missionIds: string[]): Rank {
  const total = missionIds.reduce((sum, id) => sum + (results[id]?.score ?? 0), 0);
  const average = missionIds.length ? total / missionIds.length : 0;
  return RANKS.find((r) => average >= r.minAverage) ?? RANKS[RANKS.length - 1];
}

```

`src/engine/session.ts`:

```ts
// GameSession: one attempt at one mission. Owns the machine state, the session stack (pivoting),
// the trace meter, pending prompts (password, recovery questions, web form, ending choice) and the flag check.
// The UI only calls input(), prompt(), inputMode(), complete() and reads the public fields.

import { COMMAND_LIST, COMMANDS } from './commands';
import { canRead, isDir, isFile, listDir, normalizePath } from './fs';
import { runLoginQuery } from './sql';
import { applyTier, DIFFICULTY, type TierConfig } from './difficulty';
import { buildSecrets, ClueLog, containsWord, type ClueCategory, type Secret } from './clues';
import { closest } from './suggest';
import type { Stdin } from './commands/types';
import type { FailReason, LineKind, Line, MachineDef, MissionDef, ProcDef, SessionStatus, SoundEvent, Tier } from './types';

/** Everything a command prints goes through Out, so the UI can render, type out and play sounds for it. */
export class Out {
  readonly lines: Line[] = [];
  readonly sounds: SoundEvent[] = [];
  clear = false;
  quit = false;

  print(text: string, kind: LineKind = 'out'): void {
    this.lines.push({ text, kind });
  }

  /** One line made of coloured pieces, such as an ls listing. */
  printSegments(parts: { text: string; kind: LineKind }[]): void {
    this.lines.push({ text: parts.map((p) => p.text).join(''), kind: 'out', segments: parts });
  }

  sound(event: SoundEvent): void {
    this.sounds.push(event);
  }
}

export interface Frame {
  machineId: string;
  user: string;
  cwd: string;
  /** Directory before the last cd, for cd -. */
  prev?: string;
}

export interface PromptParts {
  user: string;
  host: string;
  path: string;
  symbol: '#' | '$';
  root: boolean;
}

export interface RuntimeMachine {
  def: MachineDef;
  processes: ProcDef[];
  compromised: boolean;
}

export type Pending =
  | { kind: 'password'; user: string }
  | { kind: 'recovery'; user: string; index: number; answers: string[] }
  | { kind: 'form-user' }
  | { kind: 'form-pass'; username: string }
  | { kind: 'choice' };

export type InputMode = 'shell' | 'password' | 'text' | 'choice' | 'over';

export interface Completion {
  value: string;
  options: string[];
}

export interface SessionOptions {
  now?: () => number;
  tier?: Tier;
  /** Quick-start path: print a short mission briefing in the terminal instead of a separate briefing screen. */
  quickBriefing?: boolean;
}

const TRACE_LIMIT = 100;
/** Trace thresholds that warn the player, in order. Each fires once per attempt. */
const TRACE_WARNINGS = [70, 75, 85] as const;
const SECURITY_MULTIPLIER = 1.5;
const SECURITY_KILL_RELIEF = 20;
/** Commands whose output is never scanned for clues: hints would otherwise repeat themselves, and help text names example strings. */
const QUIET_FOR_CLUES = new Set(['help', 'hint', 'notes']);

export class GameSession {
  /** The mission as it plays on this tier (overrides applied). */
  readonly mission: MissionDef;
  readonly baseMission: MissionDef;
  readonly tier: Tier;
  readonly config: TierConfig;
  failReason: FailReason | null = null;
  readonly runtime: Record<string, RuntimeMachine> = {};
  stack: Frame[];
  trace = 0;
  hintsUsed = 0;
  history: string[] = [];
  status: SessionStatus = 'playing';
  pending: Pending | null = null;
  endingId: string | null = null;
  failedLogins = 0;
  /** Facts the achievements read at the end of an attempt. */
  readonly reads = new Set<string>();
  sudoTries = 0;
  usedAliases = false;
  usedPipe = false;
  /** Discoveries and player notes for this attempt. */
  readonly clues = new ClueLog();

  private readonly secrets: Secret[];
  private warningsGiven = 0;
  private readonly now: () => number;
  private readonly startedAt: number;
  private endedAt: number | null = null;
  private readonly quickBriefing: boolean;

  constructor(baseMission: MissionDef, options: SessionOptions = {}) {
    this.tier = options.tier ?? 'medium';
    const base = DIFFICULTY[this.tier];
    // Hard Friday: no hints, and trace runs at least as fast as hard tier, whatever tier was picked.
    this.config = baseMission.hardFriday
      ? { ...base, hintLimit: 0, traceMultiplier: Math.max(base.traceMultiplier, DIFFICULTY.hard.traceMultiplier) }
      : base;
    this.baseMission = baseMission;
    this.quickBriefing = options.quickBriefing ?? false;
    const mission = applyTier(baseMission, this.tier);
    this.mission = mission;
    this.secrets = this.config.autoClues ? buildSecrets(mission) : [];
    this.now = options.now ?? (() => Date.now());
    this.startedAt = this.now();
    for (const def of mission.machines) {
      this.runtime[def.id] = { def, processes: (def.processes ?? []).map((p) => ({ ...p })), compromised: false };
    }
    const start = this.runtime[mission.start.machine].def;
    this.stack = [{ machineId: start.id, user: start.defaultUser, cwd: mission.start.cwd }];
  }

  // ---- read-only views -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  get frame(): Frame {
    return this.stack[this.stack.length - 1];
  }

  get machine(): MachineDef {
    return this.runtime[this.frame.machineId].def;
  }

  get processes(): ProcDef[] {
    return this.runtime[this.frame.machineId].processes;
  }

  get hintLimit(): number {
    return this.config.hintLimit;
  }

  /** Seconds left on a timed tier, or null when there is no clock. */
  timeRemaining(): number | null {
    if (this.config.timeLimitSeconds === null) return null;
    return Math.max(0, this.config.timeLimitSeconds - this.elapsedSeconds());
  }

  get finished(): boolean {
    return this.status === 'won' || this.status === 'failed';
  }

  home(): string {
    return this.machine.users.find((u) => u.name === this.frame.user)?.home ?? '/';
  }

  resolve(path: string): string {
    return normalizePath(path, this.frame.cwd, this.home());
  }

  findByIp(ip: string): MachineDef | undefined {
    return Object.values(this.runtime)
      .map((r) => r.def)
      .find((d) => d.ip === ip);
  }

  elapsedSeconds(): number {
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor(((this.endedAt ?? this.now()) - this.startedAt) / 1000));
  }

  intro(): Line[] {
    const open: Line = { text: `${this.machine.hostname} :: session open as ${this.frame.user}`, kind: 'sys' };
    const help: Line = { text: "Type 'help' for commands. Every noisy action raises your trace.", kind: 'dim' };
    if (!this.quickBriefing) return [open, help];
    const scenario = this.mission.briefing.find((l) => typeof l === 'string') ?? this.mission.briefing[0];
    const scenarioText = typeof scenario === 'string' ? scenario : scenario.text;
    return [
      { text: `JOB ${String(this.mission.number).padStart(2, '0')} :: ${this.mission.codename} :: CLIENT ${this.mission.client}`, kind: 'title' },
      { text: scenarioText, kind: 'out' },
      open,
      help,
    ];
  }

  inputMode(): InputMode {
    if (this.finished) return 'over';
    const p = this.pending;
    if (!p) return 'shell';
    if (p.kind === 'password' || p.kind === 'form-pass') return 'password';
    if (p.kind === 'choice') return 'choice';
    return 'text';
  }

  /** The shell prompt in parts, so the UI can colour them the way Kali does. */
  promptParts(): PromptParts {
    const home = this.home();
    const cwd = this.frame.cwd;
    const path = cwd === home ? '~' : cwd.startsWith(`${home}/`) ? `~${cwd.slice(home.length)}` : cwd;
    const root = this.frame.user === 'root';
    return { user: this.frame.user, host: this.machine.hostname, path, symbol: root ? '#' : '$', root };
  }

  prompt(): string {
    const p = this.pending;
    if (p) {
      switch (p.kind) {
        case 'password':
          return 'Password: ';
        case 'form-pass':
          return 'password: ';
        case 'form-user':
          return 'username: ';
        case 'recovery': {
          const question = this.machine.recovery?.[p.user]?.[p.index]?.question ?? '';
          return `Q${p.index + 1}. ${question} `;
        }
        case 'choice':
          return `choose [1-${this.mission.endings?.length ?? 0}]: `;
      }
    }
    const parts = this.promptParts();
    return `${parts.user}@${parts.host}:${parts.path}${parts.symbol} `;
  }

  // ---- input -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  /** Feed one line of player input. Returns what to print and which sounds to play. */
  input(raw: string): Out {
    const out = new Out();
    this.expireIfLate();
    const mode = this.inputMode();

    if (mode === 'over') {
      out.print('Session closed.', 'dim');
      return out;
    }

    if (mode === 'password') out.print(`${this.prompt()}${'*'.repeat(raw.length)}`, 'echo');
    else out.print(`${this.prompt()}${mode === 'shell' ? raw.trim() : raw}`, 'echo');

    if (this.pending) {
      this.handlePending(raw, out);
      return out;
    }

    const line = raw.trim();
    if (line) {
      this.history.push(line);
      const start = out.lines.length;
      this.dispatch(line, out);
      if (!QUIET_FOR_CLUES.has(splitCommand(line)[0])) this.scanForClues(out.lines.slice(start));
    }
    return out;
  }

  private dispatch(line: string, out: Out): void {
    const segments = splitPipeline(line);
    if (segments.some((seg) => seg === '')) {
      out.print('bash: syntax error near unexpected token `|\'', 'err');
      return;
    }
    if (segments.length > 1) this.usedPipe = true;
    let stdin: Stdin = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
      const [name, ...args] = splitCommand(segments[i]);
      const command = COMMANDS.get(name);
      if (!command) {
        out.print(`bash: ${name}: command not found`, 'err');
        const near = closest(name, COMMAND_LIST);
        if (near.length) out.print(`Did you mean: ${near.join(', ')}?`, 'dim');
        return;
      }
      if (command.name !== name) this.usedAliases = true;
      if (i === segments.length - 1) {
        command.run(this, args, out, stdin);
        return;
      }
      // Earlier parts of a pipeline feed the next part. Errors and warnings still reach the player.
      const sub = new Out();
      command.run(this, args, sub, stdin);
      out.sounds.push(...sub.sounds);
      stdin = [];
      for (const piece of sub.lines) {
        if (piece.kind === 'err' || piece.kind === 'warn') out.lines.push(piece);
        else stdin.push(piece.text);
      }
      // A failed part ends the pipeline, the way a shell does after a non-zero exit.
      if (sub.lines.some((piece) => piece.kind === 'err')) return;
    }
  }

  /** Runs one line with root rights, for sudo. The real account comes back afterwards. */
  runAsRoot(line: string, out: Out): void {
    const frame = this.frame;
    const user = frame.user;
    frame.user = 'root';
    try {
      this.dispatch(line, out);
    } finally {
      frame.user = user;
    }
  }

  /** The account this session is logged in as can use sudo. */
  canSudo(): boolean {
    return Boolean(this.machine.users.find((u) => u.name === this.frame.user)?.sudo);
  }

  /** Ctrl+C at a password or question prompt: drops the question. Returns true when one was open. */
  cancelPending(out: Out): boolean {
    if (!this.pending) return false;
    this.pending = null;
    out.print('^C', 'dim');
    return true;
  }

  /** Records a discovery in the clue log. Does nothing on tiers without an automatic log. */
  discover(category: ClueCategory, text: string): void {
    if (this.config.autoClues) this.clues.record(category, text);
  }

  /** Looks for known secrets in command output and logs the ones that appear as whole words. */
  private scanForClues(lines: Line[]): void {
    if (this.secrets.length === 0) return;
    for (const line of lines) {
      for (const secret of this.secrets) {
        if (containsWord(line.text, secret.text)) this.clues.record(secret.category, secret.text);
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Corrects a mistyped path one segment at a time. Each segment uses the exact child when it exists,
   * otherwise the closest child the current user can read (so private names are never suggested).
   * Returns the corrected absolute path, or null when nothing close exists.
   */
  suggestPath(input: string): string | null {
    const target = this.resolve(input);
    let cur = '/';
    let changed = false;
    for (const seg of target.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
      const exact = cur === '/' ? `/${seg}` : `${cur}/${seg}`;
      if (isFile(this.machine, exact) || isDir(this.machine, exact)) {
        cur = exact;
        continue;
      }
      if (!isDir(this.machine, cur) || !canRead(this.machine, cur, this.frame.user)) return null;
      const readable = listDir(this.machine, cur)
        .map((e) => e.name)
        .filter((name) => canRead(this.machine, cur === '/' ? `/${name}` : `${cur}/${name}`, this.frame.user));
      const [match] = closest(seg, readable, 1);
      if (!match) return null;
      cur = cur === '/' ? `/${match}` : `${cur}/${match}`;
      changed = true;
    }
    return changed ? cur : null;
  }

  private handlePending(raw: string, out: Out): void {
    const p = this.pending;
    if (!p) return;

    switch (p.kind) {
      case 'password': {
        this.pending = null;
        if (raw === '?' && this.machine.recovery?.[p.user]) {
          this.pending = { kind: 'recovery', user: p.user, index: 0, answers: [] };
          out.print('Account recovery started. Answer your security questions.', 'warn');
          return;
        }
        const account = this.machine.users.find((u) => u.name === p.user);
        if (account && account.password === raw) this.grant(p.user, out);
        else this.loginFailed(out);
        return;
      }

      case 'recovery': {
        const questions = this.machine.recovery?.[p.user] ?? [];
        p.answers.push(raw);
        if (p.answers.length < questions.length) {
          p.index = p.answers.length;
          return;
        }
        this.pending = null;
        const ok = questions.every((q, i) => normalize(q.answer) === normalize(p.answers[i]));
        if (ok) this.grant(p.user, out);
        else this.loginFailed(out);
        return;
      }

      case 'form-user':
        this.pending = { kind: 'form-pass', username: raw };
        return;

      case 'form-pass':
        this.pending = null;
        this.submitForm(p.username, raw, out);
        return;

      case 'choice':
        this.choose(raw, out);
        return;
    }
  }

  // ---- actions used by commands ----------------------------------------------------------------

  /** Adds trace for a noisy action. Security processes on the current host multiply it. */
  noise(base: number, out: Out): void {
    if (this.finished) return;
    const alarmed = this.processes.some((p) => p.security);
    const amount = Math.round(base * this.config.traceMultiplier * (alarmed ? SECURITY_MULTIPLIER : 1));
    this.trace = Math.min(TRACE_LIMIT, this.trace + amount);

    const messages = [
      'the network is watching you. Slow down or get out.',
      'alarm raised. Security has your address.',
      'one more mistake and you are burned.',
    ];
    const sounds: SoundEvent[] = ['trace', 'alarm', 'alert'];
    TRACE_WARNINGS.forEach((level, i) => {
      if (this.warningsGiven > i || this.trace < level) return;
      this.warningsGiven = i + 1;
      out.print(`!! TRACE ${level}%: ${messages[i]}`, 'warn');
      out.sound(sounds[i]);
    });
    if (this.trace >= TRACE_LIMIT) {
      this.status = 'failed';
      this.failReason = 'trace';
      this.pending = null;
      this.endedAt = this.now();
      out.print('TRACE 100%. Connection severed. Mission failed.', 'err');
      out.sound('fail');
    }
  }

  /** Ends the attempt when the clock runs out. Returns true if this call ended it. */
  expireIfLate(): boolean {
    if (this.status !== 'playing') return false;
    if (this.timeRemaining() !== 0) return false;
    this.status = 'failed';
    this.failReason = 'time';
    this.pending = null;
    this.endedAt = this.now();
    return true;
  }

  lowerTrace(amount: number): void {
    this.trace = Math.max(0, this.trace - amount);
  }

  /** Opens a session on another machine. The new frame starts as that machine's default user. */
  pushFrame(machineId: string): void {
    const def = this.runtime[machineId].def;
    const home = def.users.find((u) => u.name === def.defaultUser)?.home ?? '/';
    this.stack.push({ machineId, user: def.defaultUser, cwd: home });
  }

  popFrame(): void {
    if (this.stack.length > 1) this.stack.pop();
  }

  /** Password prompt for `login <user> [password]`. */
  attemptLogin(user: string, password: string, out: Out): void {
    const account = this.machine.users.find((u) => u.name === user);
    if (account && account.password === password) this.grant(user, out);
    else this.loginFailed(out);
  }

  requestPassword(user: string): void {
    this.pending = { kind: 'password', user };
  }

  openForm(out: Out): void {
    const form = this.machine.webForm;
    if (!form) return;
    out.print(`=== ${form.title} ===`, 'title');
    out.print('Credentials are sent to the server exactly as typed.', 'dim');
    this.pending = { kind: 'form-user' };
  }

  submitFlag(text: string, out: Out): void {
    if (this.status !== 'playing') return;
    if (text.trim() !== this.mission.flag) {
      out.print('Access denied: that is not the flag.', 'err');
      out.sound('denied');
      return;
    }
    if (this.mission.endings?.length) {
      this.status = 'choosing';
      this.pending = { kind: 'choice' };
      out.print('FLAG ACCEPTED. The client is waiting for your call.', 'ok');
      out.print('What do you do with the packet?', 'warn');
      this.mission.endings.forEach((e, i) => out.print(`  ${i + 1}) ${e.label}`));
      out.sound('granted');
      return;
    }
    this.win(out);
  }

  // ---- internals -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  private grant(user: string, out: Out): void {
    const account = this.machine.users.find((u) => u.name === user);
    this.frame.user = user;
    this.frame.cwd = account?.home ?? '/';
    this.runtime[this.frame.machineId].compromised = true;
    out.print(`Access granted. Logged in as ${user}.`, 'ok');
    out.sound('granted');
    this.noise(4, out);
  }

  private loginFailed(out: Out): void {
    this.failedLogins++;
    out.print('Login incorrect.', 'err');
    out.sound('denied');
    this.noise(15, out);
  }

  private submitForm(username: string, password: string, out: Out): void {
    const form = this.machine.webForm;
    if (!form) return;
    const result = runLoginQuery(form.query, username, password, form.rows);
    if (!result.ok && result.error) {
      out.print(`SQL error: ${result.error}`, 'err');
      out.sound('alert');
      this.noise(5, out);
      return;
    }
    if (!result.ok) {
      this.failedLogins++;
      out.print('Invalid credentials.', 'err');
      out.sound('denied');
      this.noise(15, out);
      return;
    }
    out.print(`Welcome, ${result.user}.`, 'ok');
    out.sound('granted');
    for (const line of form.success) out.print(line, 'ok');
    this.noise(4, out);
  }

  private choose(raw: string, out: Out): void {
    const endings = this.mission.endings ?? [];
    const n = Number(raw.trim());
    if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > endings.length) {
      out.print(`Choose 1-${endings.length}.`, 'warn');
      return;
    }
    const ending = endings[n - 1];
    this.endingId = ending.id;
    this.pending = null;
    this.win(out, ending.text);
  }

  private win(out: Out, extra: string[] = []): void {
    this.status = 'won';
    this.endedAt = this.now();
    if (!this.mission.endings?.length) out.print('FLAG ACCEPTED. Payment queued. Mission complete.', 'ok');
    for (const line of extra) out.print(line);
    out.sound('win');
  }

  // ---- tab completion --------------------------------------------------------------------------

  complete(line: string): Completion {
    // After a pipe, complete the command that follows it.
    const pipeAt = line.lastIndexOf('|');
    if (pipeAt >= 0) {
      const inner = this.complete(line.slice(pipeAt + 1).replace(/^\s+/, ''));
      return { value: `${line.slice(0, pipeAt + 1)} ${inner.value}`, options: inner.options };
    }

    const endsWithSpace = /\s$/.test(line);
    const parts = line.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);

    if (parts.length <= 1 && !endsWithSpace) {
      const partial = parts[0] ?? '';
      const options = COMMAND_LIST.filter((c) => c.startsWith(partial));
      return finishCompletion('', partial, options);
    }

    // Aliases complete as the command they stand for: connect is ssh, scan is nmap, crack is john.
    const cmd = COMMANDS.get(parts[0])?.name ?? parts[0];
    const partial = endsWithSpace ? '' : parts[parts.length - 1];
    const base = line.slice(0, line.length - partial.length);
    let options: string[] = [];

    switch (cmd) {
      case 'cd':
      case 'ls':
      case 'cat':
      case 'grep':
      case 'head':
      case 'tail':
      case 'wc':
      case 'find':
        options = this.pathCandidates(partial);
        break;
      case 'ssh':
      case 'nmap':
      case 'ping': {
        // user@ip: the part after the @ completes against neighbours, the user part is kept as typed.
        const at = partial.lastIndexOf('@');
        const prefix = at >= 0 ? partial.slice(0, at + 1) : '';
        const ipPart = partial.slice(at + 1);
        options = this.machine.neighbors.filter((ip) => ip.startsWith(ipPart)).map((ip) => `${prefix}${ip}`);
        break;
      }
      case 'su':
        options = this.machine.users.map((u) => u.name).filter((n) => n.startsWith(partial));
        break;
      case 'decode':
      case 'base64':
        if (parts.length <= 2) options = ['base64', 'rot13', 'hex'].filter((e) => e.startsWith(partial));
        break;
      case 'kill':
        options = this.processes.map((p) => String(p.pid)).filter((pid) => pid.startsWith(partial));
        break;
      case 'help':
      case 'man':
        options = COMMAND_LIST.filter((c) => c.startsWith(partial));
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
    return finishCompletion(base, partial, options);
  }

  private pathCandidates(partial: string): string[] {
    const slash = partial.lastIndexOf('/');
    const dirPart = slash >= 0 ? partial.slice(0, slash + 1) : '';
    const namePart = partial.slice(slash + 1);
    const abs = this.resolve(dirPart || '.');
    if (!isDir(this.machine, abs) || !canRead(this.machine, abs, this.frame.user)) return [];
    return listDir(this.machine, abs)
      .filter((e) => e.name.startsWith(namePart) && (namePart.startsWith('.') || !e.name.startsWith('.')))
      .map((e) => `${dirPart}${e.name}${e.type === 'dir' ? '/' : ''}`);
  }
}

/** Splits a line on | outside quotes. Each part is one command of a pipeline. Quotes are kept for splitCommand. */
export function splitPipeline(line: string): string[] {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  let quote: string | null = null;
  for (const ch of line) {
    if (quote) {
      if (ch === quote) quote = null;
      current += ch;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '"' || ch === "'") {
      quote = ch;
      current += ch;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '|') {
      parts.push(current.trim());
      current = '';
      continue;
    }
    current += ch;
  }
  parts.push(current.trim());
  return parts;
}

/** Splits a shell line into words. Single and double quotes group words; everything else is literal. */
export function splitCommand(line: string): string[] {
  const words: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  let quote: string | null = null;
  let hasWord = false;
  for (const ch of line) {
    if (quote) {
      if (ch === quote) quote = null;
      else current += ch;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '"' || ch === "'") {
      quote = ch;
      hasWord = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (/\s/.test(ch)) {
      if (current || hasWord) words.push(current);
      current = '';
      hasWord = false;
      continue;
    }
    current += ch;
    hasWord = true;
  }
  if (current || hasWord) words.push(current);
  return words;
}

function normalize(answer: string): string {
  return answer.trim().toLowerCase();
}

function commonPrefix(items: string[]): string {
  if (items.length === 0) return '';
  let prefix = items[0];
  for (const item of items.slice(1)) {
    while (!item.startsWith(prefix)) prefix = prefix.slice(0, -1);
  }
  return prefix;
}

function finishCompletion(base: string, partial: string, options: string[]): Completion {
  if (options.length === 0) return { value: base + partial, options: [] };
  if (options.length === 1) {
    const only = options[0];
    return { value: base + only + (only.endsWith('/') ? '' : ' '), options };
  }
  const shared = commonPrefix(options);
  return { value: base + (shared.length > partial.length ? shared : partial), options };
}

```

`src/engine/sql.ts`:

```ts
// A deliberately small SQL evaluator for the staff login form.
// It exists so the injection puzzle behaves like a real database: quotes break strings, AND binds tighter
// than OR, and -- or # comments out the rest of the statement. It is not a general SQL engine.

type TokType = 'str' | 'num' | 'id' | 'kw' | 'op' | 'lp' | 'rp';

interface Tok {
  type: TokType;
  value: string;
  pos: number;
}

export class SqlError extends Error {}

const KEYWORDS = new Set(['SELECT', 'FROM', 'WHERE', 'AND', 'OR']);

function syntaxError(sql: string, pos: number): SqlError {
  const near = sql.slice(pos).split('\n')[0];
  return new SqlError(`You have an error in your SQL syntax; check the manual for the right syntax near '${near}' at line 1`);
}

export function tokenize(sql: string): Tok[] {
  const toks: Tok[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < sql.length) {
    const c = sql[i];
    if (/\s/.test(c)) {
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '#' || (c === '-' && sql[i + 1] === '-')) break;

    if (c === "'") {
      let j = i + 1;
      let value = '';
      for (;;) {
        if (j >= sql.length) throw syntaxError(sql, i);
        if (sql[j] === "'") {
          if (sql[j + 1] === "'") {
            value += "'";
            j += 2;
            continue;
          }
          break;
        }
        value += sql[j];
        j++;
      }
      toks.push({ type: 'str', value, pos: i });
      i = j + 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (c === '(') {
      toks.push({ type: 'lp', value: c, pos: i++ });
      continue;
    }
    if (c === ')') {
      toks.push({ type: 'rp', value: c, pos: i++ });
      continue;
    }
    if (c === '=' || c === ',' || c === '*' || c === ';') {
      toks.push({ type: 'op', value: c, pos: i++ });
      continue;
    }
    if (/\d/.test(c)) {
      let j = i;
      while (j < sql.length && /\d/.test(sql[j])) j++;
      toks.push({ type: 'num', value: sql.slice(i, j), pos: i });
      i = j;
      continue;
    }
    if (/[A-Za-z_]/.test(c)) {
      let j = i;
      while (j < sql.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(sql[j])) j++;
      const word = sql.slice(i, j);
      const upper = word.toUpperCase();
      toks.push(KEYWORDS.has(upper) ? { type: 'kw', value: upper, pos: i } : { type: 'id', value: word.toLowerCase(), pos: i });
      i = j;
      continue;
    }
    throw syntaxError(sql, i);
  }
  return toks;
}

/** Recursive-descent evaluator for a WHERE expression against one row. */
class WhereParser {
  private i = 0;

  constructor(
    private readonly toks: Tok[],
    private readonly sql: string,
    private readonly row: Record<string, string>,
  ) {}

  parse(): boolean {
    const result = this.or();
    if (this.i < this.toks.length) throw syntaxError(this.sql, this.toks[this.i].pos);
    return result;
  }

  private peek(): Tok | undefined {
    return this.toks[this.i];
  }

  private isKw(word: string): boolean {
    const t = this.peek();
    return t?.type === 'kw' && t.value === word;
  }

  private or(): boolean {
    let value = this.and();
    while (this.isKw('OR')) {
      this.i++;
      const right = this.and();
      value = value || right;
    }
    return value;
  }

  private and(): boolean {
    let value = this.cmp();
    while (this.isKw('AND')) {
      this.i++;
      const right = this.cmp();
      value = value && right;
    }
    return value;
  }

  private cmp(): boolean {
    const t = this.peek();
    if (t?.type === 'lp') {
      this.i++;
      const inner = this.or();
      const close = this.peek();
      if (close?.type !== 'rp') throw syntaxError(this.sql, close?.pos ?? this.sql.length);
      this.i++;
      return inner;
    }
    const left = this.value();
    const op = this.peek();
    if (op?.type !== 'op' || op.value !== '=') throw syntaxError(this.sql, op?.pos ?? this.sql.length);
    this.i++;
    const right = this.value();
    return left === right;
  }

  private value(): string {
    const t = this.peek();
    if (!t) throw syntaxError(this.sql, this.sql.length);
    this.i++;
    if (t.type === 'str' || t.type === 'num') return t.value;
    if (t.type === 'id') {
      if (!(t.value in this.row)) throw new SqlError(`Unknown column '${t.value}' in 'where clause'`);
      return this.row[t.value];
    }
    throw syntaxError(this.sql, t.pos);
  }
}

export interface LoginRow {
  username: string;
  password: string;
}

export type LoginResult = { ok: true; user: string; query: string } | { ok: false; query: string; error?: string };

/**
 * Builds the query the way a naive app would (raw string substitution), then returns the first row whose WHERE clause is true.
 * Syntax errors come back as `error` so the UI can show the database message, which often leaks the injection point.
 */
export function runLoginQuery(template: string, username: string, password: string, rows: LoginRow[]): LoginResult {
  const query = template.split('{username}').join(username).split('{password}').join(password);
  try {
    const toks = tokenize(query);
    const whereAt = toks.findIndex((t) => t.type === 'kw' && t.value === 'WHERE');
    if (whereAt < 0) throw syntaxError(query, 0);
    const body = toks.slice(whereAt + 1);
    const hit = rows.map((r, i) => ({ id: String(i + 1), ...r })).find((row) => new WhereParser(body, query, row).parse());
    return hit ? { ok: true, user: hit.username, query } : { ok: false, query };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof SqlError) return { ok: false, query, error: err.message };
    throw err;
  }
}
```

`src/engine/suggest.ts`:

```ts
// "Did you mean" helpers. Pure string functions: no session, machine or DOM access.

/** Edit distance between two strings (insertions, deletions, substitutions). */
export function levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number {
  if (a === b) return 0;
  let prev = Array.from({ length: b.length + 1 }, (_, i) => i);
  for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {
    const cur = [i];
    for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {
      const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
      cur[j] = Math.min(prev[j] + 1, cur[j - 1] + 1, prev[j - 1] + cost);
    }
    prev = cur;
  }
  return prev[b.length];
}

/** Candidates close enough to `word` to be a plausible typo, nearest first. Case is ignored. */
export function closest(word: string, candidates: readonly string[], limit = 3): string[] {
  const target = word.toLowerCase();
  if (!target) return [];
  const maxDistance = target.length < 4 ? 1 : 2;
  return candidates
    .map((c) => ({ c, d: levenshtein(target, c.toLowerCase()) }))
    .filter((x) => x.d > 0 && x.d <= maxDistance)
    .sort((x, y) => x.d - y.d || x.c.localeCompare(y.c))
    .slice(0, limit)
    .map((x) => x.c);
}
```

```ts
// Shared types for the game engine and mission data.
// Mission files are pure data built from these shapes; the engine knows nothing about specific missions.

export type LineKind = 'out' | 'echo' | 'err' | 'ok' | 'warn' | 'dim' | 'sys' | 'title' | 'glitch' | 'dir' | 'exec';

export interface Line {
  text: string;
  kind: LineKind;
  /** Optional coloured pieces. `text` is still the whole plain line, used for scanning and piping. */
  segments?: { text: string; kind: LineKind }[];
}

export type SoundEvent =
  | 'key'
  | 'key-space'
  | 'key-enter'
  | 'key-backspace'
  | 'command'
  | 'error'
  | 'enter'
  | 'alert'
  | 'alarm'
  | 'granted'
  | 'denied'
  | 'trace'
  | 'glitch'
  | 'tick'
  | 'rank'
  | 'sting'
  | 'win'
  | 'fail'
  | 'chatter'
  | 'ping'
  | 'boom'
  | 'hint'
  | 'hover'
  | 'click'
  | 'transition'
  | 'crt-on'
  | 'crt-off'
  | 'achievement'
  | 'transfer';

export interface FileDef {
  content: string;
  /** Account that owns the file. */
  owner?: string;
  /** Private files are readable only by their owner and root. */
  private?: boolean;
  /** Shown green by ls, with the execute bit in ls -l. */
  executable?: boolean;
}

export interface DirDef {
  owner?: string;
  private?: boolean;
}

export interface UserDef {
  name: string;
  password: string;
  home: string;
  /** Member of the sudoers file. Only these accounts can use sudo. */
  sudo?: boolean;
}

export interface PortDef {
  port: number;
  service: string;
  banner: string;
}

export interface ProcDef {
  pid: number;
  user: string;
  name: string;
  /** Security processes (IDS, sentry, auditd) multiply trace gain while they run. */
  security?: boolean;
}

export interface SecurityQuestion {
  question: string;
  answer: string;
}

export interface WebFormDef {
  title: string;
  /** Accounts the login table contains. Order matters: the first matching row wins. */
  rows: { username: string; password: string }[];
  /**
   * SQL template. `{username}` and `{password}` are pasted in raw, the way a careless app would.
   * Supported SQL: SELECT ... FROM ... WHERE expr, with = AND OR parentheses, quoted strings, -- and # comments.
   */
  query: string;
  success: string[];
}

export interface MachineDef {
  id: string;
  hostname: string;
  ip: string;
  /** IPs this machine can route to. Scanning and connecting only work along these edges. */
  neighbors: string[];
  defaultUser: string;
  users: UserDef[];
  ports: PortDef[];
  files: Record<string, FileDef>;
  dirs?: Record<string, DirDef>;
  processes?: ProcDef[];
  /** Security questions for account recovery, keyed by username. Triggered by typing ? at a password prompt. */
  recovery?: Record<string, SecurityQuestion[]>;
  webForm?: WebFormDef;
}

export interface HashDef {
  hash: string;
  plaintext: string;
  algo: string;
}

export interface EmailDef {
  from: string;
  date: string;
  subject: string;
  body: string;
}

export interface EndingDef {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  text: string[];
}

export type StoryLine = string | { text: string; glitch: true };

export interface MissionDef {
  id: string;
  number: number;
  codename: string;
  title: string;
  client: string;
  briefing: StoryLine[];
  debrief: StoryLine[];
  start: { machine: string; cwd: string };
  /** Must include the rig (the player's own machine, id "rig"). */
  machines: MachineDef[];
  hashes: HashDef[];
  inbox: EmailDef[];
  flag: string;
  hints: [string, string, string];
  /** A short line shown under the trace bar, for a player who needs the goal restated mid-mission. */
  objective?: string;
  /** If present, submitting the flag opens a choice that selects an ending. */
  endings?: EndingDef[];
  /** Per-tier additions. Missing tiers play the base mission unchanged. */
  tierOverrides?: TierOverrides;
  /** Walkthrough used by the test suite to prove the mission is beatable. Entries are typed exactly as a player would type them. */
  solution: string[];
  /** Daily jobs name the real-world idea they teach, so the debrief card can match it. */
  realWorldKey?: string;
  /** The same walkthrough typed with the real Kali command names. The tests play it too. */
  kaliSolution: string[];
  /** The Friday daily variant: no hints, trace at least as fast as hard tier, every decoy present. */
  hardFriday?: boolean;
  /**
   * False only for a mission whose flag can't be swapped in after the fact: it's reconstructed by the player
   * from a fragment, or only ever stored transformed (rot13, etc). Defaults to true. See src/lib/variation.ts.
   */
  varyFlag?: boolean;
}

export type Tier = 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard';

/** Extra content injected on a tier. Used for decoys: extra files, users, processes, mail. */
export interface MachineOverride {
  files?: Record<string, FileDef>;
  users?: UserDef[];
  processes?: ProcDef[];
}

export interface TierOverride {
  machines?: Record<string, MachineOverride>;
  /** Subjects of base inbox messages to drop on this tier. Applied before `inbox` is appended. */
  hideInbox?: string[];
  inbox?: EmailDef[];
}

export type TierOverrides = Partial<Record<Tier, TierOverride>>;

export type FailReason = 'trace' | 'time';

export type SessionStatus = 'playing' | 'choosing' | 'won' | 'failed';
```

`src/lib/achievements.ts`:

```ts
// Achievements: fifteen unlocks, kept in the save on this device only. Each one is a plain test over the facts from a
// finished run. Five are hidden until earned. Unlocks are computed from facts, never from anything a page can edit.

import type { GameSession } from '../engine/session';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import type { IconName } from '../components/Icon';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import { EASTER_EGG_PATH } from '../missions/shared';

export interface AchievementFacts {
  tier: Tier;
  trace: number;
  hints: number;
  score: number;
  seconds: number;
  isDaily: boolean;
  /** True when this win completes the whole campaign on this tier. */
  campaignDoneOnTier: boolean;
  /** Days in a row with a completed daily, counting today. */
  streak: number;
  /** Lifetime sudo attempts, across every run. */
  sudoTotal: number;
  usedAliases: boolean;
  usedPipe: boolean;
  eggFound: boolean;
  readEveryPublicFile: boolean;
  hourLocal: number;
}

export interface AchievementDef {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: IconName;
  hidden?: boolean;
  test: (f: AchievementFacts) => boolean;
}

export const ACHIEVEMENTS: AchievementDef[] = [
  { id: 'first-breach', title: 'First breach', description: 'Finish any job.', icon: 'check', test: () => true },
  { id: 'clean-sweep', title: 'Clean sweep', description: 'Finish a job with zero trace.', icon: 'radar', test: (f) => f.trace === 0 },
  { id: 'hard-win', title: 'Hard mode', description: 'Finish a job on Hard.', icon: 'tier', test: (f) => f.tier === 'hard' },
  { id: 'no-hints-hard', title: 'Unassisted', description: 'Finish a job on Hard with no hints.', icon: 'lock', test: (f) => f.tier === 'hard' && f.hints === 0 },
  { id: 'ghost-rank', title: 'Ghost', description: 'Rank Ghost on a single job.', icon: 'transfer', test: (f) => f.score >= 850 },
  { id: 'speed-run', title: 'Quick hands', description: 'Finish a job in under three minutes.', icon: 'terminal', test: (f) => f.seconds < 180 },
  { id: 'campaign-complete', title: 'Campaign complete', description: 'Finish every job in the campaign on one tier.', icon: 'card', test: (f) => f.campaignDoneOnTier },
  { id: 'daily-first', title: 'Today\'s job', description: 'Finish a daily challenge.', icon: 'calendar', test: (f) => f.isDaily },
  { id: 'streak-7', title: 'Seven in a row', description: 'Finish a daily challenge seven days running.', icon: 'calendar', test: (f) => f.streak >= 7 },
  { id: 'pipe-dreams', title: 'Pipe dreams', description: 'Finish a job using a pipeline.', icon: 'map', test: (f) => f.usedPipe },
  { id: 'kali-only', title: 'Real tools', description: 'Finish a job with the real command names only.', icon: 'help', hidden: true, test: (f) => !f.usedAliases },
  { id: 'sudo-five', title: 'Asking nicely', description: 'Try sudo five times.', icon: 'alert', hidden: true, test: (f) => f.sudoTotal >= 5 },
  { id: 'egg-hunter', title: 'Found the bug report', description: 'Find the hidden file on the rig.', icon: 'unlock', hidden: true, test: (f) => f.eggFound },
  { id: 'read-host', title: 'Full read', description: 'Read every public file on one host.', icon: 'settings', hidden: true, test: (f) => f.readEveryPublicFile },
  { id: 'night-owl', title: 'Night shift', description: 'Finish a job between midnight and four in the morning.', icon: 'sound-on', hidden: true, test: (f) => f.hourLocal < 4 },
];

const BY_ID = new Map(ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => [a.id, a]));

export function achievementById(id: string): AchievementDef | undefined {
  return BY_ID.get(id);
}

/** True when every public (not private) file on every remote host of the mission was opened in this attempt. */
export function readEveryPublicFileIn(session: GameSession): boolean {
  for (const machine of session.mission.machines) {
    if (machine.id === 'rig') continue;
    const publicFiles = Object.entries(machine.files)
      .filter(([, file]) => !file.private)
      .map(([path]) => path);
    if (publicFiles.length === 0) continue;
    if (publicFiles.every((path) => session.reads.has(`${machine.id}:${path}`))) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** The facts an attempt leaves behind, read once at the moment of the win. */
export function factsFromWin(session: GameSession, extra: { score: number; isDaily: boolean; campaignDoneOnTier: boolean; streak: number; sudoTotal: number; now: Date }): AchievementFacts {
  return {
    tier: session.tier,
    trace: session.trace,
    hints: session.hintsUsed,
    score: extra.score,
    seconds: session.elapsedSeconds(),
    isDaily: extra.isDaily,
    campaignDoneOnTier: extra.campaignDoneOnTier,
    streak: extra.streak,
    sudoTotal: extra.sudoTotal,
    usedAliases: session.usedAliases,
    usedPipe: session.usedPipe,
    eggFound: session.reads.has(`rig:${EASTER_EGG_PATH}`),
    readEveryPublicFile: readEveryPublicFileIn(session),
    hourLocal: extra.now.getHours(),
  };
}

/** Achievements this run earns that are not already unlocked. Order follows the list above. */
export function newlyUnlocked(facts: AchievementFacts, unlocked: readonly string[]): AchievementDef[] {
  const have = new Set(unlocked);
  return ACHIEVEMENTS.filter((a) => !have.has(a.id) && a.test(facts));
}

/** Whether the campaign is complete on a tier, counting only the campaign's own missions. */
export function campaignDone(completed: Record<string, unknown>): boolean {
  return MISSIONS.every((m) => Boolean(completed[m.id]));
}
```

`src/lib/analytics.ts`:

```ts
// Custom analytics events, on top of Vercel Web Analytics' automatic page views. One named function per event the
// product asked for, each taking only non-identifying properties — never a nickname or player id. If the analytics
// script is missing, blocked or errors, these fail silently: a dropped event never breaks the game.

import { track } from '@vercel/analytics';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';

function safeTrack(name: string, props?: Record<string, string | number | boolean>): void {
  try {
    track(name, props);
  } catch {
    // Blocked by an ad blocker, offline, or analytics not configured for this build. Nothing to do.
  }
}

export function trackLandingView(): void {
  safeTrack('landing_view');
}

export function trackStart(): void {
  safeTrack('start');
}

export function trackHandleRegistered(online: boolean): void {
  safeTrack('handle_registered', { online });
}

export type MissionScope = 'campaign' | 'daily';

export function trackMissionStarted(tier: Tier, scope: MissionScope): void {
  safeTrack('mission_started', { tier, scope });
}

export function trackMissionCleared(tier: Tier, scope: MissionScope): void {
  safeTrack('mission_cleared', { tier, scope });
}

export function trackMissionTraced(tier: Tier, scope: MissionScope): void {
  safeTrack('mission_traced', { tier, scope });
}

export function trackDailyPlayed(tier: Tier): void {
  safeTrack('daily_played', { tier });
}

export type ShareMethod = 'download' | 'share' | 'copy';

export function trackShareClicked(method: ShareMethod): void {
  safeTrack('share_clicked', { method });
}

export function trackRestoreUsed(): void {
  safeTrack('restore_used');
}
```

`src/lib/audio.ts`:

```ts
// Real recordings, played through the Web Audio API (see assets/audio/LICENSES.md for what and where from).
// Three buses: effects, music and keyboard, each with its own toggle, all under a master volume, plus a quiet
// ambient bed that runs under everything and comes up a little in missions.
// Everything fails silently: audio unsupported, not yet allowed to start (autoplay policy), or a clip that
// 404s or fails to decode. The game keeps working either way.
//
// The generative soundtrack (lib/music.ts) is a separate system, kept because it is built to never audibly loop
// over a long session the way a fixed recording would; this file only starts it and feeds it the trace.

import type { SoundEvent } from '../engine/types';
import { MusicPlayer } from './music';
import type { MusicTrack } from './save';

export interface AudioSettings {
  volume: number; // 0 to 1
  sfx: boolean;
  music: boolean;
  /** Music level, as a fraction of the effects level. 0.4 means 40 percent. */
  musicVolume: number;
  track: MusicTrack;
  /** Mechanical keyboard sound, independent of sfx. */
  keySounds: boolean;
  keySoundsVolume: number;
}

interface Graph {
  ctx: AudioContext;
  sfxBus: GainNode;
  musicBus: GainNode;
  keyBus: GainNode;
  ambientGain: GainNode;
  ambientSource: AudioBufferSourceNode | null;
  music: MusicPlayer | null;
}

const MASTER = 0.5; // global headroom
const SFX_LEVEL = 0.7;
/** Minimum gap between key sounds, so holding a key (OS auto-repeat) never becomes a continuous hiss. */
const KEY_THROTTLE_MS = 30;
/** The hum's own level within the sfx bus: quiet at rest, a little more present once a mission is live. */
const AMBIENT_MENU = 0.35;
const AMBIENT_MISSION = 0.85;

let settings: AudioSettings = { volume: 0.8, sfx: true, music: true, musicVolume: 0.4, track: 'cyberpunk-drone', keySounds: true, keySoundsVolume: 0.5 };
let graph: Graph | null = null;
let lastKey = 0;
let tension = 0;
let ambienceActive = false;

function build(): Graph | null {
  try {
    if (graph) return graph;
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
    const Ctor = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (!Ctor) return null;
    const ctx = new Ctor();
    const master = ctx.createGain();
    master.gain.value = MASTER;
    master.connect(ctx.destination);
    const sfxBus = ctx.createGain();
    const musicBus = ctx.createGain();
    const keyBus = ctx.createGain();
    const ambientGain = ctx.createGain();
    ambientGain.gain.value = ambienceActive ? AMBIENT_MISSION : AMBIENT_MENU;
    sfxBus.connect(master);
    musicBus.connect(master);
    keyBus.connect(master);
    ambientGain.connect(sfxBus);
    graph = { ctx, sfxBus, musicBus, keyBus, ambientGain, ambientSource: null, music: null };
    applySettings();
    startAmbientLoop(graph);
    return graph;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function applySettings(): void {
  if (!graph) return;
  const { ctx, sfxBus, musicBus, keyBus } = graph;
  const now = ctx.currentTime;
  sfxBus.gain.setTargetAtTime(settings.sfx ? SFX_LEVEL * settings.volume : 0, now, 0.02);
  musicBus.gain.setTargetAtTime(settings.music ? SFX_LEVEL * settings.volume * settings.musicVolume : 0, now, 0.05);
  keyBus.gain.setTargetAtTime(settings.keySounds ? SFX_LEVEL * settings.volume * settings.keySoundsVolume : 0, now, 0.02);
}

function context(): Graph | null {
  const g = build();
  if (g && g.ctx.state === 'suspended') void g.ctx.resume();
  return g;
}

export function setAudioSettings(next: AudioSettings): void {
  settings = { ...next, volume: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, next.volume)) };
  applySettings();
}

// ---- sample loading ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** Fetches and decodes a clip once; later calls (and failures) reuse the same promise. Relative URL, so a nested deploy still finds it. */
const bufferCache = new Map<string, Promise<AudioBuffer | null>>();
function loadBuffer(ctx: AudioContext, url: string): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
  let p = bufferCache.get(url);
  if (p) return p;
  p = fetch(url)
    .then((r) => r.arrayBuffer())
    .then((data) => ctx.decodeAudioData(data))
    .catch(() => null);
  bufferCache.set(url, p);
  return p;
}

function playBuffer(g: Graph, buffer: AudioBuffer, bus: GainNode, volume = 1, rate = 1): void {
  const src = g.ctx.createBufferSource();
  src.buffer = buffer;
  if (rate !== 1) src.playbackRate.value = rate;
  if (volume === 1) {
    src.connect(bus);
  } else {
    const gain = g.ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.value = volume;
    src.connect(gain).connect(bus);
  }
  src.start();
}

/** One clip per discrete sound event. Events missing here (the key variants) have their own playback path below. */
const SFX_URLS: Partial<Record<SoundEvent, string>> = {
  command: 'audio/sfx/command.mp3',
  error: 'audio/sfx/error.mp3',
  enter: 'audio/sfx/enter.mp3',
  alert: 'audio/sfx/alert.mp3',
  alarm: 'audio/sfx/alarm.mp3',
  granted: 'audio/sfx/granted.mp3',
  denied: 'audio/sfx/denied.mp3',
  trace: 'audio/sfx/trace.mp3',
  glitch: 'audio/sfx/glitch.mp3',
  tick: 'audio/sfx/tick.mp3',
  rank: 'audio/sfx/rank.mp3',
  sting: 'audio/sfx/sting.mp3',
  win: 'audio/sfx/win.mp3',
  fail: 'audio/sfx/fail.mp3',
  chatter: 'audio/sfx/chatter.mp3',
  ping: 'audio/sfx/ping.mp3',
  boom: 'audio/sfx/boom.mp3',
  hint: 'audio/sfx/hint.mp3',
  hover: 'audio/sfx/hover.mp3',
  click: 'audio/sfx/click.mp3',
  transition: 'audio/sfx/transition.mp3',
  'crt-on': 'audio/sfx/crt-on.mp3',
  'crt-off': 'audio/sfx/crt-off.mp3',
  achievement: 'audio/sfx/achievement.mp3',
  transfer: 'audio/sfx/transfer.mp3',
};

const AMBIENT_URL = 'audio/ambient/server-hum.mp3';

function startAmbientLoop(g: Graph): void {
  void loadBuffer(g.ctx, AMBIENT_URL).then((buf) => {
    if (!buf || g.ambientSource) return;
    const src = g.ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buf;
    src.loop = true;
    src.connect(g.ambientGain);
    src.start();
    g.ambientSource = src;
  });
}

/** 0 outside a mission, 1 inside one. The hum stays on throughout; only its level changes. */
export function setAmbienceActive(active: boolean): void {
  ambienceActive = active;
  if (!graph) return;
  graph.ambientGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(active ? AMBIENT_MISSION : AMBIENT_MENU, graph.ctx.currentTime, 1.5);
}

/**
 * Starts the soundtrack. Call it from a user gesture (the browser blocks sound until one happens).
 * Menus and the landing page sit at intensity 0. Missions follow the trace through setTension.
 */
export function startMusic(): void {
  const g = context();
  if (!g) return;
  if (!g.music) g.music = new MusicPlayer(g.ctx, g.musicBus);
  if (!g.music.running) g.music.start(settings.track);
  setTension(tension);
}

/** Kept for existing callers. */
export const startAmbience = startMusic;

/** Switches the soundtrack with a crossfade. Before the first click it only remembers the choice. */
export function setMusicTrack(track: MusicTrack): void {
  settings = { ...settings, track };
  graph?.music?.setTrack(track);
}

/** Called whenever the trace changes, and with 0 on menus. The soundtrack crossfades to match. */
export function setTension(trace: number): void {
  tension = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, trace));
  graph?.music?.setIntensity(tension / 100);
}

export type KeySoundVariant = Extract<SoundEvent, 'key' | 'key-space' | 'key-enter' | 'key-backspace'>;

/**
 * Which keyboard sound (if any) a keystroke gets. Pure, so it is tested without a browser. Modifier keys, arrows,
 * Tab and the like get null: they are not a "typing" sound.
 */
export function keyVariantFor(key: string): KeySoundVariant | null {
  if (key === ' ') return 'key-space';
  if (key === 'Enter') return 'key-enter';
  if (key === 'Backspace') return 'key-backspace';
  if (key.length === 1) return 'key';
  return null;
}

/** Multiplies `center` by a random factor within `±spread` (0.1 means ±10%), so no two keystrokes sound identical. */
function jitter(center: number, spread: number): number {
  return center * (1 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * spread);
}

/** Several real keystrokes per variant, sliced from two different mechanical keyboard recordings (see LICENSES.md). */
const KEY_URLS: Record<KeySoundVariant, string[]> = {
  key: [
    'audio/keys/key-1.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-2.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-3.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-4.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-5.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-6.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-7.mp3',
    'audio/keys/key-8.mp3',
  ],
  'key-space': ['audio/keys/space-1.mp3'],
  'key-enter': ['audio/keys/enter-1.mp3'],
  'key-backspace': ['audio/keys/backspace-1.mp3'],
};

/**
 * A real mechanical keystroke, picked at random from this variant's slices, with a little pitch (playback rate)
 * and volume jitter so repeated keys never sound identical.
 */
function mechanicalKey(g: Graph, variant: KeySoundVariant): void {
  const urls = KEY_URLS[variant];
  const url = urls[Math.floor(Math.random() * urls.length)];
  const rate = jitter(1, 0.08);
  const volume = jitter(1, 0.22);
  void loadBuffer(g.ctx, url).then((buf) => {
    if (buf) playBuffer(g, buf, g.keyBus, volume, rate);
  });
}

export function play(event: SoundEvent): void {
  const g = context();
  if (!g) return;

  if (event === 'key' || event === 'key-space' || event === 'key-enter' || event === 'key-backspace') {
    // Keyboard sound has its own toggle and level, so it is not gated by the general sfx switch.
    if (!settings.keySounds) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now - lastKey < KEY_THROTTLE_MS) return;
    lastKey = now;
    mechanicalKey(g, event);
    return;
  }

  if (!settings.sfx) return;
  const url = SFX_URLS[event];
  if (!url) return;
  void loadBuffer(g.ctx, url).then((buf) => {
    if (buf) playBuffer(g, buf, g.sfxBus);
  });
}
```

`src/lib/board.ts`:

```ts
// Pure rules for the global board panel shown on the debrief: what headline to show, and whether
// the post actually landed. Kept separate from the component so the rule "only say you're on the
// board after a successful post" is checked without rendering anything.

import type { SubmitResult } from './leaderboard';

/** True only once the run is actually verified on the server. Posting failed, offline, or not yet attempted all say no. */
export function isOnBoard(result: SubmitResult | null): boolean {
  return result?.status === 'saved';
}

/** The panel's headline: the celebratory line is earned, never assumed from just having a handle. */
export function boardHeadline(result: SubmitResult | null): string {
  return isOnBoard(result) ? "YOU'RE ON THE BOARD" : 'GLOBAL BOARD';
}

/**
 * "You beat 64% of players on this job (Medium)", or "One of the first 3 players to clear this" before enough
 * runs exist to make a percentile mean anything. Null when the run wasn't saved, or the server sent neither.
 */
export function percentileLine(result: SubmitResult | null, tierLabel: string): string | null {
  if (!isOnBoard(result) || result?.status !== 'saved') return null;
  if (typeof result.percentile === 'number') return `You beat ${result.percentile}% of players on this job (${tierLabel})`;
  if (typeof result.firstOfN === 'number') return `One of the first ${result.firstOfN} player${result.firstOfN === 1 ? '' : 's'} to clear this`;
  return null;
}
```

`src/lib/cloud.ts`:

```ts
// Cloud save client. Talks to the account Edge Function (register, restore, sync, rename). The local save stays the
// source of truth: every call here either succeeds and returns what the server holds, or says it is offline, and the
// game carries on with the local copy.

import type { SaveData } from './save';

const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL ?? '';
const PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? '';
const TIMEOUT_MS = 10000;

export function cloudConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(SUPABASE_URL && PUBLISHABLE_KEY);
}

export type CloudResult<T> = { ok: true; value: T } | { ok: false; offline: boolean; reason: string };

async function call<T>(body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<CloudResult<T>> {
  if (!cloudConfigured()) return { ok: false, offline: true, reason: 'Cloud save is not set up on this build.' };
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/account`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { apikey: PUBLISHABLE_KEY, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    const data = (await res.json()) as { ok: boolean; reason?: string } & Record<string, unknown>;
    if (!data.ok) return { ok: false, offline: false, reason: data.reason ?? 'The server said no.' };
    return { ok: true, value: data as unknown as T };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, offline: true, reason: 'Cloud save is offline. Your progress is safe on this device and will sync later.' };
  } finally {
    window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

export interface RegisterResult {
  recoveryKey: string;
  syncToken: string;
}

/** Creates the cloud account for this player and returns the recovery key, shown once. */
export function registerAccount(playerId: string, nickname: string, save: SaveData): Promise<CloudResult<RegisterResult>> {
  return call<RegisterResult>({ action: 'register', playerId, nickname, save });
}

export interface RestoreResult {
  playerId: string;
  nickname: string;
  syncToken: string;
  save: SaveData | null;
}

/** Restores the account behind a recovery key onto this device. */
export function restoreAccount(recoveryKey: string): Promise<CloudResult<RestoreResult>> {
  return call<RestoreResult>({ action: 'restore', recoveryKey });
}

/** Pushes this device's save and gets back the merged copy. */
export function syncSave(playerId: string, syncToken: string, save: SaveData): Promise<CloudResult<{ save: SaveData }>> {
  return call<{ save: SaveData }>({ action: 'sync', playerId, syncToken, save });
}

export function renameAccount(playerId: string, syncToken: string, nickname: string): Promise<CloudResult<{ nickname: string }>> {
  return call<{ nickname: string }>({ action: 'rename', playerId, syncToken, nickname });
}

/** Debounce for sync: several changes in a short time send one request. */
export function debounce<A extends unknown[]>(fn: (...args: A) => void, ms: number): (...args: A) => void {
  let timer: number | undefined;
  return (...args: A) => {
    if (timer !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = window.setTimeout(() => fn(...args), ms);
  };
}
```

`src/lib/daily.ts`:

```ts
// The daily challenge. One procedurally generated job per UTC day, the same for everyone in the world.
// The date is the seed. The generator is deterministic, so the server can regenerate any day and check a replay.
// Each job is built from one of three campaign ideas, with randomised machines, names, flags, decoys and hints.
// The generator also writes the walkthrough next to the job, and the tests prove every generated job is beatable.

import { toBase64 } from '../engine/encoding';
import type { EmailDef, FileDef, HashDef, MachineDef, MissionDef } from '../engine/types';
import { WORDLIST } from '../engine/commands/crypto';
import { makeRig } from '../missions/shared';

/** UTC calendar date, as YYYY-MM-DD. */
export function utcDate(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** The date before, or after, a YYYY-MM-DD date. */
export function shiftDate(date: string, days: number): string {
  const d = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** Daily number: day 1 is 2026-01-01. Shown as "DAILY 42" on screen. */
export function dailyNumber(date: string): number {
  const start = Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1);
  const day = Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
  return Math.floor((day - start) / 86_400_000) + 1;
}

/** Milliseconds until the next UTC midnight: the countdown on the landing page and menu. */
export function msUntilReset(now: Date = new Date()): number {
  const next = Date.UTC(now.getUTCFullYear(), now.getUTCMonth(), now.getUTCDate() + 1);
  return next - now.getTime();
}

/** Hard Friday: every Friday (UTC), the daily is the harder variant. */
export function isHardFriday(date: string): boolean {
  return new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`).getUTCDay() === 5;
}

/** Consecutive days with a completed daily, counting back from today (or yesterday, so a streak survives until the day is over). */
export function streakFor(completedDates: readonly string[], today: string): number {
  const done = new Set(completedDates);
  let cursor = done.has(today) ? today : shiftDate(today, -1);
  let streak = 0;
  while (done.has(cursor)) {
    streak++;
    cursor = shiftDate(cursor, -1);
  }
  return streak;
}

/** 32-bit FNV-1a hash of a string. Turns a date into a seed. */
export function hashSeed(text: string): number {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    h ^= text.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

/** mulberry32: a small seeded generator. The same seed always gives the same sequence. */
export function seededRandom(seed: number): () => number {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

const HOSTS = ['ledger-02', 'relay-7', 'ops-box', 'hangar-db', 'sat-uplink', 'cafe-wifi-3', 'meridian-web', 'quarry-nas'];
const PEOPLE = ['maya', 'sol', 'tobi', 'vera', 'kenji', 'ines', 'omar', 'lena', 'bram', 'noor'];
const WORDS = ['copper', 'lantern', 'harbor', 'velvet', 'orbit', 'cedar', 'falcon', 'quartz', 'juniper', 'meadow'];
const FLAG_WORDS = ['tin', 'salt', 'ember', 'glass', 'static', 'lamp', 'river', 'signal', 'drift', 'anchor'];
const DECOYS: Record<string, string> = {
  '/srv/share/old-menu.txt': 'Canteen menu, week 41: soup, bread, the usual. No substitutions on Fridays.',
  '/var/log/cron.log': 'CRON[2231]: nightly backup finished in 4m12s\nCRON[2240]: rotated 3 logs',
  '/srv/share/welcome.txt': 'Welcome to the shared drive. Keep it tidy. Delete your old drafts.',
};

function pick<T>(rand: () => number, items: readonly T[]): T {
  return items[Math.floor(rand() * items.length)];
}

function hex(rand: () => number, length: number): string {
  let out = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) out += Math.floor(rand() * 16).toString(16);
  return out;
}

/** Target IP in a private range that cannot clash with the rig (10.66.0.1). */
function targetIp(rand: () => number): string {
  let second = 0;
  do {
    second = 1 + Math.floor(rand() * 200);
  } while (second === 66);
  return `10.${second}.${Math.floor(rand() * 250) + 1}.${Math.floor(rand() * 200) + 20}`;
}

/** Which real-world card each daily template teaches. Keys match lib/realworld.ts. */
export const REAL_WORLD_KEYS = ['shared-note', 'encoded-secret', 'weak-hash'] as const;

/** The job for one UTC date. Pure: the same date always returns the same mission. */
export function dailyMission(date: string): MissionDef {
  const hardFriday = isHardFriday(date);
  const rand = seededRandom(hashSeed(`breach-daily:${date}`));
  const ip = targetIp(rand);
  const host = pick(rand, HOSTS);
  const user = pick(rand, PEOPLE);
  const pw = `${pick(rand, WORDS)}${10 + Math.floor(rand() * 89)}`;
  const flag = `BREACH{${pick(rand, FLAG_WORDS)}_${pick(rand, FLAG_WORDS)}_${Math.floor(rand() * 900 + 100)}}`;
  const template = Math.floor(rand() * 3) as 0 | 1 | 2;
  const home = `/home/${user}`;
  const flagPath = `${home}/flag.txt`;

  const files: Record<string, FileDef> = {};
  const decoyPaths = hardFriday ? Object.keys(DECOYS) : Object.keys(DECOYS).filter(() => rand() < 0.6);
  for (const p of decoyPaths) files[p] = { content: DECOYS[p] };

  let solution: string[];
  let kaliSolution: string[];
  let hashes: HashDef[] = [];
  let concept: string;
  let mail: EmailDef[] = [];

  if (template === 0) {
    // A password written in a shared note.
    concept = 'credentials left in a shared note';
    files['/srv/share/handover.txt'] = { content: `Handover: ${user} is on call. Login is ${user}, password ${pw}. Rotate it when the audit ends.` };
    kaliSolution = ['nmap', `ssh ${ip}`, 'ls /srv/share', 'cat /srv/share/handover.txt', `su ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ['scan', `connect ${ip}`, 'ls /srv/share', 'cat /srv/share/handover.txt', `login ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
  } else if (template === 1) {
    // Base64 is encoding, not a lock.
    concept = 'a password hidden behind base64';
    const b64 = toBase64(pw);
    files['/etc/app/settings.env'] = { content: `APP_ENV=prod\nDB_USER=${user}\nDB_PASS_B64=${b64}\nLOG_LEVEL=warn` };
    kaliSolution = ['nmap', `ssh ${ip}`, 'cat /etc/app/settings.env', `echo ${b64} | base64 -d`, `su ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ['scan', `connect ${ip}`, 'cat /etc/app/settings.env', `decode base64 ${b64}`, `login ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
  } else {
    // A weak, unsalted hash of a common password.
    concept = 'a weak hash of a common password';
    const plain = pick(rand, WORDLIST);
    const hash = hex(rand, 32);
    hashes = [{ hash, plaintext: plain, algo: 'md5' }];
    files['/opt/auth/users.db'] = { content: `${user}:${hash}:admin\nguest:${hex(rand, 32)}:user` };
    // The account's password is the common word, so this template uses it as the password.
    kaliSolution = ['nmap', `ssh ${ip}`, 'cat /opt/auth/users.db', `john ${hash}`, `su ${user}`, plain, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ['scan', `connect ${ip}`, 'cat /opt/auth/users.db', `crack ${hash}`, `login ${user}`, plain, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    mail = [];
  }

  // The password in the hash template is the wordlist word, so the account uses that word.
  const accountPassword = template === 2 ? hashes[0].plaintext : pw;

  const target: MachineDef = {
    id: 'target',
    hostname: host,
    ip,
    neighbors: [],
    defaultUser: 'guest',
    users: [
      { name: 'guest', password: '', home: '/home/guest' },
      { name: user, password: accountPassword, home: home },
    ],
    ports: [
      { port: 22, service: 'ssh', banner: 'OpenSSH 8.9p1' },
      { port: 443, service: 'https', banner: 'nginx 1.18.0' },
    ],
    files: {
      ...files,
      [flagPath]: { owner: user, private: true, content: flag },
      '/home/guest/readme.txt': { content: 'Guest access. Look around, touch nothing.' },
    },
    dirs: { [home]: { owner: user, private: true } },
  };

  const rig = makeRig([ip]);
  const dayNo = dailyNumber(date);

  return {
    id: `daily-${date}`,
    number: dayNo,
    codename: hardFriday ? `DAILY ${dayNo} :: HARD FRIDAY` : `DAILY ${dayNo}`,
    title: `${host}: ${concept}`,
    client: 'OPEN BOARD',
    briefing: [
      `Today's job is open to everyone, on the same network, the same day. Target: ${host} (${ip}).`,
      `Find the account, get in, read the flag at ${flagPath}.`,
      ...(hardFriday ? ['Hard Friday: trace runs fast and hints are off, on every difficulty.'] : []),
    ],
    debrief: [
      `The weak point was ${concept}. It is one of the oldest mistakes in the book, and it still shows up in the field.`,
    ],
    start: { machine: 'rig', cwd: '/home/ghost' },
    machines: [rig, target],
    hashes,
    inbox: mail,
    flag,
    hints: [
      `Start with what the box shares. Look for notes, config files and anything a team left lying about.`,
      `The account is ${user}. Find out how its password is stored.`,
      `${concept[0].toUpperCase()}${concept.slice(1)} is the weak point. Use the matching decode or cracking tool, then su ${user}.`,
    ],
    solution,
    kaliSolution,
    realWorldKey: REAL_WORLD_KEYS[template],
    hardFriday,
  };
}

/** The longest run of consecutive completed days, ever. */
export function bestStreak(completedDates: readonly string[]): number {
  const sorted = [...new Set(completedDates)].sort();
  let best = 0;
  let run = 0;
  let prev: string | null = null;
  for (const d of sorted) {
    run = prev !== null && shiftDate(prev, 1) === d ? run + 1 : 1;
    best = Math.max(best, run);
    prev = d;
  }
  return best;
}
```

`src/lib/deferred.ts`:

```ts
// A timeout that can be skipped: resolved early and synchronously instead of waiting out its delay.
// Used so a player's keystroke during a brief scripted animation (the password-cracker's guesser)
// finishes that animation immediately rather than being dropped by a disabled input.

export interface Skippable {
  /** Runs the scheduled work now, instead of at the scheduled time. Safe to call more than once, or after it already ran. */
  skip: () => void;
}

/** Runs `fn` once, after `ms`, or immediately via `skip()` — whichever happens first. */
export function skippable(fn: () => void, ms: number): Skippable {
  let done = false;
  const id = setTimeout(() => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    fn();
  }, ms);
  return {
    skip: () => {
      if (done) return;
      done = true;
      clearTimeout(id);
      fn();
    },
  };
}
```

`src/lib/feedback.ts`:

```ts
// "Report bug / feedback": builds a mailto link prefilled with the game version, the tier, and the mission when there
// is one. Pure, so the prefill can be checked without opening a mail client.

import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { BUG_REPORT_CONTACT } from '../missions/shared';

export interface FeedbackContext {
  version: string;
  tier: Tier;
  /** The job's codename, when the report comes from a debrief. Omitted from Settings. */
  mission?: string;
}

export function buildFeedbackMailto(ctx: FeedbackContext): string {
  const subject = 'BREACH feedback';
  const lines = [`Version: ${ctx.version}`, `Tier: ${ctx.tier}`];
  if (ctx.mission) lines.push(`Mission: ${ctx.mission}`);
  lines.push('', '(describe the issue or idea here)');
  const body = lines.join('\n');
  return `mailto:${BUG_REPORT_CONTACT}?subject=${encodeURIComponent(subject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(body)}`;
}
```
