`src/lib/funnel.ts`:

```ts
// Anonymous funnel analytics into our own Supabase, written through the submit-run Edge Function's 'events'
// action (see supabase/functions/submit-run/index.ts and the events table). A free alternative to Vercel's
// paid custom events: see src/lib/analytics.ts for those, which stay in place alongside this.
//
// Session-scoped: session_id is one random id per browser tab (sessionStorage, not localStorage), never the
// player id, never tied to a nickname or account. Events are queued and flushed in a batch with
// navigator.sendBeacon, so a slow or blocked network can never slow the game down, and every public function
// here fails silently — a dropped event never breaks play. Sends nothing at all when Do Not Track or Global
// Privacy Control is on.

import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';

const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL ?? '';
const PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? '';
const SESSION_KEY = 'breach.session';
const FLUSH_DELAY_MS = 1500;
const MAX_BATCH = 10;

export type FunnelEventName =
  | 'landing_view'
  | 'start_clicked'
  | 'daily_clicked'
  | 'mission_started'
  | 'first_command'
  | 'hint_used'
  | 'mission_cleared'
  | 'mission_traced'
  | 'mission_aborted'
  | 'score_posted'
  | 'share_clicked'
  | 'restore_used';

export interface FunnelProps {
  tier?: Tier;
  missionId?: string;
  /** A small number: seconds elapsed, a hint number, and so on. Meaning depends on the event. */
  value?: number;
}

export function funnelConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(SUPABASE_URL && PUBLISHABLE_KEY);
}

/** Do Not Track or Global Privacy Control, either one: nothing is collected or sent. Checked fresh every call. */
export function privacySignalOn(): boolean {
  try {
    const nav = navigator as Navigator & { globalPrivacyControl?: boolean };
    const docNotTrack = (window as unknown as { doNotTrack?: string }).doNotTrack;
    const dnt = nav.doNotTrack ?? docNotTrack;
    return dnt === '1' || dnt === 'yes' || nav.globalPrivacyControl === true;
  } catch {
    // Can't tell: treat as opted out, never as opted in.
    return true;
  }
}

function newId(): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') return crypto.randomUUID();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
  bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
  const hex = [...bytes].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`;
}

/** One random id per browser tab. Not the player id, and gone once the tab closes. */
export function getSessionId(): string {
  try {
    const existing = window.sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
    if (existing) return existing;
    const id = newId();
    window.sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, id);
    return id;
  } catch {
    // Storage is blocked. The id lasts for this call only, which just means this event travels alone.
    return newId();
  }
}

let queue: (FunnelProps & { event: FunnelEventName })[] = [];
let flushTimer: number | null = null;
let listenersAttached = false;

/** Flushes once the tab is hidden or closing, so a batch still in the queue isn't lost. Attached at most once. */
function attachFlushListeners(): void {
  if (listenersAttached) return;
  listenersAttached = true;
  try {
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') flushFunnel();
    });
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => flushFunnel());
  } catch {
    // No document/window to attach to. The flush timer still covers it.
  }
}

/** Sends whatever is queued right now, with sendBeacon (falling back to a keepalive fetch). Never throws. */
export function flushFunnel(): void {
  if (flushTimer !== null) {
    try {
      window.clearTimeout(flushTimer);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    flushTimer = null;
  }
  if (queue.length === 0) return;
  const batch = queue;
  queue = [];
  if (!funnelConfigured()) return;
  try {
    const sessionId = getSessionId();
    const events = batch.map((e) => ({ event: e.event, tier: e.tier, missionId: e.missionId, value: e.value }));
    const url = `${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/submit-run?apikey=${encodeURIComponent(PUBLISHABLE_KEY)}`;
    const body = JSON.stringify({ action: 'events', sessionId, events });
    const sent = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.sendBeacon === 'function' && navigator.sendBeacon(url, body);
    if (!sent) {
      void fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body, keepalive: true }).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch {
    // Blocked, offline, or sendBeacon/fetch unavailable. A dropped batch of analytics never breaks the game.
  }
}

function scheduleFlush(): void {
  if (flushTimer !== null) return;
  try {
    flushTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      flushTimer = null;
      flushFunnel();
    }, FLUSH_DELAY_MS);
  } catch {
    flushFunnel();
  }
}

/** Queues one funnel event. No-op when the board isn't configured, or Do Not Track / GPC is on. */
export function track(event: FunnelEventName, props: FunnelProps = {}): void {
  try {
    if (!funnelConfigured() || privacySignalOn()) return;
    queue.push({ event, ...props });
    attachFlushListeners();
    if (queue.length >= MAX_BATCH) flushFunnel();
    else scheduleFlush();
  } catch {
    // Never let analytics break the game.
  }
}

export const trackFunnelLandingView = (): void => track('landing_view');
export const trackFunnelStartClicked = (): void => track('start_clicked');
export const trackFunnelDailyClicked = (): void => track('daily_clicked');
export const trackFunnelMissionStarted = (tier: Tier, missionId: string): void => track('mission_started', { tier, missionId });
export const trackFunnelFirstCommand = (tier: Tier, missionId: string): void => track('first_command', { tier, missionId });
export const trackFunnelHintUsed = (tier: Tier, missionId: string, hintNumber: number): void => track('hint_used', { tier, missionId, value: hintNumber });
export const trackFunnelMissionCleared = (tier: Tier, missionId: string, seconds: number): void => track('mission_cleared', { tier, missionId, value: seconds });
export const trackFunnelMissionTraced = (tier: Tier, missionId: string, seconds: number): void => track('mission_traced', { tier, missionId, value: seconds });
export const trackFunnelMissionAborted = (tier: Tier, missionId: string, seconds: number): void => track('mission_aborted', { tier, missionId, value: seconds });
export const trackFunnelScorePosted = (tier: Tier, missionId: string): void => track('score_posted', { tier, missionId });
export const trackFunnelShareClicked = (): void => track('share_clicked');
export const trackFunnelRestoreUsed = (): void => track('restore_used');

```

`src/lib/haptics.ts`:

```ts
// A tiny per-keystroke buzz on devices that support navigator.vibrate. Off by default: a buzz on every key is more
// annoying than helpful for most people, so the player opts in from Settings. Throttled the same way the keyboard
// sound is, so holding a key down does not turn into a continuous buzz.

const THROTTLE_MS = 30;
const DURATION_MS = 8;

let enabled = false;
let lastVibrate = 0;

export function setHapticsEnabled(next: boolean): void {
  enabled = next;
}

export function hapticsSupported(): boolean {
  return typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && typeof navigator.vibrate === 'function';
}

/** Call on a relevant keystroke. Does nothing when the toggle is off, the device cannot vibrate, or it is too soon. */
export function vibrateKey(): void {
  if (!enabled || !hapticsSupported()) return;
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastVibrate < THROTTLE_MS) return;
  lastVibrate = now;
  try {
    navigator.vibrate(DURATION_MS);
  } catch {
    // Blocked or unsupported in this context. Nothing to do.
  }
}
```

`src/lib/history.ts`:

```ts
// The ops log: every attempt on this device, with its outcome, timing and the command log that produced it.
// Pure functions only, so the stats, the favourite command and the merge rules can be tested directly.

import type { RunCommand } from '../engine/replay';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';

export type Outcome = 'cleared' | 'traced' | 'aborted' | 'timed-out';

export interface AttemptRecord {
  /** Unique per attempt. Used to merge histories from two devices without duplicates. */
  id: string;
  /** ISO time the attempt ended. */
  at: string;
  missionId: string;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  tier: Tier;
  /** 1 for the first try at this job on this tier, 2 for the next, and so on. */
  attemptNo: number;
  outcome: Outcome;
  /** Base score. Zero unless the job was cleared. */
  score: number;
  points: number;
  seconds: number;
  trace: number;
  hints: number;
  commands: RunCommand[];
}

/** Newest attempts kept. Older ones are dropped to keep the save small. */
export const HISTORY_LIMIT = 200;

/** Adds an attempt, keeps the newest HISTORY_LIMIT, newest first. */
export function addAttempt(history: readonly AttemptRecord[], record: AttemptRecord): AttemptRecord[] {
  return mergeHistory(history, [record]);
}

/** Union of two histories by id, newest first, capped. Safe to call with the same attempt twice. */
export function mergeHistory(a: readonly AttemptRecord[], b: readonly AttemptRecord[]): AttemptRecord[] {
  const byId = new Map<string, AttemptRecord>();
  for (const r of [...a, ...b]) byId.set(r.id, r);
  return [...byId.values()].sort((x, y) => (x.at < y.at ? 1 : x.at > y.at ? -1 : 0)).slice(0, HISTORY_LIMIT);
}

export interface MissionStats {
  attempts: number;
  /** The attempt number of the first clear, or null if never cleared. */
  firstClearAttempt: number | null;
  bestScore: number | null;
  bestSeconds: number | null;
  /** Share of attempts that cleared the job, 0 to 1. */
  clearRate: number;
}

/** Stats for one job on one tier, from the history. */
export function missionStats(history: readonly AttemptRecord[], missionId: string, tier: Tier): MissionStats {
  const mine = history.filter((r) => r.missionId === missionId && r.tier === tier);
  const cleared = mine.filter((r) => r.outcome === 'cleared');
  const firstClear = cleared.length ? Math.min(...cleared.map((r) => r.attemptNo)) : null;
  return {
    attempts: mine.length,
    firstClearAttempt: firstClear,
    bestScore: cleared.length ? Math.max(...cleared.map((r) => r.score)) : null,
    bestSeconds: cleared.length ? Math.min(...cleared.map((r) => r.seconds)) : null,
    clearRate: mine.length ? cleared.length / mine.length : 0,
  };
}

/** The command typed most often across every attempt, with how many times. Null if nothing was typed yet. */
export function favouriteCommand(history: readonly AttemptRecord[]): { name: string; count: number } | null {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of history) {
    for (const c of r.commands) {
      const name = c.cmd.trim().split(/\s+/)[0];
      if (!name || name === '\u0003' || name.includes('{') || name.includes('|')) continue;
      counts.set(name, (counts.get(name) ?? 0) + 1);
    }
  }
  let best: { name: string; count: number } | null = null;
  for (const [name, count] of counts) if (!best || count > best.count) best = { name, count };
  return best;
}

/** Total seconds spent in attempts, counting each attempt once. */
export function totalSeconds(history: readonly AttemptRecord[]): number {
  return history.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.seconds, 0);
}

```

`src/lib/landing.ts`:

```ts
// The landing page terminal. Pure: it turns one typed line into the lines to print and an optional action.
// Visitors who type a real command get a short answer that nudges them to start playing.

import type { Line } from '../engine/types';

export type LandingAction = 'start' | 'clear';

export interface LandingReply {
  lines: Line[];
  action?: LandingAction;
}

const line = (text: string, kind: Line['kind'] = 'out'): Line => ({ text, kind });

const HELP: Line[] = [
  line('Commands you can try on this page:', 'sys'),
  line('  start      begin hacking. the real game is one step away'),
  line('  tiers      what easy, medium and hard change'),
  line('  whoami     who is at the keyboard'),
  line('  sudo       try your luck'),
  line('  about      what this is'),
  line('  clear      clear the screen'),
];

/** Answers one line typed on the landing page. Unknown commands get a shell-style error, with a hint toward start. */
export function landingReply(raw: string): LandingReply {
  const text = raw.trim();
  if (!text) return { lines: [] };
  const [name, ...rest] = text.split(/\s+/);
  const arg = rest.join(' ');

  switch (name) {
    case 'start':
    case 'hack':
    case 'play':
    case 'breach':
      return { lines: [line('Initiating breach sequence...', 'ok')], action: 'start' };
    case 'help':
    case '?':
      return { lines: HELP };
    case 'whoami':
      return { lines: [line('guest. A curious visitor. The network is not expecting you.', 'ok')] };
    case 'sudo':
      return {
        lines: [
          line('[sudo] password for guest: ', 'dim'),
          line('guest is not in the sudoers file. This incident will be reported.', 'err'),
        ],
      };
    case 'rm':
      return { lines: [line(arg.includes('-rf') ? 'Nice try. Every command here is logged.' : 'rm: nothing here is yours to delete.', 'warn')] };
    case 'cat':
      return { lines: [line(`cat: ${arg || 'missing operand'}: permission denied. Nothing here is for you yet.`, 'err')] };
    case 'ls':
      return { lines: [line('jobs/   daily/   (no postings yet. type start.)', 'dir')] };
    case 'tiers':
      return {
        lines: [
          line('EASY    forgiving trace, a tip in every briefing'),
          line('MEDIUM  the standard balance'),
          line('HARD    one hint, a fast trace, a 20 minute clock'),
          line('Pick one on the job board after you start.', 'dim'),
        ],
      };
    case 'about':
      return {
        lines: [
          line('BREACH is a hacking terminal game. You take freelance jobs, breach fake networks,'),
          line('and learn the real tools and ideas security people use. Nothing here touches a real system.', 'dim'),
        ],
      };
    case 'clear':
      return { lines: [], action: 'clear' };
    case 'nmap':
    case 'ssh':
    case 'john':
    case 'su':
      return { lines: [line(`${name}: start a job first. Type start.`, 'warn')] };
    default:
      return { lines: [line(`bash: ${name}: command not found. Try help, or type start.`, 'err')] };
  }
}

/** The line typed above the landing terminal, once it has finished typing. */
export const TAGLINE = 'Take jobs from anonymous clients. Breach fake networks. Mind your trace.';

```

`src/lib/leaderboard.ts`:

```ts
// Leaderboard client. Reads boards straight from Supabase (the database only allows reads), and sends finished runs
// to the submit-run Edge Function. The browser never sends a score: only the command log, with timing.
// Everything degrades to "offline". Local scores always save first, so the game works with Supabase down or unset.

import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import type { RunCommand, RunLog } from '../engine/replay';

// These are read at build time. Set them in .env.local for development, and in Vercel's settings for production.
const SUPABASE_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL ?? '';
const PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ?? '';
const TIMEOUT_MS = 8000;
/**
 * A cold-started Edge Function can take noticeably longer than its ~1.3s warm response; this needs to be
 * generous because the whole point is to call it early (while the player is still on the briefing or daily
 * screen, before the mission starts) and let it finish in the background. startRun also retries once.
 */
const START_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
const PLAYER_KEY = 'breach.player';

export function leaderboardConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(SUPABASE_URL && PUBLISHABLE_KEY);
}

/** An anonymous id kept on this device. Not tied to a name, an account or an email. */
export function getPlayerId(): string {
  try {
    const saved = window.localStorage.getItem(PLAYER_KEY);
    if (saved && /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(saved)) return saved;
    const id = newUuid();
    window.localStorage.setItem(PLAYER_KEY, id);
    return id;
  } catch {
    // Storage is blocked. The id lasts for this page visit only.
    return newUuid();
  }
}

/**
 * Overwrites the local player id. Used only after a recovery-key restore adopts the server's real id for
 * that account — the id getPlayerId() would otherwise keep returning is a fresh one this device generated
 * for itself before the restore, which belongs to no account and would collide on nickname at submit time.
 * Every later getPlayerId() call (run tokens, submissions) uses this id from then on.
 */
export function setPlayerId(id: string): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(PLAYER_KEY, id);
  } catch {
    // Storage is blocked; getPlayerId() already falls back to a page-visit-only id in that case.
  }
}

function newUuid(): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') return crypto.randomUUID();
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40;
  bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80;
  const hex = [...bytes].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-${hex.slice(12, 16)}-${hex.slice(16, 20)}-${hex.slice(20)}`;
}

/** What the browser sends. There is deliberately no score field. */
export interface Submission {
  playerId: string;
  nickname: string;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  date?: string;
  run: RunLog;
}

/** Builds the request body from a finished attempt's command log. Pure, so tests can check exactly what leaves the browser. */
export function buildSubmission(input: {
  playerId: string;
  nickname: string;
  scope: 'campaign' | 'daily';
  date?: string;
  missionId: string;
  tier: Tier;
  commands: RunCommand[];
  /** The token this attempt was started with, if any. Omitted when the run can't be posted (see submitRun). */
  token?: string;
}): Submission {
  return {
    playerId: input.playerId,
    nickname: input.nickname,
    scope: input.scope,
    date: input.date,
    run: { missionId: input.missionId, tier: input.tier, commands: input.commands.map((c) => ({ t: c.t, cmd: c.cmd, bs: c.bs })), token: input.token },
  };
}

export type SubmitResult =
  | {
      status: 'saved';
      score: number;
      points: number;
      improved: boolean;
      /** Percent of other players this run beats on this mission, tier and slot. Null below percentileFor's floor. */
      percentile?: number | null;
      /** Set instead of percentile while too few runs exist yet: "one of the first N players to clear this." */
      firstOfN?: number | null;
    }
  | { status: 'rejected'; reason: string }
  | { status: 'offline'; reason: string };

async function withTimeout(url: string, init: RequestInit, ms = TIMEOUT_MS): Promise<Response> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), ms);
  try {
    return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal: controller.signal });
  } finally {
    window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function headers(): Record<string, string> {
  return { apikey: PUBLISHABLE_KEY, 'content-type': 'application/json' };
}

export interface StartedRun {
  token: string;
  seed: number;
}

async function startRunOnce(input: { playerId: string; missionId: string; tier: Tier; scope: 'campaign' | 'daily'; date?: string }): Promise<StartedRun | null> {
  try {
    const res = await withTimeout(
      `${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/submit-run`,
      { method: 'POST', headers: headers(), body: JSON.stringify({ action: 'start', ...input }) },
      START_TIMEOUT_MS,
    );
    const body = (await res.json()) as { ok: boolean; token?: string; seed?: number };
    if (!body.ok || !body.token || typeof body.seed !== 'number') return null;
    return { token: body.token, seed: body.seed };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Asks the server for a run token before an attempt begins. The token pins a server-side start time and a
 * per-run seed (for flag variation). Retries once on any failure (a timeout, including a cold-started
 * function, or a network error) before giving up. Returns null only once both tries fail, or when the board
 * isn't configured — never throws. A run started without a token still plays normally; it just can't be
 * posted (see submitRun). Callers should start this as early as possible (when the briefing or daily screen
 * opens, not when the mission actually starts) so a cold start has time to finish in the background.
 */
export async function startRun(input: { playerId: string; missionId: string; tier: Tier; scope: 'campaign' | 'daily'; date?: string }): Promise<StartedRun | null> {
  if (!leaderboardConfigured()) return null;
  return (await startRunOnce(input)) ?? (await startRunOnce(input));
}

/** Sends a finished run for checking. Returns what the server decided. */
export async function submitRun(sub: Submission): Promise<SubmitResult> {
  if (!leaderboardConfigured()) return { status: 'offline', reason: 'The global board is not set up on this build.' };
  if (!sub.run.token) return { status: 'offline', reason: "Couldn't reach the board when this run started, so it only saved locally." };
  try {
    const res = await withTimeout(`${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/submit-run`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: headers(),
      body: JSON.stringify(sub),
    });
    const body = (await res.json()) as {
      ok: boolean;
      reason?: string;
      score?: number;
      points?: number;
      improved?: boolean;
      percentile?: number | null;
      firstOfN?: number | null;
    };
    if (!body.ok) return { status: 'rejected', reason: body.reason ?? 'The run was not accepted.' };
    return {
      status: 'saved',
      score: body.score ?? 0,
      points: body.points ?? 0,
      improved: Boolean(body.improved),
      percentile: body.percentile ?? null,
      firstOfN: body.firstOfN ?? null,
    };
  } catch {
    return { status: 'offline', reason: 'The global board is offline. Your score is saved on this device.' };
  }
}

export interface CampaignRow {
  nickname: string;
  points: number;
  missions: number;
  avg_score: number;
}

export interface MissionRow {
  nickname: string;
  score: number;
  seconds: number;
  trace: number;
  hints: number;
}

/** Top campaign totals for a tier. Throws when offline, so the screen can say so. */
export async function fetchCampaignBoard(tier: Tier, limit = 50): Promise<CampaignRow[]> {
  const res = await withTimeout(
    `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/campaign_totals?tier=eq.${tier}&select=nickname,points,missions,avg_score&order=points.desc&limit=${limit}`,
    { headers: headers() },
  );
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`board ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as CampaignRow[];
}

/** Top scores for one job on one tier, or one daily. */
export async function fetchMissionBoard(missionId: string, tier: Tier, slot: string, limit = 50): Promise<MissionRow[]> {
  const res = await withTimeout(
    `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/runs?select=score,seconds,trace,hints,players(nickname)&mission_id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(missionId)}&tier=eq.${tier}&slot=eq.${encodeURIComponent(slot)}&order=score.desc&limit=${limit}`,
    { headers: headers() },
  );
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`board ${res.status}`);
  const rows = (await res.json()) as { score: number; seconds: number; trace: number; hints: number; players: { nickname: string } | null }[];
  return rows.map((r) => ({ nickname: r.players?.nickname ?? '???', score: r.score, seconds: r.seconds, trace: r.trace, hints: r.hints }));
}

/**
 * Your place on the campaign board for a tier. Null unless you have a submitted campaign score on that tier,
 * so a board with nobody on it never shows a place.
 */
export async function fetchMyRank(playerId: string, tier: Tier): Promise<number | null> {
  const mine = await withTimeout(`${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/campaign_totals?player_id=eq.${playerId}&tier=eq.${tier}&select=points`, {
    headers: headers(),
  });
  if (!mine.ok) throw new Error(`totals ${mine.status}`);
  const rows = (await mine.json()) as { points: number }[];
  if (rows.length === 0) return null;

  const res = await withTimeout(`${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/rpc/player_rank`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: headers(),
    body: JSON.stringify({ p_player: playerId, p_tier: tier }),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`rank ${res.status}`);
  const rank = (await res.json()) as number;
  return typeof rank === 'number' ? rank : null;
}

export interface TopRun {
  nickname: string;
  score: number;
  commands: RunCommand[];
}

/** The best campaign run on the board for one job and tier, with its command log. Null when nobody has one yet. */
export async function fetchTopRun(missionId: string, tier: Tier): Promise<TopRun | null> {
  const res = await withTimeout(
    `${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/runs?select=score,commands,players(nickname)&mission_id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(missionId)}&tier=eq.${tier}&slot=eq.campaign&commands=not.is.null&order=score.desc&limit=1`,
    { headers: headers() },
  );
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`top run ${res.status}`);
  const rows = (await res.json()) as { score: number; commands: RunCommand[]; players: { nickname: string } | null }[];
  const top = rows[0];
  if (!top) return null;
  return { nickname: top.players?.nickname ?? '???', score: top.score, commands: top.commands };
}
```

`src/lib/merge.ts`:

```ts
// Merging the local save with the cloud copy. The local save is the source of truth on each device, so the merge only
// adds what the other copy knows and keeps the better result when both have one. Device settings always stay local.
// Pure: runs in the browser and inside the sync Edge Function, from the same bundle.

import type { MissionResult } from '../engine/scoring';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { HISTORY_LIMIT, mergeHistory } from './history';
import type { SaveData } from './save';

const TIERS: Tier[] = ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'];

function betterResult(a: MissionResult | undefined, b: MissionResult | undefined): MissionResult | undefined {
  if (!a) return b;
  if (!b) return a;
  return b.points > a.points ? b : a;
}

/** Keeps the higher points result for every job on every tier. */
function mergeCompleted(a: SaveData['completed'], b: SaveData['completed']): SaveData['completed'] {
  const out = { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} } as SaveData['completed'];
  for (const t of TIERS) {
    const ids = new Set([...Object.keys(a[t]), ...Object.keys(b[t])]);
    for (const id of ids) {
      const best = betterResult(a[t][id], b[t][id]);
      if (best) out[t][id] = best;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Attempts only ever go up, so the larger count wins. */
function mergeAttempts(a: SaveData['attempts'], b: SaveData['attempts']): SaveData['attempts'] {
  const out = { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} } as SaveData['attempts'];
  for (const t of TIERS) {
    for (const id of new Set([...Object.keys(a[t]), ...Object.keys(b[t])])) {
      out[t][id] = Math.max(a[t][id] ?? 0, b[t][id] ?? 0);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function mergeDailies(a: SaveData['dailies'], b: SaveData['dailies']): SaveData['dailies'] {
  const out: SaveData['dailies'] = {};
  for (const date of new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)])) {
    const row: SaveData['dailies'][string] = {};
    for (const t of TIERS) {
      const best = betterResult(a[date]?.[t], b[date]?.[t]);
      if (best) row[t] = best;
    }
    out[date] = row;
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Merges a remote save into a local one. `local` wins for anything that is a device preference, and for the identity
 * fields, which never come from the cloud. Everything else is the union, keeping the best result when both have one.
 */
export function mergeSaves(local: SaveData, remote: SaveData): SaveData {
  return {
    ...local,
    completed: mergeCompleted(local.completed, remote.completed),
    attempts: mergeAttempts(local.attempts, remote.attempts),
    achievements: [...new Set([...local.achievements, ...remote.achievements])],
    history: mergeHistory(local.history, remote.history).slice(0, HISTORY_LIMIT),
    dailies: mergeDailies(local.dailies, remote.dailies),
    counters: { sudoTries: Math.max(local.counters.sudoTries, remote.counters.sudoTries) },
    nickname: local.nickname ?? remote.nickname,
  };
}

```

`src/lib/music.ts`:

```ts
// Procedural dark ambient for long sessions. Three tracks, each generated from a seeded random source so nothing repeats
// on a short loop:
//   Night Shift  slow evolving pads, a soft sub-bass pulse, and a sparse distant melody
//   Signal       a minimal pulsing arpeggio, gentle filtered noise and glitchy textures
//   Cold Room    very sparse deep drones, with occasional data chirps
// Every track has a tension layer that fades in with the trace: a heartbeat-like bass pulse, faster ticks, and a filter
// that opens. Menus play the track at zero tension. Missions follow the trace.
//
// The score is pure data (trackEvents), so tests can check key, density, register and variation without audio.
// The player is browser only, created after the first click. Sine and triangle only, a low-pass on the whole bus, and a
// compressor, so nothing gets harsh.

import type { MusicTrack } from './save';

export type Rand = () => number;

export interface NoteEvent {
  /** Beat within the bar, 0 up to 4. Each bar is four beats. */
  beat: number;
  kind: 'pad' | 'sub' | 'melody' | 'arp' | 'drone' | 'chirp' | 'noise' | 'glitch' | 'heartbeat' | 'tick';
  /** MIDI note for pitched parts. Undefined for noise, ticks and heartbeats. */
  note?: number;
  /** Length in beats. */
  length: number;
  /** 0 to 1. */
  level: number;
}

export const TRACKS: MusicTrack[] = ['cyberpunk-drone', 'night-shift', 'signal', 'cold-room'];

export const TRACK_LABELS: Record<MusicTrack, string> = {
  'cyberpunk-drone': 'Cyberpunk Drone',
  'night-shift': 'Night Shift',
  signal: 'Signal',
  'cold-room': 'Cold Room',
};

/** Where the real audio for the main track and the tension layer's heartbeat live. Relative, so a nested deploy still finds them. */
const CYBERPUNK_DRONE_URL = 'audio/music/cyberpunk-drone.mp3';
const HEARTBEAT_SAMPLE_URL = 'audio/music/heartbeat.mp3';

/** Fetches and decodes a sample once, caching the result (including a failure) for every later caller. */
const sampleCache = new Map<string, Promise<AudioBuffer | null>>();
function loadSample(ctx: BaseAudioContext, url: string): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
  let p = sampleCache.get(url);
  if (p) return p;
  p = fetch(url)
    .then((r) => r.arrayBuffer())
    .then((data) => ctx.decodeAudioData(data))
    .catch(() => null);
  sampleCache.set(url, p);
  return p;
}

/** Tempo of each track's beat, in beats per minute. Slow on purpose: these are for thinking, not dancing. */
export function tempoFor(track: MusicTrack): number {
  return track === 'signal' ? 84 : track === 'cold-room' ? 54 : 66;
}

/** D minor. Melody and arpeggio are drawn from this scale. */
const D_MINOR_PENTATONIC = [62, 65, 67, 69, 72, 74];

/** Chord loops, as roots and tones relative to D (MIDI 50). Each track rotates through its loop. */
const CHORDS: { root: number; tones: number[] }[] = [
  { root: 50, tones: [53, 57, 60, 64] }, // Dm7
  { root: 58, tones: [62, 65, 69, 72] }, // Bbmaj7
  { root: 55, tones: [58, 62, 65, 69] }, // Gm7
  { root: 57, tones: [61, 64, 67, 71] }, // A7sus-ish
];

export const LOOP_BARS = CHORDS.length * 4;

function chordFor(bar: number): { root: number; tones: number[] } {
  return CHORDS[Math.floor((bar % LOOP_BARS) / 4)];
}

/**
 * Notes for one bar of a track. `bar` counts from zero. The random source decides the details: which melody note, how
 * dense the arpeggio is, when a chirp happens. The same seed gives the same bar.
 */
export function trackEvents(track: MusicTrack, bar: number, rand: Rand): NoteEvent[] {
  const chord = chordFor(bar);
  const events: NoteEvent[] = [];

  if (track === 'night-shift') {
    // Pads change slowly: one chord tone is held per beat with long releases, and the voicing shifts every two bars.
    const shift = bar % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 12;
    for (const tone of chord.tones.slice(0, 3)) events.push({ beat: 0, kind: 'pad', note: tone + shift, length: 4, level: 0.5 + rand() * 0.15 });
    // Sub bass: one soft pulse on the root, sometimes doubled.
    events.push({ beat: 0, kind: 'sub', note: chord.root - 12, length: 2, level: 0.55 });
    if (rand() < 0.4) events.push({ beat: 2.5, kind: 'sub', note: chord.root - 12, length: 1, level: 0.3 });
    // Melody: sparse, far apart, from the pentatonic. Most bars have none.
    if (rand() < 0.45) {
      const note = D_MINOR_PENTATONIC[Math.floor(rand() * D_MINOR_PENTATONIC.length)];
      events.push({ beat: 1 + rand() * 2, kind: 'melody', note, length: 2 + rand() * 2, level: 0.28 + rand() * 0.1 });
    }
  }

  if (track === 'signal') {
    // A minimal pulse: an arpeggio note on about half of the eighth notes, with the chord's tones and occasional gaps.
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      if (rand() < 0.45) continue;
      const tone = chord.tones[Math.floor(rand() * chord.tones.length)] + (rand() < 0.2 ? 12 : 0);
      events.push({ beat: i * 0.5, kind: 'arp', note: tone, length: 0.4, level: 0.2 + rand() * 0.08 });
    }
    // Gentle filtered noise under the whole bar, breathing with the bar's phase.
    events.push({ beat: 0, kind: 'noise', length: 4, level: 0.12 });
    // Glitch textures on some bars: a short burst that stutters.
    if (rand() < 0.3) {
      const at = 1 + Math.floor(rand() * 3) * 0.5;
      events.push({ beat: at, kind: 'glitch', length: 0.25, level: 0.18 });
      if (rand() < 0.5) events.push({ beat: at + 0.25, kind: 'glitch', length: 0.1, level: 0.12 });
    }
  }

  if (track === 'cold-room') {
    // Deep drones that breathe. The upper voice moves between a fourth below and an octave below, so the loop never repeats exactly.
    const upper = rand() < 0.5;
    events.push({ beat: 0, kind: 'drone', note: chord.root - 24, length: 4, level: 0.5 });
    events.push({ beat: 0, kind: 'drone', note: chord.root - (upper ? 17 : 12), length: 4, level: 0.25 });
    // Data chirps: short rising sweeps from the pentatonic, on roughly a third of bars.
    if (rand() < 0.35) {
      const chirp = D_MINOR_PENTATONIC[Math.floor(rand() * D_MINOR_PENTATONIC.length)] + 12;
      events.push({ beat: 1 + rand() * 2.5, kind: 'chirp', note: chirp, length: 0.5, level: 0.15 });
    }
  }

  return events;
}

/**
 * The tension layer for one bar: a heartbeat-like pulse whose rate climbs with intensity, and ticks that thicken.
 * At zero there is no tension layer at all.
 */
export function tensionEvents(intensity: number, rand: Rand): NoteEvent[] {
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, intensity));
  if (t <= 0.02) return [];
  const events: NoteEvent[] = [];
  // Heartbeat: two pulses per bar at low intensity, four at full, so the pulse quickens as the trace climbs.
  const pulses = t < 0.5 ? 2 : 4;
  const level = 0.1 + t * 0.35;
  for (let i = 0; i < pulses; i++) events.push({ beat: (i * 4) / pulses, kind: 'heartbeat', length: 0.4, level });
  // Ticks: sparse at first, then steady as the trace rises.
  const ticks = Math.round(t * 8);
  for (let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
    if (rand() < 0.3) continue;
    events.push({ beat: (i * 4) / Math.max(1, ticks) + rand() * 0.2, kind: 'tick', length: 0.05, level: 0.04 + t * 0.07 });
  }
  return events;
}

/** How loud each layer is and where the filter sits, for an intensity from 0 (menus) to 1 (full trace). */
export function layerMix(intensity: number): { base: number; tension: number; cutoff: number } {
  const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, intensity));
  return {
    base: 0.85 - t * 0.15,
    tension: t * t * 0.9,
    // The filter opens with the trace, but never past a level that gets harsh.
    cutoff: 900 + t * 4100,
  };
}

/** Tempo of the tension layer's heartbeat, in beats per minute. */
export function heartbeatBpm(intensity: number): number {
  return Math.round(50 + Math.max(0, Math.min(1, intensity)) * 50);
}

export function midiToHz(note: number): number {
  return 440 * Math.pow(2, (note - 69) / 12);
}

/** Seeded random source. Tests use it to replay a bar exactly. */
export function seededRand(seed: number): Rand {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

/** Reverb tail: stereo noise with a slow exponential decay. Long and soft, for a room, not a hall. */
export function impulseResponse(ctx: BaseAudioContext, seconds = 4.2): AudioBuffer {
  const length = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * seconds);
  const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(2, length, ctx.sampleRate);
  for (let c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(c);
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / length, 3.2);
  }
  return buffer;
}

/** A player for one track. Built from the score; schedules a bar ahead so timing holds even when the page is busy. */
export class TrackPlayer {
  private readonly ctx: AudioContext;
  readonly out: GainNode;
  private readonly base: GainNode;
  private readonly tension: GainNode;
  private readonly filter: BiquadFilterNode;
  private readonly reverb: ConvolverNode;
  private readonly wet: GainNode;
  private readonly track: MusicTrack;
  private readonly rand: Rand;
  private timer: number | null = null;
  private nextBar = 0;
  private barStart = 0;
  private intensity = 0;
  private stopped = false;
  /** The real recording under Cyberpunk Drone's bed. Null for the procedural tracks, or until it loads. */
  private bed: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
  /** The real heartbeat hit, used by the tension layer's heartbeat events once loaded, on every track. */
  private heartbeatBuffer: AudioBuffer | null = null;

  constructor(ctx: AudioContext, destination: AudioNode, track: MusicTrack, reverbIr: AudioBuffer) {
    this.ctx = ctx;
    this.track = track;
    this.rand = Math.random;
    this.out = ctx.createGain();
    this.out.gain.value = 0;
    this.out.connect(destination);

    this.filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    this.filter.type = 'lowpass';
    this.filter.frequency.value = layerMix(0).cutoff;
    this.filter.Q.value = 0.5;
    this.filter.connect(this.out);

    this.base = ctx.createGain();
    this.tension = ctx.createGain();
    this.base.gain.value = layerMix(0).base;
    this.tension.gain.value = layerMix(0).tension;
    this.base.connect(this.filter);
    this.tension.connect(this.filter);

    this.reverb = ctx.createConvolver();
    this.reverb.buffer = reverbIr;
    this.wet = ctx.createGain();
    this.wet.gain.value = 0.38;
    this.reverb.connect(this.wet).connect(this.out);

    void loadSample(ctx, HEARTBEAT_SAMPLE_URL).then((buf) => {
      this.heartbeatBuffer = buf;
    });
    if (track === 'cyberpunk-drone') {
      void loadSample(ctx, CYBERPUNK_DRONE_URL).then((buf) => {
        if (buf) this.attachBed(buf);
      });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Starts the real recording looping straight into `out`, bypassing the lowpass filter: a mixed track should not
   * get dull at rest the way the procedural pads are designed to. The tension layer (heartbeat, ticks) still runs
   * through the filtered bus on top of it. A no-op if this player was already stopped.
   */
  private attachBed(buffer: AudioBuffer): void {
    if (this.stopped || this.bed) return;
    const src = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buffer;
    src.loop = true;
    src.connect(this.out);
    src.start(this.ctx.currentTime);
    this.bed = src;
  }

  get running(): boolean {
    return this.timer !== null;
  }

  start(): void {
    if (this.timer !== null) return;
    this.barStart = this.ctx.currentTime + 0.15;
    this.timer = window.setInterval(() => this.schedule(), 120);
    this.schedule();
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.timer !== null) window.clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
    this.stopped = true;
    try {
      this.bed?.stop();
    } catch {
      // Already stopped or never started: fine either way.
    }
    this.bed = null;
  }

  /** Fades this track in or out over a few seconds. */
  fadeTo(level: number, seconds: number): void {
    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
    this.out.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);
    this.out.gain.setValueAtTime(this.out.gain.value, now);
    this.out.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(level, now + seconds);
  }

  /** Crossfades the tension layer and the filter. 0 is a menu, 1 is a trace near the limit. */
  setIntensity(target: number): void {
    this.intensity = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, target));
    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
    const mix = layerMix(this.intensity);
    this.base.gain.setTargetAtTime(mix.base, now, 1.2);
    this.tension.gain.setTargetAtTime(mix.tension, now, 0.9);
    this.filter.frequency.setTargetAtTime(mix.cutoff, now, 1.2);
  }

  private beatSeconds(): number {
    return 60 / tempoFor(this.track);
  }

  private schedule(): void {
    const barSeconds = this.beatSeconds() * 4;
    while (this.barStart < this.ctx.currentTime + 0.5) {
      const events = [...trackEvents(this.track, this.nextBar, this.rand), ...tensionEvents(this.intensity, this.rand)];
      for (const e of events) this.play(e, this.barStart + e.beat * this.beatSeconds(), this.beatSeconds());
      this.barStart += barSeconds;
      this.nextBar++;
    }
  }

  private play(e: NoteEvent, when: number, beat: number): void {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const dur = Math.max(0.05, e.length * beat);
    switch (e.kind) {
      case 'pad':
      case 'drone':
      case 'melody':
      case 'sub':
      case 'arp':
      case 'chirp': {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const amp = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = e.kind === 'sub' || e.kind === 'drone' ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
        const hz = midiToHz(e.note ?? 60);
        if (e.kind === 'chirp') {
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz * 0.6, when);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(hz * 1.4, when + dur);
        } else {
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz, when);
        }
        const attack = e.kind === 'pad' || e.kind === 'drone' ? Math.min(2.5, dur * 0.5) : e.kind === 'melody' ? 0.25 : 0.02;
        const peak = e.level * (e.kind === 'arp' ? 0.12 : e.kind === 'sub' ? 0.5 : 0.09);
        amp.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, when);
        amp.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(peak, when + attack);
        amp.gain.setValueAtTime(peak, when + Math.max(attack, dur - 0.3));
        amp.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, when + dur + (e.kind === 'pad' || e.kind === 'drone' ? 1.5 : 0.4));
        osc.connect(amp);
        amp.connect(this.base);
        if (e.kind === 'melody' || e.kind === 'pad' || e.kind === 'chirp') amp.connect(this.reverb);
        osc.start(when);
        osc.stop(when + dur + 2);
        return;
      }
      case 'noise':
        return this.noise(when, dur, 0.04 * e.level, 900, this.base);
      case 'glitch':
        return this.noise(when, dur, 0.22 * e.level, 3200, this.reverb);
      case 'heartbeat': {
        if (this.heartbeatBuffer) {
          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
          src.buffer = this.heartbeatBuffer;
          const amp = ctx.createGain();
          amp.gain.value = e.level * 0.9;
          src.connect(amp).connect(this.tension);
          src.start(when);
          return;
        }
        // The real sample has not loaded yet (or failed): the procedural thump still carries the beat.
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const amp = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'sine';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(68, when);
        osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(42, when + 0.18);
        amp.gain.setValueAtTime(e.level * 0.7, when);
        amp.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, when + 0.3);
        osc.connect(amp).connect(this.tension);
        osc.start(when);
        osc.stop(when + 0.35);
        return;
      }
      case 'tick': {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const amp = ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.value = 2400;
        amp.gain.setValueAtTime(e.level * 0.25, when);
        amp.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, when + 0.04);
        osc.connect(amp).connect(this.tension);
        osc.start(when);
        osc.stop(when + 0.06);
        return;
      }
    }
  }

  private noise(when: number, dur: number, level: number, centre: number, into: AudioNode): void {
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const length = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur));
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, length, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buffer;
    const band = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    band.type = 'bandpass';
    band.frequency.value = centre;
    band.Q.value = 0.8;
    const amp = ctx.createGain();
    amp.gain.setValueAtTime(level, when);
    amp.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, when + dur);
    src.connect(band).connect(amp).connect(into);
    src.start(when);
    src.stop(when + dur + 0.05);
  }
}

/**
 * The soundtrack: one track at a time, with a reverb, a compressor on the whole mix, and crossfades when the track
 * changes or the intensity moves. Built after the first click.
 */
export class MusicPlayer {
  private readonly ctx: AudioContext;
  private readonly bus: GainNode;
  private readonly compressor: DynamicsCompressorNode;
  private current: TrackPlayer | null = null;
  private retiring: TrackPlayer | null = null;
  private retireTimer: number | null = null;
  private track: MusicTrack = 'night-shift';
  private intensity = 0;
  private readonly reverbIr: AudioBuffer;

  constructor(ctx: AudioContext, destination: AudioNode) {
    this.ctx = ctx;
    this.reverbIr = impulseResponse(ctx);
    this.compressor = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
    this.compressor.threshold.value = -20;
    this.compressor.knee.value = 12;
    this.compressor.ratio.value = 3;
    this.compressor.attack.value = 0.02;
    this.compressor.release.value = 0.3;
    this.bus = ctx.createGain();
    this.bus.gain.value = 1;
    this.bus.connect(this.compressor).connect(destination);
  }

  get running(): boolean {
    return this.current !== null;
  }

  /** Starts the chosen track. Calling it again while one plays changes the track with a crossfade. */
  start(track: MusicTrack = this.track): void {
    if (this.current) {
      this.setTrack(track);
      return;
    }
    this.track = track;
    this.current = this.build(track);
    this.current.start();
    this.current.fadeTo(1, 3);
    this.current.setIntensity(this.intensity);
  }

  setTrack(track: MusicTrack): void {
    if (track === this.track && this.current) return;
    this.track = track;
    if (!this.current) return;
    const old = this.current;
    old.fadeTo(0, 2.5);
    if (this.retiring) this.retiring.stop();
    this.retiring = old;
    this.retireTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      old.stop();
      if (this.retiring === old) this.retiring = null;
    }, 2800);
    this.current = this.build(track);
    this.current.start();
    this.current.fadeTo(1, 3);
    this.current.setIntensity(this.intensity);
  }

  /** Music level. Multiplies the whole soundtrack, which the caller sets from the music slider. */
  setLevel(level: number): void {
    this.bus.gain.setTargetAtTime(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, level)), this.ctx.currentTime, 0.2);
  }

  /** 0 on menus, rising with the trace in a mission. */
  setIntensity(target: number): void {
    this.intensity = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, target));
    this.current?.setIntensity(this.intensity);
    this.retiring?.setIntensity(this.intensity);
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.current?.stop();
    this.retiring?.stop();
    this.current = null;
    this.retiring = null;
    if (this.retireTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(this.retireTimer);
  }

  private build(track: MusicTrack): TrackPlayer {
    const player = new TrackPlayer(this.ctx, this.bus, track, this.reverbIr);
    player.out.gain.value = 0;
    return player;
  }
}
```

`src/lib/nickname.ts`:

```ts
// Nickname rules for the leaderboard: 3 to 16 characters from a safe set, and a basic profanity filter.
// Shared by the browser and the Edge Function, so both apply exactly the same rules. The filter is a modest list
// with common letter substitutions undone. It is a first pass, not a guarantee, and reports still go through the
// contact on the rig.

const NICK_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,16}$/;

/** Common words the filter blocks, as plain lowercase letters. */
const BLOCKED = ['fuck', 'shit', 'bitch', 'cunt', 'nigger', 'nigga', 'faggot', 'retard', 'kike', 'rapist', 'nazi', 'porn'];

const LEET: Record<string, string> = { '0': 'o', '1': 'i', '3': 'e', '4': 'a', '5': 's', '7': 't', '@': 'a', '$': 's', '!': 'i' };

/** Lowercase, undo the usual letter swaps, and drop anything that is not a letter. */
export function foldForFilter(text: string): string {
  return [...text.toLowerCase()]
    .map((ch) => LEET[ch] ?? ch)
    .join('')
    .replace(/[^a-z]/g, '');
}

export function containsProfanity(text: string): boolean {
  const folded = foldForFilter(text);
  return BLOCKED.some((word) => folded.includes(word));
}

/** Null when the nickname is fine, otherwise the reason it is not. */
export function nicknameProblem(nick: string): string | null {
  if (!NICK_PATTERN.test(nick)) return 'Use 3 to 16 letters, numbers, _ or -.';
  if (containsProfanity(nick)) return 'That nickname is not allowed.';
  return null;
}
```

`src/lib/origins.ts`:

```ts
// CORS origin matching for the Edge Functions. Pure, so it is tested here in Node and then bundled into the
// functions the same way the engine and the merge logic are (see supabase/functions/_shared/engine-entry.ts).
//
// A pattern may contain one `*`, which matches exactly one subdomain label: `https://*.itch.zone` matches
// `https://html-classic.itch.zone`, but not `https://a.b.itch.zone` (more than one label) and not
// `https://itch.zone.evil.com` or `https://evil.com/?x=itch.zone` (the match is anchored to the whole origin string,
// scheme included, so a pattern cannot be satisfied by a prefix, a suffix or an extra path).

const WILDCARD_LABEL = '[a-zA-Z0-9-]+';

function escapeRegExp(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
}

function patternToRegExp(pattern: string): RegExp {
  const parts = pattern.split('*').map(escapeRegExp);
  return new RegExp(`^${parts.join(WILDCARD_LABEL)}$`);
}

/** Splits a comma-separated ALLOWED_ORIGINS value into trimmed, non-empty patterns. */
export function parseAllowedOrigins(csv: string): string[] {
  return csv
    .split(',')
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

/** Whether `origin` (the browser's Origin header) matches one of the allowed patterns. */
export function originAllowed(origin: string | null, allowed: readonly string[]): boolean {
  if (!origin) return false;
  return allowed.some((pattern) => patternToRegExp(pattern).test(origin));
}

/**
 * CORS headers for one request. Only includes access-control-allow-origin (echoing the exact origin, never `*`) when
 * the origin matches the allow list, with Vary: Origin so caches never mix up responses for different origins.
 * When the origin is missing or not allowed, the headers are still valid to send — the browser just will not let the
 * page read the response, which is the rejection the caller wants.
 */
export function corsHeaders(origin: string | null, allowed: readonly string[]): Record<string, string> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    'access-control-allow-headers': 'content-type, apikey, authorization',
    'access-control-allow-methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
    vary: 'Origin',
  };
  if (originAllowed(origin, allowed)) headers['access-control-allow-origin'] = origin as string;
  return headers;
}
```

`src/lib/progress.ts`:

```ts
// Campaign-level summaries built from the save data: unlocks, rank, points and the share card.
// Everything here is per tier: each difficulty has its own progress, best scores and rank.

import { rankFor, type MissionResult, type Rank } from '../engine/scoring';
import { DIFFICULTY, TIERS } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import { MISSIONS } from '../missions';
import type { SaveData } from './save';

export function missionUnlocked(save: SaveData, tier: Tier, index: number): boolean {
  if (index === 0) return true;
  return Boolean(save.completed[tier][MISSIONS[index - 1].id]);
}

/** Whether a mission is unlocked on each difficulty, for the tier picker on its briefing. */
export function unlockedTiers(save: SaveData, index: number): Record<Tier, boolean> {
  return Object.fromEntries(TIERS.map((t) => [t, missionUnlocked(save, t, index)])) as Record<Tier, boolean>;
}

export interface CampaignSummary {
  tier: Tier;
  completed: number;
  total: number;
  hints: number;
  avgTrace: number;
  points: number;
  rank: Rank;
  grid: string[];
}

export function summarize(save: SaveData, tier: Tier = save.tier): CampaignSummary {
  const results = save.completed[tier];
  const ids = MISSIONS.map((m) => m.id);
  const done = ids.map((id) => results[id]).filter((r): r is MissionResult => Boolean(r));
  const hints = done.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.hints, 0);
  const avgTrace = done.length ? Math.round(done.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.trace, 0) / done.length) : 0;
  const points = done.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.points, 0);
  const grid = ids.map((id) => {
    const r = results[id];
    if (!r) return '⬛';
    if (r.hints === 0) return '🟩';
    if (r.hints <= 2) return '🟨';
    return '⬛';
  });
  return {
    tier,
    completed: done.length,
    total: MISSIONS.length,
    hints,
    avgTrace,
    points,
    rank: rankFor(results, ids),
    grid,
  };
}

/** Wordle-style text for the clipboard. */
export function buildShareCard(summary: CampaignSummary, endingLabel: string | null): string {
  const lines = [
    `BREACH [${DIFFICULTY[summary.tier].label}]: ${summary.completed}/${summary.total} missions, Rank ${summary.rank.name}`,
    `${summary.points} points · ${summary.hints} hint${summary.hints === 1 ? '' : 's'} · avg trace ${summary.avgTrace}%`,
    summary.grid.join(''),
  ];
  if (endingLabel) lines.push(`Ending: ${endingLabel}`);
  return lines.join('\n');
}

/** Distinct missions cleared on any difficulty. Used for theme unlocks, so every tier counts toward them. */
export function clearedOnAnyTier(save: SaveData): number {
  return MISSIONS.filter((m) => TIERS.some((t) => Boolean(save.completed[t][m.id]))).length;
}

/**
 * Which difficulty actually has progress, or null if none does. `save.tier` is just the device's current
 * selection, and a fresh device (or one freshly restored onto from a recovery key) defaults it independently
 * of what was actually completed elsewhere — see mergeSaves, which keeps the merge's completed/attempts data
 * but leaves `tier` as whatever the local side already had. Callers that adopt someone else's progress
 * (restore, and nothing else today) should set `tier` from this, so the menu reflects real progress.
 */
export function tierWithProgress(save: SaveData): Tier | null {
  const best = TIERS.map((t) => ({ t, completed: summarize(save, t).completed })).reduce((a, b) => (b.completed > a.completed ? b : a));
  return best.completed > 0 ? best.t : null;
}

```

`src/lib/realworld.ts`:

```ts
// Real-world debrief cards. After a job, a short classified-file card names the real attack the job copied,
// gives one real example, and says how defenders stop it. Examples are limited to incidents that are well documented;
// where a case is only a pattern, the card says so rather than inventing details.

import type { MissionDef } from '../engine/types';

export interface RealWorldCard {
  attack: string;
  example: string;
  defence: string;
}

const CAMPAIGN: Record<string, RealWorldCard> = {
  'm01-dead-drop': {
    attack: 'Shared account, shared password',
    example: 'Shared logins and passwords written on paper or in shared notes turn up in many incident reports, because one leaked note opens every door it names.',
    defence: 'One account per person. Store secrets in a password manager, never in a shared file. Rotate credentials when people leave or hand over.',
  },
  'm02-soft-target': {
    attack: 'Cracking a weak password hash',
    example: 'In 2012 LinkedIn lost about 6.5 million password hashes. They were unsalted SHA-1, and many were cracked within days.',
    defence: 'Hash passwords with a slow, salted algorithm such as bcrypt, scrypt or Argon2. Check new passwords against lists of breached passwords.',
  },
  'm03-lighthouse': {
    attack: 'Secrets hidden with encoding',
    example: 'In 2016 Uber had AWS credentials sitting in a private GitHub repository. Attackers used them to reach data on about 57 million people.',
    defence: 'Encoding is not secrecy. Keep secrets in a secrets manager, scan repositories for keys before they are pushed, and rotate anything that has been exposed.',
  },
  'm04-blue-lines': {
    attack: 'Secrets written into logs',
    example: 'In 2018 GitHub told users that some passwords had been stored in plaintext in an internal log. Anyone with log access could read them.',
    defence: 'Scrub secrets before they reach logs, limit who can read logs, and treat log access as sensitive.',
  },
  'm05-personal-files': {
    attack: 'Account recovery from public facts',
    example: 'In 2008 a political figure\'s web email was taken over by answering its password-reset questions from public information about her life.',
    defence: 'Use random answers that are not in any public profile, or skip knowledge questions. Prefer one-time codes from an app over security questions.',
  },
  'm06-pivot': {
    attack: 'Pivoting through a trusted vendor',
    example: 'The 2013 Target breach began with credentials belonging to an HVAC vendor. Attackers moved from that low-value account into the payment network.',
    defence: 'Segment networks so a vendor login cannot reach payments. Give vendors narrow access, monitor it, and remove it when work ends.',
  },
  'm07-ledger': {
    attack: 'SQL injection',
    example: 'The 2008 Heartland Payment Systems breach came through SQL injection, and about 130 million card numbers were stolen.',
    defence: 'Use parameterised queries, give the database account only the rights it needs, and never build SQL by joining user text into it.',
  },
  'm08-ghost-protocol': {
    attack: 'Access that outlived its purpose',
    example: 'In 2013 a contractor walked out of an agency with a large set of documents, because his access was far wider than his job needed.',
    defence: 'Grant the least access a job needs, review it regularly, and revoke it fully when someone leaves. Do not leave provisioning files lying around.',
  },
};

const DAILY: Record<string, RealWorldCard> = {
  'shared-note': CAMPAIGN['m01-dead-drop'],
  'encoded-secret': CAMPAIGN['m03-lighthouse'],
  'weak-hash': CAMPAIGN['m02-soft-target'],
};

/** The card for a finished job. Daily jobs use the idea they were built around. */
export function realWorldFor(mission: MissionDef): RealWorldCard | null {
  if (mission.realWorldKey) return DAILY[mission.realWorldKey] ?? null;
  return CAMPAIGN[mission.id] ?? null;
}

export const REAL_WORLD_IDS = [...Object.keys(CAMPAIGN), ...Object.keys(DAILY)];

```

`src/lib/recoverykey.ts`:

```ts
// Recovery keys and rate limiting, shared by the browser and the Edge Function (both run this exact code).
//
// A recovery key looks like GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419: four words from a list of 64, then four digits. That is
// about 37 bits. The server stores only a keyed hash of it, so a leaked table cannot be checked offline without the
// server's secret, and restore attempts are rate limited on top of that.

/** 64 short words. Chosen to be easy to read aloud and hard to mistype. */
export const KEY_WORDS: readonly string[] = [
  'AMBER', 'ANCHOR', 'ARROW', 'ASH', 'BASALT', 'BEACON', 'BISHOP', 'BLAZE', 'BRASS', 'CANYON',
  'CEDAR', 'CIPHER', 'CLOUD', 'COBALT', 'COMET', 'COPPER', 'CORVID', 'CRATER', 'DELTA', 'DRIFT',
  'EMBER', 'ENIGMA', 'FALCON', 'FERN', 'FJORD', 'FLINT', 'FORGE', 'FROST', 'GHOST', 'GLACIER',
  'GLASS', 'GRANITE', 'HARBOR', 'HAZEL', 'HELIX', 'HOLLOW', 'INDIGO', 'IRON', 'JADE', 'JUNIPER',
  'KESTREL', 'LANTERN', 'LATCH', 'LOTUS', 'MERIDIAN', 'MERCURY', 'MIRROR', 'MOSS', 'NEEDLE', 'NEON',
  'ONYX', 'ORBIT', 'OSPREY', 'PARADOX', 'PINE', 'PRISM', 'QUARTZ', 'RAVEN', 'RIVER', 'SABLE',
  'SIGNAL', 'SILVER', 'SLATE', 'STATIC', 'SUMMIT', 'TIDE', 'TUNDRA', 'VAULT', 'VELVET', 'WILLOW',
];

export const KEY_PATTERN = /^[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-\d{4}$/;

/** A key from a random source. Pass a seeded source in tests; the browser passes crypto. */
export function generateRecoveryKey(random: () => number): string {
  const pick = () => KEY_WORDS[Math.floor(random() * KEY_WORDS.length)];
  const digits = String(Math.floor(random() * 10000)).padStart(4, '0');
  return [pick(), pick(), pick(), pick(), digits].join('-');
}

/**
 * Turns what a person typed into the canonical form: upper case, single hyphens, no stray spaces.
 * Spaces and hyphens both work as separators, so "ghost amber 7419 falcon" matches.
 */
export function normalizeRecoveryKey(input: string): string {
  return input
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/[\s_]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/-+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-|-$/g, '');
}

export function isWellFormedKey(input: string): boolean {
  return KEY_PATTERN.test(normalizeRecoveryKey(input));
}

export interface Bucket {
  windowStart: number;
  hits: number;
}

/**
 * Fixed-window rate limit. Returns whether this attempt may proceed, and the bucket to store afterwards.
 * A new window starts after windowMs. Attempts are counted even when they are refused, so hammering does not reset it.
 */
export function admit(bucket: Bucket | null, now: number, opts: { windowMs: number; max: number }): { allowed: boolean; next: Bucket } {
  const fresh = !bucket || now - bucket.windowStart >= opts.windowMs;
  const base: Bucket = fresh ? { windowStart: now, hits: 0 } : bucket;
  const next: Bucket = { windowStart: base.windowStart, hits: base.hits + 1 };
  return { allowed: next.hits <= opts.max, next };
}

/** Restore attempts: ten per bucket per hour. */
export const RESTORE_LIMIT = { windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, max: 10 };

```

`src/lib/save.ts`:

```ts
// Save system (version 2). One localStorage key. Every access is wrapped in try/catch, because storage can be
// missing, full or blocked. The game still works without it.
// Progress and best scores are kept separately per difficulty tier.

import type { MissionResult } from '../engine/scoring';
import { isTier } from '../engine/difficulty';
import type { Tier } from '../engine/types';
import type { ThemeId } from './themes';
import type { AttemptRecord } from './history';

export type MusicTrack = 'cyberpunk-drone' | 'night-shift' | 'signal' | 'cold-room';

export type TypingSpeed = 'fast' | 'normal' | 'instant';
/** 'system' follows the OS "reduce motion" setting. */
export type MotionSetting = 'system' | 'reduce' | 'full';

export interface SaveData {
  version: 2;
  tier: Tier;
  completed: Record<Tier, Record<string, MissionResult>>;
  attempts: Record<Tier, Record<string, number>>;
  theme: ThemeId;
  crt: boolean;
  typing: TypingSpeed;
  sfx: boolean;
  music: boolean;
  /** 0 to 1. Scales everything. */
  volume: number;
  motion: MotionSetting;
  /** Unlocked achievement ids. Stored here, never sent anywhere. */
  achievements: string[];
  /** Lifetime counters the achievements read. */
  counters: { sudoTries: number };
  /** Daily challenge results by UTC date, then tier. One attempt per day per tier. */
  dailies: Record<string, Partial<Record<Tier, MissionResult>>>;
  /** Ops log: every attempt on this device, newest first. */
  history: AttemptRecord[];
  /** Soundtrack. */
  track: MusicTrack;
  /** Music level, separate from effects. 0 to 1. */
  musicVolume: number;
  /** Mechanical keyboard sound on typing. Its own toggle and level, separate from Effects. */
  keySounds: boolean;
  /** 0 to 1. */
  keySoundsVolume: number;
  /** Tiny per-key vibration on devices that support it. Off by default. */
  keyHaptics: boolean;
  /** Cloud sync: the recovery key shown at registration (kept on this device only) and the sync token. */
  recoveryKey?: string;
  syncToken?: string;
  /** Set when the player chose to skip registration on the register screen. */
  registerSkipped?: boolean;
  /** Anonymous id for the leaderboard. Created on first use, never tied to a name or account. */
  playerId?: string;
  /** Nickname for the leaderboard, 3 to 16 characters. */
  nickname?: string;
}

export const SAVE_KEY = 'breach.save.v1';

function emptyByTier<T>(): Record<Tier, T> {
  return { easy: {} as T, medium: {} as T, hard: {} as T };
}

export const DEFAULT_SAVE: SaveData = {
  version: 2,
  tier: 'medium',
  completed: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
  attempts: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
  theme: 'green',
  crt: true,
  typing: 'normal',
  sfx: true,
  music: true,
  volume: 0.8,
  motion: 'system',
  achievements: [],
  counters: { sudoTries: 0 },
  dailies: {},
  history: [],
  track: 'cyberpunk-drone',
  musicVolume: 0.4,
  keySounds: true,
  keySoundsVolume: 0.5,
  keyHaptics: false,
};

function freshDefaults(): SaveData {
  return {
    ...DEFAULT_SAVE,
    completed: emptyByTier<Record<string, MissionResult>>(),
    attempts: emptyByTier<Record<string, number>>(),
  };
}

function storage(): Storage | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.localStorage) return null;
    return window.localStorage;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Version 1 kept one flat progress map and a single mute flag. Those progress records become Medium progress. */
function migrateV1(parsed: Record<string, unknown>): SaveData {
  const base = freshDefaults();
  const legacyCompleted = (parsed.completed ?? {}) as Record<string, MissionResult>;
  const legacyAttempts = (parsed.attempts ?? {}) as Record<string, number>;
  const muted = Boolean(parsed.muted);
  return {
    ...base,
    completed: {
      ...base.completed,
      medium: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(legacyCompleted).map(([id, r]) => [id, { ...r, tier: 'medium' as const, points: r.score }])),
    },
    attempts: { ...base.attempts, medium: legacyAttempts },
    theme: (parsed.theme as ThemeId) ?? base.theme,
    crt: typeof parsed.crt === 'boolean' ? parsed.crt : base.crt,
    typing: (parsed.typing as TypingSpeed) ?? base.typing,
    sfx: !muted,
  };
}

export function loadSave(): SaveData {
  try {
    const raw = storage()?.getItem(SAVE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return freshDefaults();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (parsed.version === 1) return migrateV1(parsed);
    if (parsed.version !== 2) return freshDefaults();
    const base = freshDefaults();
    const data = { ...base, ...parsed } as SaveData;
    return {
      ...data,
      tier: isTier(data.tier) ? data.tier : base.tier,
      completed: { ...base.completed, ...data.completed },
      attempts: { ...base.attempts, ...data.attempts },
    };
  } catch {
    return freshDefaults();
  }
}

export function persistSave(data: SaveData): void {
  try {
    storage()?.setItem(SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  } catch {
    // Storage is unavailable or full. Progress lasts until the tab closes.
  }
}

export function clearSave(): void {
  try {
    storage()?.removeItem(SAVE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Nothing to clear.
  }
}

/** Whether reduced motion applies, given the saved setting and the OS preference. */
export function effectiveReduceMotion(setting: MotionSetting, systemPrefers: boolean): boolean {
  if (setting === 'reduce') return true;
  if (setting === 'full') return false;
  return systemPrefers;
}

```

`src/lib/share.ts`:

```ts
// Share cards: the text you copy, and the 1200x630 PNG drawn on a canvas. Pure text is tested directly; drawing needs a browser.

import { SITE_URL } from './site';

export interface ShareFacts {
  /** "JOB 03 · LIGHTHOUSE", "CAMPAIGN", or "DAILY 42". */
  label: string;
  tier: string;
  rank: string;
  score: number;
  /** Days in a row, for dailies. Omitted elsewhere. */
  streak?: number;
  /** Extra line under the score, such as the ending or the completed count. */
  detail?: string;
}

/** The text that goes on the clipboard, and in the native share sheet. */
export function shareText(f: ShareFacts): string {
  const lines = [`BREACH ${f.label} [${f.tier.toUpperCase()}]: ${f.rank.toUpperCase()}, ${f.score} points`];
  if (f.streak !== undefined) lines.push(`Streak: ${f.streak} day${f.streak === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
  if (f.detail) lines.push(f.detail);
  lines.push(SITE_URL);
  return lines.join('\n');
}

/** What a daily's emoji row and caption need. Deliberately thin: nothing here could spoil the job's answer. */
export interface DailyStats {
  dailyNum: number;
  tier: string;
  seconds: number;
  trace: number;
  hintsUsed: number;
}

/** m:ss, no leading zero on minutes (unlike the in-game clock, which pads to fit a header). */
function shortClock(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const m = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
  const s = totalSeconds % 60;
  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * One emoji per stage of the job: the mission's three hints (green if never needed, yellow at the one you used last,
 * black once you are past it) plus a final square for how hot your trace ran. Wordle-style, and never the answer.
 */
export function shareEmoji(hintsUsed: number, trace: number): string {
  const squares: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    if (i >= hintsUsed) squares.push('🟩');
    else if (i === hintsUsed - 1) squares.push('🟨');
    else squares.push('⬛');
  }
  squares.push(trace < 34 ? '🟩' : trace < 67 ? '🟨' : '⬛');
  return squares.join('');
}

/** The copyable, Wordle-style result for a daily. This is the default share; the image card is secondary. */
export function dailyShareText(s: DailyStats): string {
  return [
    `BREACH Daily #${s.dailyNum} ${s.tier}`,
    shareEmoji(s.hintsUsed, s.trace),
    `trace ${s.trace}% · ${shortClock(s.seconds)} · ${s.hintsUsed} hint${s.hintsUsed === 1 ? '' : 's'}`,
    SITE_URL.replace('https://', ''),
  ].join('\n');
}

/** Draws the card onto a 2D canvas context. Green on black, the same look as the game. */
export function drawShareCard(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, f: ShareFacts, width = 1200, height = 630): void {
  const GREEN = '#33ff66';
  const DIM = '#1b7a3a';
  const EDGE = '#14412a';
  const mono = "Consolas, 'Courier New', monospace";

  const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(width / 2, height * 0.45, 40, width / 2, height / 2, width * 0.75);
  bg.addColorStop(0, '#06180c');
  bg.addColorStop(1, '#010402');
  ctx.fillStyle = bg;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  ctx.strokeStyle = EDGE;
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  ctx.strokeRect(48, 48, width - 96, height - 96);

  // Scanlines, the same as the CRT overlay.
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.28)';
  for (let y = 2; y < height; y += 4) ctx.fillRect(0, y, width, 1);

  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.shadowColor = GREEN;
  ctx.shadowBlur = 24;
  ctx.fillStyle = GREEN;
  ctx.font = `700 120px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillText('BREACH', 96, 220);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

  ctx.font = `400 34px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillStyle = GREEN;
  ctx.fillText(f.label, 100, 290);

  ctx.font = `700 150px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillStyle = GREEN;
  ctx.fillText(String(f.score), 100, 440);
  ctx.font = `400 30px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillStyle = DIM;
  ctx.fillText('POINTS', 100 + ctx.measureText(String(f.score)).width + 24, 440);

  ctx.font = `400 34px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillStyle = GREEN;
  ctx.fillText(`RANK ${f.rank.toUpperCase()}   ·   ${f.tier.toUpperCase()}`, 100, 500);
  if (f.streak !== undefined) {
    ctx.fillText(`STREAK ${f.streak} DAY${f.streak === 1 ? '' : 'S'}`, 100, 548);
  } else if (f.detail) {
    ctx.font = `400 28px ${mono}`;
    ctx.fillText(f.detail.slice(0, 60), 100, 548);
  }

  ctx.font = `400 26px ${mono}`;
  ctx.fillStyle = DIM;
  ctx.fillText(SITE_URL.replace('https://', ''), 100, 580);
}

/** Renders the card to a PNG blob. Browser only. */
export function renderShareCard(f: ShareFacts): Promise<Blob> {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = 1200;
  canvas.height = 630;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return Promise.reject(new Error('no canvas'));
  drawShareCard(ctx, f);
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    canvas.toBlob((blob) => (blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error('could not render'))), 'image/png');
  });
}
```

```ts
// Colour themes. The palettes live in globals.css as CSS variables keyed by data-theme.

export type ThemeId = 'green' | 'amber' | 'cyan';

export interface ThemeInfo {
  id: ThemeId;
  label: string;
  /** Missions that must be completed before the theme unlocks. */
  unlockAfter: number;
}

export const THEMES: ThemeInfo[] = [
  { id: 'green', label: 'Phosphor Green', unlockAfter: 0 },
  { id: 'amber', label: 'Amber Monitor', unlockAfter: 2 },
  { id: 'cyan', label: 'Cyan Ice', unlockAfter: 5 },
];

export function isThemeUnlocked(theme: ThemeInfo, completedCount: number): boolean {
  return completedCount >= theme.unlockAfter;
}
```

`src/lib/typeout.ts`:

```ts
// The terminal's typing effect: output is revealed a few characters at a time. Pure and framework-free,
// so the engine of the effect (and the "finish early" behaviour that keeps keystrokes from being lost
// while it runs) is tested without rendering anything.

import type { Line } from '../engine/types';
import type { TypingSpeed } from './save';

export interface Entry {
  line: Line;
  shown: number;
}

export function toEntries(lines: Line[], speed: TypingSpeed): Entry[] {
  return lines.map((line) => ({ line, shown: speed === 'instant' ? line.text.length : 0 }));
}

/** Reveals up to `step` characters of the first unfinished line. Returns the same array when nothing changes. */
export function advance(entries: Entry[], step: number): Entry[] {
  const idx = entries.findIndex((e) => e.shown < e.line.text.length);
  if (idx < 0) return entries;
  const backlog = entries.reduce((n, e) => n + (e.line.text.length - e.shown), 0);
  const amount = backlog > 400 ? step * 8 : step;
  const next = entries.slice();
  const e = entries[idx];
  next[idx] = { ...e, shown: Math.min(e.line.text.length, e.shown + amount) };
  return next;
}

/** Jumps every line straight to fully shown. Used to finish the animation instantly, so a keystroke is never lost. */
export function completeAll(entries: Entry[]): Entry[] {
  if (entries.every((e) => e.shown >= e.line.text.length)) return entries;
  return entries.map((e) => ({ ...e, shown: e.line.text.length }));
}

```

`src/lib/variation.ts`:

```ts
// Per-run flag variation. A leaderboard run's flag text changes with the server-issued token's seed, so a
// command log recorded on one run (including its final `submit <flag>`) fails if resubmitted against another.
// Pure and generic: it works for every mission whose complete flag is embedded verbatim, in plaintext, somewhere
// the player reads it directly (true for 6 of 8 campaign missions, plus every daily job). The two exceptions
// are marked on the mission itself with `varyFlag: false` — see m03-lighthouse and m04-blue-lines.

import { seededRandom } from './daily';
import type { MissionDef } from '../engine/types';

const FLAG_SUFFIXES = [
  'k7', 'q2', 'r9', 'z4', 'm1', 'x8', 't3', 'v6', 'n5', 'w0', 'p2', 'j9', 'h6', 'c1', 'f8', 'y3',
] as const;

function pick<T>(rand: () => number, items: readonly T[]): T {
  return items[Math.floor(rand() * items.length)];
}

/** Replaces every exact occurrence of `from` with `to` in every string leaf. Never mutates `value`. */
function substituteDeep<T>(value: T, from: string, to: string): T {
  if (typeof value === 'string') return value.split(from).join(to) as unknown as T;
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((v) => substituteDeep(v, from, to)) as unknown as T;
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(value)) out[k] = substituteDeep(v, from, to);
    return out as T;
  }
  return value;
}

/**
 * Varies a mission's flag deterministically from a seed. Returns a new MissionDef; the input is never changed
 * (mission files are module-level singletons shared across every attempt and test in the process).
 */
export function applyFlagVariation(mission: MissionDef, seed: number): MissionDef {
  if (mission.varyFlag === false) return mission;
  const rand = seededRandom(seed);
  const suffix = pick(rand, FLAG_SUFFIXES);
  const variedFlag = mission.flag.replace(/\}$/, `_${suffix}}`);
  return substituteDeep(mission, mission.flag, variedFlag);
}
```

`src/missions/index.ts`:

```ts
// Campaign order. To add a mission: create a file in this folder that default-exports a MissionDef,
// import it here, and append it to MISSIONS. Then add it to the tests (they run every entry automatically).

import type { MissionDef } from '../engine/types';
import m01 from './m01-dead-drop';
import m02 from './m02-soft-target';
import m03 from './m03-lighthouse';
import m04 from './m04-blue-lines';
import m05 from './m05-personal-files';
import m06 from './m06-pivot';
import m07 from './m07-ledger';
import m08 from './m08-ghost-protocol';

export const MISSIONS: MissionDef[] = [m01, m02, m03, m04, m05, m06, m07, m08];

export function getMission(id: string): MissionDef | undefined {
  return MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === id);
}
```
