```js
// src/lib/daily.ts
function utcDate(now = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date()) {
  return now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
function shiftDate(date, days) {
  const d = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
  d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + days);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}
function dailyNumber(date) {
  const start = Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1);
  const day = Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
  return Math.floor((day - start) / 864e5) + 1;
}
function isHardFriday(date) {
  return (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`)).getUTCDay() === 5;
}
function hashSeed(text) {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    h ^= text.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0;
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}
function seededRandom(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    a = a + 1831565813 >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
var HOSTS = ["ledger-02", "relay-7", "ops-box", "hangar-db", "sat-uplink", "cafe-wifi-3", "meridian-web", "quarry-nas"];
var PEOPLE = ["maya", "sol", "tobi", "vera", "kenji", "ines", "omar", "lena", "bram", "noor"];
var WORDS = ["copper", "lantern", "harbor", "velvet", "orbit", "cedar", "falcon", "quartz", "juniper", "meadow"];
var FLAG_WORDS = ["tin", "salt", "ember", "glass", "static", "lamp", "river", "signal", "drift", "anchor"];
var DECOYS = {
  "/srv/share/old-menu.txt": "Canteen menu, week 41: soup, bread, the usual. No substitutions on Fridays.",
  "/var/log/cron.log": "CRON[2231]: nightly backup finished in 4m12s\nCRON[2240]: rotated 3 logs",
  "/srv/share/welcome.txt": "Welcome to the shared drive. Keep it tidy. Delete your old drafts."
};
function pick(rand, items) {
  return items[Math.floor(rand() * items.length)];
}
function hex(rand, length) {
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) out += Math.floor(rand() * 16).toString(16);
  return out;
}
function targetIp(rand) {
  let second = 0;
  do {
    second = 1 + Math.floor(rand() * 200);
  } while (second === 66);
  return `10.${second}.${Math.floor(rand() * 250) + 1}.${Math.floor(rand() * 200) + 20}`;
}
var REAL_WORLD_KEYS = ["shared-note", "encoded-secret", "weak-hash"];
function dailyMission(date) {
  const hardFriday = isHardFriday(date);
  const rand = seededRandom(hashSeed(`breach-daily:${date}`));
  const ip = targetIp(rand);
  const host = pick(rand, HOSTS);
  const user = pick(rand, PEOPLE);
  const pw = `${pick(rand, WORDS)}${10 + Math.floor(rand() * 89)}`;
  const flag = `BREACH{${pick(rand, FLAG_WORDS)}_${pick(rand, FLAG_WORDS)}_${Math.floor(rand() * 900 + 100)}}`;
  const template = Math.floor(rand() * 3);
  const home = `/home/${user}`;
  const flagPath = `${home}/flag.txt`;
  const files = {};
  const decoyPaths = hardFriday ? Object.keys(DECOYS) : Object.keys(DECOYS).filter(() => rand() < 0.6);
  for (const p of decoyPaths) files[p] = { content: DECOYS[p] };
  let solution;
  let kaliSolution;
  let hashes = [];
  let concept;
  let mail = [];
  if (template === 0) {
    concept = "credentials left in a shared note";
    files["/srv/share/handover.txt"] = { content: `Handover: ${user} is on call. Login is ${user}, password ${pw}. Rotate it when the audit ends.` };
    kaliSolution = ["nmap", `ssh ${ip}`, "ls /srv/share", "cat /srv/share/handover.txt", `su ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ["scan", `connect ${ip}`, "ls /srv/share", "cat /srv/share/handover.txt", `login ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
  } else if (template === 1) {
    concept = "a password hidden behind base64";
    const b64 = toBase64(pw);
    files["/etc/app/settings.env"] = { content: `APP_ENV=prod
DB_USER=${user}
DB_PASS_B64=${b64}
LOG_LEVEL=warn` };
    kaliSolution = ["nmap", `ssh ${ip}`, "cat /etc/app/settings.env", `echo ${b64} | base64 -d`, `su ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ["scan", `connect ${ip}`, "cat /etc/app/settings.env", `decode base64 ${b64}`, `login ${user}`, pw, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
  } else {
    concept = "a weak hash of a common password";
    const plain = pick(rand, WORDLIST);
    const hash = hex(rand, 32);
    hashes = [{ hash, plaintext: plain, algo: "md5" }];
    files["/opt/auth/users.db"] = { content: `${user}:${hash}:admin
guest:${hex(rand, 32)}:user` };
    kaliSolution = ["nmap", `ssh ${ip}`, "cat /opt/auth/users.db", `john ${hash}`, `su ${user}`, plain, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    solution = ["scan", `connect ${ip}`, "cat /opt/auth/users.db", `crack ${hash}`, `login ${user}`, plain, `cat ${flagPath}`, `submit ${flag}`];
    mail = [];
  }
  const accountPassword = template === 2 ? hashes[0].plaintext : pw;
  const target = {
    id: "target",
    hostname: host,
    ip,
    neighbors: [],
    defaultUser: "guest",
    users: [
      { name: "guest", password: "", home: "/home/guest" },
      { name: user, password: accountPassword, home }
    ],
    ports: [
      { port: 22, service: "ssh", banner: "OpenSSH 8.9p1" },
      { port: 443, service: "https", banner: "nginx 1.18.0" }
    ],
    files: {
      ...files,
      [flagPath]: { owner: user, private: true, content: flag },
      "/home/guest/readme.txt": { content: "Guest access. Look around, touch nothing." }
    },
    dirs: { [home]: { owner: user, private: true } }
  };
  const rig = makeRig([ip]);
  const dayNo = dailyNumber(date);
  return {
    id: `daily-${date}`,
    number: dayNo,
    codename: hardFriday ? `DAILY ${dayNo} :: HARD FRIDAY` : `DAILY ${dayNo}`,
    title: `${host}: ${concept}`,
    client: "OPEN BOARD",
    briefing: [
      `Today's job is open to everyone, on the same network, the same day. Target: ${host} (${ip}).`,
      `Find the account, get in, read the flag at ${flagPath}.`,
      ...hardFriday ? ["Hard Friday: trace runs fast and hints are off, on every difficulty."] : []
    ],
    debrief: [
      `The weak point was ${concept}. It is one of the oldest mistakes in the book, and it still shows up in the field.`
    ],
    start: { machine: "rig", cwd: "/home/ghost" },
    machines: [rig, target],
    hashes,
    inbox: mail,
    flag,
    hints: [
      `Start with what the box shares. Look for notes, config files and anything a team left lying about.`,
      `The account is ${user}. Find out how its password is stored.`,
      `${concept[0].toUpperCase()}${concept.slice(1)} is the weak point. Use the matching decode or cracking tool, then su ${user}.`
    ],
    solution,
    kaliSolution,
    realWorldKey: REAL_WORLD_KEYS[template],
    hardFriday
  };
}

// src/lib/variation.ts
var FLAG_SUFFIXES = [
  "k7",
  "q2",
  "r9",
  "z4",
  "m1",
  "x8",
  "t3",
  "v6",
  "n5",
  "w0",
  "p2",
  "j9",
  "h6",
  "c1",
  "f8",
  "y3"
];
function pick2(rand, items) {
  return items[Math.floor(rand() * items.length)];
}
function substituteDeep(value, from, to) {
  if (typeof value === "string") return value.split(from).join(to);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((v) => substituteDeep(v, from, to));
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
    const out = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(value)) out[k] = substituteDeep(v, from, to);
    return out;
  }
  return value;
}
function applyFlagVariation(mission9, seed) {
  if (mission9.varyFlag === false) return mission9;
  const rand = seededRandom(seed);
  const suffix = pick2(rand, FLAG_SUFFIXES);
  const variedFlag = mission9.flag.replace(/\}$/, `_${suffix}}`);
  return substituteDeep(mission9, mission9.flag, variedFlag);
}

// src/lib/nickname.ts
var NICK_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{3,16}$/;
var BLOCKED = ["fuck", "shit", "bitch", "cunt", "nigger", "nigga", "faggot", "retard", "kike", "rapist", "nazi", "porn"];
var LEET = { "0": "o", "1": "i", "3": "e", "4": "a", "5": "s", "7": "t", "@": "a", "$": "s", "!": "i" };
function foldForFilter(text) {
  return [...text.toLowerCase()].map((ch) => LEET[ch] ?? ch).join("").replace(/[^a-z]/g, "");
}
function containsProfanity(text) {
  const folded = foldForFilter(text);
  return BLOCKED.some((word) => folded.includes(word));
}
function nicknameProblem(nick) {
  if (!NICK_PATTERN.test(nick)) return "Use 3 to 16 letters, numbers, _ or -.";
  if (containsProfanity(nick)) return "That nickname is not allowed.";
  return null;
}

  "WILLOW"

var KEY_PATTERN = /^[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-[A-Z]+-\d{4}$/;
function generateRecoveryKey(random) {
  const pick3 = () => KEY_WORDS[Math.floor(random() * KEY_WORDS.length)];
  const digits = String(Math.floor(random() * 1e4)).padStart(4, "0");
  return [pick3(), pick3(), pick3(), pick3(), digits].join("-");
}
function normalizeRecoveryKey(input) {
  return input.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\s_]+/g, "-").replace(/-+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");
}
function isWellFormedKey(input) {
  return KEY_PATTERN.test(normalizeRecoveryKey(input));
}
function admit(bucket, now, opts) {
  const fresh = !bucket || now - bucket.windowStart >= opts.windowMs;
  const base = fresh ? { windowStart: now, hits: 0 } : bucket;
  const next = { windowStart: base.windowStart, hits: base.hits + 1 };
  return { allowed: next.hits <= opts.max, next };
}
var RESTORE_LIMIT = { windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1e3, max: 10 };

// src/lib/history.ts
var HISTORY_LIMIT = 200;
function mergeHistory(a, b) {
  const byId = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const r of [...a, ...b]) byId.set(r.id, r);
  return [...byId.values()].sort((x, y) => x.at < y.at ? 1 : x.at > y.at ? -1 : 0).slice(0, HISTORY_LIMIT);
}

// src/lib/merge.ts
var TIERS2 = ["easy", "medium", "hard"];
function betterResult(a, b) {
  if (!a) return b;
  if (!b) return a;
  return b.points > a.points ? b : a;
}
function mergeCompleted(a, b) {
  const out = { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} };
  for (const t of TIERS2) {
    const ids = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...Object.keys(a[t]), ...Object.keys(b[t])]);
    for (const id of ids) {
      const best = betterResult(a[t][id], b[t][id]);
      if (best) out[t][id] = best;
    }
  }
  return out;
}
function mergeAttempts(a, b) {
  const out = { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} };
  for (const t of TIERS2) {
    for (const id of /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...Object.keys(a[t]), ...Object.keys(b[t])])) {
      out[t][id] = Math.max(a[t][id] ?? 0, b[t][id] ?? 0);
    }
  }
  return out;
}
function mergeDailies(a, b) {
  const out = {};
  for (const date of /* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)])) {
    const row = {};
    for (const t of TIERS2) {
      const best = betterResult(a[date]?.[t], b[date]?.[t]);
      if (best) row[t] = best;
    }
    out[date] = row;
  }
  return out;
}
function mergeSaves(local, remote) {
  return {
    ...local,
    completed: mergeCompleted(local.completed, remote.completed),
    attempts: mergeAttempts(local.attempts, remote.attempts),
    achievements: [.../* @__PURE__ */ new Set([...local.achievements, ...remote.achievements])],
    history: mergeHistory(local.history, remote.history).slice(0, HISTORY_LIMIT),
    dailies: mergeDailies(local.dailies, remote.dailies),
    counters: { sudoTries: Math.max(local.counters.sudoTries, remote.counters.sudoTries) },
    nickname: local.nickname ?? remote.nickname
  };
}

// src/lib/origins.ts
var WILDCARD_LABEL = "[a-zA-Z0-9-]+";
function escapeRegExp2(text) {
  return text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}
function patternToRegExp(pattern) {
  const parts = pattern.split("*").map(escapeRegExp2);
  return new RegExp(`^${parts.join(WILDCARD_LABEL)}$`);
}
function parseAllowedOrigins(csv) {
  return csv.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}
function originAllowed(origin, allowed) {
  if (!origin) return false;
  return allowed.some((pattern) => patternToRegExp(pattern).test(origin));
}
function corsHeaders(origin, allowed) {
  const headers = {
    "access-control-allow-headers": "content-type, apikey, authorization",
    "access-control-allow-methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
    vary: "Origin"
  };
  if (originAllowed(origin, allowed)) headers["access-control-allow-origin"] = origin;
  return headers;
}
export {
  MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE,
  MISSIONS,
  RESTORE_LIMIT,
  RUN_LIMITS,
  SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD,
  TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS,
  admit,
  applyFlagVariation,
  checkToken,
  corsHeaders,
  dailyMission,
  generateRecoveryKey,
  isWellFormedKey,
  mergeSaves,
  nicknameProblem,
  normalizeRecoveryKey,
  parseAllowedOrigins,
  percentileFor,
  shiftDate,
  suspicionFor,
  utcDate,
  validateRun
};
```

`supabase/functions/account/index.ts`:

```ts
// account: registration, restore by recovery key, cloud sync and rename. The only code that writes accounts or saves.
// Deno runtime. Deploy with: npx supabase functions deploy account --no-verify-jwt
//
// Recovery keys are stored as a keyed hash (HMAC with a server-side pepper). Sync tokens are stored as a SHA-256 hash.
// Restore and sync are rate limited through auth_attempts, and every save is checked for size before it is stored.

import { admit, corsHeaders, generateRecoveryKey, isWellFormedKey, mergeSaves, normalizeRecoveryKey, nicknameProblem, parseAllowedOrigins, RESTORE_LIMIT } from '../_shared/engine.js';

// Comma-separated list of exact origins or single-wildcard-subdomain patterns, e.g. 'https://a.test,https://*.b.test'.
const ALLOWED_ORIGINS = parseAllowedOrigins(Deno.env.get('ALLOWED_ORIGINS') ?? '');
const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '';
// The server-side key's env var name has changed across Supabase projects. Check whichever of these is actually set.
const SECRET_KEY_SOURCE = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY') ? 'SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY' : Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ? 'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY' : null;
const SECRET_KEY = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY') ?? Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? '';
const PEPPER = Deno.env.get('RECOVERY_PEPPER') ?? '';

// Logged once at boot. Never logs a secret value, only whether each one is present and where the key came from.
console.log(`account: boot. SUPABASE_URL=${SUPABASE_URL ? 'set' : 'MISSING'} secretKey=${SECRET_KEY_SOURCE ?? 'MISSING (checked SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)'} RECOVERY_PEPPER=${PEPPER ? 'set' : 'MISSING'}`);

const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 2_000_000;
const SYNC_LIMIT = { windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, max: 240 };
const GLOBAL_RESTORE_LIMIT = { windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, max: 500 };
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

const enc = new TextEncoder();

function hex(buf: ArrayBuffer): string {
  return [...new Uint8Array(buf)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

async function sha256(text: string): Promise<string> {
  return hex(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', enc.encode(text)));
}

async function hmac(text: string): Promise<string> {
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', enc.encode(PEPPER), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']);
  return hex(await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, enc.encode(text)));
}

function newSyncToken(): string {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(32);
  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  return hex(bytes.buffer);
}

async function rest(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
  return fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      apikey: SECRET_KEY,
      authorization: `Bearer ${SECRET_KEY}`,
      'content-type': 'application/json',
      prefer: 'return=representation',
      ...(init.headers ?? {}),
    },
  });
}

/** Logs the real reason a write failed (PostgREST's own error body), so the generic reply to the caller still leaves a trail. */
async function logFailedWrite(label: string, res: Response): Promise<void> {
  const text = await res.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');
  console.error(`account: ${label} failed: ${res.status} ${text}`);
}

/** Inserts and deletes one throwaway row, proving the database can still write. Never touches accounts or saves. */
async function health(): Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }> {
  const created = await rest('health_check', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({}) });
  if (!created.ok) {
    await logFailedWrite('health insert', created);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'insert failed' };
  }
  const rows = (await created.json()) as { id: number }[];
  const id = rows[0]?.id;
  if (id === undefined) return { ok: false, reason: 'insert returned no row' };
  const deleted = await rest(`health_check?id=eq.${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
  if (!deleted.ok) {
    await logFailedWrite('health delete', deleted);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'delete failed' };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

/** Counts an attempt in a bucket. Returns false when the bucket is over its limit. Refused attempts still count. */
async function allow(bucket: string, opts: { windowMs: number; max: number }): Promise<boolean> {
  const rows = (await (await rest(`auth_attempts?bucket=eq.${encodeURIComponent(bucket)}&select=*`)).json()) as { window_start: string; hits: number }[];
  const row = rows[0];
  const result = admit(row ? { windowStart: Date.parse(row.window_start), hits: row.hits } : null, Date.now(), opts);
  await rest('auth_attempts', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ bucket, window_start: new Date(result.next.windowStart).toISOString(), hits: result.next.hits }),
  });
  return result.allowed;
}

async function playerById(id: string): Promise<{ id: string; nickname: string; key_hash: string | null; token_hash: string | null; save: unknown } | null> {
  const rows = (await (await rest(`players?id=eq.${id}&select=id,nickname,key_hash,token_hash,save`)).json()) as never[];
  return rows[0] ?? null;
}

async function verifyToken(playerId: string, token: string) {
  const player = await playerById(playerId);
  if (!player || !player.token_hash) return null;
  return (await sha256(token)) === player.token_hash ? player : null;
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  // Computed per request: the allow-origin header echoes only the caller's origin, and only when it matches.
  // Guarded on its own: a request must still get a response (and CORS headers) even if this throws.
  let cors: Record<string, string>;
  try {
    cors = corsHeaders(req.headers.get('origin'), ALLOWED_ORIGINS);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('account: corsHeaders failed:', err);
    cors = {};
  }
  const json = (body: unknown, status = 200): Response => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, 'content-type': 'application/json' } });

  // Nested so it shares req, cors and json with the request below instead of threading them through as parameters.
  async function handle(body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Response> {
    switch (body.action) {
      case 'register': {
        const playerId = String(body.playerId ?? '');
        const nickname = String(body.nickname ?? '');
        if (!UUID.test(playerId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad player id' }, 400);
        const problem = nicknameProblem(nickname);
        if (problem) return json({ ok: false, reason: problem }, 422);

        const existing = await playerById(playerId);
        if (existing?.key_hash) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'this handle already has a recovery key' }, 409);

        // Keys are random, so a clash is vanishingly rare. Retry a few times anyway, to be safe.
        let recoveryKey = '';
        let keyHash = '';
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          recoveryKey = generateRecoveryKey(() => {
            const b = new Uint32Array(1);
            crypto.getRandomValues(b);
            return b[0] / 4294967296;
          });
          keyHash = await hmac(normalizeRecoveryKey(recoveryKey));
          const taken = (await (await rest(`players?key_hash=eq.${keyHash}&select=id`)).json()) as unknown[];
          if (taken.length === 0) break;
        }

        const syncToken = newSyncToken();
        const row = {
          id: playerId,
          nickname,
          key_hash: keyHash,
          token_hash: await sha256(syncToken),
          save: body.save ?? null,
          save_updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        const write = existing
          ? await rest(`players?id=eq.${playerId}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify({ ...row, id: undefined }) })
          : await rest('players', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(row) });
        if (write.status === 409) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that handle is taken' }, 409);
        if (!write.ok) {
          await logFailedWrite('register write', write);
          return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not register' }, 502);
        }

        return json({ ok: true, recoveryKey, syncToken });
      }

      case 'restore': {
        // Two buckets: per caller address, and one for the whole service, so spreading attempts across addresses also runs out.
        const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0].trim() ?? 'unknown';
        const perCaller = await allow(`restore:${await sha256(ip)}`, RESTORE_LIMIT);
        const overall = await allow('restore:global', GLOBAL_RESTORE_LIMIT);
        if (!perCaller || !overall) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'too many attempts. Try again later.' }, 429);

        const typed = String(body.recoveryKey ?? '');
        if (!isWellFormedKey(typed)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that does not look like a recovery key' }, 422);
        const keyHash = await hmac(normalizeRecoveryKey(typed));
        const rows = (await (await rest(`players?key_hash=eq.${keyHash}&select=id,nickname,save`)).json()) as { id: string; nickname: string; save: unknown }[];
        if (rows.length === 0) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'no save matches that key' }, 404);

        // A new sync token for this device. The old device's token stops working, which keeps only one live session.
        const syncToken = newSyncToken();
        await rest(`players?id=eq.${rows[0].id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify({ token_hash: await sha256(syncToken) }) });
        return json({ ok: true, playerId: rows[0].id, nickname: rows[0].nickname, syncToken, save: rows[0].save ?? null });
      }

      case 'sync': {
        const playerId = String(body.playerId ?? '');
        const token = String(body.syncToken ?? '');
        if (!UUID.test(playerId) || !token) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'missing player or token' }, 400);
        if (!(await allow(`sync:${playerId}`, SYNC_LIMIT))) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'syncing too often. Try again later.' }, 429);
        const player = await verifyToken(playerId, token);
        if (!player) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'this device is not signed in to that save' }, 401);

        const local = body.save as Parameters<typeof mergeSaves>[0] | undefined;
        const remote = (player.save as Parameters<typeof mergeSaves>[1] | null) ?? null;
        // Pull only: the server copy goes back as it is. Push: merge both sides, then store the result.
        const merged = local && remote ? mergeSaves(local, remote) : local ?? remote;
        if (!merged) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'nothing to sync' }, 400);
        if (local) {
          const write = await rest(`players?id=eq.${playerId}`, {
            method: 'PATCH',
            body: JSON.stringify({ save: merged, save_updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }),
          });
          if (!write.ok) {
            await logFailedWrite('sync write', write);
            return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not store the save' }, 502);
          }
        }
        return json({ ok: true, save: merged });
      }

      case 'rename': {
        const playerId = String(body.playerId ?? '');
        const token = String(body.syncToken ?? '');
        const nickname = String(body.nickname ?? '');
        if (!UUID.test(playerId) || !token) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'missing player or token' }, 400);
        const problem = nicknameProblem(nickname);
        if (problem) return json({ ok: false, reason: problem }, 422);
        if (!(await verifyToken(playerId, token))) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'this device is not signed in to that save' }, 401);
        const write = await rest(`players?id=eq.${playerId}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify({ nickname }) });
        if (write.status === 409) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that handle is taken' }, 409);
        if (!write.ok) {
          await logFailedWrite('rename write', write);
          return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not rename' }, 502);
        }
        return json({ ok: true, nickname });
      }

      case 'health': {
        const result = await health();
        return json(result, result.ok ? 200 : 500);
      }

      default:
        return json({ ok: false, reason: 'unknown action' }, 400);
    }
  }

  // Everything below can fail (a bad env var, a missing table or column, the database being unreachable). Whatever
  // breaks, the caller gets a clear JSON error instead of a bare 502, and the real cause goes to the function log.
  try {
    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors });
    if (req.method !== 'POST') return json({ ok: false, reason: 'POST only' }, 405);
    if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SECRET_KEY || !PEPPER) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'server is not configured' }, 503);

    const raw = await req.text();
    if (raw.length > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'save is too large' }, 413);

    let body: Record<string, unknown>;
    try {
      body = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return json({ ok: false, reason: 'not JSON' }, 400);
    }

    return await handle(body);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('account: unhandled error:', err instanceof Error ? (err.stack ?? err.message) : err);
    return json({ ok: false, reason: 'internal error, please try again' }, 500);
  }
});
```

`supabase/functions/submit-run/index.ts`:

```ts
// submit-run: the only thing that writes to the leaderboard. Four actions in one function:
//   start  - issues a server-side run token (start time + a per-run seed) before an attempt begins.
//   submit - (default, no action field, for the original request shape) replays the command log through the
//            same engine the game uses (bundled into engine.js by scripts/build-edge.mjs) and stores the score
//            the replay produces. Never trusts a score from the browser. Requires a valid, unused run token.
//            Also returns how this score stands against everyone else's on the same mission, tier and slot
//            (see percentileFor): a percentile once enough runs exist, or "one of the first N" before that.
//   events - anonymous funnel analytics (see src/lib/funnel.ts and the events table). Rate limited per
//            session_id through the same auth_attempts bucket the account function uses for restores.
//   health - inserts and deletes one row in health_check, for the keepalive workflow. Never touches the leaderboard.
// Deno runtime. Deploy with: npx supabase functions deploy submit-run --no-verify-jwt

import {
  MISSIONS,
  admit,
  applyFlagVariation,
  checkToken,
  corsHeaders,
  dailyMission,
  nicknameProblem,
  parseAllowedOrigins,
  percentileFor,
  shiftDate,
  suspicionFor,
  utcDate,
  validateRun,
} from '../_shared/engine.js';

// Comma-separated list of exact origins or single-wildcard-subdomain patterns, e.g. 'https://a.test,https://*.b.test'.
const ALLOWED_ORIGINS = parseAllowedOrigins(Deno.env.get('ALLOWED_ORIGINS') ?? '');
const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL') ?? '';
// The server-side key's env var name has changed across Supabase projects. Check whichever of these is actually set.
const SECRET_KEY_SOURCE = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY') ? 'SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY' : Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ? 'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY' : null;
const SECRET_KEY = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY') ?? Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY') ?? '';

// Logged once at boot. Never logs a secret value, only whether each one is present and where the key came from.
console.log(`submit-run: boot. SUPABASE_URL=${SUPABASE_URL ? 'set' : 'MISSING'} secretKey=${SECRET_KEY_SOURCE ?? 'MISSING (checked SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY)'}`);

const RATE_WINDOW_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
const RATE_MAX_HITS = 60;
const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 200_000;
const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const TIERS = ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'];

// Funnel events. Kept in sync with the check constraint on public.events (see the 20261123000000_events migration)
// and with src/lib/funnel.ts's FunnelEventName.
const EVENT_NAMES = new Set([
  'landing_view', 'start_clicked', 'daily_clicked', 'mission_started', 'first_command', 'hint_used',
  'mission_cleared', 'mission_traced', 'mission_aborted', 'score_posted', 'share_clicked', 'restore_used',
]);
const MAX_EVENTS_PER_BATCH = 25;
// One batch send per session every ~20 seconds on average. Generous for real play, tight enough to make
// flooding with fabricated session ids pointless at any real scale.
const EVENTS_LIMIT = { windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000, max: 30 };

/** Calls the Supabase REST API with the server-side key. Used only for the tables browsers cannot write to. */
async function rest(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
  return fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      apikey: SECRET_KEY,
      authorization: `Bearer ${SECRET_KEY}`,
      'content-type': 'application/json',
      prefer: 'return=representation',
      ...(init.headers ?? {}),
    },
  });
}

/** Logs the real reason a write failed (PostgREST's own error body), so the generic reply to the caller still leaves a trail. */
async function logFailedWrite(label: string, res: Response): Promise<void> {
  const text = await res.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');
  console.error(`submit-run: ${label} failed: ${res.status} ${text}`);
}

async function rateLimited(playerId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const res = await rest(`rate_limits?player_id=eq.${playerId}&select=*`);
  const rows = (await res.json()) as { window_start: string; hits: number }[];
  const now = Date.now();
  const row = rows[0];
  const fresh = !row || now - Date.parse(row.window_start) > RATE_WINDOW_MS;
  const hits = fresh ? 1 : row.hits + 1;
  const windowStart = fresh ? new Date(now).toISOString() : row.window_start;
  await rest('rate_limits', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ player_id: playerId, window_start: windowStart, hits }),
  });
  return !fresh && hits > RATE_MAX_HITS;
}

/** Same fixed-window bucket the account function uses for restore/sync, keyed by session_id instead of a caller address. */
async function eventsRateLimited(sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const bucket = `events:${sessionId}`;
  const rows = (await (await rest(`auth_attempts?bucket=eq.${encodeURIComponent(bucket)}&select=*`)).json()) as { window_start: string; hits: number }[];
  const row = rows[0];
  const result = admit(row ? { windowStart: Date.parse(row.window_start), hits: row.hits } : null, Date.now(), EVENTS_LIMIT);
  await rest('auth_attempts', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ bucket, window_start: new Date(result.next.windowStart).toISOString(), hits: result.next.hits }),
  });
  return !result.allowed;
}

/** Drops anything malformed instead of failing the whole batch. A dropped analytics event is never worth a 400. */
function sanitizeEvent(e: unknown, sessionId: string): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (typeof e !== 'object' || e === null) return null;
  const rec = e as { event?: unknown; tier?: unknown; missionId?: unknown; value?: unknown };
  if (typeof rec.event !== 'string' || !EVENT_NAMES.has(rec.event)) return null;
  const tier = typeof rec.tier === 'string' && TIERS.includes(rec.tier) ? rec.tier : null;
  const missionId = typeof rec.missionId === 'string' && rec.missionId.length > 0 && rec.missionId.length <= 80 ? rec.missionId : null;
  const value = typeof rec.value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(rec.value) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100_000, Math.round(rec.value))) : null;
  return { session_id: sessionId, event: rec.event, tier, mission_id: missionId, value };
}

/** Inserts and deletes one throwaway row, proving the database can still write. Never touches the leaderboard. */
async function health(): Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }> {
  const created = await rest('health_check', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({}) });
  if (!created.ok) {
    await logFailedWrite('health insert', created);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'insert failed' };
  }
  const rows = (await created.json()) as { id: number }[];
  const id = rows[0]?.id;
  if (id === undefined) return { ok: false, reason: 'insert returned no row' };
  const deleted = await rest(`health_check?id=eq.${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
  if (!deleted.ok) {
    await logFailedWrite('health delete', deleted);
    return { ok: false, reason: 'delete failed' };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

/** Every stored score for this mission, tier and slot, including the just-written row, run through percentileFor. */
async function percentileAgainstBoard(missionId: string, tier: string, slot: string, mine: number): Promise<{ percentile: number | null; firstOfN: number | null }> {
  const rows = (await (await rest(
    `runs?mission_id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(missionId)}&tier=eq.${tier}&slot=eq.${encodeURIComponent(slot)}&select=score`,
  )).json()) as { score: number }[];
  return percentileFor(rows.map((r) => r.score), mine);
}

/** Finds which missions a daily or campaign claim may be replayed against, and the slot its run lives in. */
function missionsFor(scope: 'campaign' | 'daily', date: string | undefined): { missions: { id: string }[]; slot: string } | null {
  if (scope !== 'daily') return { missions: MISSIONS, slot: 'campaign' };
  const today = utcDate();
  if (!date || (date !== today && date !== shiftDate(today, -1))) return null;
  return { missions: [dailyMission(date)], slot: `daily:${date}` };
}

interface TokenRecord {
  id: string;
  player_id: string;
  mission_id: string;
  tier: string;
  scope: string;
  date: string | null;
  started_at: string;
  used_at: string | null;
  expires_at: string;
}

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  // Computed per request: the allow-origin header echoes only the caller's origin, and only when it matches.
  // Guarded on its own: a request must still get a response (and CORS headers) even if this throws.
  let cors: Record<string, string>;
  try {
    cors = corsHeaders(req.headers.get('origin'), ALLOWED_ORIGINS);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('submit-run: corsHeaders failed:', err);
    cors = {};
  }
  const json = (body: unknown, status = 200): Response => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { ...cors, 'content-type': 'application/json' } });

  // Everything below can fail (a bad env var, a missing table or column, the database being unreachable). Whatever
  // breaks, the caller gets a clear JSON error instead of a bare 502, and the real cause goes to the function log.
  try {
    if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors });
    if (req.method !== 'POST') return json({ ok: false, reason: 'POST only' }, 405);

    const raw = await req.text();
    if (raw.length > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'request too large' }, 413);

    let body: {
      action?: 'start' | 'submit' | 'health' | 'events';
      playerId?: string;
      nickname?: string;
      missionId?: string;
      tier?: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard';
      scope?: 'campaign' | 'daily';
      date?: string;
      run?: { missionId: string; tier: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard'; commands: { t: number; cmd: string; bs?: number }[]; claimedScore?: number; token?: string };
      sessionId?: string;
      events?: unknown[];
    };
    try {
      body = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch {
      return json({ ok: false, reason: 'not JSON' }, 400);
    }

    if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SECRET_KEY) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'server is not configured' }, 503);

    if (body.action === 'health') {
      const result = await health();
      return json(result, result.ok ? 200 : 500);
    }

    if (body.action === 'events') {
      const sessionId = body.sessionId ?? '';
      if (!UUID.test(sessionId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad session id' }, 400);
      if (await eventsRateLimited(sessionId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'too many events' }, 429);
      const incoming = Array.isArray(body.events) ? body.events.slice(0, MAX_EVENTS_PER_BATCH) : [];
      const rows = incoming.map((e) => sanitizeEvent(e, sessionId)).filter((r): r is Record<string, unknown> => r !== null);
      if (rows.length === 0) return json({ ok: true, stored: 0 });
      const write = await rest('events', { method: 'POST', headers: { prefer: 'return=minimal' }, body: JSON.stringify(rows) });
      if (!write.ok) {
        await logFailedWrite('events write', write);
        return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not store events' }, 502);
      }
      return json({ ok: true, stored: rows.length });
    }

    if (typeof body.playerId !== 'string' || !UUID.test(body.playerId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad player id' }, 400);
    if (await rateLimited(body.playerId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'too many submissions. Try again later.' }, 429);

    if (body.action === 'start') {
      const missionId = body.missionId ?? '';
      const tier = body.tier ?? '';
      const scope = body.scope ?? 'campaign';
      if (!TIERS.includes(tier)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad tier' }, 400);
      if (scope !== 'campaign' && scope !== 'daily') return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad scope' }, 400);
      const found = missionsFor(scope, body.date);
      if (!found) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that daily job has closed' }, 422);
      if (!found.missions.some((m) => m.id === missionId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'unknown mission' }, 422);

      const seed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 2 ** 31);
      const created = await rest('run_tokens', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ player_id: body.playerId, mission_id: missionId, tier, scope, date: scope === 'daily' ? body.date : null, seed }),
      });
      if (!created.ok) {
        await logFailedWrite('token create', created);
        return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not start the run' }, 502);
      }
      const rows = (await created.json()) as { id: string; seed: number }[];
      return json({ ok: true, token: rows[0].id, seed: rows[0].seed });
    }

    // Default action: submit.
    if (!body.run) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'missing run' }, 400);

    // Find or create the player. Nicknames are unique and set once.
    const existing = (await (await rest(`players?id=eq.${body.playerId}&select=id,nickname`)).json()) as { id: string; nickname: string }[];
    if (existing.length === 0) {
      const nickname = body.nickname ?? '';
      const problem = nicknameProblem(nickname);
      if (problem) return json({ ok: false, reason: problem }, 422);
      const created = await rest('players', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ id: body.playerId, nickname }) });
      if (created.status === 409) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that nickname is taken' }, 409);
      if (!created.ok) {
        await logFailedWrite('create player', created);
        return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not create player' }, 502);
      }
    }

    const tokenId = body.run.token ?? '';
    if (!UUID.test(tokenId)) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'missing or invalid run token' }, 422);
    const tokenRows = (await (await rest(`run_tokens?id=eq.${tokenId}&select=*`)).json()) as TokenRecord[];
    const tokenRow = tokenRows[0];
    if (!tokenRow) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'missing or invalid run token' }, 422);

    const scope = body.scope === 'daily' ? 'daily' : 'campaign';
    const lastCommandMs = body.run.commands.length ? body.run.commands[body.run.commands.length - 1].t : 0;
    const tokenCheck = checkToken(
      {
        playerId: tokenRow.player_id,
        missionId: tokenRow.mission_id,
        tier: tokenRow.tier as 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard',
        scope: tokenRow.scope as 'campaign' | 'daily',
        date: tokenRow.date,
        startedAt: Date.parse(tokenRow.started_at),
        usedAt: tokenRow.used_at ? Date.parse(tokenRow.used_at) : null,
        expiresAt: Date.parse(tokenRow.expires_at),
      },
      { playerId: body.playerId, missionId: body.run.missionId, tier: body.run.tier, scope, date: body.date },
      lastCommandMs,
      Date.now(),
    );
    if (!tokenCheck.ok) return json({ ok: false, reason: tokenCheck.reason }, 422);

    // Atomically claim the token. Zero rows back means another request already consumed it.
    const claimed = await rest(`run_tokens?id=eq.${tokenId}&used_at=is.null`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify({ used_at: new Date().toISOString() }) });
    const claimedRows = claimed.ok ? ((await claimed.json()) as unknown[]) : [];
    if (!claimed.ok || claimedRows.length === 0) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that run token has already been used' }, 422);

    // Which missions the run may be replayed against, built from the token's own stored claim, varied by its seed.
    const found = missionsFor(scope, tokenRow.date ?? undefined);
    if (!found) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'that daily job has closed' }, 422);
    const base = found.missions.find((m) => m.id === tokenRow.mission_id);
    if (!base) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'unknown mission' }, 422);
    const varied = applyFlagVariation(base, tokenRow.seed);

    const result = validateRun(body.run, [varied]);
    if (!result.ok) return json({ ok: false, reason: result.reason }, 422);

    const suspicion = suspicionFor(body.run, result);

    // Campaign: keep the best score per mission and tier. Daily: one attempt per day per tier.
    const slot = found.slot;
    const mine = (await (await rest(
      `runs?player_id=eq.${body.playerId}&mission_id=eq.${body.run.missionId}&tier=eq.${body.run.tier}&slot=eq.${slot}&select=id,score`,
    )).json()) as { id: number; score: number }[];
    if (slot.startsWith('daily:') && mine.length > 0) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'you have already attempted this daily on this tier' }, 409);
    if (mine.length > 0 && mine[0].score >= result.score) {
      const pct = await percentileAgainstBoard(body.run.missionId, body.run.tier, slot, mine[0].score);
      return json({
        ok: true,
        improved: false,
        score: result.score,
        points: result.points,
        seconds: result.seconds,
        trace: result.trace,
        hints: result.hints,
        percentile: pct.percentile,
        firstOfN: pct.firstOfN,
      });
    }

    const row = {
      player_id: body.playerId,
      mission_id: body.run.missionId,
      tier: body.run.tier,
      slot,
      score: result.score,
      points: result.points,
      seconds: result.seconds,
      trace: result.trace,
      hints: result.hints,
      suspicion: suspicion.score,
      suspicion_reasons: suspicion.reasons,
      // The checked command log, so the best run for a job can be watched. Only the typed text and timing are kept.
      commands: body.run.commands.map((c) => ({ t: c.t, cmd: c.cmd })),
    };
    const write = mine.length
      ? await rest(`runs?id=eq.${mine[0].id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(row) })
      : await rest('runs', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(row) });
    if (!write.ok) {
      await logFailedWrite('run write', write);
      return json({ ok: false, reason: 'could not save the run' }, 502);
    }

    const pct = await percentileAgainstBoard(body.run.missionId, body.run.tier, slot, result.score);
    return json({
      ok: true,
      improved: true,
      score: result.score,
      points: result.points,
      seconds: result.seconds,
      trace: result.trace,
      hints: result.hints,
      percentile: pct.percentile,
      firstOfN: pct.firstOfN,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('submit-run: unhandled error:', err instanceof Error ? (err.stack ?? err.message) : err);
    return json({ ok: false, reason: 'internal error, please try again' }, 500);
  }
});

```

`supabase/migrations/20261004000000_leaderboard.sql`:

```sql
-- BREACH leaderboard schema.
-- Browsers can only READ. Writes happen in the submit-run Edge Function, which uses the server-side secret key
-- and so bypasses row-level security. There are no insert, update or delete policies for anon or authenticated users.

create table if not exists public.players (
  id uuid primary key,
  nickname text not null unique
    check (char_length(nickname) between 3 and 16 and nickname ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$'),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- One row per player, mission, tier and slot. The slot is 'campaign' for campaign jobs, or 'daily:YYYY-MM-DD'.
-- Campaign rows keep the player's best score. Daily rows are one attempt per day per tier.
create table if not exists public.runs (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  player_id uuid not null references public.players(id) on delete cascade,
  mission_id text not null,
  tier text not null check (tier in ('easy', 'medium', 'hard')),
  slot text not null check (slot = 'campaign' or slot like 'daily:%'),
  score integer not null check (score between 100 and 1000),
  points integer not null check (points >= 0),
  seconds integer not null check (seconds >= 0),
  trace integer not null check (trace between 0 and 100),
  hints integer not null check (hints >= 0),
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (player_id, mission_id, tier, slot)
);

create index if not exists runs_board_idx on public.runs (mission_id, tier, slot, score desc);

-- Rate limiting for the Edge Function. Service role only.
create table if not exists public.rate_limits (
  player_id uuid primary key references public.players(id) on delete cascade,
  window_start timestamptz not null default now(),
  hits integer not null default 0
);

-- Campaign totals per player and tier: the sum of points over every campaign job they have finished.
create or replace view public.campaign_totals
with (security_invoker = true) as
select
  r.player_id,
  p.nickname,
  r.tier,
  count(*)::int as missions,
  sum(r.points)::int as points,
  round(avg(r.score))::int as avg_score
from public.runs r
join public.players p on p.id = r.player_id
where r.slot = 'campaign'
group by r.player_id, p.nickname, r.tier;

-- Your place on a campaign board. Called as an RPC from the browser; read only.
create or replace function public.player_rank(p_player uuid, p_tier text)
returns integer
language sql
stable
security invoker
as $$
  select 1 + count(*)::int
  from public.campaign_totals t
  where t.tier = p_tier
    and t.points > coalesce((select points from public.campaign_totals where player_id = p_player and tier = p_tier), 0);
$$;

-- Row-level security: the public may read, nobody but the service role may write.
alter table public.players enable row level security;
alter table public.runs enable row level security;
alter table public.rate_limits enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "players are readable" on public.players;
create policy "players are readable" on public.players for select to anon, authenticated using (true);

drop policy if exists "runs are readable" on public.runs;
create policy "runs are readable" on public.runs for select to anon, authenticated using (true);

-- rate_limits has RLS on and no policies: no one but the service role can see it.

grant select on public.players, public.runs, public.campaign_totals to anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.player_rank(uuid, text) to anon, authenticated;

```

`supabase/migrations/20261005000000_accounts.sql`:

```sql
-- BREACH accounts and cloud save. Still no login: a player is an anonymous id, with a recovery key to restore on another device.
--
-- Security model:
--   * The browser can read nickname and the public board tables. It cannot read key_hash, token_hash or save.
--   * Only the account Edge Function writes, using the server-side key, which bypasses row-level security.
--   * Restore attempts and sync calls are rate limited through auth_attempts, which browsers cannot see at all.

-- Recovery key (hashed with a server-side pepper), sync token (hashed), and the cloud copy of the save.
alter table public.players add column if not exists key_hash text unique;
alter table public.players add column if not exists token_hash text;
alter table public.players add column if not exists save jsonb;
alter table public.players add column if not exists save_updated_at timestamptz;

-- The replay of a run, so the global #1 run can be watched. Written only by submit-run, after the replay checks it.
alter table public.runs add column if not exists commands jsonb;

-- Rate limit buckets for restore and sync. No policies, so no browser role can see or change them.
create table if not exists public.auth_attempts (
  bucket text primary key,
  window_start timestamptz not null default now(),
  hits integer not null default 0
);
alter table public.auth_attempts enable row level security;
revoke all on public.auth_attempts from anon, authenticated;

-- Column-level privacy on players: the browser sees id, nickname and created_at only.
revoke select on public.players from anon, authenticated;
grant select (id, nickname, created_at) on public.players to anon, authenticated;

-- The public tables keep working: they read only the columns the browser may see.
grant select (id, nickname, created_at) on public.players to anon, authenticated;

```

`supabase/migrations/20261119000000_service_role_grants.sql`:

```sql
-- Explicit grants for the service role the account and submit-run Edge Functions use.
-- service_role normally gets full access to every table by default on a Supabase project, so earlier
-- migrations never granted it anything directly. That default turned out to be missing on this project:
-- a live register call failed with "permission denied for table players" (42501) for service_role.
-- These grants are safe to re-run and make the dependency explicit instead of relying on a platform default.

grant usage on schema public to service_role;
grant select, insert, update, delete on public.players, public.runs, public.rate_limits, public.auth_attempts to service_role;
grant select on public.campaign_totals to service_role;
grant execute on function public.player_rank(uuid, text) to service_role;
grant usage, select on all sequences in schema public to service_role;

-- So a table or sequence created later is covered too, without another migration like this one.
alter default privileges in schema public grant select, insert, update, delete on tables to service_role;
alter default privileges in schema public grant usage, select on sequences to service_role;

```

`supabase/migrations/20261120000000_run_tokens.sql`:

```sql
-- Leaderboard hardening: server-issued run tokens, suspicion scoring, and a health-check table for the
-- keepalive workflow. See src/engine/replay.ts (checkToken, suspicionFor, SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD) and
-- src/lib/variation.ts (applyFlagVariation) for the code that uses these.

-- One row per attempt that asked to be posted. The server's own start time and seed live here, out of the
-- browser's reach. Single use: submit-run claims a token with a conditional update (used_at is.null).
create table if not exists public.run_tokens (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  player_id uuid not null references public.players(id) on delete cascade,
  mission_id text not null,
  tier text not null check (tier in ('easy', 'medium', 'hard')),
  scope text not null check (scope in ('campaign', 'daily')),
  -- The daily's UTC date. Null for a campaign token.
  date text,
  seed bigint not null,
  started_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  used_at timestamptz,
  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '3 hours')
);

alter table public.run_tokens enable row level security;
-- Service role only: no anon/authenticated policies, same pattern as rate_limits and auth_attempts.
revoke all on public.run_tokens from anon, authenticated;

-- Suspicion scoring and review state on runs. suspicion/suspicion_reasons are written by submit-run from
-- src/engine/replay.ts's suspicionFor. reviewed lets a human clear a flagged run without touching its score.
alter table public.runs add column if not exists suspicion integer not null default 0 check (suspicion between 0 and 100);
alter table public.runs add column if not exists suspicion_reasons text[] not null default '{}';
alter table public.runs add column if not exists reviewed boolean not null default false;

-- Hide flagged runs from the public board (but keep them, and keep them visible to their own player through
-- the submit-run response) until reviewed. campaign_totals is declared security_invoker = true, so it inherits
-- this same filter automatically. service_role bypasses RLS, so submit-run still sees every row.
-- Keep "70" in sync with SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD in src/engine/replay.ts.
drop policy if exists "runs are readable" on public.runs;
create policy "runs are readable" on public.runs for select to anon, authenticated using (suspicion < 70 or reviewed);

-- For a human to review in the Supabase SQL editor: select * from public.flagged_runs;
-- To clear one back onto the board without touching its score: update public.runs set reviewed = true where id = <id>;
create or replace view public.flagged_runs
with (security_invoker = true) as
select r.id, p.nickname, r.mission_id, r.tier, r.slot, r.score, r.suspicion, r.suspicion_reasons, r.created_at
from public.runs r
join public.players p on p.id = r.player_id
where r.suspicion >= 70 and not r.reviewed
order by r.suspicion desc, r.created_at desc;

grant select on public.flagged_runs to service_role;

-- A table the keepalive workflow's health action writes to and immediately deletes from, so a passing check
-- proves the database can still write, without ever touching real leaderboard data.
create table if not exists public.health_check (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  pinged_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.health_check enable row level security;
revoke all on public.health_check from anon, authenticated;

```

`supabase/migrations/20261121000000_fix_run_tokens_player_fk.sql`:

```sql
-- Fixes a live bug: run_tokens.player_id had a foreign key to players(id), but the 'start' action (which
-- inserts a run_tokens row) runs before a brand-new player's row exists in players — that row is only
-- created later, during 'submit' (see supabase/functions/submit-run/index.ts). Every first-time player's
-- very first mission start failed with a 23503 foreign key violation:
--   insert or update on table "run_tokens" violates foreign key constraint "run_tokens_player_id_fkey"
-- Confirmed from the submit-run function logs; not a grants problem (service_role's insert privilege was
-- fine — it reached the constraint check and failed there, and health_check, granted the same way in the
-- same migration, works).
--
-- The FK added no real safety: checkToken (src/engine/replay.ts) already verifies a token's player_id
-- matches the submitting player at submit time, and run_tokens rows are short-lived (single use, 3-hour
-- expiry) with no lasting data that benefits from an on-delete cascade the way runs/rate_limits/auth_attempts
-- do. Dropping it is correct, not just expedient.
alter table public.run_tokens drop constraint if exists run_tokens_player_id_fkey;

```

`supabase/migrations/20261123000000_events.sql`:

```sql
-- Anonymous funnel analytics. Browsers cannot read or write this table directly: every row is written by the
-- submit-run Edge Function's 'events' action (see supabase/functions/submit-run/index.ts), using the service-role
-- key, rate limited per session_id through the same auth_attempts bucket the account function already uses.
--
-- session_id is a random id generated per browser tab (sessionStorage, see src/lib/funnel.ts) — never the player
-- id, never tied to a nickname or account. There is no IP, no user agent, and nothing else identifying here.

create table if not exists public.events (
  id bigint generated always as identity primary key,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  session_id uuid not null,
  event text not null check (event in (
    'landing_view', 'start_clicked', 'daily_clicked', 'mission_started', 'first_command', 'hint_used',
    'mission_cleared', 'mission_traced', 'mission_aborted', 'score_posted', 'share_clicked', 'restore_used'
  )),
  tier text check (tier in ('easy', 'medium', 'hard')),
  mission_id text,
  -- A small number, meaning depends on the event: seconds elapsed for mission_cleared/traced/aborted, the hint
  -- number for hint_used. Null where an event has nothing to measure (landing_view, share_clicked, ...).
  value integer
);

create index if not exists events_event_created_idx on public.events (event, created_at);
create index if not exists events_session_idx on public.events (session_id, created_at);
create index if not exists events_mission_tier_idx on public.events (mission_id, tier);

alter table public.events enable row level security;
-- No select or insert policies for anon/authenticated: RLS is on and nobody but the service role can touch this
-- table through the API. Writes happen only inside the Edge Function, with the service-role key, which bypasses
-- RLS entirely — the same pattern as rate_limits, auth_attempts and run_tokens.
revoke all on public.events from anon, authenticated;
grant select, insert on public.events to service_role;

-- Three views for the Supabase SQL editor. None are granted to anon/authenticated; the dashboard's own SQL editor
-- connects as a superuser role that already bypasses grants, so no browser can ever read these either way.

-- Counts per funnel step over the last 7 days, in the order a player actually moves through them.
create or replace view public.funnel_last_7d
with (security_invoker = true) as
select
  event,
  case event
    when 'landing_view' then 1
    when 'start_clicked' then 2
    when 'daily_clicked' then 2
    when 'mission_started' then 3
    when 'first_command' then 4
    when 'hint_used' then 5
    when 'mission_cleared' then 6
    when 'mission_traced' then 6
    when 'mission_aborted' then 6
    when 'score_posted' then 7
    when 'share_clicked' then 8
    when 'restore_used' then 9
    else 99
  end as step,
  count(*)::int as events,
  count(distinct session_id)::int as sessions
from public.events
where created_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
group by event
order by step, event;

-- Started vs cleared vs traced vs aborted, per mission and tier, all time.
create or replace view public.mission_drop_off
with (security_invoker = true) as
select
  mission_id,
  tier,
  count(*) filter (where event = 'mission_started')::int as started,
  count(*) filter (where event = 'mission_cleared')::int as cleared,
  count(*) filter (where event = 'mission_traced')::int as traced,
  count(*) filter (where event = 'mission_aborted')::int as aborted
from public.events
where mission_id is not null
group by mission_id, tier
order by mission_id, tier;

-- Median seconds between a mission starting and the first command typed, across every session and mission that
-- has both events recorded. One row.
create or replace view public.median_time_to_first_command
with (security_invoker = true) as
with paired as (
  select
    session_id,
    mission_id,
    min(created_at) filter (where event = 'mission_started') as started_at,
    min(created_at) filter (where event = 'first_command') as first_command_at
  from public.events
  where mission_id is not null
  group by session_id, mission_id
)
select
  percentile_cont(0.5) within group (order by extract(epoch from (first_command_at - started_at))) as median_seconds,
  count(*)::int as sample_size
from paired
where started_at is not null and first_command_at is not null and first_command_at >= started_at;

grant select on public.funnel_last_7d, public.mission_drop_off, public.median_time_to_first_command to service_role;

```

`tests/achievements.test.ts`:

```ts
// Achievements: the list is well formed, each unlock fires on its own condition, nothing unlocks twice,
// and the facts come from real sessions (the easter egg, sudo tries, pipes, aliases, files read).

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GameSession } from '../src/engine/session';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import { BUG_REPORT_CONTACT, EASTER_EGG_PATH } from '../src/missions/shared';
import { ACHIEVEMENTS, achievementById, newlyUnlocked, readEveryPublicFileIn, type AchievementFacts } from '../src/lib/achievements';

const base: AchievementFacts = {
  tier: 'medium',
  trace: 40,
  hints: 2,
  score: 500,
  seconds: 600,
  isDaily: false,
  campaignDoneOnTier: false,
  streak: 0,
  sudoTotal: 0,
  usedAliases: true,
  usedPipe: false,
  eggFound: false,
  readEveryPublicFile: false,
  hourLocal: 14,
};

const unlocks = (f: Partial<AchievementFacts>) => newlyUnlocked({ ...base, ...f }, []).map((a) => a.id);

describe('achievement list', () => {
  it('has between 15 and 20 achievements with unique ids', () => {
    expect(ACHIEVEMENTS.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(15);
    expect(ACHIEVEMENTS.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(20);
    expect(new Set(ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => a.id)).size).toBe(ACHIEVEMENTS.length);
  });

  it('has the obvious ones and some hidden ones', () => {
    const ids = ACHIEVEMENTS.map((a) => a.id);
    for (const id of ['campaign-complete', 'clean-sweep', 'no-hints-hard', 'streak-7']) expect(ids).toContain(id);
    expect(ACHIEVEMENTS.filter((a) => a.hidden).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
  });

  it('every achievement has a title, a description and an icon', () => {
    for (const a of ACHIEVEMENTS) {
      expect(a.title.trim(), a.id).not.toBe('');
      expect(a.description.trim(), a.id).not.toBe('');
      expect(a.icon.trim(), a.id).not.toBe('');
    }
  });
});

describe('unlock conditions', () => {
  it('zero trace unlocks clean sweep, and not otherwise', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ trace: 0 })).toContain('clean-sweep');
    expect(unlocks({ trace: 1 })).not.toContain('clean-sweep');
  });

  it('no hints on Hard unlocks unassisted, but no hints on Medium does not', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ tier: 'hard', hints: 0 })).toContain('no-hints-hard');
    expect(unlocks({ tier: 'medium', hints: 0 })).not.toContain('no-hints-hard');
    expect(unlocks({ tier: 'hard', hints: 1 })).not.toContain('no-hints-hard');
  });

  it('a seven day streak unlocks streak-7 only at seven', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ isDaily: true, streak: 7 })).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['daily-first', 'streak-7']));
    expect(unlocks({ isDaily: true, streak: 6 })).not.toContain('streak-7');
  });

  it('finishing the campaign unlocks campaign complete', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ campaignDoneOnTier: true })).toContain('campaign-complete');
  });

  it('ghost rank, speed, pipes and real tools each follow their own facts', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ score: 850 })).toContain('ghost-rank');
    expect(unlocks({ score: 849 })).not.toContain('ghost-rank');
    expect(unlocks({ seconds: 179 })).toContain('speed-run');
    expect(unlocks({ seconds: 180 })).not.toContain('speed-run');
    expect(unlocks({ usedPipe: true })).toContain('pipe-dreams');
    expect(unlocks({ usedAliases: false })).toContain('kali-only');
  });

  it('five sudo tries unlocks asking nicely, four does not', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ sudoTotal: 5 })).toContain('sudo-five');
    expect(unlocks({ sudoTotal: 4 })).not.toContain('sudo-five');
  });

  it('night shift needs a finish between midnight and four', () => {
    expect(unlocks({ hourLocal: 3 })).toContain('night-owl');
    expect(unlocks({ hourLocal: 4 })).not.toContain('night-owl');
  });

  it('the first finish always unlocks first breach', () => {
    expect(unlocks({})).toContain('first-breach');
  });
});

describe('newlyUnlocked', () => {
  it('never returns an achievement that is already unlocked', () => {
    const got = newlyUnlocked({ ...base, trace: 0 }, ['clean-sweep', 'first-breach']).map((a) => a.id);
    expect(got).not.toContain('clean-sweep');
    expect(got).not.toContain('first-breach');
  });

  it('achievementById finds a definition and returns undefined for a stranger', () => {
    expect(achievementById('egg-hunter')?.hidden).toBe(true);
    expect(achievementById('made-up')).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('facts come from real sessions', () => {
  it('reading the hidden file on the rig records the egg, and it names the contact', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0]);
    s.input(`cat ${EASTER_EGG_PATH}`);
    expect(s.reads.has(`rig:${EASTER_EGG_PATH}`)).toBe(true);
    expect(s.machine.files[EASTER_EGG_PATH].content).toContain(BUG_REPORT_CONTACT);
  });

  it('each sudo attempt is counted', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) s.input('sudo cat /etc/shadow');
    expect(s.sudoTries).toBe(5);
  });

  it('a pipeline marks the run as using pipes, and an alias marks it as not real-tools-only', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0]);
    s.input('echo hi | wc -l');
    expect(s.usedPipe).toBe(true);
    expect(s.usedAliases).toBe(false);
    s.input('scan');
    expect(s.usedAliases).toBe(true);
  });

  it('readEveryPublicFileIn is false until every public file on a remote host has been read', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0]);
    expect(readEveryPublicFileIn(s)).toBe(false);
    for (const machine of s.mission.machines) {
      if (machine.id === 'rig') continue;
      for (const [path, file] of Object.entries(machine.files)) {
        if (!file.private) s.reads.add(`${machine.id}:${path}`);
      }
    }
    expect(readEveryPublicFileIn(s)).toBe(true);
  });
});

```

`tests/board.test.ts`:

```ts
// The global board panel's one rule: the celebratory "you're on the board" line is only shown once
// the run is actually verified by the server, never just because a handle was entered or a key was issued.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { boardHeadline, isOnBoard, percentileLine } from '../src/lib/board';
import type { SubmitResult } from '../src/lib/leaderboard';

const SAVED: SubmitResult = { status: 'saved', score: 10, points: 10, improved: true };
const REJECTED: SubmitResult = { status: 'rejected', reason: 'nope' };
const OFFLINE: SubmitResult = { status: 'offline', reason: 'offline' };

describe('isOnBoard', () => {
  it('is true only once the server verified the run', () => {
    expect(isOnBoard(SAVED)).toBe(true);
    expect(isOnBoard(REJECTED)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOnBoard(OFFLINE)).toBe(false);
    expect(isOnBoard(null)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('boardHeadline', () => {
  it('only says "you\'re on the board" after a successful post', () => {
    expect(boardHeadline(SAVED)).toBe("YOU'RE ON THE BOARD");
    expect(boardHeadline(REJECTED)).toBe('GLOBAL BOARD');
    expect(boardHeadline(OFFLINE)).toBe('GLOBAL BOARD');
    expect(boardHeadline(null)).toBe('GLOBAL BOARD');
  });
});

describe('percentileLine', () => {
  it('reports the percentile when the server sent one', () => {
    const result: SubmitResult = { ...SAVED, percentile: 64, firstOfN: null };
    expect(percentileLine(result, 'Medium')).toBe('You beat 64% of players on this job (Medium)');
  });

  it('falls back to "one of the first N" when there is no percentile yet', () => {
    const result: SubmitResult = { ...SAVED, percentile: null, firstOfN: 3 };
    expect(percentileLine(result, 'Hard')).toBe('One of the first 3 players to clear this');
  });

  it('singular for exactly one', () => {
    const result: SubmitResult = { ...SAVED, percentile: null, firstOfN: 1 };
    expect(percentileLine(result, 'Easy')).toBe('One of the first 1 player to clear this');
  });

  it('is null when neither field is present (an older server, or a saved result that sent nothing)', () => {
    expect(percentileLine(SAVED, 'Medium')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('is null unless the run was actually saved', () => {
    expect(percentileLine(REJECTED, 'Medium')).toBeNull();
    expect(percentileLine(OFFLINE, 'Medium')).toBeNull();
    expect(percentileLine(null, 'Medium')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```
