`tests/cloud-board.test.ts`:

```ts
// The board shows a place only when the player has posted. The cloud client degrades to offline, and reports server
// refusals (such as rate limits) as they are. Network calls are stubbed.

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

type Fetch = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

function reply(status: number, body: unknown): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
}

async function loadWithEnv(configured: boolean, path = '../src/lib/leaderboard') {
  vi.resetModules();
  if (configured) {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'https://example.supabase.test';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = 'sb_publishable_test';
  } else {
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
  }
  return import(path);
}

beforeEach(() => {
  // The clients use window timers. A minimal stand-in is enough under Node.
  vi.stubGlobal('window', { setTimeout: globalThis.setTimeout, clearTimeout: globalThis.clearTimeout, localStorage: undefined });
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('board place', () => {
  it('shows no place when the player has never posted, and makes no ranking call', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true);
    const calls: string[] = [];
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async (url: string) => {
      calls.push(url);
      return reply(200, []);
    }) as Fetch);
    expect(await mod.fetchMyRank('0f1e2d3c-0000-4000-8000-000000000001', 'medium')).toBeNull();
    expect(calls.some((u) => u.includes('player_rank'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('shows the place once the player has a campaign score on that tier', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true);
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async (url: string) => {
      if (url.includes('campaign_totals')) return reply(200, [{ points: 4200 }]);
      return reply(200, 3);
    }) as Fetch);
    expect(await mod.fetchMyRank('0f1e2d3c-0000-4000-8000-000000000001', 'medium')).toBe(3);
  });
});

describe('cloud client', () => {
  it('reports offline, not an error, when cloud save is not configured', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(false, '../src/lib/cloud');
    const r = await mod.restoreAccount('GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419');
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.offline).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reports offline when the network fails', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true, '../src/lib/cloud');
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => {
      throw new TypeError('network down');
    }) as Fetch);
    const r = await mod.restoreAccount('GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419');
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.offline).toBe(true);
  });

  it('passes the server refusal on as the reason, such as a rate limit', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true, '../src/lib/cloud');
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => reply(429, { ok: false, reason: 'too many attempts. Try again later.' })) as Fetch);
    const r = await mod.restoreAccount('GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419');
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) {
      expect(r.offline).toBe(false);
      expect(r.reason).toMatch(/too many attempts/);
    }
  });

  it('a successful restore returns the server copy', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true, '../src/lib/cloud');
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => reply(200, { ok: true, playerId: 'p', nickname: 'ghost', syncToken: 't', save: null })) as Fetch);
    const r = await mod.restoreAccount('GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419');
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (r.ok) expect(r.value.nickname).toBe('ghost');
  });
});

```

`tests/daily.test.ts`:

```ts
// Daily challenge: the same date gives the same job everywhere, every generated job is beatable on every tier,
// the countdown and streak logic is right, and a daily run passes the replay validator.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GameSession } from '../src/engine/session';
import { validateRun } from '../src/engine/replay';
import { TIERS } from '../src/engine/difficulty';
import type { MissionDef, Tier } from '../src/engine/types';
import { dailyMission, dailyNumber, hashSeed, isHardFriday, msUntilReset, seededRandom, shiftDate, streakFor, utcDate } from '../src/lib/daily';

const DATES = Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => shiftDate('2026-01-01', i));

function play(mission: MissionDef, steps: string[], tier: Tier): GameSession {
  const s = new GameSession(mission, { tier });
  for (const step of steps) s.input(step);
  return s;
}

describe('daily generator', () => {
  it('the same date gives the same job, every time', () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(dailyMission('2026-10-04'))).toBe(JSON.stringify(dailyMission('2026-10-04')));
  });

  it('different dates give different jobs', () => {
    const flags = new Set(DATES.slice(0, 30).map((d) => dailyMission(d).flag));
    expect(flags.size).toBeGreaterThan(25);
  });

  it('the seed is a stable hash, and the random sequence repeats for a seed', () => {
    expect(hashSeed('breach-daily:2026-10-04')).toBe(hashSeed('breach-daily:2026-10-04'));
    const a = seededRandom(7);
    const b = seededRandom(7);
    expect([a(), a(), a()]).toEqual([b(), b(), b()]);
  });

  it.each(DATES)('%s is beatable on every tier with both walkthroughs', (date) => {
    const m = dailyMission(date);
    for (const tier of TIERS) {
      const kali = play(m, m.kaliSolution, tier);
      expect(kali.status, `${date} ${tier} kali`).toBe('won');
      expect(kali.trace, `${date} ${tier} trace`).toBeLessThan(100);
      const old = play(m, m.solution, tier);
      expect(old.status, `${date} ${tier} classic`).toBe('won');
    }
  });

  it('a daily run passes the replay validator when played at human speed', () => {
    const date = '2026-10-04';
    const m = dailyMission(date);
    const commands = m.kaliSolution.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd }));
    const r = validateRun({ missionId: m.id, tier: 'medium', commands }, [m]);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('every daily job has three hints and a rig', () => {
    for (const date of DATES.slice(0, 20)) {
      const m = dailyMission(date);
      expect(m.hints).toHaveLength(3);
      expect(m.machines.some((x) => x.id === 'rig')).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

describe('Hard Friday', () => {
  it('flags Friday dates, and only Friday dates', () => {
    expect(isHardFriday('2026-01-02')).toBe(true);
    expect(isHardFriday('2026-01-01')).toBe(false);
    expect(isHardFriday('2026-01-03')).toBe(false);
  });

  it("a Friday's daily is marked hardFriday, with every decoy present", () => {
    const m = dailyMission('2026-01-02');
    expect(m.hardFriday).toBe(true);
    const target = m.machines.find((x) => x.id === 'target');
    const decoyCount = Object.keys(target?.files ?? {}).filter((p) => p.includes('old-menu') || p.includes('cron.log') || p.includes('welcome')).length;
    expect(decoyCount).toBe(3);
  });

  it('a non-Friday daily is not marked', () => {
    expect(dailyMission('2026-01-01').hardFriday).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('UTC dates, countdown and streak', () => {
  it('uses the UTC calendar date', () => {
    expect(utcDate(new Date('2026-10-03T23:59:59Z'))).toBe('2026-10-03');
    expect(utcDate(new Date('2026-10-04T00:00:00Z'))).toBe('2026-10-04');
  });

  it('numbers day one as 2026-01-01 and walks across month ends', () => {
    expect(dailyNumber('2026-01-01')).toBe(1);
    expect(dailyNumber('2026-02-01')).toBe(32);
    expect(shiftDate('2026-02-28', 1)).toBe('2026-03-01');
  });

  it('counts down to the next UTC midnight', () => {
    expect(msUntilReset(new Date('2026-10-03T23:59:59Z'))).toBe(1000);
    expect(msUntilReset(new Date('2026-10-04T00:00:00Z'))).toBe(86_400_000);
  });

  it('a streak counts consecutive completed days back from today', () => {
    expect(streakFor(['2026-10-01', '2026-10-02', '2026-10-03'], '2026-10-03')).toBe(3);
  });

  it('a streak survives until the day is over, then stops at a gap', () => {
    // Done yesterday, not yet today: the streak still shows.
    expect(streakFor(['2026-10-01', '2026-10-02'], '2026-10-03')).toBe(2);
    // A missed day breaks it.
    expect(streakFor(['2026-10-01', '2026-10-03'], '2026-10-03')).toBe(1);
    expect(streakFor([], '2026-10-03')).toBe(0);
  });
});

```

`tests/deferred.test.ts`:

```ts
// The cracker's guesser animation runs on a real timer. A player who starts typing while it is still running
// must not lose that keystroke: skip() finishes the animation immediately instead of waiting out the delay.

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { skippable } from '../src/lib/deferred';

describe('skippable', () => {
  it('runs the work once the delay elapses, if nothing skips it', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const fn = vi.fn();
    skippable(fn, 1000);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(999);
    expect(fn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('skip() runs the work immediately, so a keystroke never waits on the animation', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const timer = skippable(fn, 1000);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(10);
    timer.skip();
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('never runs the work twice: a natural fire after a skip, or two skips, both no-op', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const timer = skippable(fn, 1000);
    timer.skip();
    timer.skip();
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('skip() after the timer already fired on its own does nothing', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const timer = skippable(fn, 1000);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000);
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    timer.skip();
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });
});

```

`tests/engine.test.ts`:

```ts
// Engine-level tests: the parts of the game that are not tied to one mission.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fromBase64, fromHex, rot13, toBase64, toHex } from '../src/engine/encoding';
import { canRead, listDir, normalizePath } from '../src/engine/fs';
import { GameSession, splitCommand } from '../src/engine/session';
import { runLoginQuery, tokenize, type LoginRow } from '../src/engine/sql';
import { rankFor, scoreMission, type MissionResult } from '../src/engine/scoring';
import { DEFAULT_SAVE, loadSave, type SaveData } from '../src/lib/save';
import { clearedOnAnyTier, missionUnlocked, summarize } from '../src/lib/progress';
import { applyTier, DIFFICULTY, pointsFor } from '../src/engine/difficulty';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import type { MachineDef, Tier } from '../src/engine/types';

const ROWS: LoginRow[] = [
  { username: 'admin', password: 'Cr3st-Ledger!9' },
  { username: 'clerk', password: 'payday2024' },
];
const TEMPLATE = "SELECT id, username FROM users WHERE username = '{username}' AND password = '{password}'";

function result(score: number, tier: Tier = 'medium'): MissionResult {
  return { score, points: score, tier, seconds: 0, trace: 0, hints: 0 };
}

describe('quick-start briefing', () => {
  it('intro() is generic without quickBriefing', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: 'easy' });
    const lines = s.intro().map((l) => l.text);
    expect(lines.some((t) => t.includes(MISSIONS[0].codename))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('intro() prints the job and its opening scenario line when quickBriefing is set', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: 'easy', quickBriefing: true });
    const lines = s.intro().map((l) => l.text);
    expect(lines[0]).toContain(MISSIONS[0].codename);
    expect(lines[0]).toContain(MISSIONS[0].client);
    const scenario = typeof MISSIONS[0].briefing[0] === 'string' ? MISSIONS[0].briefing[0] : (MISSIONS[0].briefing[0] as { text: string }).text;
    expect(lines).toContain(scenario);
  });
});

describe('Hard Friday', () => {
  it('forces no hints and at least hard-tier trace speed, on every tier', () => {
    const friday = { ...MISSIONS[0], hardFriday: true };
    for (const tier of ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'] as Tier[]) {
      const s = new GameSession(friday, { tier });
      expect(s.hintLimit).toBe(0);
      expect(s.config.traceMultiplier).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(DIFFICULTY.hard.traceMultiplier);
    }
  });

  it('leaves an ordinary day alone', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: 'easy' });
    expect(s.hintLimit).toBe(3);
  });
});

describe('SQL login form', () => {
  it('accepts the right password', () => {
    const r = runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, 'clerk', 'payday2024', ROWS);
    expect(r.ok && r.user).toBe('clerk');
  });

  it('rejects a wrong password', () => {
    expect(runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, 'admin', 'nope', ROWS).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a lone quote breaks the string and returns a syntax error', () => {
    const r = runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, "admin'", 'x', ROWS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(!r.ok && r.error).toMatch(/syntax/i);
  });

  it('a tautology with a comment logs in as the first row, whatever the password', () => {
    const r = runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, "' OR '1'='1' --", 'anything', ROWS);
    expect(r.ok && r.user).toBe('admin');
  });

  it('AND binds tighter than OR, so a tautology without a comment still needs the password', () => {
    const r = runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, "' OR '1'='1", 'anything', ROWS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a numeric tautology in the password field works too', () => {
    const r = runLoginQuery(TEMPLATE, 'nobody', "' OR 1=1 --", ROWS);
    expect(r.ok && r.user).toBe('admin');
  });

  it('the SQL tokenizer treats -- and # as comments outside strings', () => {
    expect(tokenize("a = 'x' -- trailing").map((t) => t.value)).toEqual(['a', '=', 'x']);
    expect(tokenize("a = 'x' # trailing").map((t) => t.value)).toEqual(['a', '=', 'x']);
  });
});

describe('encodings', () => {
  it('round-trips base64, hex and rot13', () => {
    const text = 'BREACH{round_trip_ok}';
    expect(fromBase64(toBase64(text))).toBe(text);
    expect(fromHex(toHex(text))).toBe(text);
    expect(rot13(rot13(text))).toBe(text);
  });

  it('rejects malformed input instead of returning garbage', () => {
    expect(() => fromBase64('!!!not base64!!!')).toThrow();
    expect(() => fromHex('xyz')).toThrow();
  });
});

describe('paths and permissions', () => {
  const machine: MachineDef = {
    id: 'm',
    hostname: 'm',
    ip: '1.1.1.1',
    neighbors: [],
    defaultUser: 'guest',
    users: [],
    ports: [],
    files: {
      '/home/bob/secret.txt': { content: 's', owner: 'bob', private: true },
      '/home/guest/hi.txt': { content: 'hi' },
    },
    dirs: { '/home/bob': { owner: 'bob', private: true } },
  };

  it('normalises ., .., ~ and relative paths', () => {
    expect(normalizePath('../x/./y', '/a/b', '/home/g')).toBe('/a/x/y');
    expect(normalizePath('~/docs', '/', '/home/g')).toBe('/home/g/docs');
    expect(normalizePath('/../..', '/', '/')).toBe('/');
  });

  it('blocks private paths and their children for other users, but not for root', () => {
    expect(canRead(machine, '/home/bob/secret.txt', 'guest')).toBe(false);
    expect(canRead(machine, '/home/bob/secret.txt', 'bob')).toBe(true);
    expect(canRead(machine, '/home/bob/secret.txt', 'root')).toBe(true);
    expect(canRead(machine, '/home/guest/hi.txt', 'guest')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('lists implied directories', () => {
    expect(listDir(machine, '/home').map((e) => `${e.type}:${e.name}`)).toEqual(['dir:bob', 'dir:guest']);
  });
});

describe('shell splitting', () => {
  it('keeps quoted phrases together', () => {
    expect(splitCommand('grep "Accepted password" /var/log')).toEqual(['grep', 'Accepted password', '/var/log']);
    expect(splitCommand('  ls   -la  ')).toEqual(['ls', '-la']);
  });
});

describe('session behaviour', () => {
  const first = MISSIONS[0];

  it('starts on the rig with the prompt for its default user', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(first);
    expect(s.machine.id).toBe('rig');
    expect(s.prompt()).toBe('ghost@ghost-rig:~$ ');
  });

  it('fails the mission at 100% trace and then refuses input', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(first);
    let guard = 0;
    while (s.status === 'playing' && guard++ < 200) s.input('crack deadbeef');
    expect(s.status).toBe('failed');
    expect(s.trace).toBe(100);
    expect(s.input('ls').lines.some((l) => l.text.includes('Session closed'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('warns at 70% trace', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(first);
    const lines: string[] = [];
    let guard = 0;
    while (s.trace < 70 && guard++ < 50) lines.push(...s.input('crack deadbeef').lines.map((l) => l.text));
    expect(lines.some((t) => t.includes('TRACE 70%'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a running security process multiplies trace gain, and root-owned kills are refused for guest', () => {
    const pivot = MISSIONS[5];
    const s = new GameSession(pivot);
    s.input('connect 10.13.37.100');
    const before = s.trace;
    s.input('scan');
    expect(s.trace - before).toBe(8); // 5 * 1.5, rounded
    expect(s.input('kill 412').lines.some((l) => l.text.includes('Operation not permitted'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('recovery needs the answers, and a wrong answer fails the login', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[4]);
    s.input('connect 10.13.37.90');
    s.input('login rfoster');
    s.input('?');
    s.input('biscuit');
    s.input('lakeport');
    s.input('wrong');
    expect(s.frame.user).toBe('guest');
    expect(s.failedLogins).toBe(1);
  });

  it('the finale ignores out-of-range choices, then records the ending', () => {
    const finale = MISSIONS[7];
    const s = new GameSession(finale);
    for (const step of finale.solution.slice(0, -1)) s.input(step);
    expect(s.status).toBe('choosing');
    s.input('9');
    expect(s.status).toBe('choosing');
    s.input('3');
    expect(s.status).toBe('won');
    expect(s.endingId).toBe('sell');
  });

  it('tab completes commands and neighbour ips', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(first);
    expect(s.complete('sc').value).toBe('scan ');
    expect(s.complete('connect 10.13.37.').options).toEqual(['10.13.37.20']);
  });

  it('exit closes a remote session, and exit on the rig asks to abort', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(first);
    s.input('connect 10.13.37.20');
    expect(s.stack.length).toBe(2);
    s.input('exit');
    expect(s.stack.length).toBe(1);
    expect(s.input('exit').quit).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('scoring and ranks', () => {
  it('takes points for time, trace and hints, and never drops below 100', () => {
    expect(scoreMission(0, 0, 0)).toBe(1000);
    expect(scoreMission(60, 10, 1)).toBe(1000 - 20 - 20 - 120);
    expect(scoreMission(99999, 100, 3)).toBe(100);
  });

  it('Ghost needs every mission finished with strong scores', () => {
    const ids = ['a', 'b'];
    expect(rankFor({ a: result(900), b: result(900) }, ids).name).toBe('Ghost');
    expect(rankFor({ a: result(900) }, ids).name).not.toBe('Ghost');
  });
});

describe("save system and progress", () => {
  const blank = (): SaveData => ({ ...DEFAULT_SAVE, completed: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} }, attempts: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} } });

  it("returns fresh defaults when localStorage is unavailable (node test environment)", () => {
    expect(loadSave()).toEqual(blank());
  });

  it("unlocks missions in order, per tier", () => {
    const save = blank();
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, "medium", 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, "medium", 1)).toBe(false);
    save.completed.medium[MISSIONS[0].id] = result(500);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, "medium", 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, "hard", 1)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("summarises hints, average trace, points and the result grid for one tier", () => {
    const save = blank();
    save.completed.hard[MISSIONS[0].id] = { score: 900, points: 2250, tier: "hard", seconds: 1, trace: 10, hints: 0 };
    const summary = summarize(save, "hard");
    expect(summary.completed).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.avgTrace).toBe(10);
    expect(summary.points).toBe(2250);
    expect(summary.grid[0]).toBe("🟩");
    expect(summarize(save, "easy").completed).toBe(0);
  });

  it("theme unlocks count missions cleared on any tier", () => {
    const save = blank();
    save.completed.easy[MISSIONS[0].id] = result(500, "easy");
    save.completed.hard[MISSIONS[1].id] = result(500, "hard");
    expect(clearedOnAnyTier(save)).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("difficulty tiers", () => {
  it("applies tier overrides without touching the base mission", () => {
    const base = MISSIONS[0];
    const hard = applyTier(base, "hard");
    expect(hard).not.toBe(base);
    const baseFiles = base.machines.find((m) => m.id === "fenwick")!.files;
    const hardFiles = hard.machines.find((m) => m.id === "fenwick")!.files;
    expect(Object.keys(hardFiles).length).toBeGreaterThan(Object.keys(baseFiles).length);
    expect(Object.keys(base.machines.find((m) => m.id === "fenwick")!.files)).toEqual(Object.keys(baseFiles));
    expect(applyTier(base, "medium")).toBe(base);
  });

  it("every hard decoy is additive: base files and users remain", () => {
    for (const m of MISSIONS) {
      const hard = applyTier(m, "hard");
      for (const machine of m.machines) {
        const hm = hard.machines.find((x) => x.id === machine.id)!;
        for (const path of Object.keys(machine.files)) expect(hm.files[path]).toBeDefined();
        for (const user of machine.users) expect(hm.users.some((u) => u.name === user.name)).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });

  it("hard allows one hint; easy and medium allow three", () => {
    const hard = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "hard" });
    hard.input("hint");
    expect(hard.hintsUsed).toBe(1);
    expect(hard.input("hint").lines.some((l) => l.text.includes("No hints left"))).toBe(true);
    const medium = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "medium" });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) medium.input("hint");
    expect(medium.hintsUsed).toBe(3);
  });

  it("easy gains less trace than medium for the same noisy action", () => {
    const easy = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "easy" });
    const medium = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "medium" });
    easy.input("scan");
    medium.input("scan");
    expect(easy.trace).toBeLessThan(medium.trace);
  });

  it("hard gains more trace than medium for the same noisy action", () => {
    const hard = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "hard" });
    const medium = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "medium" });
    hard.input("scan");
    medium.input("scan");
    expect(hard.trace).toBeGreaterThan(medium.trace);
  });

  it("tab completes arguments on every tier, hard included", () => {
    for (const tier of ["easy", "medium", "hard"] as const) {
      const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier });
      expect(s.complete("sc").value).toBe("scan ");
      expect(s.complete("connect 10.13.37.").options).toEqual(["10.13.37.20"]);
    }
  });

  it("hard has a 20 minute clock that fails the attempt when it runs out", () => {
    let now = 0;
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "hard", now: () => now });
    expect(s.timeRemaining()).toBe(1200);
    now = 1199 * 1000;
    expect(s.expireIfLate()).toBe(false);
    now = 1200 * 1000;
    expect(s.expireIfLate()).toBe(true);
    expect(s.status).toBe("failed");
    expect(s.failReason).toBe("time");
  });

  it("medium and easy have no clock", () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[0], { tier: "medium", now: () => 10 ** 12 });
    expect(s.timeRemaining()).toBeNull();
    expect(s.expireIfLate()).toBe(false);
  });

  it("points are multiplied by the tier and rank is not", () => {
    expect(pointsFor(1000, "easy")).toBe(1000);
    expect(pointsFor(1000, "medium")).toBe(1500);
    expect(pointsFor(1000, "hard")).toBe(2500);
  });

  it("the alarm fires once at 75% trace", () => {
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[5], { tier: "medium" });
    s.trace = 74;
    const out = s.input("connect 10.13.37.100");
    const alarms = out.sounds.filter((x) => x === "alarm").length;
    expect(alarms).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("save migration", () => {
  it("version 1 progress becomes medium progress and mute becomes sfx off", () => {
    const v1 = { version: 1, completed: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 800, seconds: 1, trace: 2, hints: 1 } }, attempts: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: 2 }, muted: true, crt: false, theme: "amber", typing: "fast" };
    const store = new Map<string, string>([["breach.save.v1", JSON.stringify(v1)]]);
    (globalThis as unknown as { window: unknown }).window = { localStorage: { getItem: (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k: string, v: string) => store.set(k, v), removeItem: (k: string) => store.delete(k) } };
    try {
      const migrated = loadSave();
      expect(migrated.version).toBe(2);
      expect(migrated.completed.medium[MISSIONS[0].id].score).toBe(800);
      expect(migrated.attempts.medium[MISSIONS[0].id]).toBe(2);
      expect(migrated.sfx).toBe(false);
      expect(migrated.theme).toBe("amber");
    } finally {
      delete (globalThis as unknown as { window?: unknown }).window;
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/feedback.test.ts`:

```ts
// The feedback mailto: well-formed, prefilled with version and tier, mission included when given and left out otherwise.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildFeedbackMailto } from '../src/lib/feedback';
import { BUG_REPORT_CONTACT } from '../src/missions/shared';

function decode(mailto: string): { address: string; subject: string; body: string } {
  const [address, query] = mailto.replace('mailto:', '').split('?');
  const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
  return { address, subject: params.get('subject') ?? '', body: params.get('body') ?? '' };
}

describe('buildFeedbackMailto', () => {
  it('addresses the bug report contact', () => {
    const { address } = decode(buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '1.0.0', tier: 'medium' }));
    expect(address).toBe(BUG_REPORT_CONTACT);
  });

  it('is well-formed: a mailto URL with percent-encoded subject and body', () => {
    const mailto = buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '1.0.0', tier: 'hard', mission: 'Ghost Protocol' });
    expect(mailto.startsWith('mailto:')).toBe(true);
    expect(mailto).toContain('subject=');
    expect(mailto).toContain('body=');
    expect(mailto).not.toMatch(/[\s]/); // a raw space would break some mail clients; everything must be encoded
  });

  it('prefills the version and tier', () => {
    const { body } = decode(buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '2.3.4', tier: 'easy' }));
    expect(body).toContain('Version: 2.3.4');
    expect(body).toContain('Tier: easy');
  });

  it('includes the mission when given', () => {
    const { body } = decode(buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '1.0.0', tier: 'medium', mission: 'Lighthouse' }));
    expect(body).toContain('Mission: Lighthouse');
  });

  it('omits the mission line entirely when none is given, such as from Settings', () => {
    const { body } = decode(buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '1.0.0', tier: 'medium' }));
    expect(body).not.toContain('Mission:');
  });

  it('leaves room for the player to describe the issue', () => {
    const { body } = decode(buildFeedbackMailto({ version: '1.0.0', tier: 'medium' }));
    expect(body.toLowerCase()).toContain('describe');
  });
});

```

`tests/funnel.test.ts`:

```ts
// Anonymous funnel analytics: Do Not Track / GPC must fully silence it, a session id is per-tab and stable,
// events batch and flush through sendBeacon (falling back to a keepalive fetch), and nothing ever throws.

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

/** A minimal in-memory Storage, enough for getSessionId under Node. */
function fakeSessionStorage(): Storage {
  const store = new Map<string, string>();
  return {
    getItem: (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null,
    setItem: (k: string, v: string) => store.set(k, v),
    removeItem: (k: string) => store.delete(k),
    clear: () => store.clear(),
    key: (i: number) => [...store.keys()][i] ?? null,
    get length() {
      return store.size;
    },
  } as Storage;
}

function fakeWindow(sessionStorage: Storage) {
  return {
    sessionStorage,
    setTimeout: globalThis.setTimeout,
    clearTimeout: globalThis.clearTimeout,
    addEventListener: () => {},
  };
}

function fakeDocument() {
  return { visibilityState: 'visible', addEventListener: () => {} };
}

function fakeNavigator(opts: { doNotTrack?: string; globalPrivacyControl?: boolean; sendBeacon?: (url: string, data: unknown) => boolean } = {}) {
  return { doNotTrack: opts.doNotTrack, globalPrivacyControl: opts.globalPrivacyControl, sendBeacon: opts.sendBeacon };
}

async function loadWithEnv(opts: {
  configured?: boolean;
  sessionStorage?: Storage;
  navigator?: ReturnType<typeof fakeNavigator>;
  sendBeacon?: (url: string, data: unknown) => boolean;
}) {
  vi.resetModules();
  if (opts.configured ?? true) {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'https://example.supabase.test';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = 'sb_publishable_test';
  } else {
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
  }
  const storage = opts.sessionStorage ?? fakeSessionStorage();
  const nav = opts.navigator ?? fakeNavigator({ sendBeacon: opts.sendBeacon });
  vi.stubGlobal('window', fakeWindow(storage));
  vi.stubGlobal('document', fakeDocument());
  vi.stubGlobal('navigator', nav);
  return import('../src/lib/funnel');
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('funnelConfigured', () => {
  it('is true only when both env vars are set', async () => {
    const configured = await loadWithEnv({ configured: true });
    expect(configured.funnelConfigured()).toBe(true);
    const unconfigured = await loadWithEnv({ configured: false });
    expect(unconfigured.funnelConfigured()).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('privacySignalOn', () => {
  it('is on for Do Not Track "1" or "yes"', async () => {
    const mod1 = await loadWithEnv({ navigator: fakeNavigator({ doNotTrack: '1' }) });
    expect(mod1.privacySignalOn()).toBe(true);
    const mod2 = await loadWithEnv({ navigator: fakeNavigator({ doNotTrack: 'yes' }) });
    expect(mod2.privacySignalOn()).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is on for Global Privacy Control', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ navigator: fakeNavigator({ globalPrivacyControl: true }) });
    expect(mod.privacySignalOn()).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is off when neither signal is set', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ navigator: fakeNavigator({}) });
    expect(mod.privacySignalOn()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('fails closed (treated as on) if navigator cannot be read at all', async () => {
    vi.resetModules();
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'https://example.supabase.test';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = 'sb_publishable_test';
    vi.stubGlobal('window', fakeWindow(fakeSessionStorage()));
    vi.stubGlobal('document', fakeDocument());
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', undefined);
    const mod = await import('../src/lib/funnel');
    expect(mod.privacySignalOn()).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('getSessionId', () => {
  it('is stable across calls and persisted in sessionStorage, not localStorage', async () => {
    const storage = fakeSessionStorage();
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ sessionStorage: storage });
    const id = mod.getSessionId();
    expect(mod.getSessionId()).toBe(id);
    expect(storage.getItem('breach.session')).toBe(id);
  });
});

describe('track + flushFunnel', () => {
  it('does nothing — no sendBeacon, no storage write — when a privacy signal is on', async () => {
    const storage = fakeSessionStorage();
    let called = false;
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({
      sessionStorage: storage,
      navigator: fakeNavigator({ doNotTrack: '1', sendBeacon: () => ((called = true), true) }),
    });
    mod.track('landing_view');
    mod.flushFunnel();
    expect(called).toBe(false);
    expect(storage.getItem('breach.session')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('does nothing when the board is not configured', async () => {
    let called = false;
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ configured: false, sendBeacon: () => ((called = true), true) });
    mod.track('landing_view');
    mod.flushFunnel();
    expect(called).toBe(false);
  });

  it('batches queued events and sends them with sendBeacon, apikey on the URL since sendBeacon cannot set headers', async () => {
    const seen: { url: string; body: unknown } = { url: '', body: null };
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({
      sendBeacon: (url, data) => {
        seen.url = url;
        seen.body = JSON.parse(String(data));
        return true;
      },
    });
    mod.track('mission_started', { tier: 'medium', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop' });
    mod.track('hint_used', { tier: 'medium', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', value: 2 });
    mod.flushFunnel();

    const body = seen.body as { action: string; sessionId: string; events: unknown[] };
    expect(seen.url).toContain('/functions/v1/submit-run?apikey=sb_publishable_test');
    expect(body.action).toBe('events');
    expect(body.sessionId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i);
    expect(body.events).toEqual([
      { event: 'mission_started', tier: 'medium', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', value: undefined },
      { event: 'hint_used', tier: 'medium', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', value: 2 },
    ]);
  });

  it('auto-flushes once the batch reaches its cap, without waiting for the timer', async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ sendBeacon: () => ((calls += 1), true) });
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) mod.track('share_clicked');
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
  });

  it('falls back to a keepalive fetch when sendBeacon is unavailable', async () => {
    let fetchCalled = false;
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ navigator: fakeNavigator({}) }); // no sendBeacon
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', ((_url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
      fetchCalled = true;
      expect(init?.keepalive).toBe(true);
      return Promise.resolve(new Response('{}', { status: 200 }));
    }) as typeof fetch);
    mod.track('restore_used');
    mod.flushFunnel();
    expect(fetchCalled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('flushing an empty queue makes no network call', async () => {
    let called = false;
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({ sendBeacon: () => ((called = true), true) });
    mod.flushFunnel();
    expect(called).toBe(false);
  });

  it('never throws even if sendBeacon itself throws', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv({
      sendBeacon: () => {
        throw new Error('blocked');
      },
    });
    mod.track('share_clicked');
    expect(() => mod.flushFunnel()).not.toThrow();
  });
});

```

`tests/history-sync.test.ts`:

```ts
// The ops log, the cloud sync merge, the recovery key and restore rate limiting.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GameSession } from '../src/engine/session';
import { scoreMission } from '../src/engine/scoring';
import { validateRun, replayTimeline, type RunCommand } from '../src/engine/replay';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import { addAttempt, favouriteCommand, HISTORY_LIMIT, missionStats, mergeHistory, totalSeconds, type AttemptRecord } from '../src/lib/history';
import { mergeSaves } from '../src/lib/merge';
import { DEFAULT_SAVE, type SaveData } from '../src/lib/save';
import { admit, generateRecoveryKey, isWellFormedKey, KEY_WORDS, normalizeRecoveryKey, RESTORE_LIMIT } from '../src/lib/recoverykey';
import { seededRandom } from '../src/lib/daily';

function attempt(over: Partial<AttemptRecord> = {}): AttemptRecord {
  return {
    id: 'a1',
    at: '2026-10-04T10:00:00.000Z',
    missionId: MISSIONS[0].id,
    scope: 'campaign',
    tier: 'medium',
    attemptNo: 1,
    outcome: 'cleared',
    score: 900,
    points: 1350,
    seconds: 120,
    trace: 10,
    hints: 0,
    commands: [],
    ...over,
  };
}

const blank = (over: Partial<SaveData> = {}): SaveData => ({
  ...DEFAULT_SAVE,
  completed: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
  attempts: { easy: {}, medium: {}, hard: {} },
  ...over,
});

describe('ops log', () => {
  it('stats count attempts, first clear, best score and time, and clear rate', () => {
    const id = MISSIONS[0].id;
    const history = [
      attempt({ id: 'c', at: '2026-10-04T12:00:00.000Z', attemptNo: 3, score: 700, seconds: 200 }),
      attempt({ id: 'b', at: '2026-10-04T11:00:00.000Z', attemptNo: 2, outcome: 'traced', score: 0, seconds: 40 }),
      attempt({ id: 'a', at: '2026-10-04T10:00:00.000Z', attemptNo: 1, outcome: 'aborted', score: 0, seconds: 10 }),
    ];
    const s = missionStats(history, id, 'medium');
    expect(s.attempts).toBe(3);
    expect(s.firstClearAttempt).toBe(3);
    expect(s.bestScore).toBe(700);
    expect(s.bestSeconds).toBe(200);
    expect(s.clearRate).toBeCloseTo(1 / 3);
  });

  it('stats are per tier, and an uncleared job has no best', () => {
    const history = [attempt({ tier: 'hard', outcome: 'traced', score: 0 })];
    expect(missionStats(history, MISSIONS[0].id, 'medium').attempts).toBe(0);
    const hard = missionStats(history, MISSIONS[0].id, 'hard');
    expect(hard.bestScore).toBeNull();
    expect(hard.firstClearAttempt).toBeNull();
  });

  it('the favourite command is the most typed first word, and nothing is favourite before anything is typed', () => {
    expect(favouriteCommand([])).toBeNull();
    const cmds: RunCommand[] = [
      { t: 0, cmd: 'nmap' },
      { t: 1, cmd: 'ls -la' },
      { t: 2, cmd: 'ls' },
      { t: 3, cmd: 'cat x' },
    ];
    expect(favouriteCommand([attempt({ commands: cmds })])).toEqual({ name: 'ls', count: 2 });
  });

  it('adding an attempt keeps the newest first and caps the list', () => {
    let list: AttemptRecord[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_LIMIT + 5; i++) {
      list = addAttempt(list, attempt({ id: `x${i}`, at: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1, 0, i)).toISOString() }));
    }
    expect(list).toHaveLength(HISTORY_LIMIT);
    expect(list[0].id).toBe(`x${HISTORY_LIMIT + 4}`);
  });

  it('adding the same attempt twice does not duplicate it', () => {
    const list = addAttempt([attempt()], attempt());
    expect(list).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('total time sums every attempt once', () => {
    expect(totalSeconds([attempt({ seconds: 60 }), attempt({ id: 'b', seconds: 90 })])).toBe(150);
  });
});

describe('sync merge', () => {
  it('keeps the better result for a job when both copies have one', () => {
    const id = MISSIONS[0].id;
    const local = blank({ completed: { easy: {}, medium: { [id]: { score: 700, points: 1050, tier: 'medium', seconds: 1, trace: 1, hints: 0 } }, hard: {} } });
    const remote = blank({ completed: { easy: {}, medium: { [id]: { score: 900, points: 1350, tier: 'medium', seconds: 1, trace: 1, hints: 0 } }, hard: {} } });
    expect(mergeSaves(local, remote).completed.medium[id].points).toBe(1350);
    expect(mergeSaves(remote, local).completed.medium[id].points).toBe(1350);
  });

  it('achievements are the union of both copies', () => {
    const merged = mergeSaves(blank({ achievements: ['a', 'b'] }), blank({ achievements: ['b', 'c'] }));
    expect([...merged.achievements].sort()).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'c']);
  });

  it('history is the union by id, newest first', () => {
    const merged = mergeSaves(blank({ history: [attempt({ id: 'x', at: '2026-10-04T10:00:00.000Z' })] }), blank({ history: [attempt({ id: 'y', at: '2026-10-05T10:00:00.000Z' })] }));
    expect(merged.history.map((h) => h.id)).toEqual(['y', 'x']);
  });

  it('attempts and sudo counters only go up', () => {
    const id = MISSIONS[0].id;
    const merged = mergeSaves(blank({ attempts: { easy: {}, medium: { [id]: 3 }, hard: {} }, counters: { sudoTries: 2 } }), blank({ attempts: { easy: {}, medium: { [id]: 5 }, hard: {} }, counters: { sudoTries: 7 } }));
    expect(merged.attempts.medium[id]).toBe(5);
    expect(merged.counters.sudoTries).toBe(7);
  });

  it('device settings stay as they are on this device', () => {
    const merged = mergeSaves(blank({ theme: 'amber', volume: 0.2, track: 'cold-room' } as Partial<SaveData>), blank({ theme: 'green', volume: 0.9 }));
    expect(merged.theme).toBe('amber');
    expect(merged.volume).toBe(0.2);
  });

  it('dailies merge per date and tier, keeping the better result', () => {
    const r = (points: number) => ({ score: 500, points, tier: 'easy' as const, seconds: 1, trace: 1, hints: 0 });
    const merged = mergeSaves(blank({ dailies: { '2026-10-04': { easy: r(100) } } }), blank({ dailies: { '2026-10-04': { easy: r(300), hard: r(50) } } }));
    expect(merged.dailies['2026-10-04'].easy!.points).toBe(300);
    expect(merged.dailies['2026-10-04'].hard!.points).toBe(50);
  });
});

describe('recovery keys', () => {
  it('a generated key has four words and four digits, and is well formed', () => {
    const key = generateRecoveryKey(seededRandom(11));
    expect(isWellFormedKey(key)).toBe(true);
    const parts = key.split('-');
    expect(parts).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(KEY_WORDS).toContain(parts[0]);
    expect(parts[4]).toMatch(/^\d{4}$/);
  });

  it('the same seed gives the same key, and different seeds give different keys', () => {
    expect(generateRecoveryKey(seededRandom(5))).toBe(generateRecoveryKey(seededRandom(5)));
    const keys = new Set(Array.from({ length: 200 }, (_, i) => generateRecoveryKey(seededRandom(i + 1))));
    expect(keys.size).toBeGreaterThan(190);
  });

  it('typing the key loosely still matches: case, spaces and underscores', () => {
    expect(normalizeRecoveryKey('  ghost amber_falcon CEDAR 7419 ')).toBe('GHOST-AMBER-FALCON-CEDAR-7419');
    expect(isWellFormedKey('ghost amber falcon cedar 7419')).toBe(true);
    expect(isWellFormedKey('GHOST-AMBER-7419')).toBe(false);
    expect(isWellFormedKey('')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('restore rate limiting', () => {
  const opts = RESTORE_LIMIT;

  it('allows the first ten attempts in an hour and refuses the eleventh', () => {
    let bucket = null as ReturnType<typeof admit>['next'] | null;
    const results: boolean[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
      const r = admit(bucket, 1000 + i, opts);
      results.push(r.allowed);
      bucket = r.next;
    }
    expect(results.slice(0, 10).every(Boolean)).toBe(true);
    expect(results[10]).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refused attempts keep counting, so hammering does not reset the window', () => {
    let bucket = null as ReturnType<typeof admit>['next'] | null;
    for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) bucket = admit(bucket, 1000 + i, opts).next;
    expect(bucket!.hits).toBe(25);
    expect(admit(bucket, 2000, opts).allowed).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a new window opens after an hour', () => {
    const full = { windowStart: 0, hits: 99 };
    expect(admit(full, RESTORE_LIMIT.windowMs - 1, opts).allowed).toBe(false);
    expect(admit(full, RESTORE_LIMIT.windowMs, opts).allowed).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('replays match the original run', () => {
  const mission = MISSIONS[0];
  const steps = mission.kaliSolution;

  it('the replayed output equals what the original attempt printed, line for line', () => {
    // The original attempt: typed live at one command a second.
    let clock = 0;
    const live = new GameSession(mission, { tier: 'medium', now: () => clock });
    const commands: RunCommand[] = steps.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd }));
    const liveLines = commands.map((c) => {
      clock = c.t;
      return live.input(c.cmd).lines.map((l) => l.text);
    });

    const timeline = replayTimeline(mission, 'medium', commands);
    expect(timeline.map((s) => s.lines.map((l) => l.text))).toEqual(liveLines);
  });

  it('the replay reaches the same result and score the validator computes', () => {
    const commands: RunCommand[] = steps.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd }));
    const verdict = validateRun({ missionId: mission.id, tier: 'medium', commands }, MISSIONS);
    expect(verdict.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!verdict.ok) return;
    const last = replayTimeline(mission, 'medium', commands).at(-1)!;
    expect(last.lines.some((l) => l.text.includes('FLAG ACCEPTED'))).toBe(true);
    expect(verdict.score).toBe(scoreMission(verdict.seconds, verdict.trace, verdict.hints));
  });
});

describe('best streak', () => {
  it('counts the longest run of consecutive days, not the current one', async () => {
    const { bestStreak } = await import('../src/lib/daily');
    expect(bestStreak(['2026-10-01', '2026-10-02', '2026-10-03', '2026-10-09', '2026-10-10'])).toBe(3);
    expect(bestStreak([])).toBe(0);
    expect(bestStreak(['2026-10-04'])).toBe(1);
    expect(bestStreak(['2026-10-04', '2026-10-04'])).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`tests/kali.test.ts`:

```ts
// Real Kali-style commands: pipes, ls -l, cd -, find, the network tools, sudo, ssh with a user, and the aliases.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GameSession, splitPipeline } from '../src/engine/session';
import { rankForScore } from '../src/engine/scoring';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';

const FIRST = MISSIONS[0];
const PASSWORD_MISSION = MISSIONS[4];
const PORTAL_IP = '10.13.37.90';
const MIRROR_IP = '10.13.37.91';

function textsOf(s: GameSession, line: string): string[] {
  return s.input(line).lines.map((l) => l.text);
}

describe('pipelines', () => {
  it('splits on pipes outside quotes only', () => {
    expect(splitPipeline('cat a | grep "x|y" | wc -l')).toEqual(['cat a', 'grep "x|y"', 'wc -l']);
  });

  it('cat feeds grep', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /srv/portal/logs/access.log | grep rfoster')).toContain('GET /login 200 rfoster');
  });

  it('echo feeds base64 -d and tr', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'echo aGVsbG8= | base64 -d')).toContain('hello');
    expect(textsOf(s, "echo Uryyb | tr 'A-Za-z' 'N-ZA-Mn-za-m'")).toContain('Hello');
  });

  it('head, tail and wc count lines of a pipe', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /srv/portal/logs/access.log | wc -l')).toContain('3');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /srv/portal/logs/access.log | head -n 1')).toContain('GET /statement 200 rfoster');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /srv/portal/logs/access.log | tail -n 1')).toContain('GET /login 200 rfoster');
  });

  it('a failing first command stops the pipeline and shows its error', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'cat nope.txt | wc -l');
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.includes('No such file'))).toBe(true);
    expect(lines.some((l) => /^\d+$/.test(l))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('a pipe with an empty side is a syntax error', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'ls |')).toContain("bash: syntax error near unexpected token `|'");
  });
});

describe('ls, cd and find', () => {
  it('ls -l shows permissions, owners and sizes', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'ls -la /home');
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.startsWith('drwxr-xr-x') && l.endsWith('guest/'))).toBe(true);
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.startsWith('drwx------') && l.endsWith('rfoster/'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('colours directories blue and executables green in ls', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION, { tier: 'medium' });
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    const out = s.input('ls /srv/portal');
    const line = out.lines.find((l) => l.segments?.some((p) => p.kind === 'dir'));
    expect(line?.segments?.find((p) => p.kind === 'dir')?.text).toBe('logs/');
  });

  it('marks the executable backup script green in ls -l', () => {
    // The scripts folder belongs to deploy, so log in as deploy first (the first five steps of the walkthrough).
    const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[5]);
    for (const step of MISSIONS[5].solution.slice(0, 5)) s.input(step);
    expect(s.frame.user).toBe('deploy');
    const out = s.input('ls -l /opt/deploy/scripts');
    expect(out.lines.some((l) => l.segments?.some((p) => p.kind === 'exec' && p.text === 'backup.sh'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('cd - goes back to the last directory', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    s.input('cd /home');
    s.input('cd /');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cd -')).toContain('/home');
    expect(s.frame.cwd).toBe('/home');
  });

  it('cd .. and cd ~ work', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    s.input('cd ..');
    expect(s.frame.cwd).toBe('/home');
    s.input('cd ~');
    expect(s.frame.cwd).toBe('/home/ghost');
  });

  it('find lists files, filters by name and type', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'find /srv -name "*.log"')).toContain('/srv/portal/logs/access.log');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'find /srv -type d')).toContain('/srv/portal/logs');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'find /srv -type d').some((l) => l.endsWith('.log'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('find does not show what the user cannot read', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'find /home -name flag.txt').some((l) => l.includes('rfoster'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('network tools', () => {
  it('nmap -sV prints service versions and pings once per open port', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    const out = s.input(`nmap -sV ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(out.lines.some((l) => l.text.startsWith('PORT') && l.text.includes('VERSION'))).toBe(true);
    expect(out.sounds.filter((x) => x === 'ping').length).toBe(2);
  });

  it('plain nmap with an ip has no VERSION column', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    const lines = textsOf(s, `nmap ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.startsWith('PORT') && !l.includes('VERSION'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('scan is an alias of nmap, and connect of ssh', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(s.input('scan').lines.some((l) => l.text.includes('Nmap scan report'))).toBe(true);
    s.input('connect 10.13.37.20');
    expect(s.stack.length).toBe(2);
  });

  it('ssh user@ip connects, then asks for that user password', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`ssh rfoster@${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(s.stack.length).toBe(2);
    expect(s.inputMode()).toBe('password');
    s.input('Xk!29pQ-bunny');
    expect(s.frame.user).toBe('rfoster');
  });

  it('ssh without a user lands as the default user with no prompt', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`ssh ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(s.inputMode()).toBe('shell');
    expect(s.frame.user).toBe('guest');
  });

  it('ifconfig and ip a show the machine address', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'ifconfig').some((l) => l.includes('inet 10.66.0.1'))).toBe(true);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'ip a').some((l) => l.includes('10.66.0.1/24'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('ping reaches a neighbour, and says so for a host that is not one', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'ping -c 2 10.13.37.20').some((l) => l.includes('2 packets transmitted, 2 received'))).toBe(true);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'ping 10.99.99.99').some((l) => l.includes('Destination Host Unreachable'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('sudo and man', () => {
  it('sudo is refused with the joke message for an account not in sudoers', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'sudo cat /etc/shadow');
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.includes('is not in the sudoers file. This incident will be reported.'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('sudo runs the command as root for a sudoer', () => {
    const base = FIRST.machines.find((m) => m.id === 'rig')!;
    const rigWithSudo = { ...base, users: base.users.map((u) => ({ ...u, sudo: true })), files: { ...base.files, '/root/secret.txt': { content: 'root only' } } };
    const mission = { ...FIRST, machines: FIRST.machines.map((m) => (m.id === 'rig' ? rigWithSudo : m)) };
    const s = new GameSession(mission);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'sudo cat /root/secret.txt')).toContain('root only');
    expect(s.frame.user).toBe('ghost');
  });

  it('man prints a manual page with name, synopsis and example', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'man nmap');
    expect(lines).toContain('NAME');
    expect(lines).toContain('SYNOPSIS');
    expect(lines.some((l) => l.includes('alias of nmap') || l.includes('nmap - '))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('man on an alias says what it is an alias of', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'help scan').some((l) => l.includes('alias of nmap'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('aliases and names', () => {
  it('john, hashcat and crack all run the same attack', () => {
    const hash = MISSIONS[1].kaliSolution.find((step) => step.startsWith('john '))!.slice('john '.length);
    for (const name of ['john', 'hashcat', 'crack']) {
      const s = new GameSession(MISSIONS[1]);
      expect(textsOf(s, `${name} ${hash}`).some((l) => l.startsWith('MATCH'))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('su and login are the same command', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`ssh ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    s.input('su rfoster');
    expect(s.inputMode()).toBe('password');
  });

  it('tab completes real names and aliases', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(s.complete('nm').value).toBe('nmap ');
    expect(s.complete('ss').value).toBe('ssh ');
  });

  it('tab completes a neighbour after user@ in ssh', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(s.complete('ssh bob@10.13.37.').value).toBe('ssh bob@10.13.37.20 ');
  });

  it('tab completes the command after a pipe', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(s.complete('cat notes.txt | gr').value).toBe('cat notes.txt | grep ');
  });

  it('a typo of a real name still gets a suggestion', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'nmpa').some((l) => l.includes('Did you mean') && l.includes('nmap'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('ranks are per mission', () => {
  it('a clean run without hints is not Script Kiddie', () => {
    expect(rankForScore(950).name).not.toBe('Script Kiddie');
    expect(rankForScore(950).name).toBe('Ghost');
  });

  it('a weak run ranks low on its own', () => {
    expect(rankForScore(100).name).toBe('Script Kiddie');
  });
});

describe('prompt', () => {
  it('splits the prompt into parts for colouring, root shows #', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    const parts = s.promptParts();
    expect(parts).toMatchObject({ user: 'ghost', host: 'ghost-rig', path: '~', symbol: '$', root: false });
    s.input('cd /');
    expect(s.promptParts().path).toBe('/');
  });

  it('the host in the prompt changes with the machine', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`ssh ${MIRROR_IP}`);
    expect(s.promptParts().host).toBe('social-mirror');
  });
});
```

`tests/keyboard-sound.test.ts`:

```ts
// The keyboard sound and haptics: which keys get a sound, that haptics respects its toggle and throttle without a
// real device, and the new settings default to the values the feature asks for (sound on at a moderate level,
// haptics off). Synthesis itself needs a browser and is not tested here, same as the rest of audio.ts.

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { keyVariantFor, play } from '../src/lib/audio';
import { DEFAULT_SAVE } from '../src/lib/save';

describe('keyVariantFor', () => {
  it('regular printable keys get the plain click', () => {
    for (const key of ['a', 'Z', '5', '-', '.', '?']) expect(keyVariantFor(key)).toBe('key');
  });

  it('space, Enter and Backspace each get their own variant', () => {
    expect(keyVariantFor(' ')).toBe('key-space');
    expect(keyVariantFor('Enter')).toBe('key-enter');
    expect(keyVariantFor('Backspace')).toBe('key-backspace');
  });

  it('modifiers, navigation and function keys get no sound', () => {
    for (const key of ['Tab', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Control', 'Shift', 'Alt', 'Meta', 'Escape', 'F1', 'CapsLock', 'Home']) {
      expect(keyVariantFor(key), key).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe('play() with the new key variants never throws, even without a browser', () => {
  it('each key variant is a safe no-op under Node (no AudioContext)', () => {
    for (const variant of ['key', 'key-space', 'key-enter', 'key-backspace'] as const) {
      expect(() => play(variant)).not.toThrow();
    }
  });
});

describe('default save: keyboard sound on at a moderate level, haptics off', () => {
  it('keyboard sounds default on', () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_SAVE.keySounds).toBe(true);
  });

  it('the default level is moderate: not silent, not maxed', () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_SAVE.keySoundsVolume).toBeGreaterThan(0.2);
    expect(DEFAULT_SAVE.keySoundsVolume).toBeLessThan(0.8);
  });

  it('haptics default off', () => {
    expect(DEFAULT_SAVE.keyHaptics).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('haptics', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    vi.resetModules();
  });

  it('is reported unsupported when navigator.vibrate does not exist', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', {});
    const { hapticsSupported } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    expect(hapticsSupported()).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is reported supported when navigator.vibrate exists', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', { vibrate: vi.fn() });
    const { hapticsSupported } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    expect(hapticsSupported()).toBe(true);
  });

  it('does nothing while disabled, even on a device that supports it', async () => {
    const vibrate = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', { vibrate });
    const { vibrateKey } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    vibrateKey();
    expect(vibrate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('vibrates once enabled, and is throttled so holding a key does not buzz continuously', async () => {
    const vibrate = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', { vibrate });
    const { setHapticsEnabled, vibrateKey } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    setHapticsEnabled(true);
    vibrateKey();
    vibrateKey(); // immediately again: throttled
    expect(vibrate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(40);
    vibrateKey();
    expect(vibrate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('turning the toggle off stops further vibration', async () => {
    const vibrate = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', { vibrate });
    const { setHapticsEnabled, vibrateKey } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    setHapticsEnabled(true);
    vibrateKey();
    setHapticsEnabled(false);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(40);
    vibrateKey();
    expect(vibrate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('a vibrate call that throws is swallowed, not propagated', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('navigator', {
      vibrate: () => {
        throw new Error('blocked');
      },
    });
    const { setHapticsEnabled, vibrateKey } = await import('../src/lib/haptics');
    setHapticsEnabled(true);
    expect(() => vibrateKey()).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

`tests/landing.test.ts`:

```ts
// The landing page terminal: fun answers for curious visitors, and the start and clear actions.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { landingReply, TAGLINE } from '../src/lib/landing';

const texts = (raw: string) => landingReply(raw).lines.map((l) => l.text).join('\n');

describe('landing terminal', () => {
  it('start, hack and play begin the game', () => {
    for (const word of ['start', 'hack', 'play']) {
      expect(landingReply(word).action, word).toBe('start');
    }
  });

  it('help lists what visitors can try', () => {
    expect(texts('help')).toContain('start');
    expect(texts('help')).toContain('sudo');
  });

  it('whoami and sudo answer with personality, not an error', () => {
    expect(texts('whoami')).toContain('curious visitor');
    expect(texts('sudo')).toContain('This incident will be reported.');
    expect(texts('sudo rm -rf /')).toContain('This incident will be reported.');
  });

  it('rm -rf gets a dry answer', () => {
    expect(texts('rm -rf /')).toContain('Nice try');
  });

  it('a real attack command nudges toward start', () => {
    expect(texts('nmap 10.0.0.1')).toContain('start a job first');
  });

  it('clear empties the log without a line of its own', () => {
    expect(landingReply('clear')).toEqual({ lines: [], action: 'clear' });
  });

  it('an unknown command gets a shell-style error with a way forward', () => {
    const reply = landingReply('frobnicate');
    expect(reply.lines[0].kind).toBe('err');
    expect(reply.lines[0].text).toContain('command not found');
    expect(reply.lines[0].text).toContain('start');
  });

  it('an empty line says nothing', () => {
    expect(landingReply('   ')).toEqual({ lines: [] });
  });

  it('the tagline is the one the page types out', () => {
    expect(TAGLINE.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);
  });
});
```

`tests/leaderboard.test.ts`:

```ts
// startRun's cold-start retry, and the player-id bug where a recovery-key restore left getPlayerId() still
// returning the fresh id this device made up for itself before the restore — which belongs to no account,
// so submitting under it collides with the real account's nickname. Network calls are stubbed.

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

type Fetch = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

function reply(status: number, body: unknown): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
}

/** A minimal in-memory Storage, enough for getPlayerId/setPlayerId under Node. */
function fakeLocalStorage(): Storage {
  const store = new Map<string, string>();
  return {
    getItem: (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null,
    setItem: (k: string, v: string) => store.set(k, v),
    removeItem: (k: string) => store.delete(k),
    clear: () => store.clear(),
    key: (i: number) => [...store.keys()][i] ?? null,
    get length() {
      return store.size;
    },
  } as Storage;
}

async function loadWithEnv(configured: boolean, localStorage: Storage | undefined = undefined) {
  vi.resetModules();
  if (configured) {
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL = 'https://example.supabase.test';
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = 'sb_publishable_test';
  } else {
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
    delete process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
  }
  vi.stubGlobal('window', { setTimeout: globalThis.setTimeout, clearTimeout: globalThis.clearTimeout, localStorage });
  return import('../src/lib/leaderboard');
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('startRun: cold-start retry', () => {
  it('retries once after a failed first attempt and returns the token from the second', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true);
    let calls = 0;
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => {
      calls++;
      if (calls === 1) throw new TypeError('network timeout');
      return reply(200, { ok: true, token: 'tok-1', seed: 42 });
    }) as Fetch);
    const started = await mod.startRun({ playerId: 'p1', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', tier: 'medium', scope: 'campaign' });
    expect(calls).toBe(2);
    expect(started).toEqual({ token: 'tok-1', seed: 42 });
  });

  it('returns null only once both attempts fail', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true);
    let calls = 0;
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => {
      calls++;
      throw new TypeError('network down');
    }) as Fetch);
    const started = await mod.startRun({ playerId: 'p1', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', tier: 'medium', scope: 'campaign' });
    expect(calls).toBe(2);
    expect(started).toBeNull();
  });

  it('fails closed immediately, with no network call, when the board is not configured', async () => {
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(false);
    let calls = 0;
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async () => {
      calls++;
      return reply(200, {});
    }) as Fetch);
    const started = await mod.startRun({ playerId: 'p1', missionId: 'm01-dead-drop', tier: 'medium', scope: 'campaign' });
    expect(started).toBeNull();
    expect(calls).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('player id: a restore adopts the account id', () => {
  it('setPlayerId overwrites what getPlayerId returns afterwards', async () => {
    const storage = fakeLocalStorage();
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true, storage);
    const freshId = mod.getPlayerId(); // as if this were a brand-new browser, before any restore
    expect(storage.getItem('breach.player')).toBe(freshId);

    const serverId = 'aaaaaaaa-bbbb-4ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee';
    mod.setPlayerId(serverId);
    expect(mod.getPlayerId()).toBe(serverId);
    expect(mod.getPlayerId()).not.toBe(freshId);
  });

  it('restore on a fresh device, then submit, must succeed: the submission uses the restored id, not the device\'s own', async () => {
    const storage = fakeLocalStorage();
    const mod = await loadWithEnv(true, storage);

    // A fresh browser makes up its own id the first time anything asks for one.
    const freshId = mod.getPlayerId();
    // adoptRestored's fix: adopt the account's real id once a recovery key resolves.
    const serverId = 'aaaaaaaa-bbbb-4ccc-8ddd-eeeeeeeeeeee';
    mod.setPlayerId(serverId);

    let seenPlayerId: string | null = null;
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', (async (_url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const body = JSON.parse(String(init?.body ?? '{}')) as { playerId?: string };
      seenPlayerId = body.playerId ?? null;
      // What the server would actually do: the stale fresh id has never submitted, so it tries to create a
      // new player row with the restored nickname — which the real account already holds.
      if (body.playerId === freshId) return reply(409, { ok: false, reason: 'that nickname is taken' });
      return reply(200, { ok: true, improved: true, score: 900, points: 900 });
    }) as Fetch);

    const sub = mod.buildSubmission({
      playerId: mod.getPlayerId(),
      nickname: 'restored-handle',
      scope: 'campaign',
      missionId: 'm01-dead-drop',
      tier: 'medium',
      commands: [{ t: 0, cmd: 'scan' }],
      token: 'tok-1',
    });
    const result = await mod.submitRun(sub);

    expect(seenPlayerId).toBe(serverId);
    expect(seenPlayerId).not.toBe(freshId);
    expect(result.status).toBe('saved');
  });
});

```

`tests/missions.test.ts`:

```ts
// Every mission must be beatable on every difficulty tier. Each mission is played through its own `solution`,
// typed exactly as a player would type it, and must end in status "won" with trace under 100.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GameSession } from '../src/engine/session';
import { TIERS } from '../src/engine/difficulty';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import type { MissionDef, Tier } from '../src/engine/types';

/** Index of the flag submission in a solution: everything before it is the setup, everything after it is the ending. */
const flagStep = (m: MissionDef): number => m.solution.findIndex((s) => s.startsWith('submit '));

describe('mission data', () => {
  it('has unique ids and consecutive numbers', () => {
    const ids = MISSIONS.map((m) => m.id);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    MISSIONS.forEach((m, i) => expect(m.number).toBe(i + 1));
  });

  it.each(MISSIONS.map((m) => [m.codename, m] as const))('%s has briefing, debrief, three hints and a rig', (_name, m) => {
    expect(m.briefing.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(m.debrief.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(m.hints).toHaveLength(3);
    m.hints.forEach((h) => expect(h.trim().length).toBeGreaterThan(0));
    expect(m.machines.some((d) => d.id === 'rig')).toBe(true);
    expect(m.machines.some((d) => d.id === m.start.machine)).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each(MISSIONS.map((m) => [m.codename, m] as const))('%s: every machine ip is unique and every neighbour exists', (_name, m) => {
    const ips = m.machines.map((d) => d.ip);
    expect(new Set(ips).size).toBe(ips.length);
    for (const d of m.machines) {
      for (const n of d.neighbors) expect(ips, `${d.hostname} -> ${n}`).toContain(n);
    }
  });

  it.each(MISSIONS.map((m) => [m.codename, m] as const))('%s: tier overrides only reference machines that exist', (_name, m) => {
    const ids = m.machines.map((d) => d.id);
    for (const tier of Object.keys(m.tierOverrides ?? {})) {
      for (const id of Object.keys(m.tierOverrides?.[tier as Tier]?.machines ?? {})) {
        expect(ids, `${tier} override for ${id}`).toContain(id);
      }
    }
  });
});

describe.each(TIERS)('%s tier', (tier) => {
  describe.each(MISSIONS.map((m) => [m.number, m.codename, m] as const))('Mission %i %s', (_number, _codename, mission) => {
    it('is beatable with its intended solution', () => {
      const session = new GameSession(mission, { tier });
      for (const step of mission.solution) {
        expect(session.finished, `session ended before "${step}"`).toBe(false);
        session.input(step);
      }
      expect(session.status).toBe('won');
      expect(session.trace).toBeLessThan(100);
    });

    it('is beatable with the same walkthrough typed as real Kali commands', () => {
      const session = new GameSession(mission, { tier });
      for (const step of mission.kaliSolution) {
        expect(session.finished, `session ended before "${step}"`).toBe(false);
        session.input(step);
      }
      expect(session.status).toBe('won');
      expect(session.trace).toBeLessThan(100);
    });

    it('does not accept a wrong flag', () => {
      const session = new GameSession(mission, { tier });
      const stop = flagStep(mission);
      expect(stop).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
      for (const step of mission.solution.slice(0, stop)) session.input(step);

      const out = session.input('submit BREACH{definitely_not_it}');
      expect(out.lines.some((l) => l.text.includes('not the flag'))).toBe(true);
      expect(session.status).toBe('playing');
    });
  });
});
```
