`tests/music.test.ts`:

```ts
// The soundtrack's score, checked without audio: key, register, density, variation, the tension curve and the layer mix.
// Whether it sounds good is a listening question. These tests catch wrong notes, harsh registers, short loops and levels
// that would break the mix.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { heartbeatBpm, layerMix, LOOP_BARS, midiToHz, seededRand, tempoFor, tensionEvents, trackEvents, type NoteEvent } from '../src/lib/music';
import type { MusicTrack } from '../src/lib/save';

const TRACKS: MusicTrack[] = ['night-shift', 'signal', 'cold-room'];
// D minor: D E F G A Bb C. The A7 chord adds C# and B, its dominant colour, which leads back home to Dm.
const D_MINOR_PITCH_CLASSES = new Set([0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11]);
const bar = (track: MusicTrack, n: number, seed = 1) => trackEvents(track, n, seededRand(seed + n * 97));

function pitched(events: NoteEvent[]): number[] {
  return events.filter((e) => e.note !== undefined).map((e) => e.note!);
}

describe('tracks', () => {
  it.each(TRACKS)('%s: every pitched note stays in the key of D minor', (track) => {
    for (let n = 0; n < LOOP_BARS * 2; n++) {
      for (const note of pitched(bar(track, n))) {
        expect(D_MINOR_PITCH_CLASSES.has(((note % 12) + 12) % 12), `${track} bar ${n} note ${note}`).toBe(true);
      }
    }
  });

  it.each(TRACKS)('%s: nothing sits above 2 kHz, so the mix stays soft', (track) => {
    for (let n = 0; n < LOOP_BARS; n++) {
      for (const note of pitched(bar(track, n))) expect(midiToHz(note), `${track} ${note}`).toBeLessThan(2600);
    }
  });

  it.each(TRACKS)('%s: stays sparse, with well under sixteen events a bar', (track) => {
    for (let n = 0; n < 32; n++) expect(bar(track, n).length, `${track} bar ${n}`).toBeLessThan(16);
  });

  it.each(TRACKS)('%s: no obvious loop, so a long stretch does not repeat itself', (track) => {
    // Compare the first loop with the second, bar by bar. Identical bars would make a loop you can hear.
    const first = Array.from({ length: LOOP_BARS }, (_, n) => JSON.stringify(bar(track, n, 3)));
    const second = Array.from({ length: LOOP_BARS }, (_, n) => JSON.stringify(bar(track, n + LOOP_BARS, 9)));
    const same = first.filter((x, i) => x === second[i]).length;
    expect(same / LOOP_BARS).toBeLessThan(0.5);
  });

  it.each(TRACKS)('%s: the same seed gives the same bar', (track) => {
    expect(bar(track, 4, 7)).toEqual(bar(track, 4, 7));
  });

  it('the tracks sound different: different tempos and different note mixes', () => {
    expect(new Set(TRACKS.map(tempoFor)).size).toBe(3);
    const sizes = TRACKS.map((t) => bar(t, 0, 5).length);
    expect(new Set(sizes).size).toBeGreaterThan(1);
  });

  it('Night Shift has pads and a sub pulse, Signal has arpeggio and noise, Cold Room has drones', () => {
    const kinds = (t: MusicTrack) => new Set(Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, n) => bar(t, n, 2)).flat().map((e) => e.kind));
    expect(kinds('night-shift').has('pad') && kinds('night-shift').has('sub')).toBe(true);
    expect(kinds('signal').has('arp') && kinds('signal').has('noise')).toBe(true);
    expect(kinds('cold-room').has('drone')).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('tension layer', () => {
  it('is silent on menus (zero intensity)', () => {
    expect(tensionEvents(0, seededRand(1))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('the heartbeat quickens as the trace rises: 50 bpm at zero, 100 at full', () => {
    expect(heartbeatBpm(0)).toBe(50);
    expect(heartbeatBpm(1)).toBe(100);
    expect(heartbeatBpm(0.5)).toBeGreaterThan(heartbeatBpm(0.1));
  });

  it('more heartbeat pulses and more ticks at high intensity than low', () => {
    const count = (t: number, kind: string) => tensionEvents(t, seededRand(4)).filter((e) => e.kind === kind).length;
    expect(count(0.9, 'heartbeat')).toBeGreaterThan(count(0.2, 'heartbeat'));
    expect(count(0.9, 'tick')).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(count(0.2, 'tick'));
  });
});

describe('layers', () => {
  it('menus are calm: no tension, the filter closed', () => {
    const mix = layerMix(0);
    expect(mix.tension).toBe(0);
    expect(mix.cutoff).toBeLessThan(1200);
  });

  it('a high trace brings the tension up and opens the filter, monotonically', () => {
    const steps = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map(layerMix);
    for (let i = 1; i < steps.length; i++) {
      expect(steps[i].tension).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(steps[i - 1].tension);
      expect(steps[i].cutoff).toBeGreaterThan(steps[i - 1].cutoff);
    }
  });

  it('the filter never opens past 5 kHz, so the top stays soft', () => {
    expect(layerMix(1).cutoff).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5000);
  });

  it('out-of-range intensities are clamped', () => {
    expect(layerMix(-5)).toEqual(layerMix(0));
    expect(layerMix(5)).toEqual(layerMix(1));
  });

  it('A4 is 440 Hz', () => {
    expect(midiToHz(69)).toBeCloseTo(440, 6);
  });
});

```

`tests/origins.test.ts`:

```ts
// CORS origin matching: exact hosts, one safe wildcard label, and the tricks that must stay rejected.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { corsHeaders, originAllowed, parseAllowedOrigins } from '../src/lib/origins';

const ALLOWED = ['https://breachgame.xyz', 'https://*.itch.zone', 'https://*.crazygames.com', 'https://crazygames.com', 'http://localhost:3000'];

describe('parseAllowedOrigins', () => {
  it('splits, trims and drops empty entries', () => {
    expect(parseAllowedOrigins(' https://a.test , https://b.test ,, ')).toEqual(['https://a.test', 'https://b.test']);
  });

  it('an empty string parses to no origins', () => {
    expect(parseAllowedOrigins('')).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('originAllowed: the hosts this game actually needs', () => {
  it('matches the exact site', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://breachgame.xyz', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches itch.io\'s game-serving subdomain, and other itch.zone subdomains', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://html-classic.itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
    expect(originAllowed('https://v-123.itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches a CrazyGames subdomain and the bare domain', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://games.crazygames.com', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
    expect(originAllowed('https://crazygames.com', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('matches localhost for local development', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('http://localhost:3000', ALLOWED)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('originAllowed: everything else stays rejected', () => {
  it('rejects an unrelated origin', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://evil.example', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a bare wildcard domain that was not itself listed', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a suffix trick: the real domain tacked onto an attacker domain', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://itch.zone.evil.example', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a prefix trick: the real domain used as a path or query on an attacker origin', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://evil.example/itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
    expect(originAllowed('https://evil.example?x=https://real.itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects more than one subdomain label where the pattern allows only one', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://a.b.itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects the wrong scheme, since Origin always includes it', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('http://html-classic.itch.zone', ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a missing origin', () => {
    expect(originAllowed(null, ALLOWED)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects everything when the allow list is empty', () => {
    expect(originAllowed('https://breachgame.xyz', [])).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('corsHeaders', () => {
  it('echoes the exact matched origin, never a wildcard, and varies on Origin', () => {
    const h = corsHeaders('https://html-classic.itch.zone', ALLOWED);
    expect(h['access-control-allow-origin']).toBe('https://html-classic.itch.zone');
    expect(h['vary']).toBe('Origin');
  });

  it('omits allow-origin for a host that is not on the list', () => {
    const h = corsHeaders('https://evil.example', ALLOWED);
    expect(h['access-control-allow-origin']).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('omits allow-origin when there is no Origin header at all', () => {
    const h = corsHeaders(null, ALLOWED);
    expect(h['access-control-allow-origin']).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

```

`tests/progress.test.ts`:

```ts
// Per-tier mission locking: a mission unlocked on one difficulty can still be locked on another.
// The briefing's tier picker and the attempt-starting logic both read this, so a player cannot
// clear job 1 on Easy and then start job 2 on Hard before clearing job 1 there too.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { missionUnlocked, tierWithProgress, unlockedTiers } from '../src/lib/progress';
import { DEFAULT_SAVE, type SaveData } from '../src/lib/save';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';

function saveWith(completed: Partial<SaveData['completed']>): SaveData {
  return { ...DEFAULT_SAVE, completed: { ...DEFAULT_SAVE.completed, ...completed } };
}

describe('missionUnlocked', () => {
  it('the first job is always unlocked, on every tier', () => {
    const save = saveWith({});
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'easy', 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'medium', 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'hard', 0)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a later job is locked on a tier where the previous job was never cleared', () => {
    const save = saveWith({ easy: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'easy', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 } } });
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'easy', 1)).toBe(true);
    // Cleared on Easy only: still locked on Medium and Hard.
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'medium', 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(missionUnlocked(save, 'hard', 1)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('unlockedTiers', () => {
  it('reports the lock for every tier at once, so the briefing picker can disable just the locked ones', () => {
    const save = saveWith({ easy: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'easy', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 } } });
    expect(unlockedTiers(save, 1)).toEqual({ easy: true, medium: false, hard: false });
  });

  it('every tier is unlocked for job 1', () => {
    expect(unlockedTiers(saveWith({}), 0)).toEqual({ easy: true, medium: true, hard: true });
  });
});

describe('tierWithProgress', () => {
  it('is null when nothing is completed on any tier', () => {
    expect(tierWithProgress(saveWith({}))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('finds progress on a tier other than the save\'s current selection', () => {
    // DEFAULT_SAVE.tier is 'medium', but a restore can land all the completed missions on a different
    // tier than whatever the (possibly fresh) device's own default happens to be.
    const save = saveWith({ easy: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'easy', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 } } });
    expect(save.tier).toBe('medium');
    expect(tierWithProgress(save)).toBe('easy');
  });

  it('picks whichever tier has the most completed missions when more than one has progress', () => {
    const save = saveWith({
      easy: { [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'easy', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 } },
      hard: {
        [MISSIONS[0].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'hard', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 },
        [MISSIONS[1].id]: { score: 10, points: 10, tier: 'hard', seconds: 1, trace: 0, hints: 0 },
      },
    });
    expect(tierWithProgress(save)).toBe('hard');
  });
});
```

`tests/qol.test.ts`:

```ts
// Quality-of-life features: "did you mean" suggestions, help <command>, the clue log and notes,
// and the recovery nudges that change per tier.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { COMMAND_LIST, COMMANDS } from '../src/engine/commands';
import { buildSecrets, containsWord } from '../src/engine/clues';
import { applyTier } from '../src/engine/difficulty';
import { GameSession } from '../src/engine/session';
import { closest, levenshtein } from '../src/engine/suggest';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import type { Tier } from '../src/engine/types';

const FIRST = MISSIONS[0];
const PASSWORD_MISSION = MISSIONS[4];
const PORTAL_IP = '10.13.37.90';
const MIRROR_IP = '10.13.37.91';
const NEIGHBOUR_IP = '10.13.37.20';

function textsOf(s: GameSession, line: string): string[] {
  return s.input(line).lines.map((l) => l.text);
}

describe('suggest helpers', () => {
  it('computes edit distance', () => {
    expect(levenshtein('kitten', 'sitting')).toBe(3);
    expect(levenshtein('same', 'same')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('keeps only close candidates, nearest first, ignoring case', () => {
    expect(closest('LSS', COMMAND_LIST)).toEqual(['ls']);
    expect(closest('zzzzzzzz', COMMAND_LIST)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('did you mean', () => {
  it('suggests the nearest command for a typo', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'lss')).toContain('Did you mean: ls?');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'scna')).toContain('Did you mean: scan?');
  });

  it('suggests no command for junk input', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'qqqqqqqq').some((t) => t.startsWith('Did you mean'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('corrects a mistyped file name in a directory the player can read', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /srv/portal/REDME')).toContain('Did you mean: /srv/portal/README?');
  });

  it('corrects a mistyped directory segment one level at a time', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(s.suggestPath('/srv/portla/logs/access.log')).toBe('/srv/portal/logs/access.log');
  });

  it('never suggests a name the current user cannot read', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(s.suggestPath('/home/rfoster/flg.txt')).toBeNull();
    expect(textsOf(s, 'cat /home/rfoster/flg.txt').some((t) => t.startsWith('Did you mean'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('stays quiet for a path that exists', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
    expect(s.suggestPath('/srv/portal/README')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('help <command>', () => {
  it('every command has a usage, a summary and an example', () => {
    for (const name of COMMAND_LIST) {
      const spec = COMMANDS.get(name)!;
      expect(spec.usage.trim(), `${name} usage`).not.toBe('');
      expect(spec.summary.trim(), `${name} summary`).not.toBe('');
      expect(spec.example.trim(), `${name} example`).not.toBe('');
    }
  });

  it('shows usage and a real example for one command', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'help ls');
    expect(lines).toContain('ls [-la] [path...]');
    expect(lines).toContain('  e.g. ls -la /home');
  });

  it('suggests a near command name when the one asked for does not exist', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'help hlp')).toContain('Did you mean: help?');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'help zzzzzzzz')).toContain("help: no command 'zzzzzzzz'");
  });

  it('the recovery lines in the help footer show on Easy and Medium only', () => {
    const footer = (tier: Tier) => textsOf(new GameSession(FIRST, { tier }), 'help').join('\n');
    expect(footer('easy')).toContain('type ? to start account recovery');
    expect(footer('medium')).toContain('type ? to start account recovery');
    expect(footer('hard')).not.toContain('account recovery');
    expect(footer('hard')).toContain('Password prompts do not echo.');
  });
});

describe('clue log and notes', () => {
  it('records neighbour IPs automatically on Easy and Medium', () => {
    for (const tier of ['easy', 'medium'] as const) {
      const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier });
      s.input('scan');
      expect(s.clues.clues).toContainEqual({ category: 'ip', text: NEIGHBOUR_IP });
    }
  });

  it('logs each discovery once, however often it shows up', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier: 'medium' });
    s.input('scan');
    s.input('scan');
    expect(s.clues.clues.filter((c) => c.text === NEIGHBOUR_IP)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('records decoded strings automatically on Easy and Medium', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier: 'easy' });
    s.input('decode base64 aGVsbG8=');
    expect(s.clues.clues).toContainEqual({ category: 'decoded', text: 'hello' });
  });

  it('does not scan hint output, so hints do not fill the log', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION, { tier: 'medium' });
    s.input('hint');
    s.input('hint');
    expect(s.clues.clues.some((c) => c.text === MIRROR_IP)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('keeps the log empty on Hard, and lets the player add their own notes', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier: 'hard' });
    s.input('scan');
    s.input('decode base64 aGVsbG8=');
    expect(s.clues.clues).toEqual([]);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'notes').some((t) => t.includes('No automatic clue log'))).toBe(true);
    expect(textsOf(s, 'notes add the neighbour sits on the same subnet')).toContain('Note saved.');
    expect(textsOf(s, 'notes')).toContain('  1. the neighbour sits on the same subnet');
  });

  it('shows the automatic log and the player notes together on Easy and Medium', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier: 'medium' });
    s.input('scan');
    s.input('notes add check the neighbour first');
    const lines = textsOf(s, 'notes');
    expect(lines.some((t) => t.startsWith('  IP ') && t.endsWith(NEIGHBOUR_IP))).toBe(true);
    expect(lines).toContain('  1. check the neighbour first');
  });

  it('rejects an empty note and an overlong one', () => {
    const s = new GameSession(FIRST, { tier: 'hard' });
    expect(textsOf(s, 'notes add')).toContain('usage: notes add <text>');
    expect(textsOf(s, `notes add ${'x'.repeat(201)}`).some((t) => /under 200 characters/.test(t))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('finds a secret only as a whole word', () => {
    expect(containsWord(`ip ${PORTAL_IP}.`, PORTAL_IP)).toBe(true);
    expect(containsWord('ip 10.13.37.900', PORTAL_IP)).toBe(false);
    expect(containsWord('xadmin', 'admin')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('builds secrets from the remote hosts only, never the rig', () => {
    const secrets = buildSecrets(PASSWORD_MISSION);
    expect(secrets).toContainEqual({ category: 'ip', text: PORTAL_IP });
    expect(secrets).toContainEqual({ category: 'username', text: 'rfoster' });
    expect(secrets).toContainEqual({ category: 'password', text: 'Xk!29pQ-bunny' });
    expect(secrets.some((x) => x.text === 'local-only')).toBe(false);
    expect(secrets.some((x) => x.text === '10.66.0.1')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('recovery nudges per tier', () => {
  const DIRECT_SUBJECT = 'Locked out, on purpose';
  const SUBTLE_SUBJECT = 'Re: the portal';

  it('the direct recovery mail is on Easy only, with a subtler one on Medium', () => {
    const subjects = (tier: Tier) => applyTier(PASSWORD_MISSION, tier).inbox.map((m) => m.subject);
    expect(subjects('easy')).toContain(DIRECT_SUBJECT);
    expect(subjects('medium')).not.toContain(DIRECT_SUBJECT);
    expect(subjects('medium')).toContain(SUBTLE_SUBJECT);
    expect(subjects('hard')).not.toContain(DIRECT_SUBJECT);
    expect(subjects('hard')).not.toContain(SUBTLE_SUBJECT);
  });

  it('the subtler Medium mail does not name the forgot-password route', () => {
    const subtle = applyTier(PASSWORD_MISSION, 'medium').inbox.find((m) => m.subject === SUBTLE_SUBJECT)!;
    expect(subtle.body.toLowerCase()).not.toContain('forgot');
    expect(subtle.body.toLowerCase()).not.toContain('recovery');
  });

  it('the "Forgot it?" line after login shows on Easy only', () => {
    const line = (tier: Tier) => {
      const s = new GameSession(PASSWORD_MISSION, { tier });
      s.input(`connect ${PORTAL_IP}`);
      return textsOf(s, 'login rfoster').some((t) => t.includes('Forgot it?'));
    };
    expect(line('easy')).toBe(true);
    expect(line('medium')).toBe(false);
    expect(line('hard')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('the solution never reads mail, so hiding the direct mail cannot block it', () => {
    // missions.test.ts plays the solution on every tier, which is the real check.
    expect(PASSWORD_MISSION.solution.some((step) => step === 'mail' || step.startsWith('mail '))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`tests/realworld.test.ts`:

```ts
// Real-world cards: every campaign job and every daily template has a complete card.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import { dailyMission } from '../src/lib/daily';
import { realWorldFor } from '../src/lib/realworld';

describe('real-world debrief cards', () => {
  it.each(MISSIONS.map((m) => [m.codename, m] as const))('%s has a complete card', (_name, m) => {
    const card = realWorldFor(m);
    expect(card).not.toBeNull();
    expect(card!.attack.trim()).not.toBe('');
    expect(card!.example.trim()).not.toBe('');
    expect(card!.defence.trim()).not.toBe('');
  });

  it('every daily template maps to a card', () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
      const date = new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 1 + i)).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const m = dailyMission(date);
      expect(realWorldFor(m), date).not.toBeNull();
      seen.add(m.realWorldKey!);
    }
    expect(seen.size).toBe(3);
  });

  it('a card stays short enough to read in well under 30 seconds', () => {
    for (const m of MISSIONS) {
      const card = realWorldFor(m)!;
      expect(card.example.length + card.defence.length).toBeLessThan(400);
    }
  });
});
```

`tests/replay.test.ts`:

```ts
// The leaderboard replay validator: legitimate runs pass, and edited scores, fake timing, incomplete runs and
// impossible sequences are rejected. The score always comes from the replay.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  checkToken,
  percentileFor,
  suspicionFor,
  validateRun,
  MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE,
  SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD,
  TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS,
  type RunCommand,
  type RunLog,
  type TokenRow,
} from '../src/engine/replay';
import { TIERS } from '../src/engine/difficulty';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import type { Tier } from '../src/engine/types';

const MISSION = MISSIONS[0];

/** The Kali walkthrough, one command a second: how a person would play it. */
function humanRun(tier: Tier, steps: string[] = MISSION.kaliSolution): RunLog {
  return {
    missionId: MISSION.id,
    tier,
    commands: steps.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd })),
  };
}

/** A small, deterministic PRNG, so a "random" gap is reproducible across runs. */
function seededRand(seed: number): () => number {
  let s = seed;
  return () => {
    s = (s * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff;
    return s / 0x7fffffff;
  };
}

/** A fast, confident player who already knows the mission: real keystrokes, 300-600ms apart, no reading pauses. */
function fastHumanRun(tier: Tier, steps: string[] = MISSION.kaliSolution): RunLog {
  const rand = seededRand(7);
  let t = 0;
  const commands = steps.map((cmd) => {
    const row = { t, cmd };
    t += 300 + Math.round(rand() * 300);
    return row;
  });
  return { missionId: MISSION.id, tier, commands };
}

describe('replay validator: legitimate runs', () => {
  it.each(TIERS)('accepts the walkthrough on %s and computes the score itself', (tier) => {
    const r = validateRun(humanRun(tier), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!r.ok) return;
    expect(r.score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(100);
    expect(r.score).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1000);
    expect(r.trace).toBeLessThan(100);
    expect(r.points).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('accepts a run whose claimed score matches the replay', () => {
    const first = validateRun(humanRun('medium'), MISSIONS);
    expect(first.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!first.ok) return;
    expect(validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), claimedScore: first.score }, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each(TIERS)('accepts a fast human on %s: real commands 300-600ms apart, no reading pauses', (tier) => {
    const r = validateRun(fastHumanRun(tier), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok, !r.ok ? r.reason : undefined).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reports whether aliases were used, for achievements', () => {
    const old = validateRun(humanRun('medium', MISSION.solution), MISSIONS); // scan, connect, login
    expect(old.ok && old.usedAliases).toBe(true);
    const kali = validateRun(humanRun('medium', MISSION.kaliSolution), MISSIONS);
    expect(kali.ok && kali.usedAliases).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('replay validator: edited scores', () => {
  it('rejects a claimed score that differs from the replayed score', () => {
    const r = validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), claimedScore: 1000 }, MISSIONS);
    // The replay of this walkthrough is well under 1000 (it takes time), so a claimed 1000 is an edit.
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.reason).toMatch(/claimed score/);
  });
});

describe('replay validator: fake timing', () => {
  it('rejects commands closer together than a person can type them', () => {
    const run: RunLog = { missionId: MISSION.id, tier: 'medium', commands: MISSION.kaliSolution.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 50, cmd })) };
    const r = validateRun(run, MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.reason).toMatch(/too close together/);
  });

  it('rejects an entire run that is too fast overall, even with gaps that look fine', () => {
    const run: RunLog = { missionId: MISSION.id, tier: 'medium', commands: MISSION.kaliSolution.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 130, cmd })) };
    const r = validateRun(run, MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.reason).toMatch(/too fast overall/);
  });

  it('rejects timestamps that go backwards', () => {
    const commands: RunCommand[] = humanRun('medium').commands.map((c) => ({ ...c }));
    commands[5].t = commands[3].t;
    const r = validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), commands }, MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects fractional or negative timestamps', () => {
    const bad = humanRun('medium');
    bad.commands[2] = { t: 2.5, cmd: bad.commands[2].cmd };
    expect(validateRun(bad, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
    const neg = humanRun('medium');
    neg.commands[0] = { t: -1, cmd: neg.commands[0].cmd };
    expect(validateRun(neg, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('replay validator: incomplete and impossible runs', () => {
  it('rejects a run that stops before the flag is accepted', () => {
    const r = validateRun(humanRun('medium', MISSION.kaliSolution.slice(0, -2)), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.reason).toMatch(/incomplete/);
  });

  it('rejects a run that submits the wrong flag', () => {
    const steps = [...MISSION.kaliSolution.slice(0, -1), 'submit BREACH{wrong}'];
    expect(validateRun(humanRun('medium', steps), MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects commands sent after the attempt has been won', () => {
    const r = validateRun(humanRun('medium', [...MISSION.kaliSolution, 'ls']), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.reason).toMatch(/after the attempt ended/);
  });

  it('rejects a run that ends in a trace kill', () => {
    const steps = Array.from({ length: 40 }, () => 'john deadbeef');
    const r = validateRun(humanRun('easy', steps), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects an unknown mission, an unknown tier, an empty run and an oversized run', () => {
    expect(validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), missionId: 'nope' }, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), tier: 'insane' as Tier }, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), commands: [] }, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
    const huge = Array.from({ length: 2001 }, (_, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd: 'ls' }));
    expect(validateRun({ ...humanRun('medium'), commands: huge }, MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a command that is absurdly long', () => {
    const steps = [...MISSION.kaliSolution];
    steps[1] = 'echo ' + 'x'.repeat(400);
    expect(validateRun(humanRun('medium', steps), MISSIONS).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('counts commands that produced an error-kind line, for suspicion scoring', () => {
    const steps = ['bogus-command', ...MISSION.kaliSolution];
    const r = validateRun(humanRun('medium', steps), MISSIONS);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!r.ok) return;
    expect(r.errorCommands).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('run tokens: checkToken', () => {
  const claim = { playerId: 'p1', missionId: MISSION.id, tier: 'medium' as Tier, scope: 'campaign' as const };

  function baseToken(overrides: Partial<TokenRow> = {}): TokenRow {
    return {
      playerId: 'p1',
      missionId: MISSION.id,
      tier: 'medium',
      scope: 'campaign',
      date: null,
      startedAt: 0,
      usedAt: null,
      expiresAt: 1000 * 60 * 60 * 3,
      ...overrides,
    };
  }

  it('accepts a fresh, matching, unused token once enough server-side time has passed', () => {
    expect(checkToken(baseToken(), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects an already-used token', () => {
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ usedAt: 1 }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects an expired token', () => {
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ expiresAt: 100 }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects a token issued for a different player, mission, tier or scope/date', () => {
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ playerId: 'other' }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ missionId: 'other' }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ tier: 'hard' }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(checkToken(baseToken({ scope: 'daily', date: '2026-01-01' }), claim, 5000, 10_000).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects when the server saw less time pass than the run claims, beyond the tolerance', () => {
    const lastCommandMs = 60_000;
    const nowMs = lastCommandMs - TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS - 1;
    expect(checkToken(baseToken(), claim, lastCommandMs, nowMs).ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it('allows a claimed duration inside the tolerance window', () => {
    const lastCommandMs = 60_000;
    const nowMs = lastCommandMs - TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MS + 100;
    expect(checkToken(baseToken(), claim, lastCommandMs, nowMs).ok).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('suspicion scoring: suspicionFor', () => {
  it('flags a run with suspiciously regular timing, no corrections and no mistakes', () => {
    const commands: RunCommand[] = MISSION.kaliSolution.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 500, cmd, bs: 0 }));
    const run: RunLog = { missionId: MISSION.id, tier: 'medium', commands };
    const result = validateRun(run, MISSIONS);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    const suspicion = suspicionFor(run, result);
    expect(suspicion.score).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD);
    expect(suspicion.reasons.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('does not flag a normal, slightly imperfect human run', () => {
    const human = fastHumanRun('medium');
    const commands: RunCommand[] = human.commands.map((c, i) => ({ ...c, bs: i % 3 === 0 ? 1 : 0 }));
    const run: RunLog = { ...human, commands };
    const result = validateRun(run, MISSIONS);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    const suspicion = suspicionFor(run, result);
    expect(suspicion.score).toBeLessThan(SUSPICION_HIDE_THRESHOLD);
  });

  it('never flags a short run: the signals need enough commands to mean anything', () => {
    const commands: RunCommand[] = MISSION.solution.slice(0, 3).map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 500, cmd, bs: 0 }));
    const run: RunLog = { missionId: MISSION.id, tier: 'medium', commands };
    // This run doesn't finish, but suspicionFor only looks at commands/result shape, not completion.
    const fakeResult = { ok: true as const, score: 500, points: 500, seconds: 10, trace: 0, hints: 0, endingId: null, usedAliases: false, usedPipe: false, errorCommands: 0 };
    expect(suspicionFor(run, fakeResult).score).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('percentileFor', () => {
  it('reports "one of the first N" below MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE, with no percentile', () => {
    const scores = [500, 600, 700];
    expect(scores.length).toBeLessThan(MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE);
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 600)).toEqual({ percentile: null, firstOfN: 3 });
  });

  it('is exactly at the threshold: still "first of N", not yet a percentile', () => {
    const scores = [100, 200, 300, 400];
    expect(scores.length).toBe(MIN_RUNS_FOR_PERCENTILE - 1);
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 300)).toEqual({ percentile: null, firstOfN: 4 });
  });

  it('computes the percent of OTHER runs beaten, excluding the run itself from the denominator', () => {
    // 5 runs: mine (300) beats two of the other four (100, 200). 2/4 = 50%.
    const scores = [100, 200, 300, 400, 500];
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 300)).toEqual({ percentile: 50, firstOfN: null });
  });

  it('the best score on the board beats everyone else', () => {
    const scores = [100, 200, 300, 400, 1000];
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 1000)).toEqual({ percentile: 100, firstOfN: null });
  });

  it('the worst score on the board beats no one', () => {
    const scores = [100, 200, 300, 400, 1000];
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 100)).toEqual({ percentile: 0, firstOfN: null });
  });

  it('a tie is not a win: matching scores are never counted as beaten', () => {
    const scores = [300, 300, 300, 300, 300];
    expect(percentileFor(scores, 300)).toEqual({ percentile: 0, firstOfN: null });
  });
});
```

`tests/share.test.ts`:

```ts
// Share text: the Wordle-style emoji row for a daily, and the copyable caption built around it.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { dailyShareText, shareEmoji, shareText } from '../src/lib/share';

describe('shareEmoji', () => {
  it('is all green when no hints were used and trace stayed low', () => {
    expect(shareEmoji(0, 10)).toBe('🟩🟩🟩🟩');
  });

  it('marks the hint you last used yellow, and earlier ones black', () => {
    expect(shareEmoji(1, 10)).toBe('🟨🟩🟩🟩');
    expect(shareEmoji(2, 10)).toBe('⬛🟨🟩🟩');
    expect(shareEmoji(3, 10)).toBe('⬛⬛🟨🟩');
  });

  it('the final square reads the trace band, without naming the trace number', () => {
    expect(shareEmoji(0, 20)).toMatch(/🟩$/);
    expect(shareEmoji(0, 50)).toMatch(/🟨$/);
    expect(shareEmoji(0, 90)).toMatch(/⬛$/);
  });
});

describe('dailyShareText', () => {
  it('is copyable, four lines, and never spells out the flag or the solution', () => {
    const text = dailyShareText({ dailyNum: 322, tier: 'Medium', seconds: 192, trace: 34, hintsUsed: 0 });
    const lines = text.split('\n');
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(lines[0]).toBe('BREACH Daily #322 Medium');
    expect(lines[1]).toBe(shareEmoji(0, 34));
    expect(lines[2]).toBe('trace 34% · 3:12 · 0 hints');
    expect(lines[3]).not.toMatch(/^https?:\/\//);
  });

  it('singular vs plural hints', () => {
    expect(dailyShareText({ dailyNum: 1, tier: 'Easy', seconds: 0, trace: 0, hintsUsed: 1 })).toContain('1 hint');
    expect(dailyShareText({ dailyNum: 1, tier: 'Easy', seconds: 0, trace: 0, hintsUsed: 2 })).toContain('2 hints');
  });
});

describe('shareText (campaign and non-emoji share)', () => {
  it('still reads score, rank and tier the old way', () => {
    const text = shareText({ label: 'JOB 03', tier: 'hard', rank: 'ghost', score: 900 });
    expect(text).toContain('JOB 03');
    expect(text).toContain('HARD');
    expect(text).toContain('GHOST');
    expect(text).toContain('900 points');
  });
});
```

`tests/typeout.test.ts`:

```ts
// The typing-reveal effect used by the terminal. completeAll is what lets the terminal finish an
// in-progress reply instantly the moment the player starts typing, so no keystroke is lost waiting on it.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { advance, completeAll, toEntries, type Entry } from '../src/lib/typeout';
import type { Line } from '../src/engine/types';

const line = (text: string): Line => ({ text, kind: 'out' });

describe('toEntries', () => {
  it('starts at zero shown characters, except on instant speed', () => {
    const [a, b] = toEntries([line('hello'), line('world')], 'normal');
    expect(a.shown).toBe(0);
    expect(b.shown).toBe(0);
  });

  it('instant speed reveals everything right away', () => {
    const [a] = toEntries([line('hello')], 'instant');
    expect(a.shown).toBe(5);
  });
});

describe('advance', () => {
  it('reveals the first unfinished line, a few characters at a time', () => {
    const entries = toEntries([line('hello')], 'normal');
    const next = advance(entries, 2);
    expect(next[0].shown).toBe(2);
  });

  it('never overshoots the line length', () => {
    const entries: Entry[] = [{ line: line('hi'), shown: 1 }];
    expect(advance(entries, 10)[0].shown).toBe(2);
  });

  it('returns the same array once everything is fully shown, so re-rendering is skipped', () => {
    const entries = toEntries([line('hi')], 'instant');
    expect(advance(entries, 5)).toBe(entries);
  });
});

describe('completeAll', () => {
  it('jumps every line to fully shown in one step, mid-animation', () => {
    const entries: Entry[] = [
      { line: line('hello'), shown: 1 },
      { line: line('world'), shown: 0 },
    ];
    const done = completeAll(entries);
    expect(done[0].shown).toBe(5);
    expect(done[1].shown).toBe(5);
  });

  it('is a no-op (same array) once nothing is left typing, so calling it on every keystroke is cheap', () => {
    const entries = toEntries([line('hi')], 'instant');
    expect(completeAll(entries)).toBe(entries);
  });
});

```

`tests/variation.test.ts`:

```ts
// Per-run flag variation: a leaderboard run's flag changes with its token's seed, deterministically, without
// mutating the shared mission singletons, and without breaking the two missions that opt out.

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { applyFlagVariation } from '../src/lib/variation';
import { validateRun } from '../src/engine/replay';
import { MISSIONS } from '../src/missions';
import type { Tier } from '../src/engine/types';

const M01 = MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === 'm01-dead-drop')!;
const M03 = MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === 'm03-lighthouse')!;
const M04 = MISSIONS.find((m) => m.id === 'm04-blue-lines')!;

describe('applyFlagVariation', () => {
  it('varies the flag deterministically: same seed, same flag; different seed, a different one', () => {
    const a = applyFlagVariation(M01, 1);
    const b = applyFlagVariation(M01, 1);
    expect(a.flag).toBe(b.flag);
    expect(a.flag).not.toBe(M01.flag);
    expect(a.flag.startsWith('BREACH{sticky_notes_are_forever_')).toBe(true);

    const seeds = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];
    const flags = new Set(seeds.map((s) => applyFlagVariation(M01, s).flag));
    // With a pool of 16 suffixes, some of these six seeds landing on different ones is overwhelmingly likely.
    expect(flags.size).toBeGreaterThan(1);
  });

  it('never mutates the mission it varies', () => {
    const before = JSON.stringify(M01);
    applyFlagVariation(M01, 42);
    expect(JSON.stringify(M01)).toBe(before);
  });

  it('leaves m03-lighthouse and m04-blue-lines untouched: their flags are not plainly swappable', () => {
    // m03's flag is only ever stored rot13-transformed; m04's is built by the player from a log fragment.
    expect(applyFlagVariation(M03, 5)).toBe(M03);
    expect(applyFlagVariation(M04, 5)).toBe(M04);
  });

  it('a varied mission is still fully beatable, with the varied flag substituted into the final submit', () => {
    const varied = applyFlagVariation(M01, 7);
    expect(varied.flag).not.toBe(M01.flag);
    const steps = M01.solution.map((cmd) => (cmd === `submit ${M01.flag}` ? `submit ${varied.flag}` : cmd));
    const commands = steps.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd }));
    const result = validateRun({ missionId: varied.id, tier: 'medium' as Tier, commands }, [varied]);
    expect(result.ok, !result.ok ? result.reason : undefined).toBe(true);
  });

  it('replaying the original (unvaried) flag against a varied mission fails: the point of the whole feature', () => {
    const varied = applyFlagVariation(M01, 7);
    const commands = M01.solution.map((cmd, i) => ({ t: i * 1000, cmd }));
    const result = validateRun({ missionId: varied.id, tier: 'medium' as Tier, commands }, [varied]);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

```
